### [1] [Overview of ROMCIR 2026: The 6th Workshop on Reducing Online Misinformation through Credible Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05684)
*Marcos Fernández-Pichel,Marinella Petrocchi,Kevin Roitero,Marco Viviani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是关于ROMCIR研讨会背景的描述，聚焦于信息检索（IR）领域应对虚假新闻、欺骗性评论和未经验证医疗建议等各类信息污染问题的研究议程。该研讨会旨在超越传统虚假信息检测方法，探索将信息可信度与真实性因素整合为IR系统相关性维度的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字在线生态系统中充斥着各种形式的信息污染（虚假新闻、欺骗性评论、错误医疗建议），这些对个人和社会构成重大威胁。传统的信息检索系统仅关注主题相关性，而忽略了信息的可信度与真实性，因此需要重新定义相关性概念，并探索超越传统虚假信息检测方法的解决方案。

Method: 通过举办ROMCIR研讨会，召集信息检索社区共同探索以下方向：1) 识别与信息可信度和真实性相关的主客观因素；2) 将这些因素作为IR系统的基本相关性维度进行整合；3) 实现虚假信息的早期检测；4) 确保检索结果不仅真实而且对用户可解释；5) 评估大语言模型（LLMs）在无意中放大虚假信息问题方面的作用以及如何利用其支持IR系统；6) 探索人在回路（human-in-the-loop）范式的贡献。

Result: 本文为研讨会概述性论文，未报告具体实验结果。其核心成果在于界定了一个涵盖多任务、多场景的研究议程框架，提出了将真实性作为信息检索系统核心相关维度的研究范式转变。

Conclusion: 信息检索领域需要从仅关注主题相关性转向将信息可信度和真实性纳入检索核心考量，同时需要结合生成模型与人在回路范式来应对虚假信息挑战，确保用户获得既相关又真实可解释的信息。

Abstract: In the digital online ecosystem, we are surrounded by distinct forms of information pollution, posing significant threats to both individuals and society. Fake news, for instance, wields power to sway public opinion on matters of politics and finance. Deceptive reviews can either bolster or tarnish the reputation of businesses, while unverified medical advice may steer people toward harmful health practices. In light of this challenging landscape, it has become imperative to ensure that users have access to both topically relevant and factually accurate information that does not warp their perception of reality, and there has been a surge of interest in various strategies to combat misinformation through different contexts and multiple tasks. The purpose of the ROMCIR Workshop, for some years now, is precisely that of engaging the Information Retrieval community to explore potential solutions that extend beyond conventional misinformation detection approaches. Key objectives include identifying subjective and objective factors associated with information credibility and truthfulness, respectively, and integrating such factors as fundamental dimensions of relevance within IR Systems (IRSs), achieving early detection of misinformation, and ensuring that the search results retrieved are not only truthful but also explainable to the users of IRSs. Moreover, it is essential to evaluate the role of generative models such as Large Language Models (LLMs) in inadvertently amplifying misinformation problems, and how they can be used to support IRSs, together with the contribution that the human-in-the-loop paradigm can have in this context.

</details>


### [2] [What Price Fairness? Evaluating Energy - Fairness - Accuracy Trade-off in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05759)
*Abhirup Mitra,Oleg Lesota,Antonela Tommasel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了推荐系统中供应商侧公平性干预的计算和环境成本，比较了in-processing、graph-level reweighting和post-processing三种方法在多种模型、两个数据集和两种硬件设置下的表现，发现公平性的绿色成本并不均匀：post-processing将成本转移到重复服务阶段，而in-processing和graph-level方法虽避免重排序开销，但在模型、数据集和硬件间变化显著。作者呼吁将公平性感知推荐系统的评估视为准确性、公平性和计算成本之间的三方权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性感知推荐系统仅以准确性和公平性作为评价指标，忽略了计算成本和环境影响。由于公平性干预可能以不同方式影响推荐成本，如训练时方法修改模型优化，后处理方法增加推理时计算，而现有研究缺乏对这些成本的系统性测量。

Method: 在多种模型、两个数据集和两种硬件设置下，系统比较in-processing、graph-level reweighting和post-processing三类公平性干预方法，分别测量训练和推理阶段的推荐质量、供应商侧曝光度和能耗。

Result: 公平性的绿色成本不是均匀的：post-processing将成本转移至重复的serving阶段；in-processing和graph-level方法避免了重排序开销，但在不同模型、数据集和硬件之间表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 公平性感知推荐系统的评估应超越准确性与公平性的二维框架，纳入计算成本，形成准确性、公平性、计算成本三方的权衡考量。公平性干预方法的选择需要结合具体部署环境进行综合评估。

Abstract: Fairness-aware recommender systems aim to mitigate systematic imbalances in recommendation outcomes, including how visibility, relevance, and opportunities are distributed among users, items, and providers. However, these systems are usually evaluated in terms of accuracy and fairness alone, while their computational and environmental costs remain largely invisible. This omission matters because fairness interventions may affect the cost of recommendation in different ways. Training-time methods modify model optimization, post-processing methods add computation at inference time, and both may depend on the model, dataset, hardware, and deployment setting. We examine whether provider-side fairness in recommendation comes with a measurable green cost. We compare in-processing, graph-level reweighting and post-processing interventions across multiple models, two datasets, and two hardware settings. We measure recommendation quality, provider-side exposure, and energy consumption separately across training and inference stages. Our results show that the green cost of fairness is not uniform, post-processing shifts cost to repeated serving, while in-processing and graph-level methods avoid re-ranking overhead but vary substantially across models, datasets, and hardware. Findings call for evaluating fairness-aware recommendation as a three-way trade-off between accuracy, fairness, and computational cost.

</details>


### [3] [Do All Nodes Benefit Equally from Knowledge Graphs? Adaptive Node-Aware KG Fusion for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05909)
*Jaehyun Park,Minseo Jeon,Daewon Gwak,Sunuk Kim,Hanvit Lee,Jinhong Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaKG，一种自适应节点级KG融合的推荐方法，通过分别编码交互图和知识图谱，并利用对抗扰动下的CF信号稳定性来判断每个节点对KG信息的依赖程度，从而实现自适应融合，缓解传统KG感知推荐中KG信号被无差别应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG感知推荐方法不加区分地将KG信号应用到所有节点上，忽略了部分节点的CF信号本身已足够可靠这一事实，导致无差别地引入KG辅助知识可能扭曲本就可靠的协同过滤信号，无法充分发挥KG的补充作用。

Method: AdaKG采用三个核心步骤：(1) 用视图专属编码器分别编码交互图和知识图谱，避免两类异质信号混合时的相互干扰；(2) 通过测量节点CF信号在小对抗扰动下的稳定性来估算每个节点对KG的依赖程度，对越不稳定的节点赋予更大的KG贡献权重；(3) 在共享空间中自适应对齐两个视图的嵌入，并根据节点级依赖权重进行融合。

Result: 实验表明AdaKG相比基线方法取得了更强的推荐性能，并且验证了自适应融合策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过按节点自适应分配KG贡献而非全局统一地融合知识信号，AdaKG能够更好地兼顾CF信号与KG辅助知识，在KG感知推荐中取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: KG-aware recommendation has been widely studied to alleviate data sparsity by using knowledge graphs (KGs), which represent items, entities, and their relations as graphs and provide item-side knowledge. However, existing methods incorporate item knowledge without considering how much each user or item node should rely on it. As a result, they apply KG signals indiscriminately across nodes, even to nodes whose collaborative filtering (CF) signals from the interaction graph (IG) are already reliable. In this paper, we propose AdaKG (Adaptive Node-Aware KG Fusion), a novel KG-aware recommendation method that adaptively adjusts the contribution of auxiliary knowledge for each node. Since user-item interactions and item knowledge provide different types of signals, directly mixing them can distort the CF signals. To avoid this, AdaKG separately encodes the IG and KG with view-specific encoders, allowing each view to capture its own information. It then estimates how strongly each node should rely on item knowledge by measuring the stability of its CF signals under small adversarial perturbations, assigning a larger KG contribution to less stable nodes. Finally, AdaKG adaptively aligns the IG and KG embeddings in a shared space and fuses them according to the estimated node-wise reliance. Through experiments, we show that AdaKG achieves strong performance compared with its baselines and the effectiveness of our adaptive fusion strategy.

</details>


### [4] [Visual Analysis of LLM-based Entity Resolution from Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06037)
*Siyu Wu,Yi Yang,Weize Wu,Ruiming Li,Yuyang Zhang,Ge Wang,Huobin Tan,Zipeng Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大语言模型（LLM）与可视化分析技术相结合的人机协同管道，用于从科学文献中批量提取领域特定实体。以金属有机框架（MOFs）材料领域的CSD-MOFs数据集为例，通过人机协同的迭代优化，将单文档实体解析准确率提升了约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统命名实体识别方法在从大规模科学文献中提取领域特定实体时存在固有局限性。虽然GPT-4等大语言模型在实体解析方面显著优于传统机器学习方法，但如何将LLM智能与领域专家的知识有效整合，实现对批量实体解析过程的可控、可解释优化仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出一个集成先进LLM与可视化交互设计的视觉分析管道：(1) 针对MOFs材料科学领域及CSD-MOFs大型数据集，通过与领域专家协作获取标注良好的合成段落；(2) 采用人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的迭代精化机制，利用可视化分析技术实现错误分析和检索增强生成（RAG）算法的可解释性，使领域专家能交互式地将洞察融入LLM智能中。

Result: 通过RAG示例选择的案例研究评估，该人机协同方法使单文档实体解析准确率提升了约30%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将LLM能力与可视化分析技术及领域专家的人机协同相结合，能够显著改善科学文献中的领域特定实体提取效果，为大规模实体解析提供了有效的人机协同视觉分析框架。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the visual analytics support for extracting domain-specific entity from extensive scientific literature, a task with inherent limitations using traditional named entity resolution methods. With the advent of large language models (LLMs) such as GPT-4, significant improvements over conventional machine learning approaches have been achieved due to LLM's capability on entity resolution integrate abilities such as understanding multiple types of text. This research introduces a new visual analysis pipeline that integrates these advanced LLMs with versatile visualization and interaction designs to support batch entity resolution. Specifically, we focus on a specific material science field of Metal-Organic Frameworks (MOFs) and a large data collection namely CSD-MOFs. Through collaboration with domain experts in material science, we obtain well-labeled synthesis paragraphs. We propose human-in-the-loop refinement over the entity resolution process using visual analytics techniques, which allows domain experts to interactively integrate insights into LLM intelligence, including error analysis and interpretation of the retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) algorithm. Our evaluation through the case study of example selection for RAG demonstrates that this human-machine collaborative approach improved single-document entity resolution accuracy by approximately 30%.

</details>


### [5] [ExpertLens: Visualizing Embedding Spaces for Post-Hoc Explainability in MoE Enhanced Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06155)
*Effrosyni Sokli,Isaac Roberts,Alexander Schulz,Barbara Hammer,Gabriella Pasi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpertLens，一个针对MoE增强型稠密检索器的后验可解释性框架，通过判别性嵌入空间可视化和自动提取的概念激活向量，将解释视角从局部标量特征重要性转向表示层面的全局可解释性，揭示专家路由如何塑造嵌入空间结构并影响检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经检索模型（包括稠密检索器）虽性能卓越但作为黑盒缺乏可解释性。现存的针对神经排名器的后验解释方法主要聚焦于特征级别的归因，不足以捕捉学习到的嵌入空间的复杂性，因此需要一种从表示层面进行全局解释的新框架。

Method: ExpertLens采用判别性嵌入空间可视化技术，结合自动提取的概念激活向量（Concept Activation Vectors, CAVs），分析MoE增强型稠密检索器中专家路由对嵌入空间形成和检索有效性的影响。通过对专家子空间的分析，揭示主导专家与少数专家的功能分工及子空间的多语义概念布局。

Result: 在五个IR基准和两个MoE增强型稠密检索器上的实验表明：专家路由一致性地改善了嵌入空间结构，将查询与其相关文档定位到更优的几何邻域；专家子空间分析揭示了通用的主导专家，以及表现出不同语言学专业化的少数专家，子空间按多语义概念相似度排列。

Conclusion: ExpertLens为MoE增强型稠密检索器提供了有效的表示级全局可解释性分析框架，揭示了专家路由机制如何影响嵌入空间结构和检索性能，相关代码已公开。

Abstract: Neural models, including dense retrievers, have been widely adopted in Information Retrieval (IR), often delivering state-of-the-art performance. Despite their effectiveness, these models operate as black boxes, limiting the interpretability of their ranking decisions. Existing post-hoc explainability methods for neural rankers primarily focus on feature-level attributions, which can be insufficient to capture the complexity of learned embedding spaces. In this work, we propose ExpertLens, a post-hoc explainability framework for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-enhanced dense retrievers that shifts focus from local scalar feature importance to representation-level global interpretability. ExpertLens leverages discriminative embedding space visualizations jointly with automatically extracted Concept Activation Vectors to reveal how expert routing drives embedding space formulation and retrieval effectiveness. Experiments across five IR benchmarks and two MoE-enhanced dense retrievers show that expert routing consistently improves embedding space structure, positioning queries and their relevant documents into better-defined geometric neighborhoods. Analysis of expert subspaces further reveals general-purpose dominant experts, along with minority experts exhibiting distinct linguistic specialization, with subspaces arranged according to multi-semantic concept similarity. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [6] [Closing the Long-Short View Gap in Sequential Recommendation without Cached History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06219)
*Lingfeng Shi,Chengkai Huang,Lina Yao,James Caverlee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文解决序列推荐中"训练用长序列、推理用短序列"导致的性能差距问题，提出一个无需额外基础设施的两阶段微调框架：第一阶段用角度相似度替代点积并修改softmax以缓解前缀位置偏差，第二阶段仅微调bias和LayerNorm参数，在标准序列推荐骨干上即可显著缩小长-短视角性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐模型通常在长用户历史上训练，但实际推理时受实时效率约束无法使用等长序列，直接截短会导致严重性能下降。现有方案（持久化每用户状态）虽有效但引入额外基础设施开销，且在冷启动场景中帮助有限。因此需要一种轻量、无需额外存储的方案来弥合长序列训练与短序列推理之间的性能鸿沟。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段，用角度相似度评分替换常用的点积操作，并采用修改后的softmax以消除前缀位置偏差——这两个问题分别源于几何性质和数据集稀疏性；(2) 第二阶段，仅对标准序列推荐骨干中通用的bias和LayerNorm组件进行微调以进一步提升性能。两个阶段均由精心设计的损失函数引导。

Result: 在两个代表性序列推荐骨干和三个公开数据集上的大量实验证明了该框架的有效性，表明无需引入持久化状态或额外基础设施，即可显著缩小长序列训练与短序列推理之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了由于几何性质和数据集稀疏性导致的两个结构缺陷，并证明通过有针对性的两阶段微调（角度相似度+修改softmax、仅微调bias/LayerNorm）可以有效解决长-短序列视角下的性能下降问题，为序列推荐的实用化部署提供了轻量级方案。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders are typically trained on long user histories to capture rich behavioral signals, yet serving with training-length sequences is often impractical due to real-time efficiency constraints. Directly using only recent behaviors leads to a severe performance drop. To bridge this gap, existing approaches compress user histories into persistent per-user states, storing and retrieving them at inference time; while effective, they impose non-trivial infrastructure overhead and offer little remedy in cold-start scenarios. In this paper, we empirically identify two structural flaws rooted in geometric properties and dataset sparsity, and propose a novel two-stage framework to close the long-short-view performance gap. Specifically, in the first stage, we replace the commonly used dot-product with angular similarity scoring and leverage a modified softmax to counter prefix position bias. In the second stage, we fine-tune only bias and LayerNorm components, which are universal to standard sequential backbones, for further improvement. Both stages are guided by carefully designed learning objectives. Extensive experiments on two representative backbones across three public datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [7] [Relevance is not enough: A Communication-Oriented Retrieval System for Consequential Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06222)
*Avina Nakarmi,Naga Datha Saikiran Battula,Anthony Diaz,Aritra Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究面向公众科学问答的检索增强生成系统，聚焦健康、安全与环境等公共科学问题。通过一个公共水质沟通系统，发现"切题"的答案若缺乏解释和上下文仍不充分，且需考虑风险信息的情绪影响。系统按推理类型分类问题、生成追问识别缺失证据，区分已知与不确定内容，并以人物角色风格改写科学信息。消融实验表明系统使用少一个数量级的上下文且提升人类评分完整性，但标准相关度指标仅能解释约1%的人类完整性评分差异，说明完整性是与现有指标截然不同的以人为中心的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强生成系统主要优化事实正确性和切题性，但忽略了非专业用户在面对影响其生活和决策的科学问题（如健康、安全、环境）时，还需要解释、背景信息和对情绪因素的考量。标准RAG评价指标未能捕捉"完整性"这一以人为中心的维度。

Method: 系统首先按推理类型（如因果推理vs政策推理）对每个问题分类，然后生成追问问题以识别缺失证据并检索补充。系统包含区分已知与不确定信息的组件，以及将科学细节改写为通俗语言的组件，后者采用基于人设的风格（如关心邻居、行政官员）来调整语气和可读性。通过160多个问题的消融实验评测，并与社区成员共同设计完整性指标，使用微调的有监督评判模型进行评估。

Result: 消融实验表明该系统在多个配置下使用比基线少一个数量级的上下文，并在若干配置中提升了人类评分的完整性。标准相关度评分只能解释约1%的人类完整性评分变化，即使微调后的评判模型也仅与人类判断适度对齐，说明现有指标无法可靠地捕获完整性这一以人为中心的客观目标。

Conclusion: 面向非专家的科学问答系统需要超越"切题正确"的范式，必须涵盖解释、上下文和情绪敏感度。完整性是与传统相关度或事实准确性不同的独立的人本目标，需要新的评测指标和系统设计来满足公众用户在真实决策场景中的需求。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly answer scientific questions about health, safety, and the environment. But most retrieval-augmented generation systems are tuned to provide factually correct, on-topic answers rather than to help non-experts understand what those answers mean for their lives and decisions. We focus on consequential scientific questions whose results directly shape people's lives and study them through a public water-quality communication system, where residents and community leaders interpret the findings and choose actions. Their experiences show that on-topic answers can still be insufficient without explanation and context and that the emotional weight of risk information cannot be ignored. Our system first classifies each question by reasoning type (for example, causal versus policy-based), then generates follow-up questions to identify missing evidence and retrieve it. One component clearly distinguishes between what is known and what is uncertain, while another rewrites scientific details into accessible language, using persona-based styles, such as a caring neighbor or an administrative official, to adapt tone and readability. Ablations on over $160$ questions show that the system uses $\textit{an order of magnitude less context}$ and, in several configurations, improves human-rated completeness. A completeness metric co-designed with community members and a fine-tuned learned judge reveal that standard relevance scores explain about $1\%$ of variation in human completeness ratings, and even the tuned judge only moderately aligns with humans, indicating that completeness is a distinct human-centered objective that current metrics do not reliably capture.

</details>


### [8] [TaxoConf: Taxonomy-Guided Automatic Conference Program Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06604)
*Daomin Ji,Zhifeng Bao,Junhao Gan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TaxoConf是一个基于会议特定主题分类法来组织学术会议程序的系统。它为接受论文构建规范化的多父主题分类，通过前端特定主题表示论文，并利用特异性加权的最优传输距离进行二进制整数规划，在满足所有硬约束的前提下最小化会话内距离。在2025年四场会议基准测试中，TaxoConf取得最高会话连贯性（4.62/5）和与人工策划会话最接近的一致性（NMI 0.761），且无约束违规。该系统已部署于SIGIR 2026，保持91.8%的口头报告分配和全部海报程序，47名与会者评分4.26/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议程序组织是一项劳动密集型任务：论文需要被分组为具有主题连贯性的技术会话，同时要满足硬性操作约束（如作者冲突、时间限制等），而现有方法很少能同时实现主题连贯性和约束满足这两个目标。

Method: TaxoConf提出一个三阶段方法：(1) 为接受的论文构建规范化的多父级主题分类树，每篇论文用其最具体的主题前沿（frontier）来表示；(2) 从该分类法推导出特异性加权的最优传输（optimal-transport）距离度量，用于评估论文间的语义差异；(3) 将论文分配到会话问题建模为二进制整数规划，在最小化会话内距离的同时满足所有硬约束。

Result: 在四个2025年会议的基准上，TaxoConf获得最高会话连贯性（满分5分制下4.62分），与人工策划会话最接近（NMI 0.761），且无任何约束违反。在实际部署SIGIR 2026中，组织者仅需少量编辑即接受初始输出，保留了91.8%的口头报告分配和全部海报程序，无需修正硬约束违规；47名参会者现场调查将整体程序质量评为满分5分制下的4.26分。

Conclusion: TaxoConf通过引入会议特定主题分类法和最优传输距离，成功实现了程序组织的自动化和高连贯性，既能满足硬约束又能在实际大规模会议（如SIGIR 2026）中得到组织者和参会者的高度认可，且系统已公开可用。

Abstract: Conference program organization, the task of assembling accepted papers into a technical program, is labor-intensive. Papers must be grouped into topically coherent sessions under hard operational constraints, and existing methods rarely achieve both at once. To address this problem, we present TaxoConf, a system that organizes conference programs around a conference-specific topic taxonomy. TaxoConf constructs a canonicalized multi-parent taxonomy over the accepted papers and represents each paper by its frontier of most specific topics. It derives a specificity-weighted optimal-transport distance between papers from this taxonomy, then solves a binary integer programming problem that assigns papers to sessions by minimizing within-session distance subject to all hard constraints. On benchmarks built from four 2025 conferences, TaxoConf attains the highest session coherence (4.62 out of 5) and the closest agreement with human-curated sessions (NMI 0.761) while incurring no constraint violations. TaxoConf has also been deployed to generate the technical program of SIGIR 2026, where the organizers accepted the initial output with only a few edits, preserving 91.8% of oral assignments and the entire poster program while requiring no correction of hard-constraint violations, and an on-site survey of 47 attendees rated the overall program quality 4.26 out of 5. The system is publicly available at https://taxoconf.com.

</details>


### [9] [EviMap: Evidence-Grounded Hierarchical Topic Maps for Exploring Unlabeled Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06664)
*Zhiyin Tan,Changxu Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviMap是一个交互式系统，为研究者提供可审计的主题地图，通过将证据短语（而非整篇文档）组织为三级层次结构（aspects、groups、topics），使每个标签都可追溯到原始文本中的支持证据段落，在6个语料库（2,108-101,699篇文档）上展现工作流并优于平铺及层次化LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究团队在探索未知自由文本语料（如评论、报告、领域文档）时，尚无标签或查询先验，此时生成的第一张主题地图直接影响用户注意力和后续分析方向，因此必须可靠可验证。现有方案存在规模与可验证性之间的矛盾：定性编码保留证据但耗时；搜索需预设查询；聚类和主题模型可扩展但标签需用户自行解读；单次LLM摘要虽流畅但难以复现或审计。亟需一个既具规模又能溯源审计的主题概览工具。

Method: EviMap包含三个关键设计：(1) 使用模型生成的上下文描述语料库并推测利益相关者关切以指导证据提取；(2) 提取文档内证据短语，而非整篇文档，作为分析基本单元；(3) 采用嵌入聚类缩小搜索空间，交由LLM进行细粒度语义判断，将证据组织为aspects→groups→topics三级主题层次。每个节点均可回溯至原始文本中的短语跨度，支持主题下钻、原文高亮检视以及双主题交集检索。

Result: 在六个异质语料库上（涵盖2,108至101,699篇文档）进行了系统演示，并与平铺式和层次化LLM基线进行了对比。结果表明EviMap通过将每个标签锚定于逐字源文本片段，使主题地图不仅可读而且可验证。

Conclusion: EviMap通过以带证据的证据短语为分析单元、两级聚类缩小LLM语义判断空间的策略，弥合了规模与可验证性之间的鸿沟，使无标签语料的探索性主题分析既高效又可审计。系统代码、演示视频和交互面板已公开。

Abstract: Research teams and organizations often explore unfamiliar free-text collections, from survey comments and reviews to reports and domain documents, before labels, queries or coding schemes exist. At this stage, the first thematic map shapes what users notice, prioritize and carry into downstream analysis, so it should be trusted only insofar as it can be verified. Existing options force a trade-off between scale and verifiability. Qualitative coding preserves evidence but is slow. Search presupposes a query. Clustering and topic models scale but produce labels users must interpret. One-shot large language model (LLM) summaries are fluent yet difficult to reproduce or audit. We present EviMap, an interactive system providing researchers and practitioners with an auditable thematic overview of such corpora. Guided by model-generated context describing the corpus and hypothesized stakeholder concerns, EviMap extracts within-document evidence phrases and organizes them, rather than whole documents, into a three-level map of aspects, groups and fine-grained topics. Embedding-based clustering narrows the search space for finer semantic judgments by the LLM. Each node traces back to supporting phrase spans, so documents link to topics through evidence they contain and users can audit labels against the original text. Users can start from a top-level corpus map, drill into topics, inspect highlighted evidence in original documents, and combine two topics to find documents discussing both. We demonstrate this workflow across six heterogeneous corpora spanning 2,108 to 101,699 documents, with a comparison against flat and hierarchical LLM baselines. By grounding every label in verbatim source spans, EviMap makes a topic map not just readable, but verifiable. Code, demo video, and interactive dashboard are available at https://github.com/zhiyintan/EviMap.

</details>


### [10] [Who Anchors AI Overviews in Health? Baidu, Google, and the Geography of Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06798)
*Mingyue Zha,Ho-Chun Herbert Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过审计1,920个健康查询在12个国家和4种语言中的表现，系统比较了Google和Baidu的AI Overview生成式搜索系统，发现两者存在垂直整合倾向、较小国家获得较少本地引用、使用本地语言可使本地引用占比提高3.5至13.5倍，且健康免责声明在不同语言和文化间呈现多维差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正迅速融入传统搜索系统，但缺乏对跨平台、跨地域和跨语言信息差异的系统性审计研究。健康信息搜索直接关系到用户的健康决策，亟需了解AI生成搜索在信息呈现上的偏差与不均衡。

Method: 对Google和Baidu的AI Overview系统进行大规模审计，覆盖12个国家和4种语言，共审计1,920个健康查询。通过比较不同国家、语言以及健康话题（按严重程度和争议性分层，包括中医案例）来衡量信息的锚定效应、来源多样性和免责声明差异。

Result: Google和Baidu均表现出垂直整合倾向，将用户引向其自有平台而非多样化的主要信源。本地化程度较低的小国获得的国内来源引用较少。使用国家官方语言而非英文提问时，本地引用占比提升约3.5至13.5倍。健康免责声明是多维度的，随语言和文化而不同。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索正在影响健康信息的获取方式，亟需对这类影响关键健康决策的系统进行文化敏感的监督与治理。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is being rapidly incorporated into traditional search systems, yet scant work audits the information disparities across platforms, geography, and languages. We address this gap by comparing Google and Baidu's AI Overview systems for health queries, and measure informational anchors that emerge. Auditing 1,920 health queries across 12 countries and 4 languages, we find that Google and Baidu exhibit vertical integration, routing users toward their own company platforms in AI Overviews rather than a diverse set of primary sources. Smaller, lower-localization countries receive fewer domestically sourced references for health queries. Issuing the same query in a country's official language rather than English raises the share of locally sourced citations approximately 3.5- to 13.5-fold. Comparing queries across health topics of varying severity and controversy, including Traditional Chinese Medicine as an example, we also show that health disclaimers are multidimensional and vary across language and culture. We discuss how generative search influences access to health information, and the urgent need for culturally-aware oversight of these systems that influence critical health decisions.

</details>


### [11] [Measuring GEO Visibility: Prompt Corpora Define the Answer Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06811)
*Olivier Martinez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对生成引擎优化（GEO）可见度评分进行批判性综述，指出GEO评分体系依赖于提示词语料库、权重和评分规则等人为选择，这些选择可能使评分结果偏离真实用户需求。文章提出了一个系统化框架，涵盖情境标注、提示措辞、执行条件、权重设定和评分规则等要素，并引入部分识别与规范敏感性概念来区分权重未知和权重约定差异两种情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEO评分在生成式AI检索优化领域广泛应用，但现有评分方法缺乏统一的理论框架，提示词措辞、权重设定和评分指令等多种主观选择会显著影响评分结果。作者旨在批判性审视这些选择如何定义GEO评分的测量对象，揭示评分可能偏离实际用户需求的问题，并为评估GEO评分的有效性提供方法论基础。

Method: 本文采用批判性综述方法，综合了测量指标效度研究、总调查误差理论和信息检索评估三方面文献，构建了一个GEO评分框架。框架明确了需说明的情境标注、提示词表述、执行条件、权重设定和评分规则等要素；当权重未知或待定时，采用部分识别方法报告兼容数据的评分配置集，以区分数据兼容性与权重约定差异导致的变异。此外，通过受控文献语境下有无来源的答案对比来界定来源贡献，区别于对整个引擎的干预实验。所有计算均可复现，未报告新实验。

Result: 文章提出了一个用于GEO评分分析的系统化框架，识别出评分过程中影响测量结果的关键选择维度；定义了部分识别（数据兼容的评分集合）与规范敏感性（权重约定造成的变异）之间的区分；论证了单一引文不足以证明来源贡献，并界定了在受控文献语境中评估来源贡献的方法，该语境区别于对完整引擎的干预实验。

Conclusion: GEO评分本质上测量的是人为定义的"答案市场"而非真实用户需求，其有效性取决于每个选择环节的合理性。所提出的框架为GEO评分的透明度、可复现性和批判性评估提供了方法论工具，但框架的经验有效性尚未通过实验验证，需要后续研究加以检验。

Abstract: GEO (generative engine optimization) visibility scores aggregate source appearances, citations, or brand mentions in generated answers. The prompt corpus selects the situations evaluated, while weights determine their relative importance. Together they define an "answer market" that need not represent actual user demand.
  Prompt wording can alter retrieval, competing sources, and generated answers. Scoring then requires identifying the appearances, citations, or mentions of interest. If a language model performs this task, its instruction can change the score assigned to an unchanged answer. Our critical survey examines how these choices help define what a GEO score measures. It draws on research into whether indicators measure the intended phenomenon, total survey error, and information retrieval evaluation. The framework specifies situation annotation, prompt formulations, execution conditions, weights, and scoring rules.
  When weights are unknown or remain to be chosen, the framework reports sets of admissible scores. It distinguishes values compatible with data and assumptions about a target population (partial identification) from variation across weighting conventions (normative sensitivity).
  A citation alone does not establish a source's contribution. The article defines a comparison of answers generated with and without a source in a controlled documentary context, distinct from an intervention on the full engine with competing sources. The framework is supported by reproducible calculations. No new experiments are reported; its general empirical validity remains to be assessed.

</details>


### [12] [FunnelAudit: Responsibility Auditing in Multi-Route Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06964)
*Jie Li,Dudu Luo,Jiayang Niu,Ke Deng,Yongli Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FunnelAudit 是一个用于多路由推荐系统incident级责任审计的可执行框架。它通过问责契约定义争议事件、控制项与回放语义，评估所有允许的控制配置并计算分级实际责任，找出使每个控制项成为关键的最小保持结果的情景。在三个真实数据集、25.9万个用户目标的实验中，4.24-16.24%的事件存在责任控制项，其中92.55-99.64%需要非空情景（单一控制项消融只能恢复0.36-7.45%）。该框架在912万次检查中可复现所有结果，且与混合整数线性规划的解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路由推荐系统将检索、分配、融合和排序结合，导致单个引入或排除难以审计。路由重叠会隐藏逐次消融的效果，而冻结下游阶段会产生与服务行为不一致的反事实。现有方法缺乏对推荐系统进行incident级责任归属的系统性审计框架。

Method: FunnelAudit 引入可执行的责任审计框架：1) 问责契约定义争议的Top-K事件、控制项与所有者、允许的参考操作和回放语义；2) 评估每个允许的控制配置，并计算分级实际责任（graded actual responsibility）；3) 寻找最小保持结果的情景（smallest outcome-preserving contingency）使每个控制项关键；4) 证书记录该情景及配对的服务执行作为验证依据。在两级九路由漏斗（固定并集、加权配额分配或加权倒排秩融合）加SASRec排序的设置中实例化。

Result: 在三个真实交互数据集上分析了258,809个用户目标事件：4.24-16.24%存在负责任的控制项；在负责任的事件-控制项对中，92.55-99.64%需要非空情景，单控制消融仅恢复0.36-7.45%的事件。仅0.31-2.39%事件的事实结果差异即可造成21.44-54.05%的Jaccard距离。独立回放复现了所有9,121,792个检查的目标世界结果；穷举搜索与通用混合整数线性规划在所有抽样判断上一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明显式服务语义和可核查的见证（checkable witnesses）对推荐系统问责至关重要。FunnelAudit 通过提供可执行审计框架和证书机制，弥补了传统单一路由消融分析的不足，为推荐系统责任归属提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Multi-route recommender systems combine retrieval, allocation, fusion, and ranking, making individual inclusions and exclusions difficult to audit. Route overlap can hide effects from one-at-a-time ablations, while freezing downstream stages produces counterfactuals inconsistent with serving behavior.
  We introduce FunnelAudit, an executable framework for incident-level responsibility auditing. An accountability contract specifies the disputed Top-K event, controls and owners, permitted reference actions, and replay semantics. FunnelAudit evaluates every permitted control configuration and applies graded actual responsibility to find the smallest outcome-preserving contingency that makes each control pivotal. Its certificate records the contingency and paired serving executions needed to verify the judgment. We instantiate the framework in two-stage, nine-route funnels using fixed union, weighted quota allocation, or weighted reciprocal-rank fusion, followed by SASRec ranking.
  Across 258,809 user-target incidents from three real interaction datasets, 4.24-16.24% admit a responsible control. Among responsible incident-control pairs, 92.55-99.64% require a nonempty contingency, so single-control ablation recovers only 0.36-7.45%. Policies differing in factual outcomes on only 0.31-2.39% of incidents yield 21.44-54.05% Jaccard distance between responsible-route sets on matched exclusions. Independent replay reproduces all 9,121,792 checked target-world outcomes; exhaustive search and a generic mixed-integer linear program agree with every sampled judgment. These findings demonstrate the importance of explicit serving semantics and checkable witnesses for recommender accountability.

</details>


### [13] [Tracing Query Expansion Effects through Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06968)
*Fangan Dong,Weiran Shi,Zhiwei Xu,Xuri Ge,Ben He,Xin Xin,Zhumin Chen,Ying Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征追踪查询扩展（QE）在稠密检索器内部的影响机制。实验表明，有效的QE在层间集中的稀疏潜变量上引起变化，这些潜变量与检索意图和实体属性对齐；基于SAE激活引导的干预比随机干预或原始QE在四个基准上产生更一致的检索改进，为无需查询重写或模型微调的精确检索行为调控提供了轻量级方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询扩展是信息检索中的关键技术，但在现代稠密检索中效果往往不可靠，尤其是对于未经重新训练的现成强检索器。以往研究主要聚焦于扩展质量、语义漂移或最终检索结果，但很少从内部机制角度解释QE如何改变稠密检索器——缺乏对QE如何在模型内部表征层面生效的可解释性分析。

Method: 使用配对的原查询与扩展查询，通过稀疏自编码器对检索器各层的表征进行逐层分解，得到稀疏潜变量激活；从扩展引起的激活偏移中识别QE相关的潜变量；用自然语言描述和检索案例对这些潜变量进行语义解释；最后利用SAE的激活引导（activation steering）验证这些潜变量的因果有效性，并与随机干预和原始QE进行对比。

Result: 有效的QE在稀疏潜变量中诱发与检索意图和实体属性对齐的层间集中变化，而非仅扰动最终的查询嵌入。SAE激活引导能够比随机干预或原始QE更一致地提升四个基准上的检索效果，证明识别的潜变量具有因果有效性。

Conclusion: SAE能够解释QE对稠密检索器的内部影响机制，并提供了一种无需查询重写或检索器微调的轻量级替代方案，用于精确调控检索行为。这为可解释的检索行为调制开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Query expansion (QE) is a critical technique in information retrieval that enriches underspecified queries with additional textual context. However, its effect is often unreliable in modern dense retrieval, especially for strong off-the-shelf retrievers without retraining. Existing studies mainly examine expansion quality, semantic drift, or retrieval outcomes, but rarely explain how QE changes dense retrievers internally. In this work, we trace QE effects through sparse autoencoder (SAE) features. Using paired original and expanded queries, we decompose layer-wise retriever representations into sparse latent activations, identify QE-related latents from expansion-induced activation shifts, and interpret them with natural-language descriptions and retrieval cases. Our analysis shows that effective QE induces layer-concentrated changes in sparse latents aligned with retrieval intent and entity attributes, rather than only perturbing final query embeddings. SAE-based activation steering further validates these latents improve retrieval more consistently than random interventions or vanilla QE across four benchmarks, suggesting that SAEs can explain QE effects and offer a lightweight option for precise retrieval behavior modulation without query rewriting or retriever fine-tuning.

</details>


### [14] [EAGER: Enrich-and-Align Generative Query Recommendation from Clicked Items in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07143)
*Shuwei Yuan,Mingqian Ding,Luxin Liu,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EAGER框架，一个两阶段生成式查询推荐系统；通过"富化-对齐"流程，从用户点击的商品生成查询建议，旨在克服传统日志挖掘的局限性和通用LLM缺乏平台知识的缺点，已在实际电商平台部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台需要在用户信息流中展示可点击的查询建议以帮助用户细化或扩展意图。现有方法存在两方面不足：基于历史日志挖掘的方法局限于过去行为且无法捕捉长尾个性化意图；直接使用现成LLM的方法由于缺乏平台专属知识，生成的查询虽然流畅但与真实点击行为脱节。

Method: 提出两阶段框架EAGER：(1) 富化阶段(enrichment stage)：采用四阶段课程式监督微调(SFT)，从仅含商品信息逐步过渡到用户条件化，推理深度从直接到思维链(CoT)递进；每个阶段引入道理增强(rationale augmentation)、多样性正则化(diversity regularization)和自蒸馏(self-distillation)。(2) 对齐阶段(alignment stage)：通过GRPO进行后训练，采用混合奖励，包含多个基于规则的业务信号和偏好感知的点击奖励。

Result: 大量离线实验和在线A/B测试验证了EAGER的有效性，该系统已在大型电商平台生产环境中部署。

Conclusion: EAGER通过结合课程式SFT的逐步信息富化和GRPO对齐策略，成功弥合了生成式LLM与电商平台实际业务需求之间的差距，实现从点击商品生成高质量、符合真实点击行为的查询建议。

Abstract: E-commerce platforms increasingly display clickable query suggestions alongside items in the user feed, enabling users to refine or expand their intent without manually reformulating queries. Existing approaches either mine suggestions from historical logs -- limited to past behavior and blind to long-tail, personalized intents -- or rely on off-the-shelf LLMs whose lack of platform-specific knowledge yields fluent but generic queries disconnected from real click behavior. We propose EAGER (Enrich-and-AliGn gEnerative Query Recommendation), a two-stage framework for generating query suggestions from clicked items. In the enrichment stage, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) follows a four-stage curriculum that scales information richness (from item-only to user-conditioned) and reasoning depth (from direct to chain-of-thought). Each stage incorporates rationale augmentation, diversity regularization, and self-distillation. In the alignment stage, we post-train via GRPO with a hybrid reward of multiple rule-based business signals and a preference-aware click reward. Extensive offline experiments and online A/B test demonstrate the effectiveness of EAGER, which has been deployed in production at a major e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [15] [Query-Aware Token Budgeting for Efficient Late-Interaction Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07262)
*PS Rishi,Rajeev Ranjan Dwivedi,Vinod K Kurmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对晚期交互可视化文档检索中静态池化损失查询相关证据的问题，提出两阶段方案：压缩热路径索引生成候选后，在候选页面上执行查询感知的token预算分配（将阶段二选择建模为带预算的MaxSim覆盖问题），并证明裁剪版本是单调子模的。在ViDoRe基准上，贪心边际增益策略恢复了全量token分数的98.39%，且在留出及留一数据集评估中全面优于token top-k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互视觉文档检索因每页存储大量token嵌入导致存储与查询交互成本高昂，静态池化虽可降低成本但需在查询未知前决定保留哪些视觉证据，无法根据用户查询动态保留相关信息，导致检索精度显著下降。

Method: 提出两阶段检索范式：(1) 重度压缩的热路径索引负责候选生成；(2) 对候选短名单页面，在原始token集合上执行查询感知的token预算分配。将阶段二token选择形式化为带预算的MaxSim覆盖问题，证明其裁剪版本满足单调子模性，随后比较覆盖仅、簇引导、逐token和边际增益四种策略。

Result: 在十项ViDoRe任务上，直接静态池化使宏平均nDCG@5从无压缩的0.6309降至32倍池化下的0.4738。在相同候选生成机制与等效8倍池化的重排序预算下，token top-k恢复93.93%的全量分数，而贪心边际增益策略恢复98.39%。留出及留一数据集评估显示贪心方法在每个数据集上均优于token top-k。延迟分析揭示两个实用操作点：token top-k用于交互式检索，朴素贪心实现用作质量上界。

Conclusion: 晚期交互视觉检索的精度提升更依赖于查询感知的token分配而非单纯的查询无关池化，查询感知预算分配策略在压缩条件下可保持高精度，同时展示出对不同部署场景（交互式与高质量）的两档操作平衡。

Abstract: Late-interaction visual document retrievers preserve fine-grained page evidence by storing many token embeddings per page, but the resulting storage and query-time interaction costs make large-scale deployment expensive. Pooling document tokens before indexing offers a natural remedy, yet static pooling must decide which visual evidence to preserve before the query is known. We study an alternative: a heavily compressed hot-path index generates candidates, after which query-aware token budgeting operates on the original token sets of the shortlisted pages. We formulate this stage-two selection as a budgeted MaxSim coverage problem, show that a clipped version is monotone submodular, and compare coverage-only, cluster-guided, token-wise, and marginal-gain policies. On ten ViDoRe tasks with ColModernVBERT, direct static pooling reduces macro normalized discounted cumulative gain at rank five from 0.6309 without compression to 0.4738 at a thirty-two-fold pool factor. Under the same candidate-generation regime and a pool-factor-eight-equivalent reranking budget, token top-k recovers 93.93 percent of the full-token score, while greedy marginal-gain selection recovers 98.39 percent. Held-out and leave-one-dataset-out evaluations yield positive greedy improvements over token top-k on every dataset. The latency analysis reveals two useful operating points: token top-k for interactive retrieval and the naive greedy implementation as a quality upper envelope. Together, these results show that late-interaction visual retrieval benefits from query-aware allocation rather than query-agnostic pooling alone.

</details>


### [16] [Task-Blind No MORE: Multi-Task Information Flow in Unified Ranking Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07273)
*Yuchen Wang,Feng Niu,Qing Tan,Junting Lu,Baoxin Wu,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MORE（Multi-task cO-evolving Ranking modEl），一个将多任务信息流嵌入推荐排序骨干网络的方法。通过Anchor Tokens（Shared Anchors编码跨任务共性，Private Anchors编码任务特定先验）在每一层同时融合行为序列与特征，使任务信号与序列和特征表示协同演化。在工业大规模数据及Momo在线A/B测试中均优于基线，已部署至生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一的排序架构（如HyFormer、MixFormer）虽将特征交互与序列建模统一到可堆叠骨干中，但真实推荐系统几乎都需要多任务学习；而现有统一架构仅将多任务建模限制在浅层的post-backbone塔中，骨干网络内部缺乏任务感知的信息流，导致多任务信号无法在深层表示中充分差异化演化。

Method: MORE将多任务信息流嵌入骨干网络内部，引入两类Anchor Tokens并使其跨骨干层持久存在：Shared Anchors编码跨任务共性，Private Anchors捕获任务特定先验。每个block中Anchor Tokens执行三步操作：（1）从行为序列中读取任务条件化信号；（2）在任务边界掩码下与非序列特征混合；（3）通过独立分支精炼各任务表示。随着block堆叠，每个任务获得经过所有骨干层精炼的差异化表示。

Result: 在大型工业数据集上，MORE在可比参数与FLOPs预算下全面优于基线，且随模型规模增大扩展性良好。在Momo（月活数千万的社交平台）在线A/B测试中：使用时长提升3%、互动率提升3.6%、深度聊天率提升2%。已部署至生产环境，通过请求级共享计算将评分延迟降低约30%。

Conclusion: MORE通过将多任务信号嵌入骨干各层而非仅在末端融合，实现了任务表示与序列、特征表示的深度协同演化，在离线与在线场景中均验证了其有效性，且共享计算策略兼顾了效果与推理效率。

Abstract: Industrial ranking models for recommendation have scaled feature interaction and sequence modeling separately; recent architectures such as HyFormer and MixFormer unify both in a stackable backbone. Real-world recommender systems, however, nearly always require multi-task learning, yet existing unified architectures confine multi-task modeling to shallow post-backbone towers, leaving the backbone without task-aware information flow. We propose MORE (Multi-task cO-evolving Ranking modEl), which embeds multi-task information flow inside the backbone, enabling task-specific signals to co-evolve with sequence and feature representations at every layer rather than in a post-hoc fusion. It introduces Anchor Tokens that persist across backbone layers: Shared Anchors encode cross-task commonalities, while Private Anchors capture task-specific priors. In each block, Anchor Tokens (1) read task-conditioned signals from behavior sequences, (2) mix with non-sequential features under a task-boundary mask, and (3) refine per-task representations through independent branches; as blocks stack, each task obtains a differentiated representation refined through all backbone layers.
  Experiments on large-scale industrial datasets show that MORE consistently outperforms baselines across all tasks under comparable parameter and FLOPs budgets, and scales well with model size. Online A/B tests on Momo, a leading Chinese social discovery platform with tens of millions of monthly active users, yield 3% improvement in usage duration, 3.6% in interaction rate, and 2% in deep-chat rate. MORE is deployed in production with request-level shared computation reducing scoring latency by about 30%.

</details>


### [17] [Matryoshka Hash Representations for Model-Aware Compact Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07276)
*Peichun Hua,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Matryoshka Hash Representations (MHR)，一种两阶段的检索向量量化方法，将全宽度训练与前缀组织分离，使同一份二进制代码可支持多个字节预算的直接搜索，在MS MARCO和BEIR基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）依赖密集检索，在语料规模下每个文档的存储开销是主要成本。标准量化方法（如PQ）为每个文档生成短代码，但单个代码若能同时服务于多种字节预算则更具实用性——即代码的短前缀可直接搜索，使部署无需重新编码语料即可调整效率-质量平衡点。然而单一目标下训练所有前缀会导致早期位妥协，特别是低比特（如二值码）量化会加剧这一冲突。

Method: MHR采用两阶段流程：第一阶段学习较长的二值代码（全宽度训练），第二阶段冻结模型并训练额外的零初始化残差代码适配器，用于生成可直接搜索的前缀。文档以每坐标一位存储，查询端保留连续logits（类似PQ）以保证足够的表达能力。搜索过程通过FAISS FastScan实现。MHR还可用于两处下游增强：为全精度重排生成候选，以及剪枝低存储图索引（如LEANN）。

Result: 在MS MARCO上训练并零样本迁移到七个BEIR数据集，MHR在32字节预算下达到0.5561 NDCG@10和0.6535 Recall@100，超越了同预算的最佳基线；在更低字节预算下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: MHR通过解耦全宽度训练与前缀组织，有效解决了多字节预算共享代码时的妥协问题，在低比特和二值量化场景下展现出显著优势，并能增强候选生成和低存储图索引两条实用管线。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) depends on dense retrieval: each document is stored as a learned vector, and a query is answered by finding its nearest neighbors in that vector space. Keeping one full-precision vector per document is the dominant index cost at corpus scale, so retrieval systems replace each vector with a short code of a few bytes---a step called quantization. Standard quantizers such as product quantization (PQ) pick the code that reconstructs the original vector most closely.
  A single code is even more useful if it serves several byte budgets at once: when its short prefixes are each directly searchable, a deployment can set its efficiency--quality operating point without re-encoding the corpus. But training all prefixes under one objective makes the early bits a compromise across budgets---short codes improve while the full-width code degrades. Quantization to low-bit representation, such as binary codes, further sharpens the conflict. We introduce Matryoshka Hash Representations (MHR), a two-stage procedure that separates full-width training from prefix organization. MHR first learns a longer binary code, then freezes the model and trains additional zero-initialized residual code adaptors for directly searchable prefixes. Documents are stored at one bit per coordinate, while queries keep continuous logits like PQ to attain sufficient expressivity. We implement the search process with FAISS FastScan. Trained on MS MARCO and zero-shot transferred to seven BEIR datasets, MHR reaches .5561 NDCG@10 and .6535 Recall@100 at 32 bytes, surpassing the best baseline of the same budget. The advantage is more pronounced in lower budgets. The same code also strengthens two common pipelines: shortlisting candidates for full-precision reranking, and pruning a low-storage graph index such as LEANN.

</details>


### [18] [Uncertainty Quantification for LLM Agents: A Taxonomy, an Evaluation Protocol, and an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07395)
*Moule Lin,Qizhen Lan,Shuhao Guan,Weipeng Jing,Jiexin Fan,David Gregg,Goetz Botterweck*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM智能体系统中的不确定性来源于多轮对话、环境和工具交互，而非单轮问答，并提出了三轴分类法（不确定性是什么、如何估计、在智能体管道的哪个环节产生），引入轨迹级校准指标TC-ECE来取代单一的置信度分数，实验表明代理自身响应的置信度估计并不优于简单基线，且按轨迹平均会掩盖后期阶段的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已从单轮对话转向多轮、多工具、长时程的智能体系统，信任判定成为安全部署的前提。但现有不确定性量化研究几乎完全针对单轮问答，无法捕捉多轮交互、环境和工具引入的误差与不确定性，且单一置信度分数过于粗糙，无法代表整个系统的不可靠性。

Method: 提出三轴分类法组织文献：(1)不确定性是什么；(2)如何估计；(3)不确定性在智能体管道中的来源位置。引入轨迹级校准评估方法（Trajectory-Checkpoint Expected Calibration Error, TC-ECE），并通过简单反例证明步骤级校准不蕴含轨迹级校准。在四种模型、最长50步的真实代理轨迹上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明TC-ECC可以在真实代理轨迹上计算，且步骤错误沿轨迹耦合。代理自身响应的置信度估计并不始终优于简单基线。将所有轨迹平均分析会掩盖后期阶段的过度自信，按不同时域（horizon）拆分分析后该问题变得可见。

Conclusion: 智能体系统的不确定性来源贯穿整个管道，需在轨迹层面而非单步或全局平均层面进行不确定性量化与校准报告。单一置信度数字不足以反映系统可靠性，需要按不同时域报告的经验校准误差等细粒度指标来避免过度自信被掩盖。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are no longer deployed only for single-turn conversation but increasingly act as agents that plan, call tools, retrieve evidence, maintain memory, and interact over long horizons, often together with other agents through multi-turn conversations. Therefore, knowing when to trust the agentic system is a prerequisite for safe deployment. However, existing work on quantifying uncertainty for LLMs was built almost entirely for single-turn question answering. This paper argues that errors and uncertainty arise from multi-turn conversations, environments, and tools rather than from a single-turn question answering setting. It comes late, however, and is compounded in a single score that is too coarse to represent the unreliability. We organize the literature with a three-axis taxonomy, (1) what the uncertainty is, (2) how it is estimated, and (3) where uncertainty arises during an agent pipeline. We investigate step-level and trajectory-level calibration and show with a simple counterexample that the first does not imply the second. Experiments on real agent traces across four models and up to a 50-step budget show that the proposed metric and reporting protocol (Trajectory-Checkpoint Expected Calibration Error, TC-ECE) can be computed and that step errors are coupled along a trajectory. We find that confidence estimates from the agent's own responses do not consistently outperform a simple baseline. The experiments also show that averaging all trajectories together can hide overconfidence at later stages, which becomes visible when results are analyzed across different horizons. In simpler terms, this paper identifies where the uncertainty comes from in the agentic system pipeline, how to teach agents to know when they are wrong, and why one confidence number is not enough.

</details>


### [19] [EigenLI: Spectral Approximations to Late Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07561)
*Archish S,Sabyasachi Basu,Ankit Garg,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Kirankumar Shiragur*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EigenLI框架，利用晚期交互（late-interaction）模型表示的内在低秩结构，通过文档特定低维子空间的谱近似来压缩表示。EigenLI在数据集和模型上优于k-means和Ward聚类等池化方法；其衍生出的EigenLI-SV作为ANN兼容的单向量表示，在多个数据集和三个文本模型上一致优于MUVERA等对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互模型（如ColBERT）通过为每个文档生成大量token级向量来获得强检索效果，但这种表达力导致索引成本高、存储开销大且MaxSim评分昂贵。亟需一种压缩方法在保持检索效果的同时降低这些代价。

Method: EigenLI基于晚期交互表示的内在低秩结构（文档token嵌入集中在一个保留大部分检索信号的低维子空间），通过谱近似框架，利用每个文档的特定低维子空间（主导特征方向）构建精简的交互表示。此外，同一谱构造衍生出EigenLI-SV，一种由简化结构的二阶摘要导出的ANN兼容单向量表示。

Result: 在ColBERTv2和AnswerAI-ColBERT-small上，k-EigenLI（k≤32）优于k-means和Ward聚类池化方法；GTE-ModernColBERT在k=32时呈现不同权衡（聚类方法表现更好）。EigenLI-SV在多个数据集和全部三个文本模型上一致优于MUVERA等单向量替代方案。

Conclusion: 晚期交互表示具有可被谱方法利用的内在低秩结构，EigenLI通过文档特定子空间的谱近似实现了高效的表示压缩，在降低存储和索引成本的同时保持甚至提升检索质量，并提供了ANN兼容的单向量版本以支持更广泛的部署场景。

Abstract: Late-interaction models such as ColBERT achieve strong effectiveness by representing each document with many token-level vectors, but this expressivity leads to large indexing cost, storage footprints and expensive MaxSim scoring. We show that late-interaction representations exhibit an intrinsic low-rank structure: document token embeddings concentrate in a low-dimensional subspace that preserves most of the retrieval signal. Leveraging this observation, we introduce EigenLI, a spectral approximation framework that compresses late-interaction representations via document-specific low-dimensional subspaces. Unlike clustering or pooling methods, EigenLI identifies the dominant eigendirections of each document and uses them to construct reduced interaction representations. Empirically, $k$-EigenLI with $k \le 32$ outperforms k-means and Ward clustering based pooling methods on ColBERTv2 and AnswerAI-ColBERT-small; GTE-ModernColBERT exhibits a different tradeoff at $k=32$, where clustering methods perform better. The same spectral construction also yields EigenLI-SV, an ANN-compatible single-vector representation derived from the second-order summary of the reduced structure. Across multiple datasets and all three text models, EigenLI-SV consistently outperforms comparable single-vector surrogates such as MUVERA.

</details>


### [20] [Open Tabular Insight Extraction: Where Do We Stand, and Where Should We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07629)
*Daniel Gomm,Maarten de Rijke,Madelon Hulsebos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出“开放表格洞察提取”(OpenTI)这一统一框架，将表格问答、文本转SQL和数据分析代理等碎片化研究领域置于共同基线之上，并基于系统综述指出当前系统缺乏端到端覆盖、基准难以适用于开放场景，最后提出面向OpenTI的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海量表格语料（如数据湖）中的知识获取是核心研究挑战，但相关研究分散在不同社区（表格问答、text-to-SQL、数据分析代理等），各社区之间引用率低（跨标签引用仅约占同标签的六分之一），缺乏统一框架与术语，导致进展难以整合和推进。

Method: 从第一性原理出发形式化OpenTI框架，围绕用户所需的分析知识、从表格语料推导该知识的流程、结果对用户的有用性三个维度构建；整合IR、NLP、ML、数据库和HCI领域的框架与术语；基于该框架对系统与基准进行系统综述与分析，并配套交互式网站。

Result: 发现当前系统未覆盖OpenTI的端到端范围，主要聚焦于分析环节本身；现有基准大多不适合开放环境评估，其输入预设了对表格的先验知识，验证机制与真实设定不匹配。

Conclusion: 需要推进面向OpenTI的系统、评估方法和交互范式的研究议程，以真正揭示用户所需的洞察。

Abstract: Democratizing access to the knowledge held in large corpora of tables such as data lakes is emerging as a central research challenge. Research in this space is advancing and broadening in scope, increasingly supplying the components to satisfy a person's insight need end-to-end. Yet these efforts remain fragmented across communities that frame the problem under their own conventions, such as table question answering, text-to-SQL, and data analysis agents, with works six times as likely to cite within the same task label as across labels. To bring these communities onto common ground, we establish a holistic framework for this pursuit, which we refer to as Open Tabular Insight Extraction (OpenTI). We formalize OpenTI from first principles around the analytical knowledge a person needs, the procedure for deriving it from a corpus of tables, and how well a result serves the person who sought it. In doing so we consolidate frameworks and terminology across information retrieval, natural language processing, machine learning, databases, and human-computer interaction, and apply this grounding in a systematic review and analysis of systems and benchmarks that work towards OpenTI. We find that current systems do not cover the end-to-end scope of OpenTI, mainly focusing on the analysis itself, and that benchmarks are largely unfit for evaluations in an open setting as inputs presuppose knowledge of tables, and validation mechanisms do not match the setup. Finally, we distill a research agenda towards OpenTI systems, evaluation, and interaction paradigms that surface the insights users need. An interactive companion to our paper is available at https://open-tabular-insight-extraction.github.io.

</details>


### [21] [Noēsis: Deterministic-First Retrieval with Two-Tier Context Hydration for Factuality-Critical Queries on Small Local Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07663)
*Nicola Cogotti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Noesis架构的确定性优先查询平面，通过生成前的确定性判定机制（事实层、位置寻址、溯源作用域、分层上下文），使2B参数的小模型在事实完整性上达到35B模型的水平，并实现了零编造数字和单次生成调用的可追溯查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在事实关键性领域（媒体指标、医疗剂量、金融法律引用等），小型本地语言模型即使上下文中存在正确证据，仍会编造看似可信的数字和时间戳，造成比"不知道"更严重的危害。现有研究指出，7B以下模型的RAG瓶颈不在于检索质量，而在于上下文利用率。

Method: Noesis采用确定性优先策略，在生成之前完成所有确定性判断，包括：(a) 生产者侧事实层，预计算的指标事实逐字渲染且不排序；(b) 位置寻址，在查询前以零LLM成本进行跨源确定性对齐；(c) 溯源作用域作为归因约束，支持多层命名引用路由；(d) 双层上下文，由模型触发逐字水合（verbatim hydration）。

Result: 四项消融实验中：2B模型在事实完整性上达到35B模型的水平（所有运行中精确值，零编造数字）；结构化检索相比平坦RAG在2B上提升11.4个点；骨架上下文在提示缩小20-30%时保留定量答案；水合在约8秒内恢复逐字叙述（对比约29秒）。每次查询仅需单次生成调用，每个报告值均可溯源至精确来源和位置。

Conclusion: Noesis表明，通过架构性的确定性优先设计，小模型可以在受监管的事实关键领域中实现与显著更大模型相当的事实完整性，同时保证结果的可追溯性和单次生成调用效率，适用于监管领域。

Abstract: A wrong number is worse than no answer. Across factuality-critical domains -- audience metrics, scheduling and rights in media; dosages and lab values in healthcare; figures and citations in finance and legal -- a confident but fabricated value is more damaging than an honest admission of uncertainty. Yet this is the dominant failure mode we observe on small local language models: even when correct evidence is present in context, models fabricate plausible numbers and timestamps. Recent work characterizes a real limit of this regime: below 7B parameters, the bottleneck of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is not retrieval quality but context utilization.
  We present Noesis, the deterministic-first query plane of the Noesis architecture, which makes every deterministic judgment before generation. Its mechanisms follow from the ingestion architecture (subject of a separate patent application): (a) a producer-side fact layer rendering precomputed metric facts verbatim without ranking; (b) positional addressing with deterministic cross-source alignment, resolved ahead of query time at zero LLM cost; (c) provenance scoping as an attribution constraint with multi-tier named-reference routing; and (d) two-tier context with model-triggered verbatim hydration.
  Across four ablations, a 2B model reaches parity with a 35B model on factual integrity (exact values in all runs; zero confabulated numbers on absent-entity traps); structured retrieval beats flat RAG by +11.4 points at 2B; skeleton-only context preserves quantitative answers at 20-30% smaller prompts; and hydration recovers verbatim narrative in ~8s versus ~29s. Two properties matter for regulated domains: each query resolves in a single generation call, and every reported value is traceable to its exact source and position by construction.

</details>


### [22] [Cassette: Case-to-Case Structural Distillation for Efficient Legal Case Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08185)
*Yanran Tang,Ruihong Qiu,Hongzhi Yin,Xue Li,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cassette框架，通过知识蒸馏策略将重型教师检索器的能力迁移到轻量级混合学生双编码器上，以解决法律案例检索中CaseLink方法推理阶段O(n²)高计算开销的问题，在保持检索性能的同时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律案例检索对法律从业者和普通民众都有重要价值，但现有方法CaseLink虽然利用案例间图结构提升了检索精度，却在大型法律数据库上推理时因构建测试时图和计算两两词频相似度而具有O(n²)时间复杂度，导致检索耗时随候选案例数量急剧增长（如对55,192个案例需超3500秒），严重限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出Cassette框架，采用包含排序目标（ranking objective）和特征匹配目标（eigen-matching objective）的知识蒸馏策略，将训练良好的重型教师检索器的知识迁移到轻量级混合学生双编码器中。学生查询编码器采用多层感知机（MLP）实现以支持快速在线处理，学生候选编码器则采用图神经网络（GNN）架构以适合案例数据库内部的离线处理方式。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果验证了排序蒸馏的有效性，同时实现了高效率（显著降低推理时间）。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: Cassette通过知识蒸馏与混合双编码器架构，成功在保持法律案例检索性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，有效解决了CaseLink在大规模案例库上的实际部署瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Legal case retrieval (LCR) is an essential tool for not only assisting legal practitioners to efficiently retrieve precedents but also enabling ordinary individuals to find valuable legal case information without relying on expensive professional legal services. Our previous work CaseLink demonstrated the effectiveness of using case to case graph structures to improve retrieval accuracy. However, its high computational cost during inference on large-scale legal databases limits its practical use in real-world settings. The main inefficiency comes from constructing test time graphs and computing pairwise term frequency similarities of cases. This process has O(n^2) complexity for n legal cases, making the runtime prohibitive as the number of candidates grows. For example, the retrieval time for one query on a database (COLIEE2022) with 1,563 candidate cases is more than 500 milliseconds, while the runtime would increase drastically to more than 3,500 seconds for a database (LeCaRDv2) with 55,192 candidate cases. To further enhance the retrieval performance while achieving a significant speed-up, in this extension paper, Cassette framework is proposed with a distillation strategy involving ranking objective and eigen-matching objective for an effective transfer of knowledge from a powerful and well-trained heavy teacher retriever to a lightweight and efficient hybrid student dual encoder. Specifically, the student query encoder is implemented as a multilayer perceptron model designed for fast online processing, whereas the student candidate encoder adopts a GNN architecture, suitable for an offline manner within the case database. Extensive experiments are conducted on three benchmark datasets, and the results verify the effectiveness of the ranking distillation while achieving high efficiency. The code has been released on https://github.com/yanran-tang/Cassette/.

</details>


### [23] [Exploring Bottom-Up Clustering for Creating Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08310)
*Leah Woldemariam,Sudhanshu Garg,Taha Belkhouja,Charles Kim-Yip,Ali Sahami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的生成Semantic IDs的算法，通过自底向上的聚类方法保留嵌入空间的局部结构，确保标识符既唯一又保留原始嵌入的语义结构，从而提升生成式检索的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Semantic IDs的构建面临两个主要挑战：一是每个标识符需要映射到唯一产品（去重），二是需要捕获对下游任务有价值的信息。以往方法通过追加附加码字去重和使用残差量化创建层次聚类，但未能充分保留嵌入空间的局部结构。

Method: 提出一种生成Semantic IDs的算法，核心是采用自底向上（bottom-up）聚类方法来保留嵌入空间中的局部结构，从而提高Semantic IDs的聚类质量及其在下游生成式检索中的效用。

Result: 通过自底向上聚类保留局部结构，生成唯一且保留原始嵌入结构的Semantic IDs，改进了聚类质量并提升了生成式检索的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 实验表明，利用自底向上聚类生成的Semantic IDs能够同时保证标识符的唯一性和嵌入结构保留，显著改善生成式检索的效果。

Abstract: The success of generative retrieval has largely been attributed to the use of Semantic IDs, which improve over arbitrary item-level identifiers such as hashes by capturing the semantics of items. The main challenges faced when constructing Semantic IDs, however, is in mapping each identifier to a unique product and capturing information valuable to downstream tasks. Past works have appended additional codewords to de-duplicate item identifiers and utilized residual quantization to create hierarchical clusters. In this work, we present an algorithm for generating Semantic IDs that ensure the identifiers are both unique and preserve the structure of the original embedding. Key to our work is the use of bottom-up clustering to preserve local structure in the embedding space, improving the clustering quality of the resulting Semantic IDs and their utility for downstream generative retrieval.

</details>


### [24] [SequenceO1: End-to-End Ultra-Long (100K) Sequence Modeling in Recommendation with Low-Rank Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08443)
*Lin Guan,Jia-Qi Yang,Zhishan Zhao,Jiaqi Huang,Hangyu Wang,Longbin Li,Beichuan Zhang,Haonan Jiang,Jinan Ni,Xiangyu Fan,Xiaowen Li,Ziyao Ren,Yuhang Qi,Xiaolong Zhu,Xuanyuan Luo,Qiwei Chen,Yi Cheng,Lele Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SequenceO1是一个端到端的超长用户行为序列建模框架，已部署在抖音全流量上，支持高达10万次交互的历史序列。采用"先压缩后推理"的设计思路，通过可学习原型与归一化的Sketch Attention将原始历史压缩为固定大小的用户表征，再通过Target-conditioned Stacked Target-to-History Cross Attention (STCA)同时建模短期兴趣和长期偏好。系统层面结合低秩缓存、多请求用户级批处理、pipeline lift和融合的FlashSA kernel来解决存储、通信和计算开销，实现离线与在线效果的持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿级工业推荐系统需要建模超长用户行为序列（10万规模），但生产环境受限于严格的延迟、内存、通信和训练吞吐率约束。现有方法（如历史截断、多阶段行为检索、压缩终身历史、train-short/infer-long外推）要么削弱端到端优化，要么保留显著的随长度增加的成本，无法在工业场景下有效处理100K规模的长序列。

Method: SequenceO1采用两阶段"compress-then-reason"设计：(1) Sketch Attention (SA)利用可学习原型和原型级归一化将原始历史压缩为固定大小、目标无关的用户表征；(2) 目标条件化的Stacked Target-to-History Cross Attention (STCA)建模互补的时间尺度——最近10K后缀捕获短期兴趣，紧凑的sketch捕获长期偏好。系统层面通过低秩用户表征缓存、多请求用户级批处理、pipeline lift和融合的FlashSA kernel来分摊特征存储、通信和计算成本。

Result: 生产实验（已部署于抖音全流量）展示了一致的离线与在线收益。紧凑的缓存sketch保留了直接端到端扩展到100K序列ranking的大部分收益，验证了压缩方法在长序列建模中的有效性。

Conclusion: SequenceO1为高效注意力、序列压缩以及可扩展的长序列/长上下文推荐系统提供了一种实用的模型-系统协同方法，在工业规模的严格约束下实现了对10万级用户历史序列的有效端到端建模。

Abstract: Modeling long-term user behavior is central to sequential recommendation and billion-scale industrial recommender systems, yet production ranking models operate under strict latency, memory, communication, and training-throughput constraints. At the 100K scale, the challenge extends beyond attention complexity: raw sequence features must be stored, transferred, and repeatedly processed during training and online serving. Existing approaches based on history truncation, multi-stage behavior retrieval, compressed lifelong histories, or train-short/infer-long extrapolation either weaken end-to-end optimization or retain substantial length-dependent cost. We present SequenceO1, an end-to-end framework for ultra-long user behavior sequence modeling, deployed at full traffic on Douyin with histories of up to 100K interactions. SequenceO1 follows a compress-then-reason design. Its Sketch Attention (SA) uses learnable prototypes and prototype-wise normalization to compress the raw history into a fixed-size, target-agnostic user representation. Target-conditioned Stacked Target-to-History Cross Attention (STCA) then models complementary time scales: a recent 10K suffix for short-term interests and the compact sketch for long-term preferences. To make training and inference practical, SequenceO1 combines low-rank user representation caching, multi-request user-level batching, pipeline lift, and a fused FlashSA kernel to amortize feature storage, communication, and computation across targets, training instances, and consecutive requests. Production experiments show consistent offline and online gains, while the compact cached sketch retains most of the benefit of directly scaling end-to-end sequence ranking to 100K. These results provide a practical model-system approach to efficient attention, sequence compression, and scalable long-sequence and long-context recommendation systems.

</details>


### [25] [Individual Text Corpora Predict User-Specific Knowledge: Benchmarks of Individualized Knowledge Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08532)
*Christoph Wigbels,Ali Abusaleh,Markus T. Jansen,Alexander Mehler,Manuel Schaaf,Markus J. Hofmann*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨是否可以利用搜索历史构建的个体语料库（ICs）来模拟个体知识。通过316名成人的36个多项选择知识题，采用LoRA微调的Qwen3-1.7B模型进行比较，结果发现该模型在公开题目上超越参与者，但在非公开题目上表现更差；将ICs整合到RAG中虽能显著高于偶然水平的个体匹配准确率，但对个体答案的模式校准较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否能够利用个体搜索历史语料库来模拟和预测个体知识水平，从而为个性化知识评估和认知建模提供新的方法路径。

Method: 收集316名成人的个体搜索历史语料库（ICs），让其回答36道多选题；比较多种LLM后选定Qwen3-1.7B，通过LoRA进行任务特异性微调；进一步将ICs整合到检索增强生成（RAG）中以预测个体作答，并基于熵的评估基准作为校准指标。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3-1.7B在公开题目上优于参与者和德国代表性样本；但非公开题目上表现劣于参与者，暗示公开题目存在训练数据污染。RAG整合后LLM与参与者的匹配准确率显著高于偶然水平，证明存在可检测的个体知识信号；但分配给参与者答案的概率较低，校准不良。知识缺口预测仅在语料超过五百万token时有改进。

Conclusion: 个体搜索历史语料可用于模拟个体知识的某些方面，但当前模型对个体回答模式的校准仍然不足。作者提出基于熵的评估基准可作为个体化知识模拟的校准指标，为未来改进提供方向。

Abstract: This study examines whether individual text corpora (ICs) from search histories can be used to simulate individual knowledge. We collected ICs from 316 adults, who answered 36 multiple-choice knowledge items, and compared several large language models (LLMs) on this task, of which only Qwen3-1.7B proved viable. After task-specific fine-tuning via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Qwen3-1.7B outperformed both participants and a representative German norm sample on publicly available items. On non-public questions, however, the LLM performed worse than our participants, suggesting possible training data contamination for the public questions. When integrating ICs into retrieval-augmented generation to predict individual responses, LLM-participant Match accuracies significantly exceeded chance, which demonstrates a detectable individual knowledge signal. The probabilities assigned to the participants' answers were, however, low and far below the probability of correct answers, indicating poor calibration toward individual response patterns. Knowledge-gap prediction was sub-optimal, though it improved for corpora exceeding five million tokens. We discuss our entropy based evaluation benchmarks as calibration indices for individualized knowledge simulation.

</details>


### [26] [PDMR: Passage-Driven Multi-ID Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08762)
*Smail Oussaidene,Mohand Boughanem*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PDMR框架，通过为每个文档赋予多个段落级标识符来解决生成式检索中单一标识符假设导致的信息损失和查询鲁棒性不足问题。PDMR在NQ320K和MS MARCO Document上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索（GR）模型采用单一标识符假设，每个文档只映射到一个目标序列，迫使模型用单一序列表示文档的所有内容。由于文档通常具有多面性（multi-faceted），这种表示会产生信息丢失，且多个查询意图需要竞争单一生成访问路径，导致对查询变化的鲁棒性不足。

Method: PDMR将每个文档切分为多个段落，并为每个选中的段落分配一个独立的标识符，从而为检索同一文档提供多个语义入口点。针对这种一对多映射带来的监督模糊性问题，论文将训练建模为多目标学习问题，设计目标函数在多个有效的段落级标识符上分配概率质量。

Result: 在NQ320K上，PDMR在Recall@1和MRR@100上超越强生成式和非生成式基线。在MS MARCO Document上，PDMR在Recall@1和MRR@10上取得最优结果，Recall@10上保持竞争力。受控消融实验表明段落级监督、标识符设计、训练查询增强和多目标学习各自贡献互补增益。

Conclusion: PDMR通过多段落级标识符替代单一文档级标识符，有效提升了生成式检索在文档语义多样性场景下的表现，验证了多入口生成式检索的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) models map queries directly to document identifiers, replacing conventional retrieval over external sparse or dense indexes with autoregressive identifier generation. However, most generative retrieval frameworks rely on a single-identifier assumption, mapping each document to a single target sequence. This forces the model to represent all document content with one sequence. Since documents are often multi-faceted, this can lead to lossy representations and reduced robustness to query variation, where multiple query intents must compete for a single generative access path.
  In this work, we introduce Passage-Driven Multi-ID Retrieval (PDMR), a generative retrieval framework that represents documents through multiple passage-level identifiers. PDMR segments each document and assigns one identifier to each selected passage, which provides multiple semantic entry points for retrieving the same document. This multi-entry representation allows the model to align queries with specific semantic facets, thereby reducing the dependence on a single document-level target. To address the supervision ambiguity of this one-to-many mapping, we formulate training as a multi-target learning problem and explore an objective function designed to distribute probability mass across multiple valid passage-level identifiers.
  We evaluate PDMR on NQ320K and MS MARCO Document. On NQ320K, PDMR improves over strong generative and non-generative baselines on Recall@1 and MRR@100. On MS MARCO Document, PDMR achieves the best Recall@1 and MRR@10 among the reported methods, while remaining competitive on Recall@10. Controlled ablations further show that passage-level supervision, identifier design, training-query augmentation, and multi-target learning contribute complementary gains.

</details>


### [27] [REDSI: Addressing the Reproducibility and Evaluation Consistency of Differentiable Search Indexing for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08860)
*Vivien Nicolas,Hicham Randrianarivo,Pascale Sébillot,Caio Corro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReDSI，第一个支持全部三种文档标识符类型（原子、朴素、语义）的开源生成式检索实现，并配套一个参数化且文档完善的NQ320K数据集构建流程，解决了DSI框架难以复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSI虽已成为生成式检索的事实标准基准，但存在严重的复现困难：缺乏同时支持三种文档标识符类型的公开实现、已有结果差异大、NQ320K数据集的构建流程不透明且预处理方式不统一。

Method: 构建ReDSI开源框架，全面支持原子、朴素和语义三种文档标识符类型；同时提供一个参数化、文档完善的NQ320K数据构建管线，确保数据处理的透明性和可复现性。此外，在模型下缩放场景下开展大规模实验，涵盖检索效果、参数效率、训练方法和解码策略等方面。

Result: ReDSI在实验结果上与或超过先前DSI基线水平；在模型小型化条件下系统评估了不同配置的性能表现，揭示了各因素对检索效果的影响，为后续研究开辟了新方向。

Conclusion: ReDSI填补了DSI开源实现的重要空白，通过提供完整的三类标识符实现和标准化数据处理管线，提升了生成式检索研究的可复现性，并为模型下缩放场景下的检索研究提供了新的实验基础和方向。

Abstract: The differentiable search index (DSI) framework (Tay et al., 2022) has become the de facto baseline for generative retrieval. However, DSI is hard to reproduce: no public implementation covers all three original document identifier types (atomic, naive, semantic), reported results vary widely, and the ubiquitous NQ320K dataset is built from Natural Questions through diverse and underspecified preprocessing. We introduce ReDSI, the first open-source DSI implementation supporting all three identifier types, together with a parameterizable and well-documented NQ320K construction pipeline.
  Experimentally, we achieve results that are competitive with or stronger than previous DSI baselines. Moreover, we conduct extensive experiments under model downscaling, covering retrieval effectiveness, parameter efficiency, training methods and decoding strategies, opening novel directions for future research.

</details>


### [28] [Q2D-Web: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Retrieval in Agentic RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08887)
*Maximilian Schall,Sedigheh Eslami,Markus Krimmel,Antoine Chaffin,Louis Milliken,Bo Wang,Denis Bykov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍Q2D-Web，一个大规模agentic检索基准，包含1.9亿文档语料库和7万条多语言agentic搜索查询，提供三套固定相关标注，用于评估生产环境RAG流水线中的第一阶段检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共基准无法评估大规模生产RAG中的第一阶段检索：大规模语料库只有少量评估查询，而具有大量查询的基准语料规模过小；且多数基准评估人类编写的查询，而agentic RAG流水线中的第一阶段检索器服务的是分布不同的机器改写查询。

Method: 构建Q2D-Web基准：1.9亿文档网络语料库+7万条来自生产系统真实用户查询改写而成的十种语言agentic搜索查询。提供三套相关判断：agent引用、生产排名、以及两者联合并经LLM判断补充未标注池化文档以减少假阴性的组合集。对13种检索器（词法、稠密、晚期交互）进行基准测评，并探索以子语料采样近似全语料评估的方法（基于互惠排名融合的池化检索结果选取三分之一语料）。

Result: 13种检索器的相对排序对判断集选择大体不敏感，但在不同主题领域、查询语言和查询类型间差异显著。保留三分之一语料（通过互惠排名融合选取）在组合判断下保持全语料模型排序，同时仅使绝对Recall@1000提高3-7个点。

Conclusion: Q2D-Web填补了agentic检索评估的空白，且子语料采样可作为全语料评估的高效近似，支持大规模快速评测。

Abstract: Evaluating first-stage retrievers in large-scale production RAG requires a benchmark that pairs a large-scale corpus with a large set of agent-reformulated search queries based on real user queries and their conversation threads, and that labels many relevant documents per query. No existing public benchmark evaluates this setting: large-scale collections typically provide only a small number of evaluation queries, whereas benchmarks with many queries generally contain only millions of documents. Moreover, most benchmarks assess human-written queries, while the first-stage retrievers in agentic RAG pipelines serve machine-written reformulations whose distribution differs from human search behavior. To overcome these evaluation gaps, we introduce Q2D-Web (Query2Doc-Web), a large-scale agentic retrieval benchmark consisting of a 190M-document web corpus and 70k agentic search queries in ten languages, reformulated from real-world user queries in production systems. Q2D-Web provides three sets of fixed relevance judgments: agent citations, production rankings, and a combined set that unions both signals and adds LLM-based judgments of unlabeled pooled documents to reduce false negatives. We benchmark 13 retrievers including lexical, dense, and late-interaction models and find that their relative ordering is largely insensitive to the choice of judgment set, while diverging substantially across topical domains, query languages, and query types. To enable fast evaluation, we also study subcorpus sampling as an approximation to full-corpus evaluations. Retaining a third of the corpus, selected by reciprocal rank fusion over pooled retriever runs, preserves the full-corpus model ranking under the combined judgments while raising absolute Recall@1000 only by 3 to 7 points. The public leaderboard is accessible under: https://huggingface.co/spaces/perplexity-ai/q2d-web-leaderboard

</details>


### [29] [FINALLY: A Dataset Recommender System for Recommender-Systems Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08941)
*Louis Owie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍FINALLY，一个基于Web的数据集推荐系统，用于为离线推荐系统评估构建满足实验约束和集合级选择目标的可配置数据集集合。通过420次推荐运行评估，所有数据集集合满足目标大小、去重等约束要求，且实现策略遵循预期优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具在构建同时满足实验约束和集合级选择目标的数据集集合方面支持有限，而数据集选择在很大程度上决定了推荐系统算法的评估条件。

Method: FINALLY结合了必需数据集、候选池限制、元数据过滤、可配置目标集合大小、随机选择，以及基于改进的Effective Covariance和Convex Hull目标函数的多样性与非多样性策略。通过十种系统化变化配置下的420次推荐运行进行评估。

Result: 所有数据集集合满足目标大小、去重、快照成员、必需数据集和元数据过滤要求；40个确定性策略-配置组合均可复现；两种多样性策略在全部十种配置中均产生预期的多样性-非多样性得分排序；多样性策略得分高于所有Random结果，非多样性策略得分低于所有Random结果。

Conclusion: 结果确立了FINALLY工作流的技术一致性，并表明实现策略在研究的配置空间中遵循预期优化方向，但并未证明所生成选择的科学适用性、全局最优性或实际优越性。

Abstract: Dataset selection shapes the empirical conditions under which recommender-system algorithms are evaluated, yet existing tools provide limited support for constructing complete dataset sets that jointly satisfy experimental constraints and set-level selection objectives. To address this problem, I developed FINALLY, a web-based dataset recommender for constructing configurable dataset sets for offline recommender-systems evaluations. FINALLY combines required datasets, candidate-pool restrictions, metadata filters, configurable target-set sizes, Random selection, and diverse and non-diverse strategies based on adapted Effective Covariance and Convex Hull objectives. I evaluated FINALLY through 420 recommendation runs across ten systematically varied configurations. All evaluated dataset sets satisfied the applicable target-size, duplicate-avoidance, snapshot-membership, required-dataset, and metadata-filter requirements. All 40 deterministic strategy--configuration combinations were reproducible. Both the Effective-Covariance-based and Convex-Hull-based strategies produced the expected diverse-versus-non-diverse score ordering in all ten configurations. Under their corresponding objectives, the diverse strategies produced scores above all 30 configuration-specific Random results, whereas the non-diverse strategies produced scores below all 30 Random results. These results establish technical consistency for the evaluated FINALLY workflow and show that the implemented strategies follow their intended optimization directions within the investigated configuration space. They do not establish the scientific suitability, global optimality, or practical superiority of the generated selections.

</details>

### [30] [Memory as Infrastructure: Reliability Engineering for Persistent Agent Memory in Months-Long LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05510)
*Mike Helwig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在6个月以上的持续Claude Code会话线上驱动一个63.3万行代码库的LLM编码代理系统的运营经验，并描述了由此产生的开源记忆与连续性基础设施"SIx Harness"：结合混合词法-向量检索的长期记忆、精密门控上下文注入、反重复存储、抗压缩约定以及对记忆子系统本身的可靠性工程。提供了首个数月的持久代理记忆子系统的仪表化运营记录：78,933次钩子调用、85次记录故障无一静默，及从仪表读数到结构性修复的三个生产事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理正从任务级规模跨越到项目级规模——单个持续数月、经历反复上下文压缩、运行在远超任何上下文窗口的代码库上的辅助努力。目前缺乏对持久代理记忆子系统在生产开发使用中的可靠性的系统性运营记录与基础设施支撑，尤其是记忆子系统自身的可靠性工程被忽略。

Method: - 混合词法-向量检索的SQLite本地长期记忆
- 精密门控上下��注入层（带十天精度仪表）
- 反重复存储记录决策与死胡同
- 抗上下文压缩的工程约定
- 会话启动健康门与判别式失效模式
- 心跳遥测确保无故障模式可未记录通过
- 借用SRE实践的警报复现疲劳预算
- 发布带标签的预注册消融协议与MIT许可工具箱

Result: 78,933次钩子调用；85次记录故障全部非静默：头三周84次、此后一次、最后20天为零；注入层十天精度仪表对完整分母零误报；三个生产事故从仪表读数追踪到结构性修复。据此提炼出七条设计原则，同时明确承认局限（N=1、无对照组、自我报告）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了我们认为是首个在生产开发使用中对持久代理记忆子系统的数月规模仪表化运营记录，并从记录中提炼七条设计原则，发布预注册消融协议供任何团队使用MIT许可工具箱复现验证，尽管局限在于N=1、无对照组和自报告数据。

Abstract: LLM coding agents are crossing from task-scale to project-scale: single assisted efforts that run for months, across repeated context compactions, on codebases far larger than any context window. We report operational experience from one such effort (a research project under a single continuous Claude Code session line since January 2026, driving a 633,000-line codebase, with its memory subsystem continuously instrumented since July 2026) and describe SIx Harness, the open-source memory and continuity infrastructure that emerged from keeping it coherent.
  The harness combines per-project long-term memory (hybrid lexical-vector retrieval over SQLite, fully local), precision-gated context injection, anti-recurrence stores for decisions and dead-ends, conventions engineered to survive compaction, and, the part we argue is missing from operational practice, reliability engineering for the memory subsystem itself: a session-start health gate with discriminated failure modes, heartbeat telemetry designed so that no enumerated failure mode can pass unrecorded, and alert-fatigue budgeting borrowed from SRE practice.
  We present what we believe is the first months-scale instrumented operational record of a persistent agent-memory subsystem in production development use: 78,933 hook invocations; 85 recorded failures, none silent: 84 in the subsystem's first three weeks, one since, none in the final 20 days; an injection layer whose ten-day precision instrument shows zero false fires against an intact denominator; and three production incidents traced from instrument reading to structural fix. From the record we distill seven design principles, state our limitations plainly (N=1, no control arm, self-reported), and publish a tagged pre-registered ablation protocol that any team can run with the released MIT-licensed kit.

</details>
