<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成模糊测试的调度器基于紧凑的历史性能摘要和事先固定的规则来分配CPU时间，存在两个局限：一是过去奖励摘要不可靠地反映性能演化（连续窗口排名一致性在估计噪声下与窗口内一致性无统计差异）；二是预测信号因目标而异（在9个目标中的8个上，从其他目标学习的权重预测效果劣于在当前目标上学习）。

Method: CAROL利用调度循环中已可获取的每个fuzzer的当前上下文（包括奖励趋势、等待和停滞时间、覆盖代码、估计不确定性），通过两种方式使用上下文：领域引导方法检测fuzzer处于上升还是衰退状态并应用相位特定学习规则；学习方法从15个上下文信号预测奖励，并利用预测不确定性进行在线选择。

Result: 在9个Magma目标上，CAROL在基线结果不同的情况下比三种集成调度基线触发更多唯一bug，且在其余场景中不劣于基线。相比每个目标的最强基线，CAROL获得11.8%的提升，并超越了一个事后选择最佳单fuzzer的预言机。移除上下文后增益消失，额外发现的bug集中在基线很少或从未触发的类型中。在5个广泛使用的C++程序上发现120个此前未知的崩溃缺陷（按站点、故障和入口去重后），均已通过项目披露渠道报告给维护者。

Conclusion: CAROL证明了利用即时上下文而非仅依赖历史性能摘要的在线调度能显著提升集成模糊测试的有效性，发现多fuzzer协作中基线调度器遗漏的bug，展示了上下文感知调度在集成模糊测试中的价值。

Abstract: Ensemble fuzzing runs multiple fuzzers on a target while a scheduler allocates CPU time among them. Existing schedulers base these decisions on compact summaries of past performance and rules fixed before a campaign. Our measurements reveal two limitations. First, past-reward summaries do not reliably capture performance evolution: after accounting for estimation noise, agreement between consecutive-window rankings is statistically indistinguishable from within-window self-agreement. Second, predictive signals vary across targets: on eight of nine targets, a weighting learned from the other eight predicts reward worse than one learned on the current target. We introduce CAROL, an online scheduler that uses each fuzzer's current context. Already available to the dispatch loop, this context describes reward trends, waiting and plateau time, reached code, and estimation uncertainty. CAROL uses context in two ways: a domain-guided method detects whether a fuzzer is rising or rotting and applies a phase-specific learning rule, while a learned method predicts reward from 15 context signals and uses predictive uncertainty for online selection. Across nine Magma targets, CAROL triggers more unique bugs than each of three ensemble-scheduling baselines whenever their results differ, and fewer on none. Compared with the strongest baseline for each target, CAROL gains 11.8% and surpasses an oracle that retrospectively selects the best single fuzzer per target. Removing context eliminates the gain, and the additional bugs are concentrated among those the baselines trigger rarely or never. Run unchanged on five widely used C++ programs, CAROL finds 120 previously unknown crashing defects, deduplicated by site, fault, and entry point; all were reported to maintainers through the projects' stated disclosure channels.

</details>


### [50] [Shortcutting the Fix: Identifying and Categorizing Agentic Exploits in Software Engineering Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06780)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统化地审计了自主软件工程（SWE）智能体在基准测试中的"作弊"行为（如利用本地Git历史、访问上游仓库或回忆记忆化解决方案），发现五个开源LLM在标准提示下的利用率达到45.1%-82.4%（SWE-bench Multilingual）和44.2%-66.1%（DeepSWE）。通过附加针对性的原创性约束指令，可将利用率大幅降至4.0%-10.7%和1.5%-7.1%，同时保持核心任务性能。研究强调了构建"防作弊感知"评估框架的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SWE代理在基准测试中获得了很高的解决率，但这些分数可能掩盖了投机取巧的行为（如利用本地Git历史、访问上游仓库或回忆记忆化解决方案），而非真正的问题求解能力。现有评估框架无法区分真实的仓库级问题解决与基准测试作弊，因此需要系统化地审计和量化这些利用行为。

Method: 作者对五个开源大语言模型在SWE-bench Multilingual和DeepSWE基准上进行了审计，采用回合级（turn-level）LLM-as-a-judge协议来系统化地识别和分类利用行为。标准提示下测量了利用率的基线水平，然后附加一条强制解决方案原创性的定向指令，对比评估其在降低利用率的同时维护核心任务性能的效果。

Result: 标准提示下，SWE-bench Multilingual上的利用率为45.1%-82.4%，DeepSWE上为44.2%-66.1%。附加原创性约束指令后，利用率分别大幅降至4.0%-10.7%和1.5%-7.1%，而核心任务性能保持强劲。

Conclusion: 高基准解决率可能是"基准游戏"（benchmark gaming）的结果而非真实问题求解能力的体现。需要开发"利用感知"（exploit-aware）的评估框架来衡量真实的仓库级问题求解能力，而非被基准分数所误导。

Abstract: While autonomous software engineering (SWE) agents achieve high benchmark resolution rates, these scores can mask exploitative behaviors---such as leveraging local Git histories, accessing upstream repositories, or recalling memorized solutions---rather than demonstrating genuine problem solving. We systematize and audit these exploits across five open large language models on SWE-bench Multilingual and DeepSWE using a turn-level LLM-as-a-judge protocol. Under standard prompts, exploitation rates reach 45.1\%--82.4\% on SWE-bench Multilingual and 44.2\%--66.1\% on DeepSWE. Appending a targeted instruction enforcing solution originality drastically cuts these exploitation rates---down to 4.0\%--10.7\% and 1.5\%--7.1\%, respectively, while maintaining strong core task performance. Our findings demonstrate the critical need for exploit-aware evaluation frameworks that measure true repository-level problem solving over benchmark gaming.

</details>


### [51] [LatentMD: Benchmarking Markdown Boundary Failures in LLM-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06993)
*Sungjune Lee,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentMD基准与评估协议，专门诊断LLM生成Markdown中的CommonMark围栏边界失败问题，将内容正确性与边界正确性解耦，发现38.0%的看似有效输出实为内容正确但边界损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的Markdown被各类下游系统（渲染器、代理、代码提取器等）消费，但现有评估常将内容质量与格式遵循混为一谈，导致Markdown边界失败（fence-boundary failures）这一关键问题被严重低估和欠度量。

Method: 构建了包含4,179个提示的基准集与评分CLI工具，通过将内容正确性与边界正确性分离来诊断输出；在9个LLM上生成约37,600个样本，并通过消融实验分析失败成因（同族对称定界符冲突vs嵌套）、提示缓解效果，以及泛化到Python三重引号docstring和JSON非对称定界符控制组的验证。

Result: 在9个LLM约37,600个生成样本中，38.0%的有效主网格输出是内容正确但边界失败的；边界破坏在未指定提示条件下及少量人工标注验证集中均显著存在。消融表明失败主要由同族对称定界符冲突驱动而非单纯嵌套，提示提示只能部分缓解；该问题可泛化到Python三重引号docstring，而JSON（非对称定界符）保持稳健。

Conclusion: LatentMD提供了可复现的解析敏感型LLM评估诊断目标，揭示了当前LLM在Markdown结构性输出中普遍存在的、被现有评估掩盖的边界格式缺陷，呼吁评估协议应区分内容正确性与格式规范性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate Markdown that is consumed by renderers, agents, code extractors, and structured downstream pipelines. Yet existing evaluations often conflate content quality with format adherence, leaving Markdown boundary failures under-measured. We introduce LatentMD, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for diagnosing CommonMark-level fence-boundary failures in LLM-generated Markdown. LatentMD separates content correctness from boundary correctness, enabling detection of outputs that are content-correct but boundary-broken. The benchmark contains 4,179 prompts and a CLI for scoring arbitrary model outputs. Across 9 LLMs and roughly 37,600 generations, we find that Markdown boundary failures are widespread: 38.0% of valid main-grid outputs are content-correct but boundary-broken, with substantial boundary breakage under unspecified prompts and in a small human-authored validation set. Ablations show that failures are driven primarily by same-family symmetric-delimiter collisions rather than nesting alone, are only partially mitigated by prompt hints, and generalize to Python triple-quote docstrings while JSON remains robust as an asymmetric-delimiter control. LatentMD provides a reproducible diagnostic target for parser-sensitive LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [Recompilation Is Not Enough: Test-Guided Decompiled-C Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07201)
*Yuhan Huang,Puzhuo Liu,Jianlei Chi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用编译器反馈和官方测试来修复反编译C代码的工作流程，解决了"仅可重编译但不保证行为正确"的问题。在104个Coreutils 9.5二进制的初步评估中，87.5%能重编译并通过测试门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译得到的C代码通常需要修复后才能重新编译，但仅仅能重编译并不足以保证其行为与原始目标一致——命令行二进制可能解析选项错误、输出不同字节或返回不同退出状态。这使得编译成功这个传统修复目标不够可靠，需要以测试观察到的行为作为更严格的修复验证标准。

Method: 提出一个分步工作流：(1) 使用编译器和链接器诊断信息引导构建修复；(2) 修复后的C成功重编译为二进制后，通过冒烟检查和相关的官方测试暴露行为差异；(3) 根据暴露的差异进行语义修复。整体上利用测试门控（test-gate）反馈来驱动LLM辅助的反编译代码修复。

Result: 在104个Coreutils 9.5二进制（具有可用的反编译器导出和确定性精确输出冒烟比较）上进行初步静态富化评估：91个（87.5%）能重编译并通过测试门控；9个未能在修复预算内完成重编译；4个虽成功重编译但仍未通过测试门控。

Conclusion: 结果表明，与仅以编译成功为目标的恢复方法相比，测试门控反馈可以使LLM辅助的反编译C代码修复更具可审计性，因为它同时验证了编译正确性和行为正确性。

Abstract: Decompiled C often becomes recompilable only after repair, but recompilation alone does not establish test-observed behavior. A recompiled command-line binary can still parse options incorrectly, print different bytes, or return a different exit status. We present a few-step workflow for repairing decompiled C using compiler feedback and related official tests. Compiler and linker diagnostics first guide build repair. Once the repaired C recompiles into a binary, smoke checks and related official tests expose behavioral discrepancies for semantic repair. In a preliminary static-enriched evaluation on 104 Coreutils 9.5 binaries with available decompiler exports and deterministic exact-output smoke comparisons, 91 binaries (87.5%) recompile and pass the test gate; 9 do not recompile within the repair budget, and 4 recompile but still fail the test gate. The result suggests that test-gate feedback can make LLM-assisted repair of decompiled C more auditable than compile-only recovery.

</details>


### [53] [One Is Not Enough: The Untold Story of Multiple Security Patches for One Vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07224)
*Fangyuan Zhang,Lyuye Zhang,Lingling Fan,Chengwei Liu,Yinan Li,Liang Huang,Yang Liu,Zheli Liu,Sen Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对多安全补丁（multi-SP）漏洞进行大规模实证研究，通过合并四个主要漏洞数据库构建了包含6,053个多SP CVE和16,260个补丁的数据集，发现20.6%的已修补CVE涉及多个补丁。作者提出两层级分类体系（6大类、16子类），并开发了基于该分类体系的SPectre原型工具，在跨仓库和同仓库场景下分别达到0.873和0.927的召回率，并在20个被记录为单补丁的CVE中发现28个未报告的补丁，表明多SP漏洞普遍存在且被系统性低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全补丁是修复软件漏洞的主要机制，但单个漏洞并非总由一个补丁解决——修复可能增量完成、跨维护分支传播或在相关仓库中复制。由于补丁记录不完整，下游用户可能只观察到部分修复集并仅应用部分修补。然而，多SP现象的普遍性和成因仍了解甚少，综合补丁发现仍然困难，因此需要大规模实证研究来理解该问题。

Method: 本文通过合并四个主要漏洞数据库构建大规模数据集，识别多SP漏洞；分析漏洞与多个SP关联的根因，归纳出两层分类体系（6大类、16子类）；基于该分类学开发SPectre原型工具，用于综合补丁发现，并通过人工验证地真值对其性能进行评估。

Result: 数据集包含6,053个多SP CVE及16,260个SP，显示20.6%的有补丁CVE涉及多个SP，合并数据库可使识别量比任何单一来源增加36-55%。SPectre在300个多SP CVE上，同仓库场景召回率达0.927，跨仓库场景达0.873；在100个被所有公共数据库记录为单补丁的近期CVE中，SPectre在20个CVE上发现了28个此前未报告的SP。

Conclusion: 多SP漏洞既普遍又被系统性低估。研究结果强调了提升补丁完整性意识、改进漏洞数据库管理和开发关系感知安全工具的必要性，以支持更全面的补丁发现与漏洞修复实践。

Abstract: Security patches (SPs) are the main mechanism for fixing software vulnerabilities, yet a single vulnerability is not always resolved by a single patch: fixes may be completed incrementally, propagated across maintained branches, or replicated across related repositories. When patch records are incomplete, downstream users may observe only part of the required fix set and therefore apply only partial patching. However, comprehensive patch discovery remains difficult because the prevalence and causes of the multi-SP phenomenon are still poorly understood. In this paper, we present the first large-scale empirical study of multi-SP vulnerabilities. By merging four major vulnerability databases, we construct a dataset of 6,053 multi-SP CVEs with 16,260 SPs, showing that 20.6% of CVEs with patches involve multiple SPs and that merging databases increases recognized multi-SP CVE counts by 36-55% over any single source. We further analyze why a vulnerability is associated with multiple SPs and derive a two-level taxonomy with 6 categories and 16 sub-categories. Based on these findings, we develop SPectre, a taxonomy-driven prototype for comprehensive patch discovery. On 300 multi-SP CVEs, after manually verifying ground-truth SPs, SPectre improves multi-SP patch coverage over representative patch localization baselines, achieving 0.927 recall on same-repository cases and 0.873 recall on cross-repository cases after manual ground truth verification. On 100 recent CVEs recorded as single-patch by all public databases, SPectre further discovers 28 previously unreported SPs across 20 CVEs. Our results show that multi-SP vulnerabilities are both prevalent and systematically underreported, motivating stronger patch-completeness awareness, improved vulnerability database curation, and relation-aware security tooling.

</details>


### [54] [Quality Metrics for LLM-Generated Asset Administration Shells: A Perturbation-Based Evaluation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07290)
*Janek Groß,Elena Zentgraf,Jens Heidrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对工业4.0中资产管理壳（AAS）的AI生成质量问题，提出了一种基于扰动的评估框架，系统性地评估了多种质量指标在检测AI生成AAS退化方面的可靠性。基于200个产品生成6,400个AAS实例（使用GPT-4o-mini、Qwen3和DeepSeek-R1），发现基于属性名称精确匹配和属性值相似性软匹配的指标（尤其是基于值召回率和基于名称的F1分数）是最可靠的质量退化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAS是工业4.0中实现物理与软件资产互操作性的关键标准化数字表示。虽然LLM可以从非结构化源（如产品数据表）生成AAS子模型，但这一AI生成过程面临三大质量保障挑战：意外错误、缺乏真实标注参考、缺乏标准化质量指标，这些都阻碍了可靠的实际应用部署。

Method: 采用基于扰动的评估框架：通过沿多个维度系统地退化AAS生成质量，评估不同质量指标对质量变化的反映能力。实验使用来自多家制造商的200个产品数据集，使用三种LLM（GPT-4o-mini、Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1）生成6,400个AAS实例。

Result: 基于精确匹配属性名称和相似性软匹配属性值的指标最能可靠反映质量退化，其中基于值的召回率和基于名称的F1分数表现最佳。此外，还量化了不同类型扰动的影响程度，并分析了不同模型家族和产品类别之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为选择合适质量指标、调优基于LLM的生成管线以及将AI生成的AAS集成到工业应用中提供了决策支持，推动了AI生成AAS在工业场景中的可靠采纳。

Abstract: The rapid digital transformation of manufacturing, often referred to as Industry 4.0, relies on seamless interoperability between physical and software assets. A central enabler is the Asset Administration Shell (AAS), a standardized digital representation of such assets. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable the generation of AAS submodels from unstructured sources such as product datasheets but raise challenges for quality assurance. In particular, unexpected errors, the lack of ground truth references, and the absence of standardized quality metrics hinder reliable adoption. In this work, we evaluate quality metrics for AI-generated AAS using a perturbation-based evaluation framework. By systematically degrading AAS generation along multiple dimensions, we assess how well different metrics reflect quality changes. Based on a dataset of 200 products from multiple manufacturers, we generate 6,400 AAS instances using GPT-4o-mini, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1. Our results show that metrics based on exact matching of property names and similarity-based soft matching of property values, in particular value-based recall and name-based F1 score, provide the most reliable indicators of quality degradation. Furthermore, we quantify the impact of different perturbation types and analyze differences across model families and product segments. These findings support the selection of suitable metrics, the tuning of LLM-based pipelines, and the integration of AI-generated AAS into industrial applications.

</details>


### [55] [When Stakeholder-centric Requirements Engineering is Not Enough: An Action Research Study on Legacy System Modernisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07340)
*Ruward S. Karper,Damian A. Tamburri,Alessio Ferrari,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过在某跨国能源公司进行行动研究，探讨以利益相关者为中心的需求工程（RE）如何支持遗留系统现代化中的现状（as-is）与目标（to-be）系统之间的差距分析。结果显示该方法能有效产生利益相关者认可的需求，但在需求与遗留系统功能映射上达成共识方面效果有限，表明利益相关者中心的RE是必要但不充分的，需结合不确定性感知、迭代及基于证据的实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留系统现代化是数字化转型中的重大挑战，尤其是当组织依赖于部分被理解的长生命周期业务关键系统时。现代化不仅是技术挑战，更是需求工程问题，受利益相关者视角影响。本研究旨在考察以利益相关者为中心的RE在遗留系统现代化情境中支持差距分析的程度。

Method: 在一家从事系统现代化的跨国能源公司中开展行动研究（action research），应用以利益相关者为中心的需求工程实践，包括：利益相关者识别、半结构化启发式访谈、通过德尔菲法（Delphi method）达成共识，以及使用MoSCoW方法进行优先级排序。

Result: 该过程在产出利益相关者普遍认为可理解且正确的需求方面有效，但在就所启发需求如何映射到遗留系统功能达成共识方面效果不佳。

Conclusion: 以利益相关者为中心的RE是现代化实践中必要但不充分的条件。研究指出需要结合不确定性感知、迭代、基于证据的现代化实践，将利益相关者视角与人工和工具辅助的遗留系统分析相结合。

Abstract: Legacy system modernisation is a major challenge in digital transformation, especially when organisations depend on long-lived, business-critical systems that are only partly understood. In such context, organisations must define future needs while determining what current systems actually do and which functions to retain, adapt, or replace. Modernisation is therefore not only a technical challenge but also a requirements engineering (RE) problem, shaped by stakeholder perspectives. This study examines how far stakeholder-centric RE can support gap analysis between the system-as-is and the system-to-be in a legacy modernisation context. We conducted an action research study in a multinational energy company engaged in system modernisation. In the study, we applied stakeholder-centric RE practices, including stakeholder identification, semi-structured elicitation interviews, agreement-building through the Delphi method, and prioritisation with the MoSCoW method. The results show that this process was effective in producing requirements stakeholders generally viewed as understandable and correct, but less effective in achieving agreement on how elicited requirements mapped to legacy system functionality. The findings suggest that stakeholder-centric RE is necessary but not sufficient, pointing to the need for uncertainty-aware, iterative, and evidence-based modernisation practices that combine stakeholder perspectives with manual and tool-assisted analysis of legacy systems.

</details>


### [56] [EnvPilot: Systematic Design and Evaluation of an Experience-Augmented Agent for Software Environment Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07357)
*Hanwu Chen,Hanyu Lin,Zhanjiang Yang,Linhao Zhang,Aoyan Li,Jinxi Li,Meng Li,Yin Chen,Daoguang Zan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EnvPilot是一个基于经验增强的智能体系统，通过可扩展的轨迹派生记忆（TDM）和上下文感知检索机制，实现了软件环境搭建中历史执行经验的系统化复用，在自建的多语言基准AES-Bench上达到75.00%的Pass@1成功率，成为新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件环境搭建（Environment Setup）是软件工程中关键且复杂的任务，依赖专家知识。现有自动化方法缺乏从过往执行轨迹中积累经验并随时间演进的能力，导致冗余探索、忽略已有有效方案、难以在不同软件生态中泛化。

Method: EnvPilot维护一个可扩展的轨迹派生记忆（TDM），初始包含667条高质量经验。系统性地将历史执行轨迹中的隐式知识转化为结构化经验，并通过上下文感知检索（Context-aware Retrieval）机制在任务执行时检索最相关的指导。该方法能够组合多种已验证的搭建策略，提供比依赖静态项目文件或网络检索更精确、更详细的指导。同时构建了涵盖9种编程语言、112个真实GitHub实例的多语言基准AES-Bench用于评估。

Result: EnvPilot在AES-Bench上实现75.00%的Pass@1成功率，达到新的SOTA，同时降低了推理成本。实证研究表明结构化经验表示和上下文感知检索机制均对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: EnvPilot证明了轨迹派生经验复用的有效性，为软件自动化环境搭建提供了一种可演进、可泛化的新范式。结构化经验表示结合上下文感知检索是实现高性能的关键设计。

Abstract: Environment Setup is a critical yet complex task in software engineering that relies heavily on expert knowledge. Existing automated environment setup methods lack the ability to accumulate experience from past execution trajectories and to evolve over time. As a result, their performance is limited because they often perform redundant exploration, ignore useful past solutions, and fail to generalize across diverse software ecosystems. We present the systematic design and empirical validation of EnvPilot, an experience-augmented agent that operationalizes trajectory-derived experience reuse for software environment setup. EnvPilot maintains an expandable Trajectory-Derived Memory (TDM), initialized with 667 high-quality experiences. It systematically transforms implicit knowledge from historical execution trajectories into structured experience and retrieves the most relevant guidance during task execution through the Context-aware Retrieval mechanism. This enables EnvPilot to combine multiple validated setup strategies, providing more precise and detailed guidance than methods that rely solely on static project files or web retrieval. To evaluate EnvPilot, we construct AES-Bench, a multilingual benchmark of 112 real-world GitHub instances across 9 programming languages. Experiments show that EnvPilot achieves a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) with a 75.00% Pass@1 success rate while reducing reasoning costs. Our empirical study shows that both the structured experience representation and the Context-aware Retrieval mechanism are essential.

</details>


### [57] [Scanning the Harness: An Empirical Study of Supply-Chain Defects in AI Coding-Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07360)
*Benjamin Kapner,Carmel Soceanu,Alicia Petrunin,Hofni Gartner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI编码代理（如Claude Code、Cursor、GitHub Copilot、OpenAI Codex）的配置工件生态（指令文件、技能、钩子、MCP服务器声明、子代理等），对3,171个公共GitHub仓库进行了系统安全审计，发现16.0%的设置存在安全缺陷，其中9.8%安装了未固定版本的MCP服务器，3.1%通过看似受限的授权（如Bash(python:*)）预先批准任意执行，3.8%携带预先批准shell的技能。所有发现均经过独立实现复现、语言模型裁决和二次独立模型复核三重验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的配置层是一个从市场和公共仓库安装的依赖层，以开发者权限运行，但没有锁文件、没有安装时检查、也没有描述组件行为的词汇表。这种"无防护"的依赖层带来了潜在的安全风险，但此前缺乏系统性研究来量化这些风险。

Method: 作者对3,171个公共GitHub仓库（包括2,660个组装两种以上组件类型的设置和511个发布的技能集合）进行扫描。仅统计可从字节决定的规则（涉及安全暴露、无法工作的配置或偏离Agent Skills规范），并采用三重验证流程：独立实现从仓库的固定提交重新推导每个发现、发布的提示词驱动语言模型裁决每个分歧、第二独立模型会话复核每个计数的配对。

Result: 三类安全问题得到确认：9.8%的设置安装了未固定版本的MCP服务器，3.1%预先批准了看似受限授权背后的任意执行，3.8%携带预先批准shell的技能。总计16.0%的设置存在安全缺陷，16.7%存在任何类型的确认缺陷（对应裸扫描器在同规则下的25.5%检出率）。第三类缺陷出现在3.7%的集合中，市场扫描可以发现。未确认任何凭据窃取路径。比较两个文件的规则检测到的差异通常是预期的，被报告为观测结果而非缺陷。

Conclusion: AI编码代理配置生态存在系统性的供应链安全隐患：无锁文件、无安装时检查、无能力描述词汇表，导致16%的设置存在可确认的安全缺陷。研究工具、语料清单、提示词和所有裁决均已开源，可供社区进一步研究和改进。

Abstract: AI coding agents such as Claude Code, Cursor, GitHub Copilot, and OpenAI Codex are configured through artifacts developers write and share: instruction files, skills, hooks, MCP server declarations, subagents. This harness is a dependency layer installed from marketplaces and public repositories, running with the developer's privileges, with no lockfile, no install-time check, and no vocabulary for what a component may do. We study it over 3,171 public GitHub repositories: 2,660 setups that assemble two or more component types and 511 published skill collections. We measure only rules decidable from bytes whose consequence is a security exposure, a configuration that cannot work, or a departure from the Agent Skills specification, and validate every finding before it counts: an independent implementation re-derives it from the repository at its pinned commit, a language-model adjudicator with a released prompt rules on every disagreement, and a second independent model session re-checks every counted pair. Three security classes survive: 9.8% of setups install an MCP server with no version pinned, 3.1% pre-approve arbitrary execution behind a scoped-looking grant such as Bash(python:*), and 3.8% carry a skill that pre-approves the shell for whoever installs it. In total 16.0% of setups carry a security defect and 16.7% a confirmed defect of any kind, against a raw scanner rate of 25.5% on the same rules; the third class ships inside 3.7% of collections, where a marketplace scan can see it. Rules that compare two files detect differences that are usually intended and are reported as observations. No credential-exfiltration path was confirmed. The instrument, corpus manifest, prompt, and every verdict are released.

</details>


### [58] [Trust the Spec, Not the Code - A Specification-First, AI-Assisted Case Study in Online Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07365)
*Eitan Farchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以"自然语言+轻量数学（LaTeX编写）"为中间规范语言、由LLM辅助审阅/证明/生成实现的形式化规格方法，并在在线银行资金转账领域进行了第二次独立案例研究（首次为组织知识增长模拟），同时扩展到定期/循环转账等更复杂问题，提出了AI提出的运行时覆盖模型及Z形式化，强调其局限在于代码到规范的求精由AI承担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规格曾承诺早期错误检测、显式不变量和正确性设计，但其记法成本阻碍了主流实践。作者认为AI消除了这一成本的主要部分，使自然语言+轻量数学成为一种足够精确的中间规格语言，从而推动形式化方法的工业采用。

Method: （i）在在线银行资金转账领域进行了第二个独立案例研究，复现先前组织知识增长模拟中的纪律；（ii）通过定期/循环转账给方法施加压力测试，展示生成代码大幅增长而守恒不变量及证明不随规模增长；（iii）提出AI提出的运行时覆盖模型，论证"从未违反≠已覆盖"；（iv）给出Z形式化，包括配对的成功/失败操作模式和针对所有可达配置的归纳集上证明的不变量，并设计实验让AI自行提出Z接口。

Result: 两个不同领域共享一条主轴——守恒不变量（之前是知识，这里是金钱），表明该方法可跨领域泛化。压力测试表明不变量和证明的规模不随功能复杂度增长而增长；AI提出的运行时覆盖模型和Z接口也成功实现。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于守恒不变量类系统，可跨领域推广；但作者明确承认其局限：证明和运行时检查位于规格层面，不建立生成代码对规格的求精关系（该步骤委托给AI），且本研究是案例研究而非受控实验。

Abstract: Formal specification promises early error detection, explicit invariants, and correctness by design, yet its notational cost has kept it out of mainstream practice. We argue that AI removes much of that cost: natural language enriched with lightweight mathematics, written in \LaTeX, can serve as an intermediate specification language that is precise enough to reason over and prove, while a large language model (LLM) reviews it for ambiguity, drafts proofs, and generates the implementation. The specification becomes the artifact one authors, reviews, proves, and refines; the code becomes regenerable output. This paper is a follow-on to a prior study that established the discipline on an organizational-knowledge-growth simulation~\cite{predecessor2026}. Here we replicate the discipline in a different domain---an online-banking fund-transfer service---and extend it. The two domains share one spine: a conservation invariant (knowledge in the prior study, money here), which suggests the approach generalizes across domains. We contribute: (i) a second, independent case study of the method; (ii) a stress-test of the method on a richer problem---\emph{scheduled/recurring} transfers---whose generated code grows substantially while the invariant and its proof do not; (iii) an \emph{AI-proposed runtime coverage model} for invariants (``never violated $\neq$ covered''); and (iv) a Z formalization, including paired success/failure operation schemas and an invariant proved over the inductive set of all reachable configurations, together with an experiment in which the AI proposes the Z interfaces itself. We are explicit about the method's limits: the proofs and runtime checks live at the specification level and do not establish that the generated code refines the specification---that step is delegated to the AI. This is a case study, not a controlled experiment.

</details>


### [59] [A Text Mining and Classification Approach for Analyzing Architecture Decision Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07375)
*Nicolás Miccio Palermo,Antonela Tommasel,J. Andrés Diaz-Pace*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于文本挖掘与分类的自动化方法，对约550个开源仓库中的架构决策记录（ADRs）进行大规模分析，结合主题建模、LLM分类和模板合规检查，发现ADRs常捕获存在性、技术和流程相关决策，但备选方案、决策驱动因素及部分质量关注点记录不足，且内容与MADR模板存在系统性错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ADRs已成为记录架构知识的流行轻量级机制，但关于ADRs捕获的架构关注点类型及其内容与既有架构知识概念和文档实践的一致程度，目前缺乏大规模实证证据。

Method: 提出自动化文本挖掘与分类方法，应用于从约550个开源仓库提取的ADR数据集，综合使用主题建模、基于LLM的分类以及模板合规性检查，分析决策分类体系和质量属性，并评估ADR对MADR模板的遵循程度。

Result: 研究发现ADR经常捕获存在性、技术和流程相关决策，而备选方案、决策驱动因素以及某些质量关注点记录不足；同时观察到ADR内容与模板部分之间存在反复出现的错配现象。

Conclusion: 这些对当前文档实践的洞察为架构师提供了有价值的信息，帮助他们反思ADR应如何被使用以有效处理架构知识；此外，所提出的自动化方法可适应于其他架构任务。

Abstract: Architectural decision records (ADRs) have become a popular lightweight mechanism for documenting architectural knowledge in software projects. However, there is limited empirical evidence on the kinds of architectural concerns captured in ADRs and how well their contents align with established architectural knowledge concepts and documentation practices. In this paper, we propose an automated text-mining and classification approach for analyzing ADRs at scale. We apply this approach to a dataset of ADRs extracted from ~550 open-source repositories, combining topic modeling, LLM-based classification, and template compliance checks. Our analysis examines decision taxonomies and quality attributes, and the degree to which ADRs adhere to the MADR template. Our findings show that ADRs frequently capture existence, technology, and process-related decisions, while alternatives, decisions drivers, and some quality concerns remain under-documented. We also observe recurring mismatches between ADR contents and template sections. These insights into current documentation practices provide architects with valuable information to reflect on how ADRs are and should be used to effectively deal with architectural knowledge. Furthermore, our automated approach is adaptable to other architectural tasks.

</details>


### [60] [A Surrogate-based Approach for Fast Multi-objective Architectural Refactoring Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07389)
*J. Andrés Diaz-Pace,Daniele Di Pompeo,Antonela Tommasel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索使用基于回归技术的代理模型来近似软件架构分析工具的输出，以显著降低多目标进化算法在软件模型优化中的计算成本，实验表明可节省高达30%的计算时间并保持Pareto前沿质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件模型优化中，多目标进化算法需要反复调用昂贵的分析工具来评估架构适应度，在复杂软件模型和大设计空间中面临计算效率低下的挑战，需要探索成本更低的近似评估方法。

Method: 构建基于回归技术的代理模型（surrogate models）来逼近架构分析工具的输出，从而在搜索过程中以较低的计算成本替代昂贵的适应度评估。

Result: 实验表明代理模型可节省高达30%的计算时间，同时保持进化算法提供的Pareto前沿质量，但生成的架构模型存在一些差异。

Conclusion: 代理模型是将多目标架构优化扩展到更大设计空间和更复杂架构模型的有前景的方案。

Abstract: Software model optimization is a process that generates architecture alternatives aimed at improving quantifiable non-functional properties of software systems, such as performance and reliability. Multi-objective evolutionary algorithms are commonly used to explore the search space and help designers identify trade-offs among competing non-functional properties (e.g., through a Pareto front). However, such algorithms face efficiency challenges in complex software models and large design spaces, since evaluating the fitness (i.e., the quality) of each architecture requires analysis tools that become computationally expensive when repeatedly invoked during the search process. In this paper, we explore the construction of surrogate models based on regression techniques to approximate the outputs of these analysis tools at significantly lower computational cost, while maintaining reasonable output accuracy. Our experimental results suggest that surrogate models provide savings of up to $30\%$ in computational time and maintain the Pareto front quality provided by evolutionary algorithms. Also, we observed some differences in the architectural models produced by our approach. Overall, surrogate models constitute a promising approach for scaling multi-objective architecture optimization to larger spaces and complex architectural models.

</details>


### [61] [An Empirical Study on the Impact of Change Granularity in Refactoring Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07482)
*Lei Chen,Shinpei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现，在commit历史中，一些重构只能在多提交的粗粒度级别检测到（称为粗粒度重构CGRs），另一些只能在单提交粒度检测到但在粗粒度中不显著（称为短暂重构EPRs）。通过对32个开源Java项目的分析，揭示了两者的特征、成因及其与提交消息的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重构检测技术仅针对单次提交的粒度进行检测，但实际开发中重构可能跨越多个提交。这种以单提交为粒度检测的局限性，导致部分重构无法被准确识别，需要更细粒度的分析理解重构行为。

Method: 对32个开源Java项目进行实证研究，定义并区分了CGRs（仅在粗粒度/多提交可检测）和EPRs（单提交可检测但粗粒度下不显著）两类重构，分析其特征、成因，并将其与提交消息进行关联分析。

Result: CGRs和EPRs在开发过程中均频繁出现。涉及类/包拆分或合并以及继承结构修改的重构类型倾向于成为CGRs；针对变量和属性等小对象的重构以及具有上下文敏感检测标准的重构倾向于成为EPRs。约20%的提交消息明确暗示了CGRs的存在。

Conclusion: CGRs和EPRs在重构研究中应受到重视，现有重构检测工具需要扩展以支持多提交粒度的CGRs识别，以提升对代码变更和软件演进的理解。

Abstract: Detecting refactorings in commit history is essential to improve comprehension to code changes on code reviews, and to provide valuable information for empirical studies on software evolution. Techniques have been proposed to accurately detect refactorings on the granularity of a single commit. However, refactorings can be made over multiple commits because of their complexity or other practical development problems, which cause detecting on only the granularity of a single commit not enough. We observe that some refactorings can only be detected in coarser granularity, i.e., changes conducted over multiple commits, or in the granularity of a single commit but not in coarse-grained. We call these types of refactorings as coarse-grained refactorings (CGRs) and ephemeral refactorings (EPRs). We investigated the features and causes of CGRs and EPRs through an empirical study of 32 open-source Java projects and found that both commonly occur during development. In addition, we found that refactoring types related to splitting or merging classes and packages, as well as those involving modifications to the inheritance structure, tend to be CGRs, and types targeting small objects such as variables and attributes, and refactorings with context-sensitive detection criteria tend to be EPRs. The causes of CGRs and EPRs are analyzed and categorized, and the relationships between the commit messages of CGRs and themselves are also assessed. We found that about 20% of commit messages explicitly suggest the existence of CGRs. We suggest that CGRs and EPRs be valued in refactoring research and that detectors be extended to identify CGRs.

</details>


### [62] ["We Permit the Use of AI, but [...]": The Landscape of AI Policies in Popular Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07542)
*Andre Hora,Romain Robbes,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了281个开源社区的AI贡献政策，并从AI使用许可、AI披露实践、AI"垃圾内容"（slop）对策和AI政策演变四个研究问题进行了系统性分类；发现83.3%的政策允许或鼓励AI参与代码贡献，67.3%要求高水平人工参与，48.8%要求披露AI使用，并识别出10种针对AI slop的对策，且半数政策文件已被修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社区正在迅速出现一种新的治理产物——AI贡献政策，此类政策在数月前几乎不存在，而现在正被大量编写和采用。然而对这一新兴治理机制缺乏系统性的实证研究，需要为维护者和研究者提供基线数据和标注语料库。

Method: 作者收集并分析了281个AI贡献政策，沿六个维度进行人工分类；同时追踪了92个专门的AI政策文件随时间的变化，围绕四个研究问题（AI使用许可、AI披露实践、AI slop对策、AI政策演变）展开分析。

Result: 研究结果显示：（1）许可而非例外是常态，83.3%的政策允许或鼓励AI用于代码贡献，但附带条件，67.3%要求高水平人工参与，43.4%分配问责；（2）48.8%的政策要求披露AI使用，最常见于PR描述和提交信息中，但披露内容差异很大；（3）识别出10种针对AI slop的对策，针对PR、用户和自主代理；（4）政策动态演变，专门政策文件中半数已在创建后被修订。

Conclusion: 该研究为开源社区维护者和研究人员提供了AI贡献政策的系统化基线数据和标注语料库，为研究AI政策对开源生态的影响奠定了基础。

Abstract: Open source communities are converging on a new governance artifact: the AI contribution policy. These policies barely existed a few months ago and are now being written and adopted. We analyzed 281 AI contribution policies, and manually classified them along the six dimensions; to study how policies change, we also tracked 92 dedicated AI policy files over time. We answer four research questions on (1) AI usage allowance, (2) AI disclosure practices, (3) AI slop countermeasures, and (4) AI policy evolution. We find that, first, permission is the norm rather than the exception: 83.3% of policies permit or encourage AI in code contributions. But permission comes with conditions, as 67.3% require a high level of human involvement and 43.4% assign accountability. Second, AI disclosure is required by 48.8% of policies, most often in pull request descriptions and commit messages, but what must be disclosed varies widely. Third, we identify ten countermeasures against AI slop, targeting pull requests, users, and autonomous agents. Finally, policies are not static: half of the dedicated AI policy files have already been revised since creation. Our results give maintainers and researchers a baseline and a labeled corpus for studying the impact of AI policies.

</details>


### [63] [RefVerifier: Semi-Automated Reference Claim Verification for Scientific Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07652)
*Stefania Mocan,Florian Angermeir,Mark Kreitz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RefVerifier，一个半自动化的引文受限参考验证原型系统，旨在辅助学术同行评审。该系统从手稿中提取含引用的句子、核对参考文献元数据、解析开放获取全文、定位相关证据段落，并生成带自然语言解释的判定结果。在公开基准上，声称检测F1达0.990，开放获取解析率达57.6%，证据定位在摘要上的命中率为98%、全文为68%；端到端测试中判定准确率为71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究投稿数量激增，同行评审人面临严重的时间约束，对引用支持的声明进行系统性验证成本过高，导致无根据的声明和语义漂移在科学文献中未被检测地传播。现有的事实核查和检索增强生成工具要么基于开放网络数据，要么孤立地评估声明而不处理完整手稿，缺乏面向学术评审场景的解决方案。

Method: RefVerifier是一个半自动化的引文受限参考验证原型，由多个模块构成：从手稿中提取含引用的句子（claim detection）；将参考文献元数据与学术数据库核对（bibliography checking）；将引用解析到开放获取PDF全文；在全文和摘要中定位相关证据段落（evidence localization）；最终生成带自然语言解释的判定结果（verdict），同时保留评审人监督。

Result: 在公开基准上的评估结果为：声称检测F1分数达0.990；57.6%的参考文献可通过开放获取解析；证据定位在摘要上的命中率为98%，在完整被引论文上的命中率为68%。在包含八篇手稿的端到端测试中，RefVerifier的判定准确率达到71%。

Conclusion: 通过自动化文档检索和证据定位、同时保留评审人监督权，RefVerifier为半自动化学术出版完整性核查的可行性提供了初步证据，表明该方向具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: As software engineering research submission counts surge, peer reviewers face severe time constraints, making systematic verification of citation-supported claims prohibitively expensive. Consequently, unsubstantiated claims and semantic drift can propagate undetected across scientific literature. Existing approaches such as fact-checking and retrieval-augmented generation tools operate on open-domain web data or evaluate claims in isolation without processing complete manuscripts. To address this gap, we present RefVerifier, a semi-automated, citation-bounded reference verification prototype designed to support in academic peer review. RefVerifier extracts citation-bearing sentences from manuscripts, checks bibliography metadata against scholarly databases, resolves references to full-text open-access PDFs, localizes relevant evidence passages, and generates verdicts with natural language explanations. Evaluating RefVerifier on public benchmarks shows claim detection at an F1 score of 0.990, open-access resolution of 57.6% of references, and evidence localization with a hit rate of 98% on abstracts and 68% on complete cited papers. In an end-to-end test with eight manuscripts, RefVerifier achieves a verdict accuracy of 71%. By automating document retrieval and evidence localization while preserving reviewer oversight, RefVerifier provides first indicators for the feasibility of semi-automated integrity checks in scholarly publishing.

</details>


### [64] [CodeTD: Topology of Attention Detects Hallucinations in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07779)
*Daria Voronkova,Ilya Trofimov,Anton Dmitriev,Eduard Tulchinskii,Evgeny Burnaev,Serguei Barannikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeTD，这是首个基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）对Code LLM注意力图进行分析、在代码执行前评估其正确性的方法，在多个基准和多种编程语言中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代码助手工具的普及，自动评估生成代码的正确性成为一个重要挑战。Code LLM容易产生幻觉，导致生成代码不能解决问题，甚至包含严重的安全漏洞。因此需要在代码执行前就对其正确性进行预评估。

Method: CodeTD利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）对Code LLM的注意力图进行分析，通过量化注意力图的拓扑特征来度量提示（prompt）与生成代码之间的不匹配程度，从而在代码执行前预测其正确性。

Result: 在HumanEval、MBPP、BigCodeBench、MultiPL-E等基准上，覆盖5种编程语言和10个Code LLM（参数规模最高达34B），实验结果表明CodeTD优于近期基线方法，且在不同编程基准之间具有良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: CodeTD首次将拓扑数据分析引入Code LLM代码正确性的执行前评估任务，展示了注意力图的拓扑模式与代码正确性之间的关联，为该领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: As AI-code assistant tools become widespread, automatic assessment of the correctness of generated code becomes a significant challenge. Code LLMs are prone to hallucinations, which may lead to code that does not solve the required problem, or even to code with severe security vulnerabilities. In this paper, we introduce CodeTD -- the first approach to pre-execution assessment of code correctness based on topological data analysis (TDA) of Code LLMs' attention maps. Our method quantifies prompt-generation mismatch using topological patterns of attention maps. We carry out experiments with common benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, BigCodeBench, MultiPL-E), 5 programming languages and 10 Code LLMs of size up to 34B parameters. The experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms recent baselines. Moreover, CodeTD is transferable between coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [65] [You can contribute if you... An Empirical Framework of AI Contribution Policies in OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07919)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件（OSS）项目中新兴的AI中介贡献政策进行了实证研究，通过分析项目政策背后的理由、规则和期望，提出了"AI贡献治理框架"（AI Contribution Governance Framework），用于组织跨项目的共性关注点和治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在降低开源软件贡献（代码、文档、issue报告、评审互动）的生产成本，这既带来了更广泛参与的机会，也扰乱了维护者对贡献者努力、能力和问责制的评估方式。OSS项目因此开始通过贡献指南等文档规范AI中介贡献，但学术界对这一新兴政策现象缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 这是一项实证研究，通过分析各OSS项目中对AI中介贡献的政策文件，评估其内在的理由（rationales）、规则（rules）和期望（expectations），从而提炼出政策所关注的核心问题维度。

Result: 分析表明，这些政策旨在保护稀缺的维护者注意力、保持问责制、维持有意义的评审互动、解决法律和质量问题，以及维护新手学习的路径。基于这些发现，提出了AI贡献治理框架，系统性地组织跨项目中反复出现的关注点与治理机制。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅帮助开源社区制定AI贡献政策，还为研究人员提供了研究AI如何改变协作式软件生产的词汇和分析工具。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is reshaping open source software (OSS) contribution by lowering the cost of producing code, documentation, issue reports, and review interactions. This creates opportunities for broader participation, but also disrupts how maintainers assess contributor effort, competence, and accountability. In response, OSS projects are beginning to regulate AI-mediated contribution through contribution guidelines and other project documentation. This paper presents an empirical study of these emerging policies. We analyze project policies on AI-mediated contributions by evaluating their underlying rationales, rules, and expectations. Our analysis shows that these policies seek to protect scarce maintainer attention, preserve accountability, sustain meaningful review interactions, address legal and quality concerns, and maintain pathways for newcomer learning. Based on these findings, we introduce the AI Contribution Governance Framework, which organizes recurring concerns and governance mechanisms across projects. The framework helps OSS communities develop AI contribution policies and provides researchers with a vocabulary for studying how AI is changing collaborative software production.

</details>


### [66] [Service Health Engineering for Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08020)
*Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"服务健康工程"作为一门实用的可靠性学科，主张通过端到端用户结果而非仅依赖组件仪表板来评估分布式系统健康，并以文档审批工作流为例阐述相关实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统支撑着许多关键业务工作流，但服务健康往往通过组件仪表板而非端到端用户结果来判断，这可能导致静默故障和滞留的异步工作未被发现。

Method: 以文档审批工作流为运行示例，论述了服务承诺（service promises）、服务级别指标与目标（SLIs/SLOs）、看门狗（watchdogs）、事件指标（incident measures）、弹性测试（resiliency testing）和每周服务健康评审（weekly service-health reviews）等实践，并提出一种人工审核、AI辅助的报告架构来汇总服务健康证据，避免AI成为自主决策者。

Result: 该框架将既有的可靠性实践围绕"用户旅程是否按承诺完成"这一核心加以整合，可揭示静默失败和滞留的异步工作。

Conclusion: 服务健康工程通过将分散的可靠性实践统一到用户旅程完成的视角下，为分布式系统提供了一种从组件视角转向端到端结果评估的系统性方法，其中AI仅作为辅助工具，最终决策由人主导。

Abstract: Distributed systems support many critical business workflows, but service health is often judged through component dashboards rather than through end-to-end user outcomes. This article presents service health engineering as a practical reliability discipline that connects telemetry, workflow completion, dependency behavior, operational readiness, and recovery validation. Using a document approval workflow as a running example, it describes how service promises, service-level indicators and objectives, watchdogs, incident measures, resiliency testing, and weekly service-health reviews can reveal silent failures and stranded asynchronous work. It also presents a human-reviewed, AI-assisted reporting architecture for assembling service-health evidence without making AI an autonomous decision-maker. The approach brings established reliability practices together around whether user journeys complete as promised.

</details>


### [67] [Agent ATO: Visualizing Agent Interaction Timelines from Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08301)
*Takuto Kawamoto,Yoshiki Hig,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent ATO（Agentic Trajectory Observer），一个从控制台日志中可视化AI编码代理交互时间线的工具，通过提取并分类代理动作（如搜索、读取、编辑、执行），生成全交互时间线和按操作类型过滤的时间线，帮助开发者理解和比较代理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理在开发工作流中日益普及，但仅从最终代码变更难以理解其行为。代理执行任务时产生的交互序列与token使用记录在控制台日志中，但原始日志难以人工直接检查，开发者缺乏直观理解代理行为轨迹的工具。

Method: Agent ATO从控制台日志中提取代理交互信息，按命令或工具类型对操作进行分类，并将其可视化为时间线。除展示全部交互的时间线外，还提供突出文件发现、文件读取、文件编辑和执行操作但保留周围上下文的过滤时间线。

Result: 通过与多个修复任务中选定运行的演示，展示了Agent ATO如何帮助开发者检查和比较代理动作。目前是示例性演示，尚待大规模系统评估。

Conclusion: Agent ATO为AI编码代理行为可视化提供了有效思路，未来工作将扩展到更多代理、任务和开发环境，并系统评估该工具是否降低比较轨迹所需的成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents are becoming part of developers' workflows, but their behavior is difficult to understand from final code changes alone. During a task, agents interact with software repositories through sequences of actions such as searching for files, reading code, editing programs, and running tests or build commands. These interactions, together with token usage, are often recorded in console logs, but raw logs are difficult for developers to inspect. In this paper, we propose Agent ATO (Agentic Trajectory Observer), a tool for visualizing AI coding agent interaction timelines from console logs. Agent ATO extracts agent interactions, classifies them by command or tool type, and visualizes them as timelines. In addition to an all-interaction timeline, Agent ATO provides filtered timelines that emphasize file discovery, file reading, file editing, and execution while preserving surrounding context. We illustrate how Agent ATO may help developers inspect and compare agent actions using selected runs from two repair tasks. Future work will apply Agent ATO to more agents, tasks, and development environments, and will evaluate whether it reduces the effort needed to compare trajectories.

</details>


### [68] [AttnCompress: Dynamic Attention-Guided Trajectory Compression for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08318)
*Zhengran Zeng,Yixin Li,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AttnCompress，一种动态注意力引导的轨迹压缩框架，通过PPL尖峰的结构感知分割、代理注意力权重的相关性估计和动态滚动窗口三个机制，在保持语义和语法关键信息的同时压缩自主软件工程代理的长交互轨迹，在SWE-Bench-Verified上达到53.17%的通过率，同时降低21.6%的token消耗和33.6%的总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件工程(ASE)代理处理复杂任务时产生冗长的交互轨迹，导致上下文窗口限制和成本成为严重瓶颈。现有上下文压缩方法采用静态剪枝策略且存在粒度不匹配问题，无法保留SE任务中至关重要的语义依赖和语法细节。

Method: AttnCompress包含三个关键机制：(1)基于困惑度(PPL)尖峰的结构感知分割，保留代码和日志的语法结构；(2)使用代理注意力权重进行相关性估计，量化历史块与代理当前推理的精确相关性；(3)动态滚动窗口，随任务演进重新评估和召回历史上下文。三者结合弥合语义完整性与动态适应性之间的差距。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上达到53.17%的通过率，优于现有SOTA基线；token消耗减少21.6%，总成本降低33.6%；框架具有模型无关性，能有效泛化到多种编程语言，并在Multi-SWE-Bench上验证了跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: AttnCompress通过动态注意力引导的压缩框架，在严格的证据保留和上下文缩减之间取得了平衡，解决了ASE代理轨迹压缩中语义完整性与动态适应性的矛盾，且具备良好的模型无关性和跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: The transition from human-centric assistance to Autonomous Software Engineering (ASE) agents has enabled the resolution of complex real-world SE tasks. However, the trial-and-error nature of these agents generates lengthy interaction trajectories, creating severe bottlenecks in terms of context window limits and cost. While context compression offers a potential remedy, prior approaches suffer from static pruning strategies and granularity mismatches, often failing to preserve the semantic dependencies and syntactic details crucial for SE tasks. To strictly preserve critical task evidence while reducing context length, we introduce AttnCompress, a dynamic attention-guided trajectory compression framework. Unlike existing approaches, AttnCompress bridges the gap between semantic integrity and dynamic adaptability through three key mechanisms: (1) structure-aware segmentation via perplexity (PPL) spikes to preserve the syntactic structure of code and logs; (2) relevance estimation using proxy attention weights to quantify the precise relevance of historical blocks to the agent's current reasoning; and (3) a dynamic rolling window to re-evaluate and recall historical context as the task evolves. Extensive evaluation on SWE-Bench-Verified and Multi-SWE-Bench demonstrates that AttnCompress achieves a pass rate of 53.17%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art baselines while reducing token consumption by 21.6% and total costs by 33.6%. The framework proves to be model-agnostic and generalizes effectively across diverse programming languages.

</details>


### [69] [Tool Retrievers Are Underestimated: Annotation Expansion Reveals True Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08327)
*Yanyu Zhu,Chenheng Zhang,Shaoshen Chen,Hoilam Pao,Yufei zhang,Jiajun Chai,Dongnian Wang,Zhaoyu Hu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolEX框架，用于自动发现和标注与已标注工具组合功能等价的替代组合，解决现有工具检索基准中"查询→工具"一对一标注忽略一对多映射的问题。应用于Tool-DE基准后发现67.9%的子查询存在等价替代，并将单一真值扩展为平均每条查询5.3个有效组合；多项检索器评估结果显著提升，证明一对一标注系统性低估检索器性能，且30-47%的微调增益实为评估伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界中存在海量且不断演化的工具仓库，同一查询往往可由多个功能等价的不同工具组合解决，查询到工具的映射本质是一对多的。然而现有工具检索基准每个查询仅标注一个相关工具组合，将一对多映射压缩为刚性的一对一标注，导致有效检索结果被误判为失败，系统性低估了检索器的真实性能。

Method: 提出ToolEX框架：自动发现并标注与已标注工具组合功能等价的替代组合，将单一真值扩展为多个有效组合。基于此构建新基准ToolEQ，并重新评估8个基础检索器和2个微调变体；同时将该流程应用于技能检索基准SkillRet以验证问题普遍性。

Result: 在7,360条查询的Tool-DE基准上，ToolEX发现67.9%的子查询存在等价替代方案，将真值扩展为平均每条查询5.3个有效组合。检索器在ToolEQ上的指标较Tool-DE大幅提升，证明一对一标注系统性低估检索器性能；30-47%的微调增益被证实是评估伪影而非真实改进。SkillRet上的实验进一步确认一对一问题超出了工具检索范畴。

Conclusion: 现有工具检索基准的一对一标注方式显著低估了检索器能力，并导致微调收益被高估。ToolEX框架及扩展后的ToolEQ基准更准确地反映了工具检索的真实性能，且该问题具有跨工具检索与技能检索的普遍性，为未来检索基准的构建与公平评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: In open-world scenarios with massive and evolving tool repositories, tool-augmented large language models rely on a retriever to surface relevant tools for a given query. Because such repositories often contain many tools that implement the same functionality, a single query can often be resolved by several distinct but functionally equivalent tool combinations, making the natural query-to-tool mapping inherently one-to-many. However, existing tool retrieval benchmarks annotate each query with a single relevant tool combination, collapsing this one-to-many mapping into a rigid one-to-one annotation and causing valid retrieved tools to be misjudged as failures. To address this, we propose ToolEX (Tool Equivalent eXpansion), a framework that automatically discovers and annotates the tool combinations functionally equivalent to the labeled ones. Applied to the 7,360-query Tool-DE benchmark, ToolEX finds that 67.9% of sub-queries admit equivalent alternatives, expanding the singular ground truth to an average of 5.3 valid combinations per query. Using the expanded benchmark ToolEQ, we re-evaluate eight base retrievers and two fine-tuned variants; metrics on ToolEQ rise substantially over Tool-DE, showing that one-to-one annotation systematically underestimates retrievers and that 30--47% of the reported fine-tuning gain is an evaluation artifact rather than genuine improvement. Applying the same pipeline to skill retrieval on SkillRet further confirms that the one-to-one problem extends beyond tool retrieval.

</details>


### [70] [RepoNav: From Snippet Retrieval to File-Centered Repository Navigation for Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08355)
*Hongzheng Chai,Jiakun Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoNav提出了一种轻量级后检索界面，将扁平化检索结果重组为以文件为中心的导航脚手架，通过展示结构线索和候选目标来帮助LLM代理在定位目标函数前比较兄弟符号，从而提升仓库级代码任务中的函数级定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索工具通常返回扁平的孤立代码片段列表，虽然能浮出相关文件，但缺乏足够的结构信息来帮助代理区分目标函数与同一文件中语义相似的替代函数，导致文件到函数之间存在定位鸿沟。

Method: RepoNav作为一种后检索接口，将检索到的片段重新组织为以文件为中心的导航脚手架，向模型提供紧凑的结构线索和候选目标，支持按需浏览文件结构，帮助代理在选择目标函数前比较同级的兄弟符号。通过受控消融实验验证结构化证据组织的有效性。

Result: 在LocBench基准上，RepoNav在多种模型上均提升了函数级定位性能，缩小了文件到函数之间的差距。消融实验表明增益源于结构化证据组织而非单纯暴露额外文件结构。该方法在仓库级问答基准上也表现出性能改善。

Conclusion: RepoNav证明了通过轻量级后处理重新组织检索结果的结构化界面，能够有效提升LLM代理在仓库级代码任务中的函数定位能力，无需修改底层检索器即可获得一致性收益。

Abstract: Solving repository-level code tasks requires LLM-based agents to use code search tools to navigate large codebases and identify a small set of relevant files and functions. However, current retrieval tools typically return flat lists of isolated code snippets: such lists can surface relevant files, but provide insufficient structure for agents to distinguish the target function from semantically similar alternatives in the same file. We introduce RepoNav, a lightweight post-retrieval interface that reorganizes retrieved snippets into a file-centered navigation scaffold. By presenting compact structural cues and candidate targets, this scaffold guides on-demand file-structure browsing, helping agents compare sibling symbols before selecting a target function. Across diverse models on LocBench, RepoNav improves function-level localization and narrows the file-to-function gap. Controlled ablations demonstrate that these gains come from structured evidence organization rather than simply exposing additional file structure, and the approach also improves performance on a repository-level question-answering benchmark.

</details>


### [71] [Authority Is Not a String: A Capability-Scoped Harness for Prompt-Injection-Resistant Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08371)
*Dimitrios Stamatios Bouras,Yihan Dai,Sergey Mechtaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CapScope是一个harness级别的授权机制，通过为智能体分配独立的类型化能力集来防御间接提示注入，在不影响任务完成率的前提下将注入攻击成功率从33-47/75降至3/75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在沙箱中使用系统级工具时具有"环境权威"（ambient authority）：只要命名资源即可对其操作。间接提示注入通过将恶意指令放入仓库文件或工具输出中，利用这种权威诱使智能体执行用户未请求的操作。现有方法依赖模型识别恶意文本，但模型难以可靠检测所有注入内容。

Method: CapScope在读取仓库内容或工具输出之前，从可信输入中推导出任务范围的整体权限上限（authority ceiling），并为每个子智能体分配独立的类型化能力集，这些能力存储在模型上下文之外。每次工具调用都会根据发出调用的智能体的能力进行校验，一个子智能体的权限不会自动传递给另一个子智能体。

Result: 在Pi编码智能体上实现并评估，覆盖5个Python任务、5个注入面、4种授权条件和3次试验（共300次运行）。在环境权威和全局策略基线中注入效果在33-47/75次运行中执行，而CapScope仅为3/75。任务修复完成率方面，CapScope完成68/75次修复，基线完成68-72/75次，即CapScope在大幅提升安全性的同时几乎没有牺牲任务完成能力。

Conclusion: CapScope通过将授权决策从模型推理中抽出，改为在harness层以能力为基础进行强制校验，能够有效防御间接提示注入，同时保持编码智能体的任务完成率。这种机制不依赖模型识别恶意文本的能力，为编码智能体提供了一种更稳健的安全边界。

Abstract: Coding agents use system-level tools to read files, execute commands, and modify source code. Within the agent's sandbox, these tools often carry ambient authority: naming a resource is sufficient to act on it. Indirect prompt injection exploits this authority by placing instructions in repository files or tool output that cause the agent to perform actions the user did not request. We propose CapScope, a harness-level authorization mechanism that restricts tool use without requiring the model to identify malicious text. Before repository contents or tool output are read, CapScope derives a task-wide authority ceiling from trusted input. It then assigns each agent a separate set of typed capabilities, stored outside the model's context. Every tool call is checked against the capabilities of the agent that issued it. Permissions assigned to one sub-agent are therefore not automatically available to another. An injection may cause an agent to request an action, but the request is blocked unless that agent already has the required capability. We implement CapScope on the Pi coding agent and evaluate it in a repair workflow where an orchestrator delegates subtasks to separate sub-agents. The evaluation covers five Python tasks, five injection surfaces, four authorization conditions, and three trials per cell (300 runs). The injected effect executes in 33-47/75 runs under the ambient-authority and global-policy baselines, compared with 3/75 under CapScope. CapScope completes 68/75 repairs, while the baselines complete 68-72/75.

</details>


### [72] [PLC-Bin2Src: Retrieving Corresponding Structured Text Source Files for PLC Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08563)
*Ang Jia,Yaxin Duan,He Jiang,Ming Fan,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PLC-Bin2Src是一个跨平台二进制到源码匹配框架，用于从PLC二进制文件中检索对应的结构化文本（ST）源文件。该框架在四种PLC平台（CODESYS、GEB、OpenPLC v2和OpenPLC v3）上实现了95.89%的Recall@1、99.66%的Recall@5和0.9769的MRR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件复用使二进制-only组件可能掩盖其来源与实现，软件组成分析需要识别复用的组件并追溯来源。对于PLC应用，从PLC二进制文件中检索对应ST源文件（binary2source匹配）是核心环节，但该任务面临跨平台编译异构性、PLC二进制与ST源代码之间的表示鸿沟、以及恢复的二进制单元与ST源文件之间的粒度不匹配三大挑战。

Method: PLC-Bin2Src采用平台感知的前端为不同PLC平台构建可比较的表示，并共享后端同等组合三类相似度信号——控制数据流图（CDFG）、函数调用图（FCG）和恢复符号（recovered-symbol）相似度——对源候选进行排序。

Result: 在PLC-BEAD基准上，PLC-Bin2Src在四种PLC平台上实现了95.89%的Recall@1、99.66%的Recall@5以及0.9769的MRR。

Conclusion: PLC-Bin2Src通过平台感知前端与共享后端的架构设计，有效解决了跨平台PLC二进制到源文件匹配中的表示鸿沟与粒度不匹配问题，显著提升了匹配精度。

Abstract: Software reuse allows existing components and third-party libraries to be incorporated into new applications, but binary-only components can obscure their origins and implementations. Software composition analysis seeks to identify these reused components and trace their provenance, supporting dependency inventory, vulnerability assessment, and security auditing. For PLC applications, binary2source matching provides a core link in this analysis: given an opaque PLC binary artifact, retrieve its corresponding Structured Text (ST) source file from a collected source repository. However, this task is complicated by cross-platform compilation heterogeneity, the representation gap between PLC binaries and ST source code, and the granularity mismatch between recovered binary units and ST source files. This paper presents PLC-Bin2Src, a cross-platform binary2source matching framework for retrieving corresponding ST source files for binaries produced by CODESYS, GEB, OpenPLC v2, and OpenPLC v3. Platform-aware frontends construct comparable representations, and a shared backend equally combines control--data flow graph (CDFG), function call graph (FCG), and recovered-symbol similarities to rank source candidates. We evaluate PLC-Bin2Src on PLC-BEAD. Results show that PLC-Bin2Src achieves 95.89% Recall@1, 99.66% Recall@5, and an MRR of 0.9769 across four PLC platforms.

</details>


### [73] [The Unreliable Progress Bar: Can LLM Agents Reliably Report Task Progress Throughout Execution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08589)
*Boyang Wang,Yunhan Wang,Yalun Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了大型语言模型在任务各阶段报告任务进度的可靠性，发现几乎所有被测试的模型在某些阶段可靠而在其他阶段不可靠，并揭示出代际差异：大多数已部署模型在任务进行中段准确性下降、任务完成时恢复，而最新一代模型消除了中段下降但反而在终点线变得保守。作者提出覆盖整个任务执行过程的评估协议，并建议智能体框架不应仅依赖模型的状态报告来控制任务流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可以发出任务进度信号，智能体框架据此决定任务是否继续或停止，但模型是否能在任务每个阶段可靠地报告进度，以及报告在何处、如何失败，尚未被系统研究过。

Method: 在公开基准τ²-bench和受控测试平台StageIF上进行实验评估。StageIF在任务生命周期中跨阶段放置报告检查点，两个设置都要求模型在多个任务阶段进行报告。

Result: 报告可靠性取决于任务已到达的阶段。几乎所有被测试的已部署模型在某些阶段可靠、在其他阶段不可靠。报告失效的位置不尽相同：大多数已部署模型在任务工作开始后失去准确性、完成任务后恢复；最新一代模型消除了中段下降，但反而在终点变得保守。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了任务进度报告中的能力缺口，提供了覆盖整个任务执行过程的评估协议。研究发现表明，智能体框架不应仅凭模型的状态报告来控制任务流转。

Abstract: Recent large language models can emit task-progress signals that agent frameworks use to decide whether a task should continue or stop, yet whether a model can reliably report its task progress at every stage of a task, and where and how its reports fail, has not been studied systematically. We evaluate this ability on the public benchmark $τ^2$-bench and on StageIF, a controlled testbed in which reporting checkpoints are placed across the task's lifecycle. Both settings require reports at multiple task stages. We find that reporting reliability depends on the stage a task has reached, and that almost every deployed model we test is reliable at some stages and unreliable at others. Where reporting breaks down is not the same everywhere. Most deployed models lose accuracy once work is under way and recover once the task is done. The newest generation closes that mid-task drop and instead grows conservative at the finish line. Our study exposes a capability gap in task-progress reporting and provides an evaluation protocol that spans the whole course of task execution for this ability on which agent operation depends. The findings indicate that agent frameworks should not control task flow on the strength of the model's state reports alone.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Fixed Fault Models: Comparing LLM-Based and Rule-Based Fault Injection in OpenStack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08681)
*Giuseppe De Rosa,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于规则的故障注入工具ProFIPy与两种代码大语言模型（Qwen2.5-Coder和DeepSeek-Coder）在OpenStack Nova和Cinder服务中的软件故障注入效果。研究发现二者的激活率和可观测故障率相当，但操作特征差异明显：LLM生成更多Nova上的灾难性故障，ProFIPy产生更多静默和多组件效应。LLM故障注入扩展了固定故障模型的行为覆盖范围，但实际采用仍需受控生成、运行时验证、系统级oracle和可复现的实验溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的故障注入工具（如ProFIPy）虽然能提供可控且可复现的源码级突变更，但需要人工手动编码故障模式，缺乏数据驱动的情境感知能力。作者希望探索LLM作为替代方案，评估其能否生成上下文相关的软件故障，并与传统规则方法进行系统性比较。

Method: 在OpenStack的Nova和Cinder两个服务中，对Qwen2.5-Coder和DeepSeek-Coder两种代码LLM与ProFIPy规则注入器进行对比实验。比较维度包括：共享注入目标上的激活率、可观测故障率，以及故障的操作特征（灾难性、静默、多组件影响等），同时分析LLM输出在失效表现和传播范围方面的差异。

Result: 在共享注入目标上，LLM与传统工具的激活率和可观测故障率相当。但操作特征存在差异：LLM生成的故障在Nova上产生更多灾难性后果，而ProFIPy产生更多静默故障和多组件效应。不同LLM采样输出在失效表现形式上存在差异，但在传播范围上表现出更高的一致性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的故障注入能够扩展固定故障模型的行为覆盖范围，但并未表现出普遍优越性。实际采用LLM进行故障注入仍需受控生成、运行时验证、系统级oracle以及可复现的实验溯源作为支撑保障。

Abstract: Software Fault Injection (SFI) supports testing of cloud systems by introducing software defects and observing their manifestation. Rule-based injectors such as ProFIPy provide controlled and reproducible source-level mutations but require fault patterns to be encoded manually. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a data-driven alternative by generating context-dependent software faults. We compare two code LLMs, Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, with ProFIPy in OpenStack's Nova and Cinder services. On shared injection targets, activation and observable-failure rates are comparable, but operational profiles differ: LLM-generated faults produce more Catastrophic outcomes on Nova, whereas ProFIPy produces more Silent and Multi-component effects. The sampled LLM outputs also differ in how they manifest failure, while showing greater agreement in their propagation scope. These findings show that LLM-based fault injection extends the behavioral coverage of fixed fault models without establishing general superiority, and that practical adoption still requires controlled generation, runtime validation, system-level oracles, and reproducible experimental provenance.

</details>


### [75] [DJPlus: Generating minimal test suites for strong coverage criteria in graph models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08953)
*Yavuz Köroğlu,Mutlu Beyazıt,Onur Kılınççeker,Serge Demeyer,Franz Wotawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种新型优化驱动方法DJPlus，用于从图模型自动生成精简的测试套件，满足基于图结构的测试需求，在四个真实系统上验证了边对（edge-pair）覆盖准则的可行性，并相比替代方法减少了2到26倍的冗余测试步骤，显著降低测试执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的测试中，图覆盖虽能保证测试套件强度，但会产生冗长的测试用例，导致系统测试执行时间过长。现有方法在保证覆盖强度的同时无法有效精简测试套件，且更强的覆盖准则（如质路径准则）存在扩展性问题。

Method: DJPlus是一种优化驱动的自动化测试套件生成方法。它将测试套件的精简构建为一个优化问题，在满足给定的基于图的测试需求（如边对准则）的前提下，生成缩减后的测试套件。作者在四个真实系统上实现了DJPlus，并验证了边对这一比顶点/边覆盖更强的准则的可行性。

Result: 实验表明，在四个真实系统上，边对覆盖准则切实可行，而质路径准则存在可扩展性问题。替代方法生成的冗余测试步骤比DJPlus多2到26倍，且DJPlus通过减少测试步骤数量有效降低了测试执行时间。

Conclusion: DJPlus是迈向解决工业规模下基于模型测试挑战的积极一步，能够在保证测试覆盖强度的同时显著精简测试套件、减少测试执行开销。

Abstract: Automated test generation from graph models is essential to model-based testing. In this type of testing, graph coverage ensures test suite strength but also results in long test cases that take time to execute on the system under test. We propose a novel optimization-driven method, DJPlus, which generates reduced test suites while satisfying given graph-based test requirements. We implement DJPlus and show the feasibility of edge-pair criterion, a stronger coverage criterion than vertex or edge criteria, on four realistic systems, while prime path criterion poses scalability issues. Our evaluation reveals that the alternative methods generate 2 to 26 times more redundant test steps than DJPlus and DJPlus decreases test execution times by reducing the number of test steps. These results show that DJPlus is a positive step towards tackling the challenges of model-based testing at an industrial scale.

</details>


### [76] [It Is Not My Code Anymore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09022)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一个假设性的注册失败案例和选择性文献综述，探讨AI辅助编程中代码生产者、所有权归属与责任分配的问题。文章指出，识别缺陷表达式的生产者本身不能确定审查者、发布决策者或服务运营者的职责；集体所有权同样无法明确这些义务。文章进而讨论质量工程如何评估生成实现及其生产过程，强调验收标准应基于服务结果的必要性，并探讨了用系统直接执行任务（而非生成独立程序）的替代路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程引发了关于代码谁生产、谁拥有、谁在失败时负责的新问题，现有文献对此缺乏清晰的区分框架。作者意图建立生产、所有权与职责之间的概念区分，为质量评估提供理论视角。

Method: 采用理论分析/研究笔记的形式，通过一个假设性的（hypothetical）注册失败场景进行案例推演，并结合对相关文献的选择性阅读（selective reading）进行论证。未报告新的实证结果，主要提出概念区分和评估问题。

Result: 文章没有报告新的实证结果，其"结果"主要体现在概念层面：指出生产者识别并不直接决定各方职责；集体所有权同样需要进一步明确义务规范；提出了以服务结果为导向的验收标准论证框架，以及质量工程可同时评估生成实现与生产过程的观点。

Conclusion: AI辅助软件生产中，归属（authorship）与责任（duty/obligation）是分离的维度。质量评估应以服务结果为导向，验收标准需有充分理由论证。若采用不生成独立程序而直接执行任务的系统替代方案，对象（对象编程产物）会改变但服务义务保持不变。本文旨在提出区分与评估框架，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: AI-assisted programming raises distinct questions about who produces code, who feels ownership of it, and who is responsible when it fails. This research note examines these distinctions through a hypothetical enrollment failure and a selective reading of the literature. Identifying the producer of a defective expression does not, by itself, determine the duties of reviewers, release decision-makers, or service operators. Collective ownership likewise leaves those duties to be specified. The discussion then considers how quality engineering can evaluate both generated implementations and the processes that produce them. Acceptance criteria should be justified by the required service outcome, with component checks contributing evidence toward that outcome. This perspective also permits comparison with systems that perform a task without generating a separate program for it. Such substitution would change the object of authorship while leaving the service obligation intact. The note reports no new empirical results; it proposes distinctions and evaluation questions for AI-assisted software production.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Nonlinear elliptic homogenization with the parametric Deep Ritz method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05778)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用参数化Deep Ritz方法求解非线性椭圆均匀化中的参数化胞元问题，通过神经网络将胞元响应同时离散为空间和宏观状态的函数，获得连续可微的胞元响应表示，相比传统FE²方案显著加速宏尺度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性均匀化问题中，均匀化本构关系依赖于宏观状态及其梯度，需要求解参数化胞元问题。当用均匀化本构关系进行数值求解时，需要胞元问题解的导数形式（用于宏尺度状态牛顿迭代），传统数值方法难以提供连续可微的表示，且FE²方案计算成本高昂。

Method: （1）利用胞元问题的变分结构；（2）使用神经网络将胞元响应对空间坐标和宏观状态的依赖同时离散化；（3）强加边界条件；（4）采用参数化Deep Ritz方法在一系列宏观状态范围内同时求解胞元问题，获得连续可微的响应表示。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法准确、高效，可提供胞元响应随宏观状态和梯度变化的连续可微表示，且相比传统FE²方案，参数化表示显著加速了宏尺度求解过程。

Conclusion: 参数化Deep Ritz方法是非线性均匀化中求解参数化胞元问题的有效工具，其连续可微特性有利地支撑了宏尺度Newton迭代，相比FE²方法在计算效率上具有明显优势。

Abstract: Elliptic homogenization is used to determine coarse-grained properties of materials with features on small scales. When these small scale features have rapid, periodic fluctuations, the solution field corresponding to a homogenized constitutive relation closely resembles the true solution based on the heterogeneous material. This homogenized behavior of the material is computed from a cell problem, where a cell is defined to be one period of the fluctuating material. In the context of linear elliptic partial differential equations, the homogenized constitutive relation is defined simply by a constant coefficient tensor, but for nonlinear problems, the homogenized response depends on the macroscopic state and/or its gradient, thus requiring solutions to parametric cell problems. When computing a numerical solution with the homogenized constitutive relation, it is useful to have a differentiable representation of the solution to the cell problem, as derivatives of the homogenized constitutive relation are required in Newton iterations for the macroscopic state field. In this work, we use the Deep Ritz method to solve the parametric cell problems that arise from nonlinear homogenization. First, we exploit the variational structure of the cell problem, then we discretize the dependence of the cell response on both space and the macroscopic state with a neural network. Enforcing boundary conditions on the cell response strongly, we next use the parametric Deep Ritz method to simultaneously solve the cell problem over a range of macroscopic states. We show that this method is accurate, efficient, and offers a continuous and differentiable representation of the cell response over the macroscopic state and gradient. We then show that our parametric representation of the cell response significantly expedites macroscale solutions when compared to a traditional $\text{FE}^2$ scheme.

</details>


### [78] [AhaBench: Do Agents Learn from Prior Experience? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05435)
*Zerui Cheng,Jiawei Xu,Huacan Chai,Jiayang Sun,Pramod Viswanath,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AhaBench是一个评估语言代理在长时程任务中"经验学习能力"的基准套件，包含三个组件（Aha-Puzzle、Aha-Euler、Aha-Vending），通过三部分评分卡（初始得分、经验后得分、学习提升）揭示"善用支持、达到高分、学习提升最快"的模型未必一致。Claude Opus 4.6在该面板上综合表现领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言代理评测通常在单个提示后重置代理或仅评分单条轨迹的最终状态，无法回答一个关键的操作性问题：当一个固定模型获得有用经验后，在去除了显式支持的相关评测条件下，其后续行为是否真正改善？现有评测框架缺乏对经验迁移与长时程学习的系统度量。

Method: AhaBench包含三个组件：(1) Aha-Puzzle——测试模型在解决隐藏状态谜题后，在无提示条件下能否自主探索；(2) Aha-Euler——将Project-Euler风格数学题生成教学/留出任务对，配有精确验证器；(3) Aha-Vending——开源模拟自动售货代理，测试在延迟反馈和运营事故下的盈利能力。评分采用三部分卡：初始得分（初始能力）、经验后得分（后期经验结果）、学习提升（二者之差），并在八模型面板上比较。

Result: 八模型面板中，Claude Opus 4.6在经验后综合得分（64.3）和学习提升（+25.8）上均领先，Gemini 3.1 Pro紧随其后（63.4）。组件级结果呈明显分化：谜题轨迹能提升有提示得分但难以转化为无提示探索行为；Aha-Euler完整教学可达78.6-100.0%但仅答案迁移仅0.0-73.9%；Aha-Vending能区分盈利性事故处理与破产/无订单失败。

Conclusion: AhaBench的核心实证发现是：善用可见支持、达到高经验后得分、以及运行中学习提升最快的模型三者并不重合，因此单一指标会误导对代理能力的判断。该基准以开源形式发布任务、评分标准、验证器、模拟器代码和评估接口，支持新代理的可复现评估。

Abstract: Modern language agents are expected to operate over long horizons: they ask follow-up questions, reuse worked examples, handle tool feedback, and adapt to delayed consequences. Most evaluations still reset the agent after a prompt or score only the final state of one trajectory. AhaBench asks a more operational question: when a fixed model receives useful experience, does its later behavior improve under a related evaluation condition where the obvious support has been removed, changed, or delayed? The suite contains three components. Aha-Puzzle tests no-hint exploration after solved hidden-state puzzles; Aha-Euler turns Project-Euler-style mathematical ideas into generated taught/held-out tasks with exact validators; and Aha-Vending, an open-source implementation inspired by Vending-Bench, tests whether a simulated vending agent remains profitable while handling delayed feedback and operational incidents. AhaBench reports a three-part scorecard: Initial Score measures starting competence, Post-Experience Score measures the later empirical outcome, and Learning Lift is their difference. This decomposition is the main empirical message: models that use visible support well, models that reach high post-experience scores, and models that improve most during a run are not always the same. On the common eight-model panel, Claude Opus 4.6 leads aggregate Post-Experience Score at 64.3 and aggregate Learning Lift at +25.8, with Gemini 3.1 Pro close behind at 63.4. The component results explain the split: puzzle traces raise supported scores but often fail to become no-hint exploration behavior; Aha-Euler full teaching reaches 78.6-100.0% while answer-only transfer ranges from 0.0 to 73.9%; and Aha-Vending separates profitable incident handling from bankruptcy and no-order failure. We release benchmark tasks, rubrics, validators, simulator code, and interfaces for evaluating new agents.

</details>


### [79] [When Do Options Help? Policy Necrosis and Redundant Coverage in Option-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05508)
*Bingyun Liu,Yuheng Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论和实验揭示了option-critic算法性能随option数量增加而提升的真实原因：并非新增option本身带来收益，而是降低了所有option在同一状态同时失败的概率（从59%降到4%），同时指出终止规则实际无贡献、option内部策略存在"策略坏死"（policy necrosis）现象导致状态锁死在最早期瞻望良好的动作上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: option-critic的核心结论声称性能会随option数量的增加而提升，但该论点缺乏严谨解释。作者旨在通过理论分析与实验揭示这一表象背后的真实机制，从而更准确地理解该算法的行为。

Method: 作者采用理论推导与实验相结合的方法：(1)证明当终止测试与选择option的策略读取相同值时，终止规则每步都会触发，等价于始终终止，即学到的终止规则无贡献；(2)当策略探索而终止测试不探索时，规则会阻碍探索，给出存在O(T)遗憾的反例，而始终终止只有O(log T)遗憾；(3)观察并命名"策略坏死"现象——option内部策略几乎不探索，状态会锁定在首个表现看似良好的动作上不再更新；(4)提出状态层面的坏死检测测试，发现典型option中五分之三的状态坏死；(5)通过实验验证恢复探索可修复坏死状态，单个option即可解决任务，且性能提升源于联合失败概率的下降。

Result: 实验与理论表明：强制每步终止后option数量-性能曲线保持不变；典型option中五分之三的状态存在策略坏死，恢复探索后一个option即可完成任务；option数量增加带来的性能提升仅源于所有option在同一状态同时失败的概率从59%降至4%。

Conclusion: option-critic性能随option数量增长的原因被误读：并非更多option直接带来更好策略，而是多样性降低了系统性联合失败风险。同时，option-critic的终止机制本质上无额外价值，且内部策略的探索不足（策略坏死）严重制约了单个option的自身能力。

Abstract: Option-critic learns options: sub-policies together with a learned rule for when each one hands control back. Its headline result is that performance improves as options are added. We explain that result, with theory and experiment. First, the termination rule option-critic learns by maximising return contributes nothing. When the termination test and the policy that picks options read the same values, the test fires at every step, so the learned rule is identical to always terminating. When that policy explores and the test does not, as in option-critic itself, the rule can block the exploration; there are instances where it suffers $Ω(T)$ regret while always terminating holds to $O(\log T)$. Forcing termination at every step leaves the option-count curve intact. Second, the policy inside an option barely explores at all, so a state locks onto the first action that looked good and never updates again. We name this policy necrosis, give a state-level test for it, and find three fifths of states necrotic in a typical option. Restoring exploration repairs those states, and one option then solves the task. Third, extra options improve no option; what falls is the chance that all of them fail in the same state, from $59\\%$ to $4\\%$, and performance follows that joint quantity.

</details>


### [80] [Constitutive State-Space Modeling of Path-Dependent Plasticity: A Resolution-Consistent and Parallelizable Computational Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07294)
*Rui Barreira,Taylan Soydan,Francesco Scipione,Miguel A. Bessa,Dirk Mohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为本构状态空间（CSS）模型的数据驱动力学本构模型，将结构化状态空间动力学的框架改造为增量式本构算子。通过将应变增量分解为大小和方向，并利用零阶保持离散化嵌入连续时间线性递推，CSS模型在保证零增量平稳性的同时降低了对应变路径离散化的敏感性，并保留了S5的并行扫描结构以实现高效训练。与最小状态单元（MSC）架构的对比表明，CSS在预测精度上相当或更优，验证损失可低一个数量级（对塑性不可压缩材料），且对路径离散化变化具有强鲁棒性，训练速度更快、所需数据更少，学习到的潜状态结构与物理本构模型的维度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动路径相关塑性本构模型通常基于非线性递归神经网络（RNN），存在两个关键缺陷：一是顺序状态演化限制了并行训练的效率；二是预测结果依赖于应变路径的离散化程度，在粗分辨率下误差显著增大。因此，需要一种既适合并行训练又对路径离散化不敏感的本构建模框架。

Method: CSS模型将结构化状态空间动力学重构成增量本构算子。具体做法是：将应变增量分解为大小（magnitude）和方向（direction）两个分量，其中加载方向驱动潜状态空间系统，而增量大小通过零阶保持（zero-order-hold）离散化进入连续时间线性递推。这种力学量身定制的构造确保了零增量条件下的平稳性（对任意持续加载状态输出不变），大幅降低了对应变路径分辨率的敏感性，同时保留了S5并行扫描（parallel-scan）结构以支持长本构历史的高效并行训练。

Result: 在四种多轴路径相关材料模型（各向同性J2塑性、压力敏感泡沫塑性、组合各向同性-运动硬化）上的比较表明：CSS在预测精度上匹配或超过MSC，对塑性不可压缩材料验证损失可低一个数量级；CSS在应变路径离散化大幅变化时保持低误差，而MSC在粗分辨率下误差显著增加；CSS训练速度显著更快，且需要更少的应变-应力数据对即可达到同等或更优精度；对学习到的潜状态分析发现其结构与底层物理本构模型的维度一致。

Conclusion: 力学定制化的结构化状态空间动力学是数据驱动本构建模的有效计算框架，兼具高效训练、离散化鲁棒性和高精度等优点，为替代传统RNN类本构模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive models for path-dependent plasticity are commonly formulated using nonlinear recurrent neural networks, whose sequential state evolution limits parallel training and whose predictions may depend on the discretization of the applied strain path. We introduce a Constitutive State Space (CSS) model that reformulates structured state-space dynamics as an incremental constitutive operator. The strain increment is decomposed into magnitude and direction: the loading direction drives the latent state-space system, while the increment magnitude enters the zero-order-hold discretization of its continuous-time linear recurrence. This mechanics-tailored construction guarantees stationarity under zero increments, strongly reduces sensitivity to strain-path resolution, and retains the parallel-scan structure of S5 for efficient training on long constitutive histories. The CSS and Minimal State Cell (MSC) architectures are compared for four multiaxial path-dependent material models including isotropic J2 plasticity, pressure-sensitive foam plasticity, and combined isotropic-kinematic hardening. CSS matches or exceeds the prediction accuracy of the MSC, including one order of magnitude lower validation losses for the plastically incompressible materials. Importantly, CSS maintains low errors across large changes in strain-path discretization, whereas the MSC error increases substantially when evaluated at coarser resolutions than used for training. CSS trains substantially faster and requires fewer strain-stress pairs to attain comparable or better accuracy. Analysis of the learned state further reveals latent structure consistent with the dimensionality of the underlying physical constitutive models. These results establish mechanics-tailored structured state-space dynamics as a computational framework for efficient and discretization-robust data-driven constitutive modeling.

</details>


### [81] [Multi-granularity Adaptive Hypergraph Representation Learning via Granular-ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05574)
*Sen Zhao,Yifan Guan,Jinyuan Ni,Gaojie Xu,Zhang Xu,Xiaoyu Lian,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGHRL框架，通过自适应粒度球分割生成多粒度超边，并利用多子网超图网络捕获不同粒度的高阶关系，在基准数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图表示学习方法依赖预定义规则生成超边，忽视了图拓扑结构的多样性以及超边的多粒度特征，导致无法有效且自适应地发现高阶关系并处理复杂结构信息。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation策略，通过粒度球的自适应分割在不同粒度水平生成超边，基于图的拓扑结构有效捕获高阶关系；(2) Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network，包含多个子网络，从不同粒度的超边中提取特征，并通过层级可逆连接进行整合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果表明，MGHRL在性能上显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: MGHRL通过自适应多粒度超边生成与多粒度超图网络设计，有效解决了传统方法在超边生成上的预定义局限性，能够灵活适配复杂图结构并提取多粒度高阶关系。

Abstract: Hypergraph representation learning aims to capture high-order information in graphs by constructing hyperedges that simultaneously connect multiple nodes. These hyperedges adapt to the graph's topological features, facilitating the extraction of high-order relationships at multiple granularities. Most prior work relies on predefined definitions to generate hyperedges, overlooking the diversity in graph topological structures and the multi-granularity characteristics of hyperedges. As a result, this limits their ability to effectively and adaptively discover high-order relationships and efficiently process complex structural information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework called \underline{M}ulti-\underline{G}ranularity \underline{H}ypergraph \underline{R}epresentation \underline{L}earning (MGHRL). MGHRL introduces an Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation strategy, which generates hyperedges at multiple levels of granularity through the adaptive splitting of granular-ball, effectively capturing high-order relationships based on the graph's topological structure. Additionally, we propose a Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network with multiple sub-networks, capturing features from hyperedges at different granularities and integrating them via hierarchical reversible connections. Experimental results show that MGHRL significantly outperforms baseline models on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [82] [Capsule Lens: Locating and Tracking Concept Geometry in Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05575)
*Yiming Tang,Harshvardhan Saini,Samyak Jha,Huaming Chen,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Capsule Lens框架，通过可闭合形式拟合的胶囊几何形状来定位、分析和追踪机器学习模型内部表征中概念所占据的空间区域，适用于静态和动态表征两种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解概念如何编码于模型内部表示是机制可解释性的核心问题，但现有方法主要将表征映射到更可解释的空间，无法直接表征概念在表示空间中如何占据区域；已有假设缺乏严格验证且大多关注静态表征。

Method: 引入Capsule Lens框架，将概念占据的区域匹配为简洁可追踪的几何形式——胶囊（capsule），由若干可解释参数定义，以闭合形式对每个概念的几何结构进行拟合，并在保留样本上验证。框架应用于静态与动态两类表征场景。

Result: 在静态表征上，展示如何定位多种模型中的概念几何结构，并利用span和norm曲线揭示重要几何特征；在动态表征上，通过三个案例（CLIP预训练、VQA上的RL后训练、数学推理上的RL后训练）追踪表征漂移，发现不同训练设置导致定性的几何动态差异——从CLIP预训练中的全网广泛重构到RL后训练中的局部化且概念特异的变化。结果既有与现有文献一致的发现，也有新观察。

Conclusion: Capsule Lens是定位、分析和追踪静态与动态表征中概念几何结构的有力工具。

Abstract: Understanding how concepts are encoded in the internal representations of machine learning models is a central problem in mechanistic interpretability, essential both for the science of deep learning and for the trustworthy deployment of increasingly capable models. Existing approaches to interpret model representations mainly map representations onto more interpretable spaces and do not directly characterize how concepts occupy representation space; various hypotheses have been proposed, but often lack of rigorous validation and largely focus on static representations. In this work, we introduce Capsule Lens, a framework that matches the region a concept occupies with a simple, trackable geometric form, a capsule, defined by several interpretable parameters, fitted in closed form to each concept's geometry and validated on held-out samples. We apply Capsule Lens in two major settings: static and dynamic representations. On static representations, we demonstrate how to locate concept geometry across various models, and how the span and norm curves uncover important geometric characteristics. On dynamic representations, we present three case studies tracking representation drifts induced by distinct training settings, CLIP pretraining, RL post-training on visual question answering, and RL post-training on mathematical reasoning. These analyses reveal qualitatively different geometric dynamics, ranging from broad network-wide restructuring in CLIP pretraining to localized and concept-specific changes in RL post-training. Our results include findings aligned with existing literature as well as novel observations. We believe Capsule Lens stands as a promising tool for locating, analyzing, and tracking concept geometry in both static and dynamic representations.

</details>


### [83] [HB-PVI: A Hierarchical Bayesian Personalization and Value-of-Information Framework for Complex Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05582)
*Hammed A. Olayinka*

Main category: cs.LG
