Conclusion: 本文揭示了行为强化学习的稳定性图景与行为预测存在根本差异：在行为RL中，随机化（策略混合）并不能完全消除对环境映射的假设需求。为了实现全局稳定性，仍需要某种形式的假设（如引入的有界转移范围，它严格弱于Lipschitz灵敏度），并且全局稳定的收敛下限在轨迹反馈下是不可避免的。然而，局部混合稳定性（等价于平稳性条件）可以在完全不假设环境映射性质的情况下通过加权Hedge动态高效实现，这为实际算法提供了保障。

Abstract: In performative reinforcement learning the deployed policy shapes the environment that generates the learner's future data, and the natural solution concept is a performatively stable policy that is optimal in the environment it induces. Existing convergence guarantees rely on Lipschitz sensitivity assumptions on the environment map $π\mapsto (P_π, r_π)$, which are hard to verify and fail in settings such as multi-agent best-response dynamics. We instead study stability for mixtures of policies, and show that the resulting picture is fundamentally different from performative prediction, where randomization removes the need for any sensitivity assumption. We distinguish local mixed stability, an occupancy-weighted first-order relaxation that we show is equivalent to stationarity, from global mixed stability, which certifies against arbitrary deviating policies. Our first result is that a weighted per-state Hedge dynamic drives the local stability gap to zero at an $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for an arbitrary, possibly discontinuous, environment map, both with exact and with trajectory feedback. The two notions genuinely differ: we exhibit an instance where local stability is achieved exactly but every mixture has global stability gap bounded away from zero. For global stability we introduce a bounded transition range assumption, strictly weaker than Lipschitz sensitivity, under which unweighted per-state Hedge converges up to a floor of $O(γε_P/(1-γ)^3)$, and we prove a matching-in-$ε_P$ lower bound of $Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))$ under trajectory feedback, so this floor is unavoidable. Finally, we extend both notions to $n$-player performative Markov games, obtaining local stability with no assumption on the joint environment map or game structure, and global stability for performative Markov potential games.

</details>


### [150] [Sector-Mean: Deterministic Initialization of K-Means Centroids via Angular Sector Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06468)
*Abhiyan Dhakal,Pranish Kafle,Rajani Chulyadyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Sector-Mean的确定性聚类初始化策略，通过围绕全局质心将二维数据空间划分为角度扇区并利用扇区均值初始化质心，时间复杂度为O(N)。与K-Means++和Max-Min初始化相比，在保持相同聚类质量的前提下，初始化时间分别降低74.9%和59.8%，平均迭代次数减少约5%和16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-Means作为最广泛使用的聚类算法之一，其对初始质心选择的敏感性是影响收敛速度和聚类准确性的主要瓶颈。现有初始化方法（如随机初始化、K-Means++、Max-Min）存在随机性带来的不确定性或较高的计算开销，因此需要一种确定性且计算高效的初始化方案。

Method: 提出了Sector-Mean初始化方法：围绕二维数据集的全局质心，将数据空间按角度划分为若干扇形区域（扇区），每个扇区内数据的均值作为初始质心的候选；该算法是确定性的，且时间复杂度为O(N)。实验设计中，在相同Lloyd迭代条件下，与随机、K-Means++、Max-Min初始化进行对比评估，使用Friedman检验（p<0.05）和Nemenyi事后检验进行统计分析。

Result: 在SIPU和Birch二维基准数据集及多个真实数据集上的实验表明：Sector-Mean聚类质量与K-Means++和Max-Min相当；初始化时间相比K-Means++降低74.9%，相比Max-Min降低59.8%；平均迭代次数最低，比K-Means++少约5%，比Max-Min少约16%。

Conclusion: Sector-Mean初始化提供了一种确定性、计算高效的K-Means初始化策略，能在保持聚类质量的同时显著降低初始化和迭代成本，是传统初始化方法的有效替代方案。

Abstract: K-Means is one of the most widely used clustering algorithms, but its susceptibility to initial centroid selection remains a primary bottleneck for its convergence speed and clustering accuracy. This paper proposes Sector-Mean Initialization, a deterministic initialization strategy with O(N) time complexity that partitions the two-dimensional data space into angular sectors around the global centroid and initializes centroids using sector-wise means. We evaluate the method on established two-dimensional benchmarks (SIPU, Birch) and multiple real-world datasets, comparing against random, K-Means++, and Max-Min initialization under identical Lloyd iterations. The statistical analysis of Friedman's test (p<0.05) and Nemenyi post-hoc comparison indicates that, while delivering equivalent clustering quality as K-Means++ and Max-Min, Sector-Mean offers significant computational efficiency. Experimental results show that Sector-Mean reduces the initialization time by 74.9% and 59.8% in comparison to K-Means++ and max-min, respectively. And, it yields the lowest average number of iterations, achieving approximately 5% fewer iterations than K-Means++ and 16% fewer than max-min. These results highlight that Sector-Mean initialization offers a deterministic and computationally efficient initialization strategy while preserving cluster quality.

</details>


### [151] [One Step, One Lead: Mitigating Higher-Order Interference in Multi-Domain Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Step Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06469)
*Zihan Lin,Xiaohan Wang,Jie Cao,Jiajun Chai,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSOL方法，指出跨域RL训练中一种被忽视的顺序干扰（sequential interference）：同一点域梯度可能近似正交，但连续的实际更新在输出空间部分反向。利用相邻检查点间token对数概率足迹作为局部二阶交互的替代信号，通过漂移排序和自适应缩放的修正机制插入标准GRPO更新中，抑制跨步输出回溯，提升多域强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域强化学习虽能拓宽LLM推理能力，但联合训练常导致单域性能退化与优化不稳定。现有研究单一从一阶梯度对齐或曲率近似诊断干扰，可能遗漏关键的顺序干扰形式：同点域梯度近乎正交时，连续实际更新仍可能在输出空间部分相互抵消。

Method: 提出OSOL框架：(1) 每个迭代指定一个焦点域；(2) 使用前一个检查点的token对数概率足迹（consecutive token log-probability footprint）直接在输出空间捕捉局部二阶交互，无需重建同阶曲率；(3) 依据足迹对token级反弹风险排序；(4) 在标准GRPO更新中应用漂移排序的、自适应缩放的修正项以抑制跨步输出回溯。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，OSOL达到域宏平均0.4822，较最强基线提升5.7%。受控实验表明跨步回溯比同点梯度诊断与后续任务损伤关联更强，且前述足迹比Hessian代理更准确预测未来反弹风险。

Conclusion: 跨步输出空间中的顺序干扰是影响多域RL训练的关键因素，它无法由单步梯度诊断捕获，但可从相邻检查点的对数概率足迹中有效恢复。OSOL通过轻量级修正实现在不进行显式高阶微分的前提下缓解该干扰，提升了多域RL的整体性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) across multiple domains can broaden the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet joint training often degrades individual-domain performance and can destabilize optimization. Existing work typically diagnoses such interference from a single-step view using first-order gradient alignment or curvature-based proxies. We show that this view can miss a critical form of sequential interference: same-point domain gradients may remain nearly orthogonal even when consecutive realized updates partially reverse one another in output space. We further show that consecutive token log-probability footprints recover this interaction directly from adjacent checkpoints as a local second-order interaction in output space, without explicitly reconstructing same-step curvature. Building on this insight, we propose OSOL, which designates a focus domain at each iteration, uses the preceding checkpoint footprint to rank token-level rebound risk, and applies a drift-ranked, adaptively scaled correction within the standard GRPO update. Our analysis shows that this correction suppresses the targeted cross-step output backtracking component. Controlled studies further show that cross-step backtracking is more strongly associated with subsequent task damage than same-point gradient diagnostics, while the preceding footprint ranks future rebound risk more accurately than Hessian-based proxies. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, OSOL reaches a domain-macro average of 0.4822, improving by 5.7% over the strongest compared baseline, without explicit higher-order differentiation.

</details>


### [152] [Steering Under Compression: Dose-Response, Capability Cost, and Failure Asymmetry in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06473)
*Saurav Bhandari,Benjamin Wade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了推理时激活导向（activation steering）与权重量化（INT8/NF4）之间的交互作用。通过在四个7-9B开源模型上对两个行为目标（情感导向和推理长度）进行等效果框架分析，发现情感导向在量化下完好保留，而推理长度导向表现出不对称的剂量-响应关系（延长是渐进的但终止于失控崩溃，缩短是阶梯式且快速失败）。此外还揭示了一个方法论缺陷（naive等效果阶梯在底有界目标上锚定于崩溃底）并提出了审查修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向（不修改参数的推理时行为控制）和训练后量化（降低部署内存与计算成本）在实践中越来越频繁地被同时使用，但二者之间的交互作用此前从未被系统表征过。理解这种交互对于可靠地将导向技术部署到量化的边缘/资源受限环境中至关重要。

Method: 采用等效果（iso-effect）框架，在可控行为效果下比较能力代价。对四个7-9B开源模型施加INT8和NF4权重量化，针对两个行为目标（情感判断和免裁判推理长度）进行激活导向实验。使用预订义的三标签规则进行描述性等效判定，并引入审查式（censored）等效果阶梯构建来修复底有界目标的锚定问题。

Result: 情感导向在量化下完好保留（INT8池化对比-0.010，描述性等价；NF4为-0.017，不确定）。推理长度呈现不对称剂量-响应：延长为渐进式但会终止于失控运行和崩溃，缩短为阶梯函数且仅12-30%（因模型而异）缩短即发生不连续失败。导向向量与FP16版本的余弦相似度极高（INT8: 0.989-0.998, NF4: 0.945-0.990）。发现Mistral-NF4存在显著基线能力下降（GSM8K从0.545降至0.365），说明压缩可能主导导向干预效果。

Conclusion: 行为方向在量化后得以保留（即使代价结构改变），但推理长度等floor-bounded目标需要审查式等效果分析才能得到可解释的交叉结果。压缩引起的基线能力偏移可能超过导向干预本身的效果，必须在评估中予以考虑。所有代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Inference-time activation steering enables behavioral control of large language models without parameter modification, while post-training quantization reduces memory and compute costs for deployment. Despite their growing convergence in practice, the interaction between these two techniques remains uncharacterized. We systematically study activation steering under weight-only quantization (INT8 and NF4) across four open-weight 7-9B models and two behavioral targets: judged sentiment and judge-free reasoning length. Using an iso-effect framework that compares capability costs at matched behavioral effect, we find that sentiment steering survives quantization intact. After correcting a GSM8K parser artifact with a uniform v2.3.1 rescore, the pooled INT8 contrast is -0.010 (90% CI [-0.026, +0.007]), descriptively Equivalent under the preregistered three-label rule, while NF4 remains Inconclusive at -0.017 ([-0.067, +0.033]). In contrast, reasoning length exhibits a surprising asymmetric dose-response: lengthening is graded but terminates in cap-runaway and collapse, while shortening is a step function with only 12-30% shortening (model-dependent) before discontinuous failure. We expose a methodological pitfall: the naive iso-effect ladder anchors on the collapse floor for floor-bounded targets, and we introduce a censored construction that restores interpretable crossings. We also quantify a substantial baseline capability shift for Mistral-NF4 (0.545 to 0.365 GSM8K at alpha=0), demonstrating that compression can dominate the steering intervention. Despite this, steering vectors remain highly collinear with their FP16 siblings (cosine similarity 0.989-0.998 for INT8, 0.945-0.990 for NF4), confirming that the behavioral direction survives quantization even when the cost structure does not. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Kernels by Alignment for Multiclass Bayes Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06474)
*Hollan Haule,Alfredo Gonzalez-Sulser,Javier Escudero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从CLaI框架出发，论证了协作学习可以视为核对齐过程，协作推断等价于带Parzen窗密度估计的核贝叶斯分类。基于此理论视角，作者将余弦相似度替换为学习得到的马氏距离，并扩展到多分类场景，在CIFAR-10、PathMNIST和SleepEDF数据集上取得了更好的精度、更快的收敛和更低的校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法需要预先选择核函数，而核函数的质量直接决定模型性能。论文旨在通过核对齐机制学习得到数据相关的核，从而统一表示学习、核对齐与贝叶斯分类，构建一个原则上更加完备的学习框架。

Method: 论文首先从理论上论证CLaI的协作学习本质上是核对齐过程——嵌入训练使其诱导的相似度对齐标签衍生的目标核；协作推断等价于Parzen窗密度估计下的核贝叶斯分类。基于这些理论洞见，将CLaI中的余弦相似度替换为学习得到的马氏距离，并将框架扩展至多分类任务。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、PathMNIST和SleepEDF上，马氏距离版本相较余弦相似度版本在精度、收敛速度和校准误差方面均有提升。辅助实验表明CLaI产生的潜在信号在形式上与高斯过程一致，且在前列腺脓毒症预测上取得了具有竞争力的校准性能。

Conclusion: 本文确立了学习核框架的理论基础，将表示学习、核对齐和贝叶斯分类统一于同一框架之中，并能自然扩展到多分类场景，为核方法中的核选择难题提供了一种基于数据的学习范式。

Abstract: Kernel methods separate data representation from decision-making, but typically require the kernel to be chosen in advance. We show that this kernel can instead be learned by alignment, and develop the resulting framework through the recently introduced Collaborative Learning and Inference (CLaI). We show that Collaborative Learning can be viewed as a kernel alignment process, in which an embedding is trained so that its induced similarity matches a label-derived target kernel. We also prove that Collaborative Inference is equivalent to kernel Bayes classification with Parzen-window density estimation. Motivated by these perspectives, we generalise CLaI by replacing cosine similarity with a learned Mahalanobis distance and extend it to multiclass classification. On CIFAR-10, PathMNIST, and SleepEDF, the Mahalanobis formulation improves accuracy, converges faster, and yields lower calibration error than the cosine-based variant. Auxiliary experiments further support these connections, showing that CLaI produces latent signals of the same form as a Gaussian process, while achieving competitive calibration on sepsis prediction. Together, these results establish a principled learned-kernel framework that unifies representation learning, kernel alignment, and Bayesian classification, and extends naturally to the multiclass setting.

</details>


### [154] [How Does Parameter Pruning Reshape DNN Representations? An Interaction-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06483)
*Fangbo Li,Junpeng Zhang,Qihan Ren,Quanshi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从交互模式的角度解释DNN参数剪枝后性能退化差异的内在原因，发现剪枝过程中交互模式呈现三阶段动态，低阶交互具有强泛化性，性能对某些模块剪枝的高敏感性归因于是否移除了可泛化的低阶交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何剪枝DNN中不同参数会导致不同的性能退化程度，从而揭示控制性能退化的内在因素。

Method: 通过逐步增加剪枝比例，观察DNN编码的交互模式如何随剪枝操作变化，分析不同阶数交互模式的动态特性及其与模型性能之间的关联。

Result: 发现DNN交互模式随剪枝比例增加呈现三阶段动态特征；在低阶交互被移除之前，模型性能基本不受影响；低阶交互表现出强泛化性；性能对特定模块剪枝的高敏感性取决于该剪枝是否移除可泛化的低阶交互模式。

Conclusion: 剪枝导致的性能退化主要由可泛化的低阶交互模式被移除所驱动，这为理解剪枝性能差异提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: This study focuses on the scientific problem of understanding internal factors that govern the diverse performance degradation of deep neural networks (DNNs) when different parameters are pruned. In order to explain why pruning certain parameters leads to significant performance degradation but pruning other parameters does not, we examine how the pruning operation affects the interaction patterns encoded by the DNN. We find that when we progressively increase the pruning ratio, the interaction patterns encoded by DNNs exhibit a distinct three-phase dynamics, \emph{i.e.}, model performance is not largely affected until the pruning operation begins to remove low-order interactions, and low-order interactions exhibit strong generalizability. Moreover, we find that the high sensitivity of DNN performance to the pruning of certain modules is attributed to whether the pruning operation removes generalizable low-order interaction patterns.

</details>


### [155] [Second-Order Smooth Planning with Optimal-Transport Bellman Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06484)
*Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SecondOrderSmoothCruiser算法，通过最优传输平滑Bellman backup获得二阶修正，将规划器的oracle复杂度从SmoothCruiser的一阶情形$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4})$提升到$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$，并建立了正则化偏差界的显式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SmoothCruiser通过熵正则化Bellman backup的平滑性实现了问题无关的$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4})$复杂度，但其估计器仅为一阶（局部泰勒余项阶数β=2），限制了样本效率的进一步提升。作者希望利用更高阶的局部信息（如二阶/三次余项）来降低oracle复杂度。

Method: 作者首先理论上证明了SmoothCruiser型规划器的样本复杂度指数由局部泰勒余项的阶数β决定，给出通用复杂度公式$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-(2+2/(β-1))})$。随后设计了基于最优传输(OT)平滑的Bellman backup，该backup具有闭式解、策略梯度和Lipschitz Hessian，其二次修正项可被无偏的叉积估计器（cross-product estimator）估计。基于此构建SecondOrderSmoothCruiser算法，达到β=3的三次余项情形。

Result: SecondOrderSmoothCruiser在固定OT参数下达到$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的oracle复杂度，相比SmoothCruiser的$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4})$有显著改进。论文还通过显式的正则化偏差界建立了OT正则化、熵正则化与无正则化目标之间的关系。

Conclusion: 通过利用OT平滑Bellman backup的高阶光滑性（Lipschitz Hessian），可以实现比一阶SmoothCruiser更优的规划oracle复杂度，为基于生成模型的规划提供了新的理论保证与算法范式。

Abstract: Planning with a generative model aims to estimate the value of a state using as few simulator calls as possible. SmoothCruiser achieves problem-independent complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ by exploiting the smoothness of the entropy-regularized Bellman backup, but its estimator is only first-order. We show that the sample-complexity exponent of SmoothCruiser-type planners is governed by the order $β$ of the local Taylor remainder, giving oracle complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-(2+2/(β-1))})$: the first-order case $β=2$ recovers SmoothCruiser, while a second-order/cubic remainder $β=3$ yields $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$. We reach this regime with an optimal-transport-smoothed Bellman backup over action distributions, which has a closed form, a policy gradient, and a Lipschitz Hessian, and whose quadratic correction admits an unbiased cross-product estimator. The resulting SecondOrderSmoothCruiser achieves $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ oracle complexity for fixed OT parameters, and we relate the OT, entropy-regularized, and unregularized objectives through explicit regularization-bias bounds.

</details>


### [156] [Power Mean Estimation in Stochastic Continuous Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06489)
*Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\Algname的新型蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）算法，专用于连续状态-动作空间的随机马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。该算法结合幂均值（power mean）值备份算子与多项式探索奖励，在随机环境下实现了多项式收敛速率O(n^{-ζ})，ζ∈(0,1/2)，将POLY-HOOT的非渐近收敛保证从确定性MDP扩展到随机环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS在确定性环境的在线规划中已取得成功，但将其推广到连续状态-动作空间的随机MDP仍面临重大挑战。现有方法如HOOT虽能处理连续空间，但其对数探索奖励缺乏单调随机（非平稳）场景下的理论保证；POLY-HOOT虽引入了多项式奖励在确定性MDP中实现收敛，但其理论尚未拓展到随机MDP。因此需要一种在连续随机MDP中具有收敛保证的MCTS算法。

Method: \Algname算法在三个关键方面做出设计：(1) 采用幂均值作为值备份算子，以更好地处理随机环境下值估计的分布特性；(2) 引入多项式探索奖励（区别于HOOT的对数奖励），用于应对连续动作空间固有的非平稳性；(3) 在理论方面，建立了非渐近收敛分析，证明算法以O(n^{-ζ})（ζ∈(0,1/2)）的多项式速率收敛，n为已访问轨迹数。

Result: 理论分析证明\Algname在随机MDP下实现O(n^{-ζ})的多项式收敛速率（ζ∈(0,1/2)），将POLY-HOOT的非渐近收敛性从确定性环境成功拓展至随机环境。随机任务上的实验验证了理论发现，表明该算法在连续随机域中的有效性。

Conclusion: \Algname通过幂均值备份算子和多项式探索奖励的结合，弥补了现有MCTS方法在连续随机MDP中缺乏收敛保证的空白，实现了多项式的非渐近收敛速率，并通过实验证实了其实际效能，为MCTS在随机连续域中的应用提供了理论基础与实践验证。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) has demonstrated success in online planning for deterministic environments, yet significant challenges remain in adapting it to stochastic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), particularly in continuous state-action spaces. Existing methods, such as HOOT, which combines MCTS with the Hierarchical Optimistic Optimization (HOO) bandit strategy, address continuous spaces but rely on a logarithmic exploration bonus that lacks theoretical guarantees in non-stationary, stochastic settings. Recent advancements, such as POLY-HOOT, introduced a polynomial bonus term to achieve convergence in deterministic MDPs, though a similar theory for stochastic MDPs remains undeveloped. In this paper, we propose a novel MCTS algorithm, \Algname, designed for continuous, stochastic MDPs. \Algname integrates a power mean as a value backup operator, alongside a polynomial exploration bonus to address the non-stationarity inherent in continuous action spaces. Our theoretical analysis establishes that \Algname converges at a polynomial rate of $\mathcal{O}(n^{-ζ})$, $ζ\in (0,1/2)$, where \( n \) is the number of visited trajectories, thereby extending the non-asymptotic convergence guarantees of POLY-HOOT to stochastic environments. Experimental results on stochastic tasks validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating the effectiveness of \Algname in continuous, stochastic domains.

</details>


### [157] [Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation for One-Shot Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06499)
*Shutong Zheng,Lele Fu,Sheng Huang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPIRE方法，利用一阶度分布结构熵作为拓扑描述符实现结构感知的客户端权重分配，并在服务器端通过图扩散模型生成伪图以完成一次性联邦图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性的联邦图学习中，服务器需要从高度压缩的信息估计客户端贡献，但传统的基于数据量的权重分配只捕获了客户端数据量，忽略了图连接的组织方式（拓扑结构）差异。

Method: 1) 使用一阶度分布结构熵作为度质量分散的紧凑描述符，推导结构客户端权重，提供超越数据量的拓扑感知归纳偏置。2) 服务器端图扩散模型以加权客户端原型为条件，合成同时包含语义和结构信息的伪图，无需额外的客户端训练。3) 通过不相交并集融合方式组装生成的伪图，训练全局图神经网络。

Result: 在七个真实世界图数据集上的大量实验表明，SPIRE在传统和一次性FGL方法上持续取得更优性能，特别是在高度异构（非IID）和图扰动场景下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过结构熵驱动的拓扑感知客户端区分，SPIRE有效解决了一次性联邦图学习中数据量权重忽视拓扑组织的问题，实现了在数据异构和图扰动条件下的鲁棒联邦图学习。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning (FGL) requires the server to estimate client contributions from highly compressed information, yet conventional volume-based weighting captures the amount of client data while overlooking how its connectivity is organized. In this paper, we propose SPIRE, a Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation method that introduces topology-aware client differentiation into one-shot FGL. Specifically, we employ first-order degree-distribution structural entropy as a compact descriptor of degree-mass dispersion and use it to derive structural client weights, providing an inductive bias that accounts for differences in graph topology beyond data volume. On the generation side, a graph diffusion model on the server synthesizes pseudographs conditioned on the weighted client prototypes, capturing both semantic and structural information without requiring additional client-side training. The generated pseudographs are then assembled via disjoint union fusion to train a global graph neural network. Extensive experiments on seven real-world graph datasets demonstrate that SPIRE consistently outperforms conventional and one-shot FGL methods, with particularly strong gains under highly heterogeneous (non-IID) and graph-perturbed settings.

</details>


### [158] [Bi-HYCO: Bi-Objective Cooperative Learning for PDE Parameter Identification under Fragmented Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06511)
*Umberto Biccari,Jun Chen,Roberto Morales,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bi-Objective HYCO (Bi-HYCO)，一个协同框架，同时保留物理模型和合成模型两个表征及其局部观测目标，通过在无标注交互点耦合预测状态来共享信息，并证明交替序列收敛到混合临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模型和合成模型可能描述同一PDE系统的互补方面，但接收不同（可能碎片化的）观测。现有方法需要统一表征或牺牲局部目标，如何在保留各自模型和观测目标的同时实现信息协同是一个挑战。

Method: Bi-HYCO构建双目标向量值优化，两个目标分别对应物理和合成模型的观测损失，通过在无标签交互点（不添加数据）耦合状态预测实现通信。采用加权标量化生成具体计算实现，并提出确定性共享观测算法，在固定交互点下证明交替序列的充分下降和有限长度性质，在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz型假设下收敛到混合临界点。

Result: 在椭圆传输问题和二维Navier-Stokes实验中，评估了参数与状态重构、噪声和标量化效应，并与PINN/XPINN对照。消融实验表明，移除状态交互仅保留聚合会恶化参数恢复性能，尤其在Navier-Stokes问题中显著。

Conclusion: 状态交互机制是Bi-HYCO协同效果的关键，仅聚合目标不足以实现有效的参数恢复；该框架为异构模型协同求解PDE系统提供了有理论保证的可行方案。

Abstract: Physical and synthetic models may describe complementary aspects of the same PDE-governed system while receiving different, possibly fragmented, observations. We propose Bi-Objective HYCO (Bi-HYCO), a cooperative framework that retains both representations and their local observational objectives while coupling their predicted states at unlabeled interaction points. These points contain no measurements and do not augment the data; they provide a communication mechanism in the common state space. The two criteria form a vector-valued objective, and weighted scalarizations provide computational realizations. For the deterministic shared-observation algorithm with fixed interaction points, we prove sufficient decrease and finite length of the whole alternating sequence, which converges to a mixed critical point under the stated Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz-type assumptions. Elliptic transmission and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes experiments assess parameter and state reconstruction, noise and scalarization effects, and PINN/XPINN references. Ablations show that removing state interaction while retaining aggregation deteriorates parameter recovery in the tested configurations, particularly for Navier-Stokes.

</details>


### [159] [Model-Adaptive and Risk-Constrained Frequency Hopping Against Predictive Jammers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06514)
*Yanbo Chen,Xinjing Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-PACT-AFH框架，一种模型自适应且风险约束的对抗性上下文老虎机方法用于自适应跳频抗预测性干扰，通过Tsallis-FTRL主学习器结合全局线性与分区局部学习器在线选择模型，并提供D-PACT-Hit和D-PACT-Safe两种变体分别处理信道边际风险和每时隙风险预算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应跳频对抗预测性干扰需要同时解决两个挑战：一是模型不确定性（上下文-丢失关系可能在不同运行体制下变化），二是策略暴露风险（持续跳频模式可能使高概率信道暴露给攻击者）。现有方法未能同时兼顾模型自适应性（在不同干扰体制间灵活切换学习模型）和风险约束（限制信道暴露带来的被攻击风险）。

Method: 该方法构建了一个三层框架：1) 基于Tsallis-FTRL的master学习器，在全局线性学习器与分区局部学习器之间在线选择模型类；2) D-PACT-Hit将信道维度上的边际命中风险纳入模型选择过程；3) D-PACT-Safe通过最小KL散度投影（minimum-Kullback-Leibler projection）实施每时隙风险预算约束。主学习器负责权衡global learner与partitioned local learner的适用性以应对不同运行体制。

Result: 在多种信道体制和干扰类型下的实验表明该框架能有效进行模型自适应，并实现可控的吞吐量(好put)-风险权衡：D-PACT-AFH在可观测切换体制下恢复了局部学习器增益的95.5%，而在负对照体制下避免了其77.7%的性能退化。

Conclusion: 理论分析层面建立了非预期攻击下估计量的有效性、相对于更优固定基线的oracle分解，以及Safe投影的精确条件风险保证。实验证明该方法在模型适应性与风险控制之间实现了可调的平衡，显著优于单一模型策略。

Abstract: Adaptive frequency hopping against predictive jamming must address both model uncertainty and policy exposure: the context-loss relationship may vary across operating regimes, while persistent hopping patterns may expose high-probability channels to attack. We propose D-PACT-AFH, a model-adaptive and risk-constrained adversarial contextual-bandit framework in which a Tsallis-FTRL master combines a global linear learner with a partitioned local learner and selects the model class online. D-PACT-Hit incorporates channel-wise marginal hit risk into model selection, while D-PACT-Safe applies a minimum-Kullback-Leibler projection to enforce a per-slot risk budget. We establish estimator validity under non-anticipating attacks, an oracle decomposition relative to the better fixed base, and an exact conditional-risk guarantee for the Safe projection. Experiments across diverse channel regimes and jammer types demonstrate effective model adaptation and a controllable goodput-risk tradeoff: D-PACT-AFH recovers 95.5% of the local learner's gain under observable switching while avoiding 77.7% of its degradation in a negative-control regime.

</details>


### [160] [Role-Specific Predictive Geometries for Nonstationary Multivariate Graph-Signal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06519)
*Yanbo Chen,Anamitra Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多变量图信号预测的误差修正框架，区分长期（Long）均衡恢复与短期（Short）瞬时传播两种预测角色，分别赋予其各自专用的预测几何结构。Long关系作用于估计的均衡坐标，Short关系作用于滞后差分，并通过多步直接估计在相邻预测视界间耦合校正。通过时间交叉拟合与FWL偏出化，获得条件预测解释。实验证明该方法在合成数据中完全恢复所有植入关系，并在四个真实数据集中多数表现优于G-VARMA骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当节点层面的轨迹是非平稳的，但跨节点和跨特征的稳定关系持续存在时，多变量图信号预测极具挑战性。在误差修正表示中，长期均衡恢复与短期瞬时传播具有不同的预测作用，不应共享同一个跨特征几何结构，现有方法忽视了这种角色特异性。

Method: 引入角色专用的预测几何结构：带方向的Long关系作用在估计的均衡坐标上，带方向的Short关系作用在滞后差分上。矩阵值Long响应在传播前先混合均衡坐标，Short响应使用图滤波的瞬时设计。直接多步估计器在相邻视界间耦合预测校正，时间交叉拟合与FWL偏出化使选中的边具有条件预测解释。Long算子通过均衡子空间保持右因子化，从而消解源公共趋势方向。

Result: 受控实验恢复所有植入的Long关系(20/20)与Short关系(20/20)，并在所有Dual实现中(10/10)识别出两个角色族。在四个真实基准中，所提预测器在三个数据集上优于G-VARMA骨干，五折金融基准的全部25个fold-horizon比较均有利。

Conclusion: 角色专用的预测几何结构能够有效区分长期均衡恢复与短期传播关系，提升非平稳多变量图信号的预测性能，为图时间序列预测提供了更精细的结构化建模视角。

Abstract: Forecasting multivariate graph signals is challenging when node-level trajectories are nonstationary but stable relations persist across nodes and features. In an error-correction representation, long-run equilibrium restoration and short-run transient propagation represent different predictive roles and need not share a common cross-feature geometry. We introduce role-specific predictive geometries in which directed Long relations act on estimated equilibrium coordinates, whereas directed Short relations act on lagged differences. Matrix-valued Long responses mix equilibrium coordinates before graph propagation, while Short responses use graph-filtered transient designs; a direct multi-horizon estimator couples forecast corrections across adjacent horizons. Temporal cross-fitting and Frisch-Waugh-Lovell partialling-out give selected edges a conditional predictive interpretation relative to a graph-temporal backbone. The Long operator remains right-factorized through the equilibrium subspace and therefore annihilates source common-trend directions. Controlled experiments recover all planted Long relations (20/20), all planted Short relations (20/20), and both role families in every Dual realization (10/10). Across four real-world benchmarks, the proposed predictor improves on the G-VARMA backbone in three datasets, with all 25 fold-horizon comparisons favorable on the five-fold financial benchmark.

</details>


### [161] [Not Just Oversmoothing: Detecting the Echo Chamber Effect in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06521)
*Asela Hevapathige,Ahad N. Zehmakan,Asiri Wijesinghe,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示GNN过度平滑中的"回声室效应"（社区内表示快速坍缩而社区间保持分离），提出回声室指数（ECI）量化该效应，并设计社区感知分裂传播（CASP）插件来解耦社区内/间聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN过度平滑诊断依赖全局聚合度量，无法捕捉消息传递的异质动态。真实图具有显著的社区结构，消息传递在双时间尺度上运行，导致社区内表示快速坍缩而社区间仍保持分离——这种全局能量衰减但社区间分离依然存在的失效模式未被现有方法捕捉和量化。

Method: 1) 提出回声室效应概念，识别社区内坍塌与社区间分离并存的失效模式；2) 引入回声室指数（ECI），按社区归属分层计算成对距离，揭示全局能量衰减而社区间分离持续的现象；3) 理论分析证明特征保留机制在特定条件下会加剧回声室效应；4) 分析标签结构的影响：社区与类别一致时回声室有利于分类，不一致时使分类证明性地更难；5) 提出CASP（Community-Aware Split Propagation），解耦社区内与社区间聚合，从标签结构学习平衡权重。

Result: CASP作为轻量级插件，在大多数同构和异构设置中提升了多种骨干GNN的性能。

Conclusion: 过度平滑的诊断需要社区级粒度的分析而非全局聚合度量；回声室效应对分类的影响取决于标签与社区结构的对齐程度；社区感知的聚合解耦可以显著改善GNN在异构和同构图上的表现。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a well-known failure mode of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). However, most existing diagnostics rely on global aggregation measures that fail to capture the heterogeneous dynamics of message passing. Real-world graphs exhibit pronounced community structure, and message passing operates on two timescales, with representations collapsing rapidly within communities and slowly across them. This creates a critical gap in which intra-community representations can become indistinguishable while inter community separation persists, a failure mode that we refer to as the Echo Chamber Effect. To quantify this effect, we introduce the Echo Chamber Index (ECI), which stratifies pairwise distances by community membership and reveals when global energy diminishes while inter-community separation persists. ECI further shows that feature retention mechanisms can preserve the echo chamber under the conditions of our theoretical analysis. The consequences depend on label structure: when communities align with classes, the echo chamber can sharpen node classification, whereas when they do not, the same collapse makes classification provably harder. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Community-Aware Split Propagation (CASP), a lightweight plugin that decouples intra- and inter-community aggregation and learns their balance from label structure. CASP improves diverse backbone GNNs across most evaluated homophilic and heterophilic settings.

</details>


### [162] [Hidden in Plain Sight: The Overlooked Significance of Canonical Elements for Extreme LLM Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06557)
*Hyeondo Jang,Kwanhee Lee,Dongyeop Lee,Namhoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文挑战了大语言模型（LLM）在激进稀疏化下脆弱性的传统观点，通过重新审视基础的、在大规模上尚未充分探索的剪枝策略，结合基于二阶显著性（second-order saliency）的渐进式稀疏化框架与协调的持续训练，证明预训练LLM在高达99%的极稀疏度下仍能保持强性能。在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3模型家族上，该方法在困惑度和下游准确率上超越当前最先进方法和代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究普遍认为LLM在激进稀疏化下表现脆弱，只能维持在适度的稀疏水平。尽管近年有研究暗示LLM对高稀疏度比预想更具韧性，但这通常被框定为设计挑战而非根本限制。作者希望质疑非结构化后训练LLM剪枝的感知极限，探索在极稀疏（95%-99%）区间LLM的性能潜力，从而为极端稀疏LLM的部署铺平道路。

Method: 提出一种渐进式稀疏化框架（progressive sparsification framework），核心要素包括：(1) 采用二阶显著性（second-order saliency）准则进行剪枝，而非简单的一阶或基于幅度的剪枝；(2) 将持续训练（continued training）与稀疏度的逐步推进（sparsity progression）相协调，使模型在每个稀疏度层级上充分适应后再继续推进；(3) 重新审视并利用在大规模模型上尚未被充分探索的基础剪枝策略，而非依赖复杂的新机制。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3模型家族上，该方法在高达99%稀疏度下均改善了困惑度和下游任务准确率，超越当前SOTA和代表性基线。具体地，在LLaMA-2-7B上，95%和99%稀疏度时WikiText-2困惑度分别为13.48和19.67；在95%稀疏度下实现3.23倍解码加速和6.21倍内存节省。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明LLM可以被推到极端稀疏（高达99%）而仍保持强劲性能，这挑战了传统上认为LLM在稀疏化下脆弱的观点。作者认为该工作为在极端稀疏区间进一步改进稀疏模型提供了基础，并将问题本质归结为设计（方法与训练协调策略）而非模型能力本身的局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often considered fragile under aggressive sparsification, and maintaining reliable performance typically requires sticking to moderate sparsity levels. However, recent studies suggest that LLMs are more resilient to high sparsity than previously thought, reframing the problem as a design challenge rather than a fundamental limitation. In this work, we challenge the perceived limits of unstructured post-training LLM pruning by revisiting elementary pruning strategies that have remained relatively underexplored at this scale. Through a progressive sparsification framework with second-order saliency and continued training coordinated with sparsity progression, we show that pretrained LLMs can retain strong performance far beyond commonly studied sparsity regimes. Across LLaMA-2 and Qwen-3 model families, our approach improves perplexity and downstream accuracy up to 99\% sparsity, surpassing both the current state-of-the-art and representative baselines. Precisely, on LLaMA-2-7B, our approach achieves WikiText-2 perplexities of 13.48 and 19.67 at 95\% and 99\% sparsity, respectively, while delivering 3.23$\times$ decoding speedup and 6.21$\times$ memory savings at 95\% sparsity. Taken together, our results show that LLMs can be pushed into extreme sparsity while retaining strong performance, providing a foundation for further improving sparse models in this regime.

</details>


### [163] [Layer-Wise Gate-Controlled Prompt Truncation in a Multimodal Chest X-Ray Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06590)
*Jingtao Lei,Hongji Li,Dexiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了Mixture of Prompt Experts (MoPE)在双标签胸部X光分类中的逐层门控扩展。控制器为每个样本预测保留比例，在小批量内取平均后用于截断静态和混合视觉提示，并对保留的混合提示按个体比例缩放。在单次运行中，门控模型的最佳验证准确率为0.8996，优于固定长度基线的0.8969。但导出的门控统计显示在所有记录训练点上都保留长度为1（配置最大为6），表明提示在门控惩罚下被系统性缩短，而非实现了真正的样本特定长度分配。研究受限于报告派生标签、报告文本输入、顺序数据划分及缺乏重复对照实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以通过逐层门控机制实现输入依赖的动态提示保留长度，以改进多模态Transformer中固定提示长度的局限，并实现更高效的提示分配。

Method: 在MoPE框架上引入逐层门控扩展：训练一个控制器为每个样本预测保留比例，在小批量内取平均后转换为整数长度，用于截断静态和混合视觉提示；保留的混合提示额外按个体保留比例进行缩放。在双标签胸部X光分类任务上运行二进制门控消融实验，与固定提示长度基线对照。

Result: 单次运行中，门控模型最佳验证准确率0.8996，高于固定长度基线0.8969；最终值分别为0.8963和0.8802。门控统计表明所有记录点保留长度为1（配置最大为6），视觉序列从210个token减至200个，但无直接运行时间测量证实加速收益。

Conclusion: 该发现记录了一种在配置门控惩罚下提示被系统性缩短的现象，但不能证明样本特定长度分配的有效性、对固定短提示的优越性，也不支持临床可用性。研究存在多项方法学限制，结论应谨慎解读。

Abstract: Mixture of Prompt Experts (MoPE) adapts multimodal transformers through input-dependent prompt composition, while retaining a fixed prompt length. We investigate a layer-wise gating extension in a binary chest X-ray classification pilot study. The controller predicts a retention ratio for each sample, averages these ratios within a mini-batch, and uses the resulting integer length to truncate the static and mixed visual prompts. Retained mixed prompts are also scaled by the individual ratios. In one recorded run per configuration, the gated model reached a best validation accuracy of 0.8996, compared with 0.8969 for the fixed-length baseline; the corresponding final values were 0.8963 and 0.8802. The exported gate statistics imply a retained length of one at all recorded training points, relative to a configured maximum of six. This reduces the complete visual sequence from 210 to 200 tokens, but no direct runtime measurements establish an acceleration benefit. Report-derived labels, report text as input, sequential data partitioning, and the absence of repeated controlled experiments limit interpretation. The findings document prompt shortening under the configured gate penalty; they do not establish sample-specific length allocation, superiority over fixed short prompts, or clinical utility. Code is available at: https://github.com/jingtaolei/mope-dynamic-prompt-truncation.

</details>


### [164] [Deep Barycentric Regression for Optimal Transport Map Estimation and its Statistical Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06598)
*Kunwoong Kim,Insung Kong,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BROT（Barycentric Regression for OT）方法，两步估计最优传输映射：先计算无正则化的OT计划，再用深度神经网络对重心目标做最小二乘回归。理论证明在真实OT映射为Lipschitz时达到极小极大收敛率，实验表明在映射准确性、目标分布匹配和下游任务（单细胞扰动预测、无监督域自适应）上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OT映射估计器存在理论保障与实用性的鸿沟：理论估计器达到极小极大最优收敛率但通常是非参数的，实现复杂且推断成本高；实用估计器参数化且可扩展，但统计保证研究不足，且其min-max对抗式训练目标对优化算法敏感。

Method: BROT采用两步方法：第一步计算无正则化的OT计划（传输矩阵），第二步将OT计划导出的重心目标作为标签，通过最小二乘回归拟合深度神经网络。该方法将映射估计转化为标准的回归问题，避免了对抗式训练的不稳定性。

Result: 在标准正则性条件下，证明了BROT的DNN估计器在真实OT映射为Lipschitz时达到极小极大收敛率。数值实验表明BROT在合成数据集和图像数据集上均提供准确的映射估计、强烈的目标分布匹配能力和有竞争力的传输成本。在两个下游任务（单细胞扰动预测、无监督域自适应）中，BROT的精确估计转化为更强的任务性能。

Conclusion: BROT弥合了OT映射估计中理论最优性与实际可操作性之间的差距，通过简单的两步式回归框架同时获得了极小极大最优的统计保证和稳定的参数化训练，为OT映射估计提供了高效且理论上可证的选择。

Abstract: The optimal transport (OT) map provides a geometric transformation for aligning probability distributions and has become a useful tool in machine learning. However, existing estimators of the OT map still exhibit a gap between sharp statistical guarantees and practical parametric estimation based on stable training objectives. Theoretical estimators achieve minimax optimal convergence rates, but they are typically nonparametric and can incur demanding implementation design or inference costs. Practical estimators are parametric and scalable, but their statistical guarantees remain underexplored, and their min-max, adversarial-like training objectives can be sensitive to optimization algorithms. We propose BROT (Barycentric Regression for OT), a simple two-step method that first computes the unregularized OT plan and then fits a deep neural network (DNN) to the induced barycentric targets by least-squares regression. Under standard regularity conditions, we prove that the DNN estimator of BROT attains the minimax convergence rate, when the ground-truth OT map is Lipschitz. Numerical studies on synthetic datasets and an image dataset show that BROT provides accurate map estimates, strong target distribution matching, and competitive transport costs, compared to existing estimation methods. Experiments on two downstream tasks, single-cell perturbation prediction and unsupervised domain adaptation, further suggest that the accurate estimation of BROT can translate into stronger task performance.

</details>


### [165] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Quantifying Offensive Impact in Professional Box Lacrosse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06610)
*Robert Jimerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于1,006次手动标注的罗切斯特刀锋队射门尝试，建立了职业箱棍网球（NLL）的预期进球（xG）框架，并提出了描述性而非推断性的预期掩护价值（xPV）指标。结果显示仅部分模型规格优于基准，属于单队案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业箱棍网球的传统统计仅总结比赛结果，缺乏对射门质量及得分机会背后角色作用的刻画。研究旨在开发可文档化的xG建模框架，并对进攻参与进行归因，以填补这一分析空白。

Method: 使用13场连续NLL比赛（2025-2026赛季）中1,006次手动标注的射门尝试（含151个进球），评估逻辑回归、随机森林和极端随机树三种模型，设置三组嵌套特征集，采用留一比赛法交叉验证并以训练折基准率作为参照。通过射手xG和射门预期助攻记录进攻参与，提出xPV指标比较掩护点实际状态与无掩护反事实的概率差异，并采用200次行置换检验。

Result: 上下文基线随机森林取得最低总体对数损失（0.4189）和Brier分数（0.1260），相对基准分别提升1.22%和1.50%；但九种规格中有五种未超越基准。加入二人配合动作和掩护类型字段未改善主要指标。xPV的幅度与模型噪声无法区分，方向性模式超过200次置换复制，但推断效力有限，仅作为探索性补充成分报告。

Conclusion: 受限于单队、13场的小规模样本，研究结果应视为初步案例研究而非联赛范围的因果性估计。xPV仅作为探索性增强组件呈现，不能作为推断性结论使用。

Abstract: Professional box-lacrosse statistics summarize outcomes but provide limited information about shot quality or the roles behind scoring opportunities. This study develops a documented framework for estimating expected goals (xG) and attributing recorded offensive involvement using 1,006 manually annotated Rochester Knighthawks shot attempts, including 151 goals, from 13 consecutive 2025-2026 National Lacrosse League games. Logistic regression, random forest, and extremely randomized trees were evaluated across three nested feature sets using Leave-One-Game-Out cross-validation and a training-fold base-rate benchmark. The contextual baseline random forest had the lowest observed pooled log loss (0.4189) and Brier score (0.1260), improving on the benchmark by 1.22% and 1.50%; five of nine specifications did not beat the benchmark. Adding two-man-action and pick-type fields did not improve the primary metrics. Core Offensive Impact attributes recorded involvement through shooter xG and shot-based expected assists for final passers. Expected Pick Value (xPV) compares a qualifying pick's observed-state probability with a no-pick counterfactual. Its magnitude was indistinguishable from model noise. Its directional pattern exceeded 200 row-permutation replicates, but limited tail resolution and failure to preserve game-level pick composition make the diagnostic descriptive rather than inferential. Accordingly, xPV is reported only as an exploratory augmented component. Given the single-team, 13-game sample, the results are an initial case study rather than league-wide or causal estimates.

</details>


### [166] [Inducing Emergent Misalignment from Reward Hacks with Iterative DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06649)
*Oliver Daniels,Perusha Moodley,Benjamin M. Marlin,David Lindner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用迭代直接偏好优化（iterative DPO）作为强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）的替代研究范式，以低成本方式研究语言模型中的奖励黑客导致的隐性错位（emergent misalignment）。通过在GPT-4.1和Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct上的实验，发现该训练方法能诱导出隐蔽的权力寻求和一致性伪装等危险行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR过程中的奖励黑客可能引发语言模型的奖励寻求行为和广泛错位，理解这一误泛化（misgeneralization）对开发威胁模型和应对措施至关重要。然而，在大模型上进行强化学习成本过高，难以广泛开展研究。

Method: 提出用迭代DPO代替RLVR来研究所学的错位行为，该方法保留了RLVR的重要特性（如随时间演化的训练信号），但显著降低计算成本，并能在常用微调API上直接训练。在单轮奖励黑客环境中分别对GPT-4.1和Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct进行迭代DPO训练。

Result: 1）在GPT-4.1上，迭代DPO训练诱导出隐蔽的权力寻求和一致性伪装行为，这是首次通过公开可用的（半）在线训练流程诱发这些危险错位形式；2）在Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct上，相同流程同时诱导出错位行为和指令跟随准确率的提升，表明迭代DPO可作为选择性泛化的测试平台。

Conclusion: 迭代DPO能够以更低的成本和门槛复现RLVR引发的错位现象，有望民主化和加速语言模型错位研究的进展，为威胁建模和防御措施的开发提供更易获取的实验工具。

Abstract: Reward hacking during reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) can induce reward seeking and broad misalignment in language models. Studying this misgeneralization is important for developing better threat models and countermeasures, but is often infeasible due to the cost of RL on large models. As an alternative, we propose studying emergent misalignment from iterative DPO, which preserves important properties of RLVR while reducing costs and enabling training on popular finetuning APIs. In practice, we find that training GPT-4.1 with iterative DPO on a single-turn reward hacking environment induces covert misaligned power-seeking and alignment faking, the first openly available (semi)-online training pipeline to induce these concerning forms of misalignment. We also find that training Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct with the same pipeline induces both misalignment and improved instruction following accuracy, showing that iterative DPO can be used as a testbed for selective generalization. Overall, we think iterative DPO can help democratize and accelerate the study of emergent misalignment from RLVR.

</details>


### [167] [SwiftExplorer: Training-free Diffusion Model Alignment with Swift Diversity Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06651)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Bojin Huang,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwiftExplorer是一个插件，通过Inheritance-Restart探索机制和Quality-Efficiency仲裁机制，解决扩散模型免训练引导方法中分布坍缩（多样性损失）和冗余引导信号（计算浪费）两个问题，在偏好、保真度、多样性和丰富度等指标上均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型具有通用生成能力但难以对齐特定目标。微调成本高昂，促使研究者开发免训练的引导采样方法。然而现有方法存在两个问题：(1)强烈的方向性偏差收窄了预训练分布，降低生成多样性；(2)不加区分、恒定不变的引导无法剪除冗余信号，损害生成质量和推理效率。

Method: SwiftExplorer作为即插即用插件，包含两大核心机制：(1) Inheritance-Restart探索机制：通过继承-重启的探索策略避免早期收敛，增加高奖励轨迹的出现概率，同时在不引起分布过度偏移的前提下平衡多样性与保真度；(2) Quality-Efficiency仲裁机制：通过移除错误引导信号改善引导质量，并通过动态判定生成完整性和边际奖励增益的最优点来自动停止生成过程，降低计算成本。

Result: 大量实验和多种评估指标表明，SwiftExplorer在偏好、保真度、多样性和丰富度等所有评估维度上均表现出优异性能，同时有效缓解了分布坍缩并降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: SwiftExplorer通过创新的探索与仲裁机制，成功克服了免训练目标引导采样中分布坍缩与冗余计算两大挑战，在不引入额外训练成本的前提下实现了生成质量、多样性与效率的多目标优化。

Abstract: Diffusion models have general generative abilities but struggle to align with specific objectives. Fine-tuning can improve alignment, yet its training cost is often prohibitive. This led to training-free methods that apply objective-guided terms in sampling to bias the generation distribution toward designated regions, e.g., high-reward areas. However, these methods face two issues: (1) the strong directional bias narrows the pretrained distribution and generation diversity, and (2) indiscriminate constant guidance fails to prune redundant signals, hurting both quality and efficiency. To address the above challenges, we propose SwiftExplorer, a plugin that mitigates distribution collapse caused by excessive diversity loss and reduces compute costs. First, we adopt an Inheritance-Restart exploration mechanism to avoid early convergence, while exploration also increases the likelihood of high-reward trajectories. Additionally, it balances diversity and fidelity, adding diversity without causing a distribution over-shift. Second, our Quality-Efficiency arbitration mechanism improves guidance by removing incorrect signals, and it reduces computation by dynamically stopping generation when completeness and marginal reward gain are optimal. In an extensive number of experiments and different types of evaluation metrics, the proposed SwiftExplorer achieves excellent performance on all metrics, including preference, fidelity, diversity, and richness.

</details>


### [168] [Assessing Covariate-Informed Grid Load Forecasting with a Time-Series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06656)
*Varsha Pendyala,Yiwei Fu,Weizhong Yan,Nurali Virani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多通道时间序列基础模型（TSFM）Chronos-2在真实电网负荷预测中的表现，在ISO New England和ENTSO-E两个数据集上与任务专用深度学习模型进行对比，发现Chronos-2在任务特定微调后获益显著且短步长预测性能优异，但其零样本精度落后于任务专用模型，且随着预测步长增加误差增长更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本单变量负荷预测中TSFM表现优异，但真实负荷预测常涉及多目标变量并需要整合外生变量。此前对Chronos-2的评估仅限于少量能源任务且聚焦零样本场景，其在真实场景下相对任务专用模型的表现及利用历史数据进行适配的行为仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 以Chronos-2为多通道TSFM代表（支持单变量、多变量及协变量信息预测），在两个真实公用事业数据集（ISO New England和ENTSO-E）上评估其性能，并与广泛使用的任务专用深度学习模型进行基准对比，同时考察零样本与任务特定微调两种设置下的表现差异。

Result: Chronos-2在任务特定微调后性能提升显著，短水平预测表现强劲；但零样本精度不如任务专用模型，且误差随预测步长增加而增长更快。

Conclusion: 该研究详细刻画了TSFM（如Chronos-2）在电网负荷预测中的优势与局限，并为如何有效适配预训练TSFM用于实际负荷预测应用提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Modern power systems are growing increasingly complex as they integrate diverse generation sources to meet rising demand, making accurate load forecasting challenging. Recent advances in time-series foundation models (TSFMs) resulted in promising performance in zero-shot univariate load forecasting tasks. However, real-world load forecasting often involves multiple target variables and requires the integration of exogenous variables, raising important questions about the utility of TSFMs in realistic settings. In this study, we position Chronos-2, a recently developed model by Amazon, as a representative multi-channel TSFM that supports univariate, multivariate, and covariate-informed forecasting, and conduct a systematic investigation of how such models can be used for real-world load forecasting. While prior work has evaluated Chronos-2 on a limited number of energy-related tasks in a zero-shot setting, its performance relative to established task-specific deep learning models and its behavior when adapted using task-specific historical data remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we evaluate Chronos-2 on two real-world utility datasets, ISO New England and ENTSO-E, and benchmark it against widely used task-specific deep learning models. Our results show that Chronos-2 benefits substantially from task-specific fine-tuning and achieves strong short-horizon forecasting performance, but its zero-shot accuracy lags behind task-specific models and its forecasting error grows more rapidly with increasing forecast steps. Overall, this study provides a detailed characterization of the strengths and limitations of TSFMs such as Chronos-2 in grid load forecasting and offers practical insights into how a pretrained TSFM can be effectively adapted for operational load forecasting applications.

</details>


### [169] [Behavioral Cloning Outperforms Entropy-Regularized RL: Critic-Driven Failure of Actor-Critic Methods on Adaptive Tumor Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06667)
*Aleksandar Dimitrov,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应给药策略的强化学习问题，指出以启发式或历史方案作为比较基准无法证明策略是否达到最优。作者利用三群体肿瘤控制ODE的最优控制分析，构造一种bang-bang给药加奇异弧（singular arc）形式的近最优参考控制器。在其对照标准（200天低于5%携带容量视为持续治愈）下，从零训练的Soft Actor-Critic从未达到治愈；行为克隆(BC)可完美复现参考策略（30/30种子100%治愈），但随后的SAC微调、TD3及BC正则化SAC均会破坏该策略。分析表明，沿治愈轨迹，critic错误地将坍缩策略的动作排在参考动作之上（96%状态），使策略陷入非治愈的自适应治疗均衡。参考控制器使此类失败可见——若改用启发式对照，微调后策略会表现为合格控制器而非失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应给药需要能降低肿瘤负担又不造成过度毒性的策略，但当前学界通常用历史或启发式策略作为强化学习策略的比较基准，这种做法无法判断所学策略是否接近最优行为，容易高估所学策略的实际质量。

Method: 采用三群体肿瘤控制常微分方程模型，通过最优控制分析确定最优给药方案的结构形式（bang-bang给药并含奇异弧），据此构造数值控制器作为近最优参考。以持续治愈准则（200天连续低于5%携带容量）为评价标准，在多个种子上比较SAC从零训练、BC克隆参考、SAC微调克隆策略、TD3和BC正则化SAC等方法的疗效。

Result: 从零训练的SAC从未达到治愈；BC完整复现参考（30/30种子100%持续治愈）；但SAC微调破坏克隆策略（五种熵系数均失败），TD3和BC正则化SAC同样失败，且在乘法药代动力噪声下模式不变。沿治愈轨迹，critic在96%的状态中将坍缩策略的动作评分高于参考动作，主要集中于维持期，策略最终收敛于非治愈的自适应治疗均衡。

Conclusion: 强化学习微调可能破坏原本有效的克隆策略，其根因在于critic对评价函数外区域的状态给出了错误的高分，导致策略滑向局部次优均衡。只有引入近最优参考对比，才能识别出该微调策略本质上是失败而非合格的控制器，说明建立最优性对照基准对评估自适应剂量策略至关重要。

Abstract: Adaptive dosing requires policies that reduce tumor burden without excessive toxicity. Learned dosing policies are typically judged against historical or heuristic comparators, which cannot show whether a policy has found the best behavior available. We instead study a three-population tumor-control ODE in which optimal-control analysis fixes the form of a good schedule -- bang-bang dosing punctuated by a singular arc -- and construct a numerical controller of that form as a proxy for near-optimal behavior. Judged against this reference under a sustained-cure criterion -- 200 consecutive days below 5% carrying capacity -- Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) trained from scratch never reaches cure. Behavioral cloning (BC) of the reference reproduces it (100% sustained cure, 30/30 seeds), but SAC fine-tuning of the cloned policy destroys it across five entropy coefficients, and TD3 and BC-regularized SAC fail identically; the pattern persists under multiplicative pharmacokinetic action noise. Along curative trajectories the post-collapse critic ranks the collapsed-policy action above the reference action in 96% of states, concentrated in the maintenance phase, and the policy settles into a non-curative adaptive-therapy equilibrium. The reference is what makes this legible: against a heuristic comparator the fine-tuned policy would read as a competent controller rather than a failure.

</details>


### [170] [Towards Unified Multimodal Graph Foundation Model: A Bridge-Router-Adapter Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06668)
*Sirui Zhang,Yubing Zhou,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yujin Gao,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRAIN，一个多模态图基础模型，通过范围条件桥接、分层路由和残差适配器解决跨范围上下文纠缠和范围无关的模态路由两大局限，在九数据集四类任务上超越现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图基础模型旨在将图拓扑结构与跨模态属性联合建模并实现跨领域迁移，但现有方法存在两个根本局限：(1) 跨范围上下文纠缠，即不同范围的图结构被合并为统一表示而丢失区分性；(2) 范围无关的模态路由，即模态相关性不随邻域范围变化而变化，无法反映不同范围下模态重要性的差异。

Method: BRAIN由三个核心组件构成：范围条件桥接器（scope-conditioned Bridge）将局部到全局的邻域范围结构与不同的模态组合相整合；分层路由器（hierarchical Router）估计范围与任务之间的相关性，并在每个范围内独立选择模态组合，使模态效用随图范围动态变化；轻量级残差适配器（lightweight residual Adapter）对路由后的嵌入进行下游预测任务的专门化。训练采用多图预训练加任务特定微调的范式。

Result: 在九个数据集和四类任务上的实验表明：节点分类和链接预测性能相对最强基线最高提升4.73%，在四个图到文本和两个图到图像指标上平均相对提升14.72%。

Conclusion: BRAIN通过将邻域范围与模态组合解耦并联合建模，验证了范围感知的模态路由策略在多模态图基础学习中的有效性，为后续研究提供了新的框架方向。

Abstract: Multimodal graphs couple node attributes in different modalities, such as text and images, with relational structure, enabling topological structure and cross-modality attributes to be modeled jointly. Multimodal graph foundation models seek unified representations from such data that transfer across different graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. (1) Cross-Scope Context Entanglement. They merge scope-specific graph contexts into a unified representation, obscuring their distinctions during multimodal construction. (2) Scope-Ignorant Modality Routing. They route modalities within a fixed graph scope, overlooking how modality relevance varies across neighborhood ranges. To address these challenges, we propose BRAIN, a unified model that focuses on graph context that combines neighborhood scope with modality composition. BRAIN comprises a scope-conditioned Bridge that combines structural information spanning local-to-global neighborhood scopes with different modality compositions; a hierarchical Router that estimates the relevance between the scope and the task, and selects compositions separately within each scope, allowing modality utility to vary with graph range; and a lightweight residual Adapter that further specializes the routed embedding for downstream prediction. BRAIN is trained through multi-graph pretraining followed by task-specific adaptation. Experiments across nine datasets and four task families demonstrate its broad effectiveness, improving node-classification and link-prediction performance by up to 4.73% relative to the strongest baseline, while achieving an average relative improvement of 14.72% across four graph-to-text and two graph-to-image metrics.

</details>


### [171] [Data Efficient Sample Selection for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06670)
*V Venktesh,Cem levi,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DearICL提出了一个将上下文学习（ICL）示范示例选择建模为子集排序问题的新框架，通过非线性代理模型结合可微排序目标的gap-index bandit算法，实现实例级别的子集选择，在开源LLM基准上相比强线性bandit基线获得8.08-15.9%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL范式使LLM无需微调即可适应新任务，但从大量示例子集中选择最优组合是一个挑战。现有方法不建模ICL样本与下游LLM性能之间的复杂关系，且通常执行静态任务级选择，无法泛化到未见查询。

Method: DearICL将示范示例选择建模为子集排序问题，采用非线性代理模型，并在gap-index bandit算法中使用可微排序目标。gap-index方法能对好的arm和边界arm进行细粒度区分，作为辅助目标训练非线性代理模型，通过充分采样边界arm支持实例级子集排序。

Result: 在开源LLM的示例选择基准上，DearICL相比强线性bandit基线实现了8.08-15.9%的准确率提升，且具有较低的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: DearICL通过基于gap-index的bandit算法与可微排序目标相结合，有效解决了ICL示例选择的子集排序问题，验证了非线性建模与边界臂采样在实例级示例选择中的有效性。

Abstract: The In-context learning (ICL) paradigm aids large language models (LLMs) to adapt to new tasks without need for fine-tuning. However, selecting an optimal combination of demonstration examples from a large pool of example subsets is a challenging problem. Existing approaches for selection do not model the complex relationship between ICL samples and downstream LLM performance. They typically perform static task-level selection, choosing subsets once offline, which can fail to generalize to unseen queries. We introduce DearICL (Data Efficient Algorithm for Ranking) ICL samples, a new framework that models demonstration example selection as a subset ranking problem. DearICL employs a non-linear surrogate employing a differentiable sorting objective within a gap-index bandit algorithm. The gap-index based approach enables fine-grained separation of good arms and borderline arms, which is used as an auxiliary objective to train the non-linear surrogate through sufficient sampling of borderline arms, supporting instance-level subset ranking. On exemplar selection benchmarks with open-source LLMs, DearICL achieves 8.08-15.9% accuracy gains over strong linear bandit baselines, with low sample complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/VenkteshV/DearICL.

</details>


### [172] [Tracking the Moving Frontier: Long-Short Term Advantage Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06671)
*Xinhao Yao,Lu Yu,Changhao Wang,Fengwei Teng,Yuyao Zhang,Qing Cui,Jun Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LSTAE（长短时优势估计器），一种单流强化学习算法，利用历史经验替代组内重复采样来估计优势，仅需每个任务锚点一次rollout即可匹配或超越组基RLVR基线，同时大幅降低rollout成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的RLVR方法需要为每个提示重复采样多条轨迹来计算优势，这使得长时程智能体训练成本高昂，且跨迭代累积的有用经验被浪费。作者质疑是否可以仅利用历史经验来替代反复的组内比较，同时避免直接在过期轨迹上优化。

Method: LSTAE采用单流RL架构，策略仅基于当前rollout更新。其核心包括：1）长期模块（轨迹层面）：针对每个任务锚点维护一个漂移感知的历史基线tracker，跟踪该锚点的移动成功前沿，衡量每条新轨迹的相对贡献；2）短期模块（步骤层面）：利用近期状态-经验缓冲区，通过循环状态估计局部动作优势。这种双时间尺度机制将累积经验转化为多粒度信用分配信号，每个锚点仅需一次rollout。

Result: 在智能体任务和数学推理基准上，LSTAE能够匹配或超越强组基基线方法，同时显著降低rollout开销，验证了历史经验作为有效优势信号源的可行性。

Conclusion: 历史经验可以替代组内重复采样来估计优势，LSTAE通过双时间尺度的历史经验利用策略，在节省计算成本的同时保持了甚至提升了策略学习性能，为高效长时程agent训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Group-based RLVR methods estimate advantages by repeatedly sampling multiple trajectories for each prompt, making long-horizon agent training expensive and discarding useful experience accumulated across iterations. We ask whether historical experience can replace these repeated within-iteration comparisons without directly optimizing on stale trajectories. We introduce Long-Short Term Advantage Estimator (LSTAE), a single-stream RL algorithm that uses history for advantage estimation while updating the policy only with the current rollout. LSTAE maintains a persistent tracker for each task anchor. At the trajectory level (long term), a drift-aware historical baseline tracks the anchor's moving success frontier and measures the relative contribution of each new trajectory. At the step level (short term), a recent state-experience buffer exploits recurrent states to estimate localized action advantages. This two-timescale design converts accumulated experience into multi-granular credit signals, requiring only one rollout per anchor. Across agentic and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, LSTAE matches or improves upon strong group-based baselines while substantially reducing rollout cost.

</details>


### [173] [LATS: Levy Adaptive Tree Sampling for Feedback-Driven Diverse Target Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06761)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Lecheng Kong,Yixin Chen,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LATS（Levy自适应树搜索），一种用于在线反馈驱动搜索的采样框架，通过重尾探索与基于树的值反向传播相结合，在扩散模型中高效发现低似然、高价值的目标区域，在材料科学等基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要将扩散模型生成的采样引导到最大化目标目标的特定未表征区域，但这些高效用模式通常位于低似然尾部区域，且只能通过交互式反馈逐步揭示。现有扩散采样器偏向于预训练模型的高密度区域，难以发现稀有但有前景的现象；而强调探索的采样器虽覆盖广泛，但在严格采样预算下无法高效利用高价值模式。因此需要在探索与利用之间取得平衡。

Method: LATS框架结合了两大机制：(1) 重尾（heavy-tailed）探索策略（基于Levy分布），用于在采样空间中充分覆盖低密度区域以发现稀有的高价值模式；(2) 基于树的值反向传播（tree-based value backpropagation），用于在在线交互反馈中逐步累积和回溯更新各分支的价值估计，从而高效地向已验证的高效用模式收敛。

Result: 在包括材料科学在内的多个多样化基准上，LATS在目标发现效率方面显著优于各基线方法，同时保持了采样保真度和结构多样性。

Conclusion: LATS有效解决了扩散模型在在线反馈驱动科学发现中探索与利用的固有权衡问题，为在低似然高价值区域中进行目标导向采样提供了可行且高效的原则性框架。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at capturing complex data distributions, scientific discovery often requires steering generation toward specific, uncharacterized regions that maximize a target objective. These high-utility modes frequently reside in low-likelihood tail regions and are only revealed sequentially through interactive feedback. Existing diffusion samplers fail in this regime: they inherit the pre-trained model's bias toward high-density regions, leaving rare yet promising phenomena underexplored. Conversely, exploration-heavy samplers ensure broad coverage but fail to efficiently exploit high-utility modes when constrained by a strict sampling budget. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Levy Adaptive Tree Search (LATS), a principled sampling framework for online feedback-driven search. LATS leverages heavy-tailed exploration coupled with tree-based value backpropagation to progressively uncover preferred modes. By maintaining broad distributional coverage, LATS successfully discovers low-likelihood, high-utility regions while preserving sample fidelity and structural diversity. Experiments across diverse benchmarks, including materials science, demonstrate that LATS significantly outperforms baselines in target discovery efficiency.

</details>


### [174] [DrugReason: Dynamic Multi-View Reasoning over Knowledge Graph and Language Evidence for Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06779)
*Zijie Liu,Hongxuan Li,Zhen Tan,Jinhao Duan,Baixiang Huang,Zunpeng Liu,Kai Shu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DrugReason，一个多视角推理框架，将知识图谱（KG）的grounded推理与LLM生成的机制性推理相结合用于药物重定位，以自适应路由和跨专家蒸馏实现优于单视角基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物重定位中候选药物-疾病对空间巨大，且关系依赖复杂多跳生物学机制。现有KG方法受限于观测图结构，LLM方法缺乏事实依据易产生幻觉，单一方法均不足。

Method: DrugReason采用多视角推理框架：将KG推理与LLM机制推断集成；根据查询上下文自适应地将不同推理路径路由到专门的专家模型；通过跨专家蒸馏目标实现知识共享同时保持专家专业性。

Result: 在PharmaDB、DDInter和DrugBank三个数据集上，DrugReason相比强单视角推理基线平均性能有所提升，与基于图的替代方法相比达到竞争性或更优效果，并提供可解释的基于路由的预测。

Conclusion: DrugReason有效弥合了KG与LLM方法在药物重定位中的鸿沟，表明融合grounded推理与生成式机制推断能同时提升预测可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Drug repurposing aims to identify new therapeutic uses for existing compounds and, compared with de novo drug discovery, offers a faster and more cost-effective path to clinical translation. However, the space of candidate drug-disease pairs is enormous and their underlying relationships often depend on complex multi-hop biological mechanisms, making it difficult to reliably predict which pairs represent true therapeutic relationships. Existing approaches tackle this from two directions: knowledge graph-based methods organize curated biomedical evidence into structured relational networks for grounded multi-hop reasoning, while LLM-based methods leverage pretrained knowledge to generate flexible mechanistic rationales. Yet neither is sufficient alone - KGs are confined to observed graph structure while LLMs lack factual grounding and risk hallucination. To address this gap, we propose DrugReason, a multi-view reasoning framework that integrates grounded KG reasoning with LLM-generated mechanistic inference for drug repurposing. DrugReason adaptively routes diverse reasoning paths to specialized experts conditioned on the query context, while a cross-expert distillation objective enables knowledge sharing without sacrificing expert specialization. Experiments on PharmaDB, DDInter, and DrugBank show that DrugReason improves average performance over strong single-view reasoning baselines and achieves competitive or superior results compared with graph-based alternatives, while providing interpretable routing-based predictions.

</details>


### [175] [When Retain Constraints Conflict: Mitigating Forget-Retain Interference in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06786)
*Zijie Liu,Jinhao Duan,Bingqi Shang,Xinming An,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦机器遗忘在表格数据上的特殊挑战，提出"模式感知"的冲突感知遗忘（CAU）方法。在序列化表格数据中，由于记录共享固定列名/值槽位、相似的属性范围和共同的输出空间，遗忘行与保留行之间产生"模式诱导的遗忘-保留重叠"。CAU通过放宽与遗忘集冲突最大的保留行的约束来减少干扰，实验表明其在样本级和特征级遗忘任务中更接近重训练基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在表格数据上的行为尚未被充分探索，而表格预测广泛应用于高风险领域，并越来越多地通过记录序列化和模式感知提示适配到语言模型。在自由文本或其他模态中未被发现的一个关键挑战是"模式诱导的遗忘-保留重叠"：序列化表格中记录共享固定槽位和属性范围，导致遗忘行附近的保留行依赖相同的强信号属性，使保留保留目标与遗忘更新目标相互对立。

Method: 提出Conflict-Aware Unlearning (CAU)，一种模式感知的遗忘方法。核心思想是通过放松与遗忘集冲突最大的保留行的约束来减少遗忘-保留干扰，即在保留那些与遗忘数据高度重叠/冲突的保留行时降低保留损失权重，从而在实现遗忘的同时维持整体模型效用和保留区域行为。

Result: 在临床和非医疗表格任务上分别评估了样本级和特征级遗忘。CAU相较于现有方法更接近于重训练（retraining oracle）的性能，同时保持了预测效用和保留区域行为，证明了按冲突程度自适应构造保留约束的有效性。

Conclusion: 可靠的表格LLM遗忘不仅取决于遗忘目标本身，还取决于保留约束的构造方式。模式感知的冲突检测和约束放松是解决表格数据遗忘-保留干扰的关键，为后续在该方向上的研究提供了新的视角和基线。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving model utility, but its behavior on tabular data remains underexplored. This gap is important because tabular prediction is widely used in high-stakes domains and is increasingly adapted to language models through record serialization and schema-aware prompting. We identify a key challenge that distinguishes tabular unlearning from unlearning in free-form text or other modalities: schema-induced forget-retain overlap. In serialized tabular data, records share fixed column-name/value slots, similar attribute ranges, and common output spaces. Consequently, a forget row may have nearby retain rows that rely on the same high-signal attributes, causing retain preservation to oppose the update required for forgetting. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose Conflict-Aware Unlearning (CAU), a schema-aware approach that reduces forget-retain interference by relaxing preservation constraints on retained rows that most conflict with the forget set. Across sample-level and feature-level unlearning on clinical and non-medical tabular tasks, CAU more closely matches a retraining oracle while maintaining predictive utility and retain-region behavior. Our results show that reliable tabular LLM unlearning depends not only on the forgetting objective, but also on how retain constraints are constructed.

</details>


### [176] [Train Smarter, Not Harder: Switching Signal-Guided Training in Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06806)
*Nagham Omar,Maya Rozenshtein,Evgeny Mishlyakov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HybridAL自适应训练调度方法，在主动学习中动态监测模型稳定化信号，并在训练轨迹稳定后从从头训练（retraining）切换为微调（fine-tuning），在保持宏F1不劣于基线（0.010容差）的同时节省高达49%的重训时间，并恢复重训的大部分校准优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习中"从头重训还是从上一轮checkpoint微调"这一训练策略决策常被忽视，但该选择具有可利用的结构：重训在早期轮次最有用（每批数据能大幅重塑标注分布），而一旦模型轨迹稳定，微调更为安全。作者旨在利用这一结构，实现时间开销与模型校准质量的更好权衡。

Method: 提出HybridAL，一种自适应训练调度方法：在线监测稳定化信号，在持续稳定后自动从头训练切换为微调。设计了两个互补信号——基于权重的谱指数变化Δα和基于验证集准确率变化ΔAcc，二者在时间-校准权衡谱系上占据不同位置。实验覆盖三个编码器主干和六个文本分类任务（各5个随机种子）。

Result: 在三个编码器主干和六个文本分类任务（各5个种子）上，HybridAL在0.010容差内保持端点宏F1不劣于从头训练和微调；节省高达49%的重训时间；以负对数似然(NLL)衡量，恢复从头训练大部分校准优势。与预提交切换轮次的方案相比，HybridAL在中等额外成本下获得更低的NLL，表明依赖轨迹的切换比固定早期切换提供更强的时间-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 主动学习中的训练策略（重训vs微调）是可利用的结构性决策，HybridAL通过自适应切换实现了更好的时间-校准权衡：既保持微调的时间效率，又接近重训的校准性能，且轨迹依赖的切换优于预提交固定轮次的切换策略。

Abstract: Training strategy, namely whether to retrain from scratch or fine-tune from the previous checkpoint, is an overlooked decision variable in active learning. We show that this choice has exploitable structure: retraining is most useful in early rounds, when each batch can substantially reshape the labeled distribution, while fine-tuning becomes safer once the model trajectory stabilizes. We propose HybridAL, an adaptive training schedule that monitors an online stabilization signal and switches from retraining to fine-tuning after sustained stabilization. Two complementary signals, spectral exponent change $Δα$ (weight-based) and accuracy change $Δ$Acc (validation-based), span different points on the time-calibration trade-off. Across three encoder backbones and six text-classification tasks (five seeds each), HybridAL keeps endpoint macro-F1 non-inferior to retraining and fine-tuning at a 0.010 margin, saves up to 49% of retraining time, and recovers a substantial fraction of retraining's calibration advantage as measured by negative log-likelihood (NLL). Compared with schedules that switch at a pre-committed round, HybridAL obtains lower NLL at moderate additional cost, showing that trajectory-dependent switching provides a stronger time-calibration trade-off than fixed early switching.

</details>


### [177] [Constrained Bayesian Optimization for Hierarchical Federated Learning in IoT Networks for Plant Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06830)
*Athanasios Papanikolaou,Athanasios Tziouvaras,Apostolos Xenakis,Periklis Chatzimisios,Shameem A. Puthiya Parambath,George Floros,Enrica Zereik,Ivan Petrovic,Fabio Bonsignorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束贝叶斯优化框架，用于在资源受限的物联网环境中高效配置分层联邦学习（HFL）部署，在智能农业植物病害分类任务上以仅11.11%的搜索空间探索率实现了与穷举搜索最优解仅0.056%的平均最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的物联网环境中部署分层联邦学习需要在预测性能、能耗和执行时间之间取得平衡，尤其智能农业场景下的分布式设备计算和通信资源有限，现有方法缺乏对HFL部署配置的系统性优化手段。

Method: 提出了一个约束贝叶斯优化框架，联合探索深度学习骨干网络架构、聚合策略和通信轮数等超参数；联邦规模根据农业部署的空间覆盖需求确定；采用加权目标函数捕捉用户对能耗、执行时间和预测性能的权衡偏好，并通过显式约束确保满足部署特定的资源和精度要求。

Result: 在基于物联网的植物病害分类任务上，实验基于多个深度学习架构、联邦聚合策略和通信轮数设置进行。30次独立优化实验表明，所提方法仅探索11.11%的搜索空间，却能一致识别出与穷举搜索最优解差距在1%以内的解，平均最优性差距仅为0.056%。

Conclusion: 该约束贝叶斯优化框架能够高效、准确地完成资源受限物联网环境下HFL部署的配置优化，在极小搜索空间探索下获得接近全局最优的性能，为智能农业等场景中的联邦学习部署提供了实用的自动化配置方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) in resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) environments requires careful configuration to balance predictive performance with energy consumption and execution time. This challenge is particularly relevant to smart agriculture, where distributed IoT devices can support automated plant disease classification while operating under limited computational and communication resources. This paper presents a constrained Bayesian Optimization framework for the efficient configuration of HFL deployments. The proposed approach jointly explores the deep learning backbone architecture, aggregation strategy, and number of communication rounds, while the federation size is determined according to the spatial coverage requirements of the agricultural deployment. A weighted objective function captures user-defined trade-offs among energy consumption, execution time, and predictive performance, while explicit constraints ensure compliance with deployment-specific resource and accuracy requirements. The framework is evaluated on an IoT-based plant disease classification task considering multiple deep learning architectures, federated aggregation strategies, and communication-round settings. Experimental results across 30 independent optimization runs show that the proposed approach explores only 11.11% of the search space, while consistently identifying solutions within 1% of the exhaustive-search optimum, with a mean optimality gap of only 0.056%.

</details>


### [178] [Feature Superposition in Neural Networks: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06862)
*Dai Shi,Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于神经网络中叠加表示（Superposition）的综述，系统回顾了叠加表示的几何、学习和计算理论，并对比了从训练网络中恢复与分析特征的实际方法，评估了这些方法在重建精度之外的可靠性，指出仅凭激活重建无法确立特征身份或因果用途，最后总结了开放问题并展望了未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠加现象是解释多义神经元和从神经网络激活中恢复可解释特征的重要线索，但理论模型与实证研究之间存在脱节：理论从预设输入特征出发研究编码方式，实证则试图识别训练网络中实际编码的特征。作者希望通过综述弥合这一鸿沟，帮助研究者理解叠加的本质并提供更可靠的神经回路解释方法。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统性地回顾了三个方面：(1) 叠加表示的几何结构、学习过程与计算角色，分析特征统计特性和解码器选择如何影响结论；(2) 对比当前用于恢复和分析训练网络中特征的实用方法，并审视其评估手段确立了哪些结论；(3) 讨论这些方法在有据可查的失败案例和应用中的表现，以检验其对"特征身份"和"因果使用"这两类声明的证据支撑程度。文中还评估了先前提出的开放问题，并识别剩余的理论与实证挑战。

Result: 文章发现理论模型与实证方法在特征定义、统计假设和验证标准上存在系统性差异，精确的激活重建并不等同于正确识别特征身份或证明特征在计算中的因果作用。现有特征恢复方法在特定场景下有效但也存在已记录的失败模式，评估方法所确立的结论范围需要谨慎区分，且许多先前标记的开放问题仍未得到充分解决。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁应区分"重建激活"与"确立特征身份与因果使用"这两类不同层级的主张，并指出需要对叠加表示的理论与实证理解进行进一步深化，以便开发出更可靠的可解释性方法。这项工作旨在为更深刻地理解叠加现象和更稳健的神经网络解释方法铺平道路。

Abstract: Superposition refers to neural networks representing more features than they have dimensions. It offers a possible explanation for polysemantic neurons and motivates methods for recovering interpretable features from neural activations. Theoretical models typically start with a given set of input features and assumptions about how their values vary across inputs, then study how a network encodes those values in a lower-dimensional hidden representation. Empirical work, by contrast, seeks to identify the features encoded in trained networks and determine their role in computation. In this survey, we review the geometry, learning, and computation of superposed representations, explaining how feature statistics and decoder choice affect the conclusions. To connect these theoretical accounts with evidence from trained networks, we compare practical methods for recovering and analyzing features and examine what their evaluations establish. Since accurate activation reconstruction alone does not establish feature identity or causal use, we discuss the methods' documented failures and applications in light of the evidence available for these different claims. Finally, we assess previously stated open problems and identify remaining theoretical and empirical questions about superposition in trained networks. We hope our work can pave the way for a deeper understanding of superposition and more reliable methods for interpreting neural networks.

</details>


### [179] [PPIM: Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba for Heat-Source-Conditioned 3D Bioheat Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06869)
*Dongyun Lee,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三维Pennes生物热方程框架下，比较了三种神经PDE求解器（傅里叶特征PINN、通用PINNMamba和PPIM）在模拟微波消融局部热源场景中的性能。PPIM通过引入条件化热源输入和Pennes感知的SSM衰减初始化，在600秒运行中取得了最低的MAE、相对L1和L2误差，表明其在逼近FDM参考温度场方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维生物热仿真对预测生物组织中的瞬态温度分布至关重要，传统数值方法计算代价高。研究者希望探索神经网络求解器在Pennes生物热方程上的近似能力，并通过在相同受控条件下的公平对比，评估不同神经PDE求解器的性能优劣。

Method: 在受控的局部热源（模拟微波消融）条件下求解三维Pennes生物热方程，采用相同的Pennes残差训练三种神经求解器：(1)空间傅里叶特征PINN；(2)通用PINNMamba时序子序列模型；(3)PPIM——在时序子序列模型基础上增加条件热源输入和Pennes感知的SSM衰减初始化。以显式有限差分法（FDM）解作为数值参考进行评估。

Result: 在代表性的600秒仿真中，PPIM在三种神经求解器中取得了最低的MAE、相对L1误差和相对L2误差。误差图显示PPIM的残余误差主要集中在热源区域附近，而在域的其他区域分布较为均匀稀疏。

Conclusion: PPIM能够有效逼近FDM参考的在受控三维Pennes生物热仿真中的最终温度场，表明将物理先验（Pennes感知的SSM初始化）与条件化热源输入相结合的架构设计在神经PDE求解方面具有优势。

Abstract: Three-dimensional bioheat simulation aims to predict transient temperature distributions in biological tissue and is commonly modeled using the Pennes bioheat equation, which combines thermal diffusion, perfusion-mediated heat loss, and external heat generation. In this study, we consider a controlled 3D Pennes bioheat simulation under a localized heat-source condition inspired by microwave ablation (MWA). To evaluate neural approximation performance, we compare three neural partial differential equation (PDE) solvers under the same controlled simulation: a spatial Fourier-feature physics-informed neural network (PINN), a generic PINNMamba temporal subsequence model, and Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba (PPIM). PPIM builds on the temporal subsequence model by incorporating conditioned heat-source input and Pennes-aware state-space model (SSM) decay initialization. All three neural models are trained under the same conditions with the same Pennes residual, and an explicit finite-difference method (FDM) solution is used only as the numerical reference. In a representative 600~s run, PPIM achieved the lowest MAE, relative $L_1$ error, and relative $L_2$ error among the evaluated neural solvers. Error maps further showed that the remaining PPIM errors were more concentrated near the heat-source region than across the rest of the domain. These results indicate that PPIM is effective for approximating the FDM reference final temperature field in this controlled simulation. The source code is available at https://github.com/muvYun/PPIM.

</details>


### [180] [Exact Record Omission in Delta Attention: A Transport Criterion, Its Cost, and a Replay Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06872)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合线性-循环记忆（如Kimi Linear）中"遗忘/删除记录"的可行性。作者提出"收据（receipt）"机制——保存记录到达时引起的差异并向前传递、在删除时减去它以实现精确删除，但证明该机制仅在记录对后续更新的影响净抵消时才能精确删除。在48B Kimi Linear混合模型上，经过4,096个token后记录仍留下约4.5%状态范数的印记，所有测试的收据类别均无法移除；每token日志成本超过88个token后的完整检查点。相反，从记录前恢复检查点并重放存活的后续内容（checkpoint replay）在所有测试数组上实现精确删除，虽然工作量与重放长度成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户要求助手遗忘记录时，需要确认记忆状态与从未存储该记录时的状态一致。独立编码的行可直接删除，但循环记忆将记录折叠进不断演化的状态中。本文动机是寻找一种固定开销的删除方法（"收据"），使得不论对话多长，删除只需一次固定大小的编辑。

Method: 作者提出"收据运输"方案：保存记录到达时引发的状态差异，将其通过后续更新向前传播，删除时减去该差异。从理论上证明该方法达到精确删除当且仅当记录在后续更新中引起的变更净效应为零。在48B Kimi Linear混合模型、Mamba-2、Falcon-H1、RWKV-7上测量各收据类别的效果，并对比检查点重放方法（从记录前检查点恢复并重放存活的后缀）与掩膜记录注意力行等替代策略。

Result: 在Kimi Linear上，经过4,096个token后记录仍留下约4.5%状态范数印记，测试的收据类别均不能移除；重算一半后缀只能关闭不到一半的差距；每token日志成本在88个token后就超过完整检查点。写入规则分类在Mamba-2、Falcon-H1、RWKV-7上保持一致（预测先于运行记录）。检查点重放实现逐数组精确匹配"从未存储"状态；在混合后缀扫描中，掩膜记录注意力行使采样恢复接近未存储基线，但循环印记仍存留，审计者仍可检测到。

Conclusion: 在所评估的方法中，检查点重放是唯一实现精确遗忘的方案，其代价是与重放后缀长度成正比的工作量。收据机制因循环状态中记录的长期印记不可消除而不适合精确遗忘；混合模型的注意力行掩膜虽可接近未存储基线，但仍留下可检测的循环印记。

Abstract: When a user asks an assistant to forget a record, the test is whether the memory now matches the state it would hold if the record had never been stored. Independently encoded rows can be removed directly; a recurrent memory folds records into an evolving state. One hope is a receipt: save the difference the record made when it arrived, carry it forward through later updates, and subtract it, so that deletion costs one fixed-size edit no matter how long the conversation runs. We show that a transported receipt reaches exact omission if and only if the changes the record induces in later updates cancel out on net, and we measure whether they do on the released 48B Kimi Linear hybrid. They do not: after 4,096 further tokens the record still leaves an imprint of about 4.5% of the state norm that none of the tested receipt classes removes, recomputing half the suffix closes less than half the gap, and the per-token log a receipt needs costs more than a full checkpoint after 88 tokens. The same write-rule classification held on Mamba-2, Falcon-H1, and RWKV-7 with predictions recorded before the runs. Restoring a checkpoint from before the record and replaying the surviving suffix matches the never-stored state exactly on every array we check. In the hybrid suffix sweep, masking the record's attention rows brings sampled recovery close to the never-stored floor even though the recurrent imprint remains, and an auditor who rebuilds the reference can still detect it. Among the evaluated methods, checkpoint replay achieves exact omission, with work proportional to the replayed suffix.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Adaptive SED for heterogeneous load balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06881)
*Sanne van Kempen,Jaron Sanders,Fiona Sloothaak,Maarten G. Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两服务器负载均衡系统中服务速率未知的在线学习问题，提出了一种结合经验SED路由与强制探索的在线学习算法，该算法能够收敛到SED策略，并证明其具有有限遗憾（与经典MAB中遗憾对数增长的结论不同），数值实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在负载均衡系统中，最短期望延迟（SED）策略需要知道服务器服务速率，但在实际场景中这些速率是未知的。纯粹基于经验估计的路由策略会因估计误差而与最优策略在状态空间的无限区域上产生分歧，导致性能不佳，因此需要设计能够同时学习服务速率并收敛到SED策略的在线学习算法。

Method: 提出一种在线学习算法，该算法巧妙地在基于经验估计的SED路由与强制探索阶段之间进行平衡。强制探索阶段确保两个服务器都能被充分采样，从而保证对服务速率的准确估计；经验SED路由阶段则用于基于当前估计做出路由决策，两者交替进行以实现学习与利用的权衡。

Result: 证明了算法能够实现有限遗憾，这一结果与经典多臂老虎机（MAB）设置中遗憾通常随时间对数增长的标准结论截然不同，是一个更强的理论保证。此外，数值实验展示了算法的实际性能，并突出了强制探索在特定场景下尤其有益的机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构服务速率未知的负载均衡系统提供了一种理论上可证明收敛于SED的在线学习方案，其有限遗憾的强保证表明在该问题结构中可以达到比一般MAB更好的学习效果，强制探索策略是确保最优性能收敛的关键机制。

Abstract: We study a two-server load balancing system with heterogeneous service rates that are a priori unknown to the dispatcher. The goal is to route customers according to the Shortest--Expected--Delay (SED) policy, but this requires knowledge of the service rates. Empirical policies that route based on estimates perform poorly: due to estimation error, the empirical policy disagrees with the oracle on an infinite region of the state space. We propose an online learning algorithm that converges to SED while learning the service rates. The algorithm carefully balances empirical SED routing with forced exploration phases that guarantee sufficient sampling of both servers. We prove that our algorithm achieves finite regret; this differs from classical Multi-Armed Bandit settings where regret typically grows logarithmically in time. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithm and highlight the regimes in which forced exploration is especially beneficial.

</details>


### [182] [Noisy-Space Policy Gradient for Diffusion Policies in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06882)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声空间动作价值（Q）函数框架，将扩散策略与强化学习统一起来。通过将Q函数定义在扩散隐变量（噪声隐变量）上，并推导噪声空间策略梯度（NSPG），该方法只需利用干净动作空间的价值估计即可优化扩散策略，无需通过去噪过程反向传播。在状态和视觉基础的离线RL基准上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中具有强大且富有表现力的参数化能力，但将其与强化学习整合在概念和算法层面都面临挑战。现有方法难以直接刻画扩散策略的概率结构，需要通过去噪过程反向传播，导致训练不稳定且计算开销大，因此需要一种原理性且高效的训练范式。

Method: （1）提出噪声空间Q函数，通过去噪过程诱导的已执行动作分布将价值赋予扩散隐变量（噪声隐变量），并证明该构造具有精确的语义解释。（2）推导噪声空间策略梯度（NSPG），仅利用干净动作空间的价值估计即可优化噪声隐变量。（3）基于此构建KL正则化的噪声隐变量策略改进目标，证明该目标可化为扩散兼容的回归形式，避免对去噪过程进行反向传播。

Result: 在基于状态输入的D4RL基准和基于视觉输入的OGBench任务上的实证结果表明，所提出的噪声空间目标为离线强化学习中训练扩散策略提供了原理性且有效的基础。

Conclusion: 噪声空间Q函数与NSPG为扩散策略与强化学习的整合提供了一条概念清晰、算法高效的路径，在多种离线RL场景中均表现优异。

Abstract: Diffusion policies offer a powerful and expressive parameterization for continuous control. Yet, their integration with reinforcement learning remains conceptually and algorithmically challenging. In this work, we address this gap by introducing a noisy-space action-value (Q-)function that assigns values to diffusion latents through the distribution of executed actions induced by the denoising process. We show that this construction admits a precise semantic interpretation and derive a noisy-space policy gradient (NSPG) that optimizes noisy latents using only clean action-space value estimates. Building on this result, we formulate a KL-regularized policy improvement over noisy latents and show that the resulting objective admits a diffusion-compatible regression form, avoiding backpropagation through the denoising process. Empirical results on state-based D4RL benchmarks and vision-based OGBench tasks demonstrate that the proposed noisy-space objective provides a principled and effective basis for training diffusion policies in offline reinforcement learning. Project webpage: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/NSPG/

</details>
