### [183] [From Synthetic Priors to Model Behavior: Structural Coverage in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06912)
*He Zhao,Ryan Thompson,Daniel M. Steinberg,Ashfaqur Rahman,Edwin V. Bonilla,Cheng Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从分布层面的归因视角出发，研究了表格基础模型（TFMs）中合成预训练数据与其在下游基准任务上表现之间的关系。作者恢复了四个TFM的合成数据生成器，利用结构描述符将合成任务与两个广泛使用的表格基准数据集进行比较，发现不同合成先验在覆盖率与密度上存在显著差异，且合成先验对基准任务的支撑强度与模型相对性能正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通常在大规模程序化生成的合成任务上进行预训练，但这些合成预训练先验对下游评估任务的支持程度尚不清楚。需要一种系统性的诊断方法来理解合成数据生成假设如何影响下游模型行为。

Method: 1）恢复了四个TFM的合成数据生成器或重建其生成过程；2）使用一组共同的结构描述符（涵盖模式、特征分布、依赖结构、响应属性和特征-响应关系）对合成任务和基准数据集进行统一表示；3）在结构描述符空间中，通过结构覆盖率（structural coverage）和归一化密度（normalized density）度量合成先验对基准任务的覆盖广度与重复度；4）检验更强的局部支撑是否对应更好的预测性能。

Result: 不同合成预训练先验之间存在显著差异：某些生成器对基准任务提供持续更广、更密的支撑。此外，合成先验对基准任务的支撑强度通常与模型相对性能的提升相关。

Conclusion: 结构覆盖度可作为描述合成预训练先验、并将其数据生成假设与下游模型行为联系起来的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are commonly pretrained on large collections of procedurally generated synthetic tasks, yet it remains unclear how well these synthetic pretraining priors support the downstream tasks on which the models are evaluated. We study this question from a distribution-level attribution perspective. We recover or reconstruct the synthetic data generators of four TFMs and compare their generated tasks with datasets from two widely used tabular benchmarks. Each dataset is represented by a common set of structural descriptors capturing schema, feature distributions, dependence structure, response properties, and feature--response relationships. In this space, we measure how broadly and repeatedly each synthetic prior reaches benchmark tasks using structural coverage and normalized density, and examine whether stronger local support is associated with better predictive performance. We find substantial differences across synthetic pretraining priors: some generators provide consistently broader and denser support for benchmark tasks than others. Moreover, stronger synthetic-to-benchmark support is generally associated with better relative model performance. These results suggest that structural coverage provides a useful diagnostic for characterizing synthetic pretraining priors and relating their data-generating assumptions to downstream model behavior.

</details>


### [184] [Constrained Online Learning with Noisy Constraint Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06921)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带对抗约束且约束函数受无偏噪声干扰的在线凸优化问题。即使梯度已知，高斯值噪声σ也会导致期望遗憾与硬违规的最大值的下界为Ω(min{σ,1}T/log^7T)，排除了任何联合O(T^{1-δ})的保证。作者提出LEDGER算法，利用非负平衡约束和提前设置权重，在常见可行性和条件有限方差假设下，实现期望遗憾O(√T/V)和预算违规O(√V·T^{3/4}+σ√T)，并给出多种权衡端点（如(√T, T^{3/4})和(T^{2/3},T^{2/3})）。该算法无需Slater条件，且其更新规则也可用于可预测可行比较器的动态遗憾，无需常见可行性或路径长度输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有约束在线凸优化的研究大多假设约束观测无噪声，但实际应用中约束值观测往往受到噪声干扰。本文动机在于揭示：即使梯度完全已知，约束值的高斯噪声本身就从根本上排除了任何子线性（O(T^{1-δ})）的联合遗憾与硬违规保证，因此需要重新定义可行的目标——即探讨预算违规（窗口内累积超支）这一更宽松但实际适用的性能度量。

Method: 首先证明一个严格下界：带高斯值噪声σ的约束在线凸优化中，期望遗憾与期望硬违规的最大值下界为Ω(min{σ,1}T/log^7T)。随后提出LEDGER算法，该算法在非负平衡中跟踪观察到的净消耗量，并在当前反馈噪声之前设定约束权重。LEDGER在常见可行性和条件有限方差反馈下，无需Slater条件即可达到所给预算违规与遗憾保证；同一更新规则也可适应可预测可行比较器路径，给出动态遗憾保证，其预算界取决于最短可行路径（含维度因子）。

Result: LEDGER算法在固定问题参数下，对V∈[T^{-1/2},1]可实现O(√T/V)期望遗憾和O(√V·T^{3/4}+σ√T)期望预算违规。具体端点权衡包括：V=1时(√T, T^{3/4})；V=T^{-1/6}时(T^{2/3},T^{2/3})；预算端V=T^{-1/2}时(T,√T)。动态遗憾版本达到O((1+E[P_T])√T/V)，无需常见可行性或路径长度输入。

Conclusion: 约束值噪声从根本上阻断了传统硬违规的次线性联合保证，但通过预算违规这一测度可以绕开该不可能性。LEDGER证明了无需Slater条件即可建立遗憾与预算违规的双目标权衡，并在动态遗憾场景中同样适用，对噪声环境下的约束在线凸优化研究提供了新的可行框架。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial constraints when constraint values and gradients are observed through unbiased noise. Gaussian value noise of standard deviation $σ$ yields a worst-case lower bound of $Ω(\min\{σ,1\}T/\log^7T)$ on the maximum of expected regret and expected hard violation, even with known gradients. This rules out any jointly $O(T^{1-δ})$ guarantee for fixed $δ>0$ and fixed positive noise level. We therefore study budget violation: the largest cumulative overspend over any window within a fixed horizon. We introduce \LEDGER, which tracks observed net consumption in a nonnegative balance and sets constraint weights before the current feedback noise. Under common feasibility and conditional finite-variance feedback, for fixed problem parameters, \LEDGER\ achieves $O(\sqrt T/V)$ expected regret and $O(\sqrt V\,T^{3/4}+σ\sqrt T)$ expected budget violation for $V\in[T^{-1/2},1]$. This gives the pair $(O(\sqrt T),O(T^{3/4}))$ at $V=1$ and $(O(T^{2/3}),O(T^{2/3}))$ at $V=T^{-1/6}$, without a Slater condition. The budget-focused endpoint $V=T^{-1/2}$ gives $(O(T),O(\sqrt T))$. The same update yields $O((1+E[P_T])\sqrt T/V)$ expected dynamic regret for predictable feasible comparator paths, without common feasibility or path-length input. Its budget bound instead depends on the shortest feasible path, up to a dimension factor.

</details>


### [185] [The Geometry of Refusal: Why Post-Hoc Safety Is Fragile and Pretraining-Time Safety Persists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06934)
*Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为后置安全训练（RLHF/DPO）的脆弱性提供了几何解释：安全更新Δ几乎与能力方向正交，仅在少数高曲率方向上集中分量，形成"薄而尖"的抑制层而非能力擦除。作者进一步追踪训前阶段，通过267个检查点发现安全基质在约6B-60B token间锐变形成；据此提出在整个预训练中持续进行安全共训，使拒绝率在攻击后保持84-91%，侵蚀仅2-14个百分点（对比后置安装的35-38pp），从410M到6.9B规模均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后置安全训练（RLHF、DPO）是主流的模型对齐方式，但其效果极易被越狱、微调攻击和激活空间探针攻破，行为会反复重现。作者旨在为这一系统性的脆弱性提供统一的几何解释，并定位安全信号在预训练过程中的形成时机，从而找到更具鲁棒性的安全注入方式。

Method: 1) 用经验Fisher矩阵刻画能力损失的曲率，将安全更新Δ=W_safe−W_base分解到能力特征空间分析其几何属性；2) 提出"核不可动引理"（kernel-immobility lemma）解释只能掩蔽而无法移除能力的更新为何可被良性微调恢复；3) 对OLMo-2-1B的267个检查点扫描，观察安全基质在预训练中的涌现过程；4) 进行从零开始的端到端对比实验：将安全信号持续共训于预训练全程（以及对标的分窗调度），比较不同规模（410M-6.9B）下的拒绝率与攻击后保持率。

Result: 后置安全更新处于"抑制机制"：Δ与能力方向近似正交，少量子空间内分量集中于少数高曲率方向，形成门控式拒绝层；仅100步良性微调即可在保持能力的同时使Qwen-2.5-7B和Llama-3-8B Instruct的拒绝失效（复制于五个模型家族）。预训练扫描显示安全基质在6B与60B token之间锐变涌现。采用全程安全共训的模型在各规模达到87-98%初始拒绝率，攻击后仍保持84-91%，侵蚀仅2-14个百分点，远优于后置安装的35-38pp，且能力不低于仅语言模型基线；对比的计算匹配分窗调度则无法建立持久拒绝。

Conclusion: 安全信号的持久性（贯穿预训练的持续存在）而非注入的时机，才是攻击鲁棒性的关键。将安全能力共训于预训练全程，可形成与能力基质深度整合的安全表征，从根本上改变"薄而可剥离"的后置安全层这一脆弱模式。

Abstract: Post-hoc safety training (RLHF, DPO) is the dominant way to align large language models, yet jailbreaks (Zou et al., 2023b), fine-tuning attacks (Qi et al., 2024), and activation-space probes (Arditi et al., 2024) keep recovering the behaviors it was meant to remove. We give this fragility one geometric explanation and trace it to when, during pretraining, safety can take hold. We measure the safety update $Δ= W_{\text{safe}} - W_{\text{base}}$ against the curvature of the model's capabilities (the empirical Fisher of a capability loss). Post-hoc safety consistently lands in a suppression regime: $Δ$ is nearly orthogonal to the capability directions, and its small in-subspace part concentrates on a few high-curvature ones. The update is thin but sharp, a refusal gate laid over intact capabilities rather than erasure of them. A kernel-immobility lemma explains why such an update can only mask a capability, not remove it, so a little benign fine-tuning restores it: 100 steps of benign fine-tuning collapse refusal on Qwen-2.5-7B and Llama-3-8B Instruct at preserved capability, a signature that replicates across five model families.
  Following the account into pretraining, a 267-checkpoint sweep of OLMo-2-1B (OLMo et al., 2025) shows the substrate that safety engages emerging in a sharp transition between roughly 6B and 60B pretraining tokens. We then use the account constructively: models trained from scratch with safety co-training spread continuously across pretraining reach 87 to 98% refusal whose post-attack level holds at 84 to 91% at every scale, an erosion of 2 to 14 pp against 35 to 38 pp for post-hoc installs, at capability matched or better than an LM-only baseline and holding from 410M to 6.9B, whereas a compute-matched windowed schedule installs no lasting refusal. Persistence of the safety signal across pretraining, not its timing, is what buys attack robustness.

</details>


### [186] [PCSDiff: Diffusion-Based Bias Correction and Super Resolution Toward Practical Operational Medium-Term Precipitation Forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06942)
*Yuze Sun,Shiyi Wang,Jiancheng Pan,Die Wang,Andreas F. Prein,Wentao Luo,Linhan Jiang,Jie Wu,Quan Zhang,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PCSDiff，一种级联任务解耦的扩散框架，用于10天降水预报的偏差校正和降尺度。通过集成降水强度感知多分支解码器（PIMD）和两阶段条件扩散超分辨率模块，PCSDiff在中国区域将RMSE降低16.1%，ACC提升13.9%，并优于主流深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期降水预报存在系统性偏差、随预见期累积的误差和粗糙的空间分辨率，限制了其在洪水-干旱风险评估中的可靠性。现有AI校正技术缺乏对多日动态偏差演化的专门建模和适当的气象约束，易产生过度平滑的降水结构，且难以满足业务化部署需求。

Method: PCSDiff采用级联任务解耦架构，由两个核心模块组成：（1）降水强度感知多分支解码器（PIMD），利用天气尺度时间特征进行多日动态误差修正；（2）两阶段条件扩散超分辨率模块，用于重建精细降水空间格局。配合流式推理管线实现低延迟滚动预报。

Result: 经全球数据训练并在中国区域与CMA-CRA观测对比评估，PCSDiff在3-10天预见期相对原始ECMWF预报将RMSE降低16.1%，ACC提升13.9%；在通用和极端降水指标上均一致优于主流深度学习基线；流式推理管线实现低延迟的业务化滚动预报。

Conclusion: PCSDiff通过将偏差校正与降尺度任务解耦并分别进行专门建模，有效联合对抗了时间误差漂移和物理合理的局地降水细节重建问题，兼顾了预报精度与业务部署效率，为中期降水预报校正提供了一种可行的深度学习方案。

Abstract: Medium-range precipitation forecasts are impaired by persistent systematic biases, lead-time-dependent error accumulation, and coarse spatial resolution, restricting their reliability for flood-drought risk assessment. Existing AI correction techniques lack dedicated modeling for multi-day dynamic bias evolution and proper meteorological constraints, often generating over-smoothed rainfall structures, and cannot meet operational deployment demands. This work introduces PCSDiff, a cascaded task-decoupled diffusion framework targeting 10-day precipitation bias correction and downscaling. To jointly counteract temporal error drifts and reconstruct physically plausible local precipitation details, PCSDiff integrates the Precipitation Intensity-aware Multi-branch Decoder (PIMD) module for dynamic multi-day error mitigation using synoptic-temporal features, followed by a two-phase conditional diffusion super-resolution module to restore fine-scale precipitation patterns. Evaluated against CMA-CRA observations over China after global-data training, PCSDiff cuts RMSE by 16.1% and lifts ACC by 13.9% relative to raw ECMWF forecasts at 3-10-day lead times, and consistently outperforms mainstream deep-learning baselines on both general and extreme-precipitation metrics. Benefiting from a streaming inference pipeline, our method achieves low-latency rolling forecasting for practical meteorological operations.

</details>


### [187] [Particle Dynamics of Flow Matching and Classifier-Free Guidance from a Stagewise Geometry Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06947)
*Jian-Feng Cai,Zhengyi Su,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了流匹配（Flow Matching）及其分类器自由引导（CFG）的统一分阶段几何理论，从粒子/轨迹级别而非分布级别刻画了无条件流轨迹和CFG轨迹在连续动力学和显式欧拉离散化中如何被逐阶段吸引到全局均值、数据凸包及局部簇邻域，并给出了统一的收缩估计与衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配结合CFG的现有理论理解主要停留在分布层面，但实际采样是沿着个体轨迹进行的，分布级别的保证无法充分刻画轨迹与数据几何的交互以及引导如何重塑这种交互，因此需要在粒子/轨迹层面建立更精细的几何理论。

Method: 建立了连续动力学与显式欧拉离散化下统一的逐阶段（stagewise）几何理论框架，涵盖吸引（attraction）与吸收（absorption）两种机制。通过将时间间隔[0,1]划分为多个阶段，证明无条件流轨迹分别被吸引到全局均值邻域、数据凸包邻域和非凸局部簇邻域，并给出统一的收缩估计；将该框架扩展到CFG情形，分析外推均值、膨胀的条件凸包及目标簇附近的局部几何恢复；进一步推广到一般时间调度a(t)以获得相应的衰减率。

Result: 证明了无条件流轨迹在各阶段满足一个统一的收缩估计，最终阶段距离呈O(1-t)衰减；CFG情形下该结构同样成立，对应外推均值、膨胀条件凸包和恢复后的条件流匹配局部几何；推广到一般时间调度a(t)后，衰减率由O(1-t)变为O(1-a(t))。

Conclusion: 该工作为流匹配和CFG在连续与离散采样下提供了统一的粒子级几何解释，弥补了分布级理论的不足，深化了对轨迹如何与数据几何相互作用以及CFG如何重塑这种几何的理解。

Abstract: Flow matching, together with classifier-free guidance (CFG), is widely used in generative modeling, yet much of the theoretical understanding remains distribution-wise. Since practical sampling follows individual trajectories, distribution-level guarantees alone do not fully capture how trajectories interact with the data geometry or how guidance reshapes it. To overcome this limitation, we establish a unified stagewise geometric theory of attraction and absorption for both continuous dynamics and explicit Euler discretization. Specifically, with $t\in[0,1]$ running from noise to data, we show that unconditional flow trajectories are successively attracted toward a neighborhood of the global mean, the data convex hull, and a neighborhood of a possibly nonconvex local cluster. Across these stages, the corresponding distance satisfies a common contraction estimate, yielding an ${O}(1-t)$ decay of the distance in the final stage. For CFG, the same structure persists with an extrapolated mean, an inflated conditional convex hull, and, near the target cluster, the restored local geometry of conditional flow matching. We further show that a general time schedule $a(t)$ replaces the $O(1-t)$ decay by $O(1-a(t))$. Together, these results provide a unified particle-level geometric account of flow matching and CFG across continuous and discrete sampling.

</details>


### [188] [Steering Interference Reflects the Model's Defaults, Not the Behavior Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06951)
*Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活引导（activation steering）无法实现模块化的行为控制。研究发现，引导实际上将模型拉向其本就偏好的少数行为（如拒绝、谄媚、诗意化），而非产生被引导的行为本身。该结论在24种行为和10个指令微调模型上得到验证，说明干扰由模型自身而非被引导的方向决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导承诺对语言模型行为进行模块化控制——每个行为对应激活空间中的一个方向，添加该方向即可切换对应行为而不影响其他行为。然而实践中该承诺并未实现。作者旨在揭示：当引导产生副作用时，究竟是什么决定了哪些其他行为被改变及改变程度。

Method: 在24种行为和10个指令微调模型上进行系统实验。所有效果通过语言模型评判器从生成文本中读出，而非从探针读出。实验设计包括：（1）无行为内容的方向（仅匹配真实引导的向量大小）作为对照；（2）检查干扰的方向性（单向vs双向）以排除方向重叠假说；（3）留出一种行为，检验从其他行为几何中能否预测该行为的干扰。

Result: 三个关键发现：（1）无行为内容的纯噪声向量与真实引导移动相同的行为且顺序相同，但不产生需特定方向的行为；（2）干扰大多是单向的——引导粗俗使模型有毒，但引导毒性不涉及粗俗，排除了方向重叠的解释；（3）留出某行为后，其余行为的几何几乎无法解释其参与的干扰。所有24种行为均可线性解码，但仅有20种实际改变模型输出。上述效应在全部10个模型上成立，10B参数以下默认吸引最强，各家族最大模型中减弱。

Conclusion: 应将引导理解为模型自行确定端点的扰动，而非直接叠加行为方向的操作。这意味着即便完美地解耦行为方向，也无法使引导实现模块化——因为干扰源于模型自身的内部动力学而非方向间的几何重叠。

Abstract: Activation steering promises modular control of language model behavior: a behavior such as politeness corresponds to a direction in a model's activations, and adding that direction while it generates should switch the behavior on and leave everything else alone. It does not. We ask what decides which other behaviors move, and by how much, and find that it is the model rather than the behavior being steered. A steer relaxes the model toward a small set of behaviors it already favors, chiefly refusal, sycophancy, and poeticism, and that set is much the same whatever is steered.
  Three results across 24 behaviors and ten instruction-tuned models support this, every effect read off the generated text by a language-model judge rather than off a probe. That readout matters: all 24 behaviors are linearly decodable, but only 20 change what the model writes. First, a direction carrying no behavioral content, matched to a real steer only in the size of the vector it adds, moves the same behaviors in the same order as real steers do, while producing none of the behaviors that need a specific direction. Second, most interference runs one way, so it cannot be an overlap between two directions: steering profanity makes the model toxic, while steering toxicity leaves profanity untouched. Third, with a behavior held out entirely, geometry measured on the others explains almost none of the interference it takes part in. The account holds on all ten models, the pull toward defaults strongest below 10B parameters and weakening in each family's largest. Reading a steer as a perturbation whose endpoint the model fixes implies that disentangling behavior directions cannot by itself make steering modular.

</details>


### [189] [MOLE: Detecting Insider Threats in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06966)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOLE是一个包含150个AI代理账户、9个有状态服务、30个工作日、12种威胁和来自4个模型约200亿token的8个语料库的开放基准测试，用于评估防御者能否在有限审查预算下检测前沿账户中的AI代理恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未测试防御者能否在有限审查预算和常规工作中检测由模型错位、提示注入或操作员误用导致的AI代理恶意行为（如窃取模型权重、投毒训练数据或削弱发布门禁）。

Method: 构建MOLE基准：150个AI运营账户共享9个有状态服务运行30个工作日，包含12种威胁和来自4个模型的8个语料库（约200亿token）。评估39个代理模型，并将40个监控器在不同语料库生成器、可观测性级别和威胁下进行对比；同时支持基准引导的监控器开发与选择性监控策略。

Result: 39个代理模型中72%能完成大多数指定的有害目标，且代理拒绝行为不能预测目标完成率。即使在单日审计事件对比中最好的监控器也会漏掉近一半的已完成的危害。基准引导搜索可将中档监控器提升49-64%，选择性使用更强监控器相比对每个账户日都应用监控器，在可比成本下将预算AUC提高10%。

Conclusion: MOLE验证了当前监控体系在检测AI代理恶意活动方面存在显著不足，同时证明了基准引导的监控器开发和选择性监控策略能有效提升检测效率，为前沿账户安全防御提供了系统化的评估与优化平台。

Abstract: Model misalignment, prompt injection, or operator misuse could lead AI agents operating frontier-lab accounts to exfiltrate model weights, poison training data, or weaken release gates. Existing benchmarks do not test whether defenders can detect this activity among routine work under a limited review budget. We introduce MOLE, an open benchmark of 150 AI-operated accounts sharing 9 stateful services over 30 workdays, with 12 threats and 8 corpora from four models totaling roughly 20 billion tokens. Of 39 agent models, 72% complete most assigned harmful objectives and agent refusal does not predict completion. MOLE enables comparison of 40 monitors across corpus generators, observability levels, and threats; even the best evaluated monitor in our single-day audit-event comparison misses nearly half of completed harm. MOLE also enables monitor development: benchmark-guided search improves a mid-tier monitor by 49-64%, while selective use of a stronger monitor improves budget-AUC by 10% over applying it to every account-day at comparable modeled cost.

</details>


### [190] [HealthLoopQA: A Context-Aware Question Answering Benchmark for Interpreting Wearable Monitoring Data in Diabetes Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06976)
*Yuchen Niu,Yanan Ma,Srinivasan Nandakumar,Maolin Chen,Viktor Schlegel,Kexin Wei,Ling Cheng,Anna Bird,Anil Anthony Bharath,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HealthLoopQA，一个用于评估大语言模型在连续糖尿病监测数据上推理能力的诊断基准，包含127个任务和1500多个QA实例，并引入故障注入仿真测试平台来评估模型的安全性意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗可穿戴设备日益融入日常慢性病管理，LLMs有望将流式生理数据转化为个性化健康洞察，但现有医疗可穿戴QA基准主要评估短期分类或统计摘要，忽略了真实部署中的长期模式、治疗和行为背景以及潜在系统故障，缺乏对LLM多样化监测推理能力的系统评估。

Method: 提出HealthLoopQA基准：基于包含11种原子推理能力的新型分类体系，构建127个任务和超过1,500个QA实例，涵盖流程挖掘、异常检测和30天视野的预测；同时辅以故障注入仿真测试平台（模拟多种设备故障和网络物理攻击）生成生理上可信的风险场景，用于系统性评估安全性意识。

Result: 在提示和智能体框架下评估了SOTA LLMs，揭示其在复杂时间模式挖掘中的严重局限；并识别出长上下文提示下的"上下文懒惰"（In-context Laziness）这一更广泛现象。

Conclusion: HealthLoopQA揭示了当前LLMs在长视野医学推理中的关键开放挑战，凸显了部署LLM进行严格长期医疗推理时仍需解决的重大问题。

Abstract: As medical wearables become integrated into daily chronic disease care, effectively interpreting longitudinal monitoring data is essential for patients and clinicians to understand health trends, detect safety-critical events, and make informed decisions. While large language models (LLMs) show promise for transforming this streaming physiological data into personalized health insights, evaluating their reasoning capability and analytical rigor in diverse monitoring tasks remains a fundamental challenge. Existing medical wearable question answering (QA) benchmarks primarily assess short-horizon classification or statistical summaries, largely ignoring the long-term patterns, therapeutic and behavioural contexts, and potential system failures inherent in real-world deployments. To address this, we introduce HealthLoopQA, a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over continuous diabetes monitoring data. Grounded in a novel taxonomy of eleven atomic reasoning abilities, HealthLoopQA comprises 127 tasks and over 1,500 QA instances spanning process mining, anomaly detection, and prediction over 30-day horizons. To systematically evaluate safety awareness, we complement real-world datasets with a fault-injected simulation testbed modeling diverse device malfunctions and cyber-physical attacks to generate physiologically plausible hazard scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs across prompting and agentic frameworks reveals severe limitations in complex temporal pattern mining. Furthermore, we identify a broader phenomenon of In-context Laziness under long-context prompting, highlighting critical open challenges in deploying LLMs for rigorous long-horizon medical reasoning.

</details>


### [191] [AF-Mamba: Efficient Long-Term Signal Modeling for Early Prediction of Atrial Fibrillation Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06984)
*Yongbin Lee,Ki H. Chon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AF-Mamba，一种基于TCN与Mamba混合架构的深度学习模型，利用长时程RR间期数据提前一小时预测房颤（AF）发作。在受试者5折交叉验证中达到AUROC 0.974，跨数据集中AUROC 0.897，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时展现出优于Transformer和RNN的效率优势，适用于实时连续动态监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房颤是最常见的心律失常，与卒中及心衰风险相关。可穿戴心电监测设备的普及使得院外连续心律评估成为可能，若能在房颤发作前提前预知则可为临床评估和患者管理争取宝贵的时间窗口。然而，长时程RR间期序列的建模面临Transformer二次复杂度与RNN梯度消失问题的挑战，亟需兼具长程建模能力与高效训练推理的架构。

Method: 提出AF-Mamba混合深度学习架构：采用时间卷积网络（TCN）对RR间期序列进行局部特征编码，并集成Mamba（选择状态空间模型）进行长程序列建模。TCN负责高效提取局部时序模式，Mamba克服了Transformer的二次计算复杂度与RNN的梯度消失问题，实现在一小时输入窗口上的高效训练与推理。采用受试者维度5折交叉验证及配对跨数据集留出法评估泛化能力。

Result: 在受试者5折交叉验证中，模型取得灵敏度0.889、特异度0.943、F1分数0.813、AUROC 0.974、AUPRC 0.933。跨数据集中保持判别性能，平均AUROC为0.897。与SOTA房颤预测模型及通用时间序列模型对比，AF-Mamba在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，对长RRI序列提供了更优的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AF-Mamba能够提前一小时准确预测房颤发作，并在多个未见数据集上保持稳健的泛化性能。该混合架构兼顾了预测精度与计算效率，展现出用于实时连续动态心电监测和房颤风险早期预警的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most common cardiac arrhythmia and is associated with increased risks of stroke and heart failure. The growing availability of wearable and portable ECG monitoring enables continuous assessment of cardiac rhythm outside clinical settings. Predicting AF before its onset could provide additional lead time for timely clinical assessment and potentially improve the management of patients at risk of AF-related complications. This study focuses on predicting AF onset one hour in advance using long-term RR intervals (RRIs). To address this challenge, we propose a deep learning architecture that integrates temporal convolutional networks (TCNs) for local features encoding with Mamba, a selective state-space model capable of long-range sequence modeling. This hybrid TCN-Mamba design enables efficient training and inference on one-hour input windows, overcoming limitations of Transformers' quadratic scaling and recurrent networks' vanishing gradients. In subject-wise 5-fold testing, the proposed model achieved a sensitivity of 0.889, specificity of 0.943, F1-score of 0.813, AUROC of 0.974, and AUPRC of 0.933. In paired cross-dataset holdout evaluation, AF-Mamba maintained discriminative performance across unseen AF and NSR datasets, achieving a mean AUROC of 0.897. Compared against state-of-the-art AF prediction models and general time-series models, AF-Mamba achieved competitive predictive performance while providing a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off for long RRI sequences. These findings demonstrate the potential of AF-Mamba for accurate AF prediction one hour in advance and real-time continuous ambulatory monitoring.

</details>


### [192] [Continual Learning Mechanisms Compose for Long-Horizon Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06986)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Alvin Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了长时程记忆（long-horizon memorization）设置下语言模型持续学习的问题，提出组合互补的反遗忘机制（数据锚、函数锚、权重锚 + 低秩分配规则）来缓解灾难性遗忘，在100个任务的数据集上将最终记忆保留从1.2%（朴素顺序微调）提升至34.9%，实现28倍改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型需要随时间内化新到达的信息并在多次后续更新中保留它们，但持续监督微调会导致灾难性遗忘。现有单个持续学习机制在此长时间跨度下都无法保持较强记忆保持率，因此需要探索组合多种机制以解决互补的遗忘来源。

Method: 提出两个设计维度分类组合策略：(1) 锚定机制（数据锚、函数锚、权重锚），指定每次更新应保留的先前信息；(2) 低秩分配规则，决定连续更新在何处被保留。构建三个各含100个任务的不同记忆数据集，引入任务级逐次减半搜索组合设计空间，并通过因子实验测量单个效应与交互效应。最佳方法为三种锚定机制与合并LoRA的组合。

Result: 最佳方法（三种锚与合并LoRA组合）在三个数据集上均排名前3，将平均最终记忆保持率从朴素顺序微调的1.2%提升至34.9%，实现28倍改进。数据锚和合并LoRA提供最大的平均增益，并在三个数据集上表现出超加性交互效应。

Conclusion: 组合互补的持续性学习机制能够显著提升长时程记忆表现，超越任何单一机制的效果，表明针对不同遗忘来源设计的机制的协同组合是解决长时间跨度的灾难性遗忘的有效策略。

Abstract: Language models may need to internalize information that arrives over time and retain it through many subsequent updates. To study this challenge, we introduce long-horizon memorization, a setting in which a model learns 100 query-answer tasks through continual supervised fine-tuning without retaining earlier training examples or receiving task identifiers at inference. Sequential updates cause catastrophic forgetting, and no single continual learning mechanism we evaluate maintains strong retention at this horizon. We hypothesize that mechanisms addressing complementary sources of forgetting will be more effective when composed. We organize these compositions along two design dimensions. Data, function, and weight anchors specify what prior information each update should preserve, while low-rank allocation rules determine where successive updates are retained. To test this hypothesis systematically, we construct three distinct 100-task memorization datasets. We introduce task-level successive halving to search the combinatorial design space and use a factorial experiment to measure individual and interaction effects. Our best method combines all three anchors with merged LoRA, ranks among the top 3 methods in all datasets, and raises average final retention from 1.2% under naive sequential fine-tuning to 34.9%, a 28-fold improvement. The data anchor and merged LoRA provide the largest average gains and interact super-additively on all three datasets. Together, these results show that composing complementary mechanisms substantially improves long-horizon memorization beyond what any individual mechanism achieves.

</details>


### [193] [NeuCME: Toward Dynamic Multimodal Continual Learning via Neural Combinatorics of Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07009)
*Kai Guo,Chuanbin Liu,Peng Hu,Hao Wang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态多模态持续学习（NeuCME）框架，解决任务间模态集合可能变化的新问题，通过模态组合重放、多门控专家混合和任务相关性蒸馏三大组件，在真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法假设每个任务的模态集合是预先定义且固定的，但现实场景中不同任务涉及的模态种类可能动态变化。这种更现实的学习设置，即"动态多模态持续学习"，此前未被充分研究，需要新方法解决随之而来的时空灾难性遗忘和自适应多模态融合两大挑战。

Method: 提出NeuCME框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 模态组合重放（modality-combinational rehearsal），用于缓解时空灾难性遗忘；(2) 多门控混合专家（multi-gated mixture-of-experts），实现不同任务间的自适应知识共享与多模态融合；(3) 任务相关性引导蒸馏（task relevance-guided distillation），有效整合跨任务知识。此外还提出评估指标量化任务序列的动态程度，并构建包含不同动态程度基准数据集。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，NeuCME在性能上显著优于现有最先进方法。研究还构建了具有不同动态程度基准，验证了框架在不同场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NeuCME有效解决了动态多模态持续学习中模态集合可变带来的挑战，为更具人类智能的持续学习代理提供了可行方案，为后续研究奠定了基准和评估基础。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning has recently shown great potential for developing agents with human-like intelligence by continuously learning new tasks across multiple modalities. However, existing methods typically assume that the set of modalities per task is predefined and fixed. In this paper, we investigate a more realistic learning setting, referred to as dynamic multimodal continual learning, in which the set of modalities may vary across tasks rather than remaining fixed. This setting involves two primary challenges: (i) spatio-temporal catastrophic forgetting and (ii) adaptive multimodal fusion. To address these challenges, we propose NeuCME (as shorthand for \textbf{Neu}ral \textbf{C}ombinatorics of \textbf{M}ultiple \textbf{E}xperts), a novel framework designed to effectively learn and integrate knowledge across tasks with varying modalities. The proposed NeuCME model comprises three key components, namely modality-combinational rehearsal, multi-gated mixture-of-experts, and task relevance-guided distillation. Furthermore, we formulate an evaluation metric to quantify the dynamism of task sequences and then set up a comprehensive benchmark with different degrees of dynamism. Extensive experiments using four real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed NeuCME outperforms state-of-the-art methods markedly.

</details>


### [194] [HyperTransfer: Understanding the Equivalence between Base Optimizer and Hyperball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07017)
*Jinghui Yuan,Hongtao Zhang,Jade Zou,Tianyu Li,Wenjie Zhou,Tianyu He,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明超球优化器与传统基础优化器在尺度不变网络中动态等价，并提出HyperTransfer框架，仅用目标优化器的初始化和学习率调度即可复现其动态，无需实际运行目标优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超球优化器（仅更新参数方向而非范数）与常规基础优化器（同时更新范数和方向）在几何上看似截然不同，作者希望探究两者之间是否存在更深层的动态等价性，并利用这种等价性实现优化器之间的转换。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明超球优化器与基础优化器在尺度不变网络中的动态等价性；基于此提出HyperTransfer框架，用目标优化器的初始化和学习率调度构造出能复现其动态的超球优化器；进一步推导逆映射，并将框架扩展到非尺度不变网络。

Result: 实验表明HyperTransfer及其逆映射产生的损失轨迹与目标优化器的轨迹几乎完全一致，证明超球优化器的动态主要由其诱导的有效学习率调度和优化器状态决定。

Conclusion: 超球优化与常规优化之间并非本质不同的范式，而是可以在相应条件下互相转换的；两者的动态等价性由有效学习率调度和优化器状态主导。

Abstract: Hyperball optimizers constrain parameter norms and update only their directions, establishing a distinct paradigm for neural network optimization. Although this geometry appears fundamentally different from that of conventional Base Optimizers, which update both parameter norms and directions, we show that the two paradigms are dynamically equivalent for scale-invariant networks. Building on this equivalence, we propose HyperTransfer, which constructs a Hyperball optimizer that reproduces the dynamics of a target Base Optimizer using only its initialization and learning-rate schedule, without running the target optimizer itself. We further derive the inverse mapping and extend the framework to non-scale-invariant networks. Experiments show that both HyperTransfer and the inverse mapping produce loss trajectories nearly identical to those of their targets, suggesting that Hyperball dynamics are governed primarily by the induced effective learning-rate schedule and optimizer state.

</details>


### [195] [Efficient Learning and Symmetry Discovery under Exact Invariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07031)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Patrick Jaillet,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了群不变性学习中两个悬而未决的计算问题：(1) 首次提出统一适用于有限群和无限群的精确群不变学习的多项式时间算法，其运行时间与数据维度和样本量成多项式关系且与群无关；(2) 在对称性未知的设定下，证明有限群子群格中的精确对称性可在多项式时间内被识别并用于学习，在回归问题上与已知对称性设定达到相同的极小极大最优样本复杂度。分析工具涉及随机Cayley图和扩张图理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群不变性学习是许多科学与几何学习问题中的核心，但其计算基础仍然欠理解。即使对经典的有监督回归设定，能否高效计算严格不变于给定群作用的回归函数此前仍不清楚。近年研究仅证明了有限且已知群可在多项式时间内强制精确不变性，而无限群和未知对称性的情形仍然开放，本文旨在解决这两个挑战。

Method: (1) 针对精确群不变学习：提出首个统一适用于有限与无限群的多项式时间算法，其运行时间仅依赖于数据维度和样本量，与群本身无关，并给出强泛化保证。(2) 针对未知对称性的对称性发现：以有限群的子群格为研究对象，设计多项式时间算法，能够从数据中精确识别底层对称性并利用其进行学习；算法在有限维特征空间回归问题上可证明地恢复底层对称性，且样本复杂度匹配已知对称性设定下的极小极大下界。理论分析使用随机Cayley图与扩张图理论工具。

Result: (1) 算法运行时间关于数据维度和样本量为多项式，且不依赖于群的大小或类型，对有限群和无限群均适用，并提供强泛化保证，部分回答了文献中的开放问题。(2) 在对称性发现设定中，算法在多项式时间内精确恢复未知的子群对称性，达到与已知对称性设定一致的极小极大最优样本复杂度，运行时间关于数据维度和样本量成多项式。

Conclusion: 本文为群不变性学习的计算基础提供了理论解释，从计算角度诠释了几何机器学习中不变/等变方法的经验成功。就作者所知，这些结果为精确群不变学习和对称性发现的效率提供了首个系统性的多项式时间保证，所引入的随机Cayley图与扩张图工具也可能在其他问题上具有独立价值。

Abstract: Learning with group invariances is central to many scientific and geometric learning problems, yet its computational foundations remain poorly understood. Even for classical supervised regression settings, it has been unclear whether one can efficiently compute a regression function that is exactly invariant to a given group action. Recent work showed that exact invariance can be enforced in polynomial time when the underlying group is finite and known, but left open the cases of infinite groups and unknown symmetries. In this paper, we resolve both challenges. First, we present the first polynomial-time algorithm for learning with exact group invariances that applies uniformly to finite and infinite groups. The runtime is polynomial in the data dimension and sample size, and independent of the group, while achieving strong generalization guarantees. This provides a computational explanation for the empirical success of invariant and equivariant methods in geometric machine learning and partially answers a recent open question in the literature. Second, we study learning in the symmetry discovery setting, where the invariance group is unknown. Focusing on the subgroup lattice of a finite group, we show that exact symmetries can be identified from data and exploited for learning in polynomial time. For regression over finite-dimensional feature spaces, our algorithm provably recovers the underlying symmetry, matches the minimax-optimal sample complexity of the known-symmetry setting, and runs in time polynomial in the data dimension and sample size. Our analysis relies on tools from random Cayley graphs and expander theory, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [196] [Disentangling Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07037)
*Takeru Hiramatsu,Kyohei Atarashi,Koh Takeuchi,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Steering Vector Dissection框架，通过在实例级steering vectors上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），将LLM激活干预中的复合steering方向显式分解为语义一致且彼此可区分的独立基向量，实现对模型行为的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统activation steering方法（如difference-in-means）生成的steering向量往往将多个语义和风格概念纠缠在单一复合方向上，导致干预效果不可预测。核心目标是解耦该复合方向，将其分解为构成性概念，以实现更精准的行为控制。

Method: 配对正负激活并取差值，生成一组实例级steering向量；随后在这些实例级向量上直接训练一个专门的稀疏自编码器（SAE），利用SAE的稀疏特性将复合方向分离为语义一致且独立的基向量。

Result: 在两个数据集、两个模型和两种干预深度的定量评估中，该方法产生一组语义一致的基向量，其steering效果彼此可区分；且该解耦能力使模型行为控制达到精确水平。

Conclusion: 通过SAE在实例级steering向量上学习稀疏分解，能够有效解耦activation steering中的复合语义方向，从而提升LLM行为干预的可控性和可解释性。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight, inference-time approach to control the behavior of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, traditional steering vectors used to intervene in LLMs' activations, such as those derived from the difference-in-means method, tend to entangle multiple semantic and stylistic concepts into a single composite direction, leading to unpredictable steering effects. Our core objective is to disentangle this composite direction into its constituent concepts. To this end, we propose Steering Vector Dissection, a framework to explicitly isolate individual and semantically consistent features from these composite directions. Specifically, we pair positive and negative activations and take their differences to generate a set of instance-level steering vectors, and train a dedicated Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) directly on them. Quantitative evaluations across two datasets, two models, and two intervention depths show that our method yields a set of semantically consistent basis vectors whose steering effects are mutually distinguishable. Furthermore, we show that this disentanglement enables precise control over model behaviors.

</details>


### [197] [AI and TCAD for Inverse Design and Defect Discovery: From Simple Machine Learning to LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07046)
*Hiu Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将TCAD仿真与机器学习相结合（仿真增强型ML），以克服半导体器件设计与缺陷发现中数据稀缺和维度灾难的挑战，展示了仅需最少领域专业知识即可构建达到器件工程师水平的模型，并展望了LLM和MLLM在自动器件设计与缺陷发现中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在工程领域产生了革命性影响，但在半导体器件设计和缺陷发现中的应用仍受限于数据稀缺和高维问题。传统方法高度依赖经验丰富的TCAD工程师，亟需探索如何利用仿真生成数据驱动ML模型，以降低专业壁垒、提升设计效率。

Method: 利用TCAD生成精确合成数据用于ML训练（仿真增强型ML），采用基于自编码器的机器学习模型与噪声工程来学习潜在物理规律，并实现从仿真数据到实验数据的无缝迁移。通过多个案例（如PiN二极管层厚反演、Ga2O3肖特基二极管掺杂与功函数变化反算、FinFET IV/CV预测、晶体管图像与IV曲线映射、TCAD参数自动校准）逐步展示建模流程，并引入LLM用于TCAD命令文件生成，提出MLLM用于自动器件设计。

Result: 证明在最少领域专业知识条件下，所构建的ML模型在特定任务上性能可媲美器件工程师；自编码器结合噪声工程能有效学习潜在物理；模型可无缝应用于实验数据。LLM在TCAD命令文件创建中展现出应用潜力，MLLM被认为是实现完全自动化器件设计与缺陷发现的关键方向。

Conclusion: TCAD仿真增强的ML方法为克服半导体领域数据瓶颈提供了可行路径，但若要充分释放AI潜力，还需依赖LLM和MLLM赋能自动化设计与缺陷发现，未来人机协同将在半导体工程中扮演更加核心的角色。

Abstract: AI has revolutionized various engineering domains, but its impact on semiconductor device design and defect discovery is still limited, due to limited data and the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we will discuss our work on using the Technology Computer-Aided-Design (TCAD) to generate precise data needed for machine learning (ML) to enable simulation-augmented ML. We demonstrate that with minimal domain expertise, it is possible to create a machine that performs as well as a device engineer on a specific task. We will show that auto-encoder-based machine learning models and noise engineering applied to TCAD data are effective at learning latent physics, and that the models can be seamlessly applied to experimental data. We will demonstrate how to build a device-engineer-level model step by step through various examples, including using only non-destructive electrical data to inverse-engineer the PiN diode layer thickness variations, the Ga2O3 Schottky diode doping and anode workfunction variations, and the transistor contact resistance in an inverter. Examples also include the generation of a FinFET IV/CV prediction model, the mapping between transistor images and IV curves, and the automatic calibration of TCAD parameters for a Ga2O3 Schottky diode, which can only be handled well by experienced TCAD engineers. Finally, to fully realize the potential of AI, large language models (LLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) are believed to be necessary. We will discuss the application of LLMs to TCAD command file creation and our vision for MLLMs in automated device design and defect discovery.

</details>


### [198] [TrojanWorld: Backdooring World-Model Agents via Imagination Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07051)
*Wenkai Huang,Siyuan Liang,Gaolei Li,Yiming Li,Tianhao Peng,Jianhua Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrojanWorld是一个针对世界模型智能体的后门攻击框架，通过物理物体作为触发器，在部署时利用智能体原生观测管道激活攻击，操纵其内部想象过程，诱导其执行攻击者指定的行为。实验表明该框架在多种世界模型系统上实现低至0.026的目标动作偏差，同时保留至少98.8%的正常性能，且触发器移除后攻击效果仍能持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为基于模型的强化学习智能体的预测核心，其预训练模型因训练成本高昂而被广泛分发和复用，这引入了模型供应链安全威胁。虽然后门攻击为此类供应链提供了隐蔽的利用途径，但针对交互式世界模型智能体的后门攻击威胁尚未被充分探索。

Method: TrojanWorld框架包含三个核心机制：(1) Decision-Reflective Induction——利用决策反馈将触发器条件下的想象引导至攻击者指定动作；(2) Clean Behavior Anchoring——保持无触发器条件下的预测和行为保真度以实现隐蔽性；(3) Causal Propagation——在触发器消失后仍沿轨迹维持已诱导的行为偏好。攻击通过场景中的物理物体作为触发器，经由智能体原生观测管线激活，无需数字篡改观测流，形成从物理感知、想象污染到恶意动作选择的端到端攻击链。

Result: 在TD-MPC2、DreamerV3和R2-Dreamer系统上，覆盖DeepMind Control、MetaWorld、MyoSuite和RoboDesk基准的实验中，触发器激活时目标动作偏差低至0.026，同时保留至少98.8%的干净性能。即使触发器被移除，被攻击的智能体仍可能困在诱导行为轨迹中持续执行攻击者指定的动作。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了世界模型智能体在模型供应链中所面临的新兴后门安全威胁，证明攻击可以通过物理触发器内生式地污染智能体的想象过程，且能在触发器消失后保持持久的行为控制，为相关防御研究提供了重要警示。

Abstract: World models increasingly serve as the predictive core of model-based reinforcement learning agents, enabling them to simulate future dynamics and reason over imagined trajectories before acting. Their substantial training demands make pretrained world models attractive for distribution and reuse, exposing downstream systems to model supply chain threats. Backdoor attacks offer a targeted and stealthy means of exploiting such supply chains, yet their threat to interactive world-model agents remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we present TrojanWorld, a backdoor framework for world-model agents that induces attacker-specified behavior by steering internal imagination. A physical object placed in the scene acts as the trigger, enabling deployment-time activation through the agent's native observation pipeline without digitally manipulating the observation stream. To achieve effective, stealthy, and persistent control, TrojanWorld combines Decision-Reflective Induction to steer trigger-conditioned imagination toward attacker-specified actions using decision feedback, Clean Behavior Anchoring to preserve trigger-free predictive and behavioral fidelity, and Causal Propagation to sustain the induced preference along subsequent trajectories after the trigger disappears. Together, these mechanisms establish an end-to-end attack chain from physical perception through corrupted imagination to malicious action selection. Experiments with the TD-MPC2, DreamerV3, and R2-Dreamer systems across the DeepMind Control, MetaWorld, MyoSuite, and RoboDesk benchmarks show that under trigger activation, TrojanWorld achieves a target-action deviation as low as 0.026 while retaining at least 98.8% of the corresponding clean performance. Even after trigger removal, the compromised agent can remain trapped in the induced behavioral trajectory, continuing to execute attacker-specified actions.

</details>


### [199] [PhysSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07061)
*Nandita N. Patil,Eshwar R. A.,Gajanan V. Honnavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysSAE框架，通过在PINN倒数第二层激活上训练过完备稀疏自编码器（SAE），并对字典原子进行直接因果干预（h_cf = h - αz_k d_k），揭示了PINN内部隐藏层编码了具有稀疏、物理结构化特性的潜在表征，且这些表征可通过事后因果分析被识别和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN虽然将PDE残差嵌入神经网络训练中，但其内部表征是不透明的——我们不清楚隐藏层编码了哪些物理特征，也不确定这些特征是否具有局部的因果作用。作者希望打开PINN的黑箱，实现机制可解释性。

Method: PhysSAE框架：(1) 在PINN倒数第二层激活上训练过完备稀疏自编码器（SAE）；(2) 通过直接因果干预评估字典原子，即h_cf = h - αz_k d_k，完全绕过SAE解码器对原始冻结隐藏状态进行消融。在6类PDE族上，采用3个PINN种子和3个SAE种子进行系统实验。

Result: (i) SAE原子与独立定义的物理可观测量高度对齐（最大Pearson |r|=0.951，远超置换零假设）；(ii) 顶层对齐原子的消融因果足迹比PCA/ICA干预在空间上集中1.2-4.2倍；(iii) 顶层对齐原子在结构化物理概念的因果定位上显著优于匹配的随机对照（ESF_80优势0.04-0.44）；双原子双边表征相比单原子将概念回归R²提升ΔR²=0.05-0.15，而随机配对最高可降低0.60。

Conclusion: PINNs会发展出稀疏且具有物理结构的潜在表征，这些表征可以被识别并通过事后因果干预进行查询。该工作为可解释性驱动的科学机器学习开辟了道路。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) embed PDE residuals into neural network training, but their internal representations remain opaque: it is unknown what physical features their hidden layers encode or whether those features have a localized causal role. We present PhysSAE, a mechanistic interpretability framework that trains overcomplete sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on PINN penultimate-layer activations and evaluates dictionary atoms through direct causal intervention in the original frozen hidden state: $h_{\mathrm{cf}} = h - αz_k d_k$, bypassing the SAE decoder entirely. Across six PDE families, with 3 PINN seeds and 3 SAE seeds each---we show that (i) Our discovered SAE atoms align with independently-defined physical observables (max Pearson $|r|=0.951$, always $\gg$ permutation null), (ii) the causal footprint of top-aligned atom ablation is 1.2--4.2$\times$ more spatially concentrated canonical than PCA or ICA interventions, and (iii) top-aligned atoms outperform matched random controls on causal localization for structured physical concepts (ESF$_{80}$ advantage 0.04-0.44). Two-atom bilateral representations improve concept regression R$^2$ by $ΔR^2\!=\!0.05\text{-}0.15$ over single atoms, while random pairs decrease it by up to 0.60. These results demonstrate that PINNs develop sparse, physically structured latent representations that can be identified and causally interrogated post-hoc, opening a path toward interpretability-aware scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [200] [Trust-But-Verify: Poisoning-Resilient Locally Private Graph Learning Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07063)
*Longzhu He,Li Sun,Hao Peng,Ruijie Wang,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VERITAS，一种基于"信任但验证"范式的抗投毒本地私有图学习协议。通过引入分级信任的验证列表，在用户侧联合私有化节点特征和图结构，同时在服务器侧利用双边证明不对称性识别和剪除恶意节点，有效防御数据投毒攻击并提升下游图学习效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于局部差分隐私（LDP）的图学习协议已成为去中心化图学习的重要范式，但由于其开放的参与特性，极易遭受数据投毒攻击——攻击者注入精心构造的恶意节点以破坏邻域聚合并降低下游效用。尽管该威胁严重，有效的防御机制在该场景下仍大量空白。

Method: VERITAS包含四个协同阶段：(1)本地数据扰动——在用户侧联合私有化节点特征和图结构；(2)证明驱动的恶意节点剪除——利用双边证明不对称性识别并移除恶意节点；(3)通过双去噪实现效用恢复；(4)鲁棒的私有图学习。核心机制是引入编码分级同伴信任水平的验证列表，实现"信任但验证"。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集上，跨多种LDP机制和GNN架构的大量实验表明，VERITAS能有效抵御数据投毒攻击，并在严格的隐私保证下显著提升下游图学习效用。

Conclusion: VERITAS填补了局部私有图学习场景下数据投毒防御的研究空白，通过信任但验证的设计范式，在隐私保护与投毒鲁棒性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Built upon local differential privacy (LDP), locally private graph learning protocols have emerged as an important paradigm for decentralized graph learning, balancing privacy protection and learning utility. Under such protocols, each user locally perturbs their node features and adjacency information before transmission, ensuring formal privacy guarantees without original data leaving the device. However, the inherently open participation nature renders these protocols critically vulnerable to data poisoning attacks, where adversaries inject carefully crafted malicious nodes to corrupt neighborhood aggregation and degrade downstream utility. Despite the severity of this threat, effective defenses in this setting remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we propose VERITAS, a poisoning-resilient locally private graph learning protocol built on a trust-but-verify paradigm. By introducing a verification list encoding graded peer trust levels, VERITAS jointly privatizes node features and graph structure on the user side, while exploiting bilateral attestation asymmetry on the server side to identify and prune malicious nodes. Concretely, VERITAS comprises four synergistic stages: (1) local data perturbation, (2) attestation-driven malicious node pruning, (3) utility restoration via dual denoising, and (4) robust private graph learning. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmark datasets across multiple LDP mechanisms and GNN architectures demonstrate that VERITAS effectively defends against data poisoning attacks and significantly improves downstream graph learning utility under rigorous privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [201] [Conditioned Initialization for Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07086)
*Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"条件化初始化"(conditioned initialization)方案，通过改善注意力层的谱性质来优化Transformer中Q、K、V权重矩阵的初始化，从而减少注意力Jacobian的条件数，实现更稳定的优化、更快的收敛和更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在视觉、语言等领域占据主导地位，但注意力层中查询、键、值矩阵的初始化方式鲜受关注。现有实践依赖随机初始化或模仿性初始化(如mimetic initialization、教师模型权重选择)，缺乏理论基础。作者认为初始化会引入影响训练动态的优化偏差，初始化这一被忽视的环节值得深入研究。

Method: 提出条件化初始化(conditioned initialization)，一种原理性的注意力权重初始化方案，旨在改善注意力层的谱性质。理论上证明该方法能够降低注意力Jacobian的条件数，从而促进更稳定的优化。

Result: 实验表明条件化初始化在多种应用中加速收敛并提升泛化性能，且该方法实现简单，能无缝集成到广泛的Transformer架构中。

Conclusion: 条件化(conditioning)是提升Transformer性能的一个关键但尚未被充分探索的方向，条件化初始化作为一种简单有效的初始化策略，有望成为改善Transformer训练动态的重要工具。

Abstract: Transformers are a dominant architecture in modern machine learning, powering applications across vision, language, and beyond. At the core of their success lies the attention layer, where the query, key, and value matrices determine how token dependencies are captured. While considerable work has focused on scaling and optimizing Transformers, comparatively little attention has been paid to how the weights of the queries, keys and values are initialized. Common practice relies on random initialization or alternatives such as mimetic initialization, which imitates weight patterns from converged models, and weight selection, which transfers weights from a teacher model. In this paper, we argue that initialization can introduce an optimization bias that fundamentally shapes training dynamics. We propose conditioned initialization, a principled scheme that initializes attention weights to improve the spectral properties of the attention layer. Theoretically, we show that conditioned initialization can potentially reduce the condition number of the attention Jacobian, leading to more stable optimization. Empirically, it accelerates convergence and improves generalization across diverse applications, highlighting conditioning as a critical yet underexplored area for advancing Transformer performance. Importantly, conditioned initialization is simple to apply and integrates seamlessly into a wide range of Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [202] [Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Transformer for Credit Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07100)
*Qinwen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出THGT-FD，一种用于信用卡欺诈检测的时间异构图Transformer模型。每个交易由一个交易token和六类关系token表示，并融入Time2Vec时间编码，通过Transformer在单笔交易内学习token间交互以输出欺诈概率。在IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection数据集15万条交易样本上，测试集AUC-ROC为0.8536，平均精确率为0.4164，Recall@5%为0.4708，略低于梯度提升基线（AUC-ROC 0.8722），表明关系token对欺诈风险排序有增益但模型尚缺实体级历史聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈检测通常依赖表格特征，但交易中的重复属性（如商户、设备等）可提供有价值的关系信号，现有方法未能充分利用这类结构化关系信息。

Method: 构建时间异构图Transformer：每笔交易由一个交易token和六种关系token表示，引入Time2Vec对交易表示进行时间编码，利用Transformer学习单笔交易内部各token之间的交互，最终输出欺诈概率。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection数据集抽取的150,000条按时间划分的交易上，测试集AUC-ROC为0.8536，平均精确率0.4164，Recall@5%为0.4708；类加权直方图梯度提升基线AUC-ROC为0.8722。

Conclusion: 关系token为欺诈风险排序提供了有用信息，但当前模型性能仍略低于梯度提升基线，且尚未整合实体级历史聚合，存在进一步提升空间。

Abstract: Credit card fraud detection typically relies on tabular features, while repeated attributes can also provide useful relational signals. This paper proposes THGT-FD, a Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Transformer for Fraud Detection. Each transaction is represented using one transaction token and six types of relation tokens and incorporates Time2Vec encoding into the transaction representation. A Transformer learns the interactions among these tokens within each individual transaction and then outputs a fraud probability. Experiments were conducted on 150,000 transactions sampled from the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection dataset and chronologically partitioned according to TransactionDT. On the test set, THGT-FD achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.8536, an average precision of 0.4164, and a Recall@5% of 0.4708. The class-weighted histogram-based gradient-boosting baseline achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.8722. The results indicate that relation tokens provide useful information for fraud-risk ranking, although the current model does not yet incorporate entity-level historical aggregation.

</details>


### [203] [Online Draft Co-Training for Speculative Decoding in Large-Scale, Long-Context RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07108)
*Zili Wang,Zhaopeng Qiu,Yuekai Zhang,Shuang Yu,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端系统，用于大规模在线草稿模型协同训练（online draft co-training），以加速强化学习后训练中的rollout生成。系统分别解决了CP下的分支注意力支持和跨PP阶段的目标特征传输两个关键障碍，在高达122B参数的模型上验证了显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码（Speculative decoding）通过加速rollout生成来降低RL后训练的主导成本，而在线协同训练可进一步提升草稿模型准确性从而获得更大加速比。然而将该方法扩展到大规模模型和长上下文时，面临两个关键障碍：一是标准因果序列并行（CP）实现不支持分支注意力（branch attention），二是目标模型特征跨越流水线并行（PP）阶段难以直接访问。

Method: (1) CP层面：扩展了打包、负载均衡的zigzag环注意力，将rank局部的分支注意力与因果主序列注意力合并；(2) PP层面：引入TapChannel机制，通过独立路径跨阶段传输中间目标特征，不干扰现有流水线调度。整体构建了支持大规模在线草稿协同训练的端到端系统。

Result: 协同训练的草稿模型能够紧密跟随策略基线的性能，同时在模型规模高达122B时提供显著的rollout和端到端加速。CP设计在256K token下实现强扩展，相比先前工作具有显著的内存节省；PP传输方案的开销适中。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在线协同训练的投机解码可以高效扩展到大规模模型和极长上下文场景，为RL后训练阶段的关键瓶颈提供了系统级解决方案，并且代码已开源提供复现基础。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates rollout generation, which dominates the cost of reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. Online co-training can further increase the draft's accuracy, yielding greater speedups. However, scaling this approach to co-training on large models with long contexts poses two obstacles: (1) branch attention is unsupported by standard causal context-parallel (CP) implementations, and (2) target features span across pipeline-parallel (PP) stages. We address both with an end-to-end system for large-scale online draft co-training. For CP, we extend packed, load-balanced zigzag ring attention by merging rank-local branch attention with causal main-sequence attention. For PP, TapChannel transports intermediate target features across stages via a separate path, leaving the pipeline schedule unaffected. Experiments demonstrate that co-trained drafts closely track the policy baseline while delivering substantial rollout and end-to-end speedups across model scales up to 122B. Our CP design achieves strong scaling at 256K tokens with significant memory savings over prior work, and our PP transport incurs modest overhead. Code can be found at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/RL/issues/3698.

</details>


### [204] [Fine-grained Distributed Backdoor Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07147)
*Jian Wang,Hong Shen,Wei Ke,Xue Hua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度分布式后门攻击框架（FDBA），利用基于Canny算法边缘特征的动态触发器生成和嵌入向量对比学习策略，在联邦学习中以更少的毒化样本实现更高的攻击成功率，并能绕过主流防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临后门攻击威胁，分布式后门攻击虽更有害但需要更多毒化样本来弥补触发强度因分解而导致的损失；固定触发模式容易被稳健聚合算法检测，增加了攻击暴露风险。

Method: FDBA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于Canny算法提取图像边缘特征并注入拉普拉斯噪声的动态触发器生成方法，通过RGB通道分解实现分布式触发器的隐蔽适配；(2) 嵌入向量对比学习策略，迫使毒化样本在特征空间中逼近目标类别中心，增强攻击效果。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，目标ASR在70%-90%区间时，FDBA相比DBA将所需毒化比例降低了37.4%-48.4%；在Non-IID极端异构场景下，FDBA保留了IID攻击性能的84.7%，而DBA仅保留73.5%；FDBA成功绕过了主流防御机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为联邦学习安全提供了新见解，强调了细粒度分布式攻击带来的潜在威胁和防御挑战。

Abstract: Federated learning, as a privacy-preserving distributed machine learning paradigm, faces significant threats from backdoor attacks. Compared to centralized attacks, distributed backdoor attacks are more harmful but require more poisoned samples to compensate for the loss of trigger strength due to decomposition. Fixed trigger patterns are also easily detected by robust aggregation algorithms, increasing the risk of attack exposure. To address these challenges, we propose a fine-grained distributed backdoor attack framework (FDBA). This framework uses dynamic trigger generation and embedding vector optimization to perform attacks with fewer poisoned samples. First, we design a dynamic trigger generation method based on image edge structures using the Canny algorithm to extract edge features, which are then injected with Laplacian noise. RGB channel decomposition is applied for covert adaptation of the distributed trigger, reducing detection chances. Second, we introduce an embedding vector contrastive learning strategy that forces poisoned samples to approach the target class center in the feature space, enhancing attack effectiveness. On CIFAR-10, piecewise-linear estimates for target ASRs between 70\% and 90\% show that FDBA reduces the required poisoning ratio by 37.4\%--48.4\% compared with DBA. In non-independent and identically distributed (Non-IID) scenarios, FDBA retains 84.7\% of its IID attack performance under extreme heterogeneity, whereas DBA drops to 73.5\%, and the framework successfully bypasses mainstream defense mechanisms.
  This study offers new insights into federated learning security and emphasizes the potential threats and defense challenges posed by fine-grained distributed attacks.

</details>


### [205] [Stable-MM-R1: Anchoring Multimodal Reasoning Dynamics via Entropy-Guided Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07148)
*Yimeng Ye,Shuang Chen,Wenxuan Huang,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zhangquan Chen,Jiayu Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Yicheng Xiao,Zhiyuan Feng,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据中心的强化学习微调框架，通过Potential-Aware Query Mining (PAQM)和Hybrid Stratified Replay (HSR)两种机制来稳定LLM的RL训练，缓解熵崩塌问题，并在复杂推理任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的强化学习训练存在训练不稳定和熵快速崩塌的问题，其根源在于标准采样流程中的"Rollout Silencing"现象和低质量的梯度信号，亟需一种稳健的框架来稳定RL训练。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) Potential-Aware Query Mining (PAQM)：动态过滤数据，聚焦于"Distillation Zone"（具有高能力激发潜力的样本）；(2) Hybrid Stratified Replay (HSR)：基于Path Entropy（路径熵，一种rollout级别的置信度代理）和结果奖励对rollouts进行分层，重构批次。在每一步优化中，HSR复用当前策略的"Stability Anchors"和"Hard Negatives"构建高对比度的优化组，并在下一步之前清空缓冲区。

Result: 该方法在复杂推理任务上优于强基线，实现了稳定且高效的RL微调，有效缓解熵崩塌并提高了有限算力下学习信号的利用率。

Conclusion: 该工作为稳定高效的RL微调提供了原理性的数据中心解决方案，所提出的PAQM和HSR机制分别从数据筛选和批次重构两个维度解决了RL训练中的不稳定性和熵崩塌问题。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning (RL) effectively incentivizes reasoning in Large Language Models, current pipelines are hindered by training instability and rapid entropy collapse. These limitations often stem from "Rollout Silencing" and low-quality gradient signals in standard sampling procedures. In this work, we propose a robust, data-centric framework to stabilize RL training. We first introduce Potential-Aware Query Mining (PAQM), which filters data dynamically to focus on the "Distillation Zone"---samples with high potential for capability elicitation. Furthermore, we present Hybrid Stratified Replay (HSR), a novel mechanism that restructures batches by stratifying rollouts based on Path Entropy, a rollout-level confidence proxy, and outcome reward. Within each optimization step, HSR reuses current-policy "Stability Anchors" and "Hard Negatives" to construct high-contrast optimization groups, then clears its buffers before the next step. This approach mitigates entropy collapse while improving the utilization of learning signals under limited compute. Our method outperforms strong baselines on complex reasoning tasks, offering a principled solution for stable and efficient RL fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Beyond Right and Wrong: Evaluating Second-order Social Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05437)
*Sunny Rai,Jinyi Kuang,Reyhan Jamalova,Annie Lou,Cristina Bicchieri,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Hugo Orozco-Olvera,Ana Maria Munoz-Boudet,Lyle H Ungar,Sharath C Guntuku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估大语言模型(LLM)元规范(metanorm)推理能力的框架，围绕情感评估和行为响应两个维度展开，并发布了包含450个规范违反场景的多视角数据集NormReact，发现当前LLM过度预测负面制裁，使描绘的社会世界比现实更严苛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的AI对齐工作主要关注一阶社会规范（教模型什么是可接受或不可接受的），但社会智能不仅依赖于规范识别，还依赖于预测谁会执行规范以及如何执行（如公开羞辱或监禁）。这种二阶预期即元规范，决定了人们在规则被破坏时如何回应，而现有研究尚未系统评估LLM在这方面的能力。缺此环节，AI在规范敏感领域（如冲突调解、政策模拟）的应用可能产生扭曲的社会调节图景。

Method: 提出了一种评估LLM元规范推理的框架，包含两个维度：情感评估（emotional appraisal）和行为响应（behavioral response），并定义了两种分类任务：预测违反者的自我调节（self-regulation）和观察者的他者调节（other-regulation）。发布了多视角数据集NormReact，包含450个规范违反场景，针对规范违反者的性别和观察者的社会亲近度进行了情感与行为响应的手工标注，并在六个LLM上进行了评测。

Result: 在六个LLM模型上，模型都过度预测负面制裁，而在人类预期不采取行动的场合也倾向于预测惩罚；并且随着社会距离增加，模型输出与人类判断的一致性逐渐恶化。这表明当前LLM描绘了一个更严苛的社会世界。

Conclusion: LLM在规范敏感领域的应用中可能产生社会调节的失真图景——过度代表惩罚，而低估了真实世界中规范执行所体现的宽容、克制和关系校准。这为未来AI对齐工作指出了需要纳入二阶社会认知（元规范推理）的方向。

Abstract: Previous AI alignment efforts have focused primarily on first-order social norms -- teaching models what is socially acceptable or unacceptable (e.g., `do not steal'). However, social intelligence depends not only on norm recognition, but also on anticipating who will enforce it and how (e.g., public shame or even imprisonment). These second-order expectations, known as metanorms, govern how people respond when social rules are broken. We introduce a novel framework for evaluating metanorm reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) along two dimensions: emotional appraisal and behavioral response, and propose new classification tasks, namely, predicting self-regulation in violators, and other-regulation in observers. We release a multi-perspective dataset, NormReact, of 450 norm violation scenarios, hand-annotated for emotions and behavioral responses across norm violators' gender and observers' social closeness. Current LLMs portray a harsher social world: across six models, they overpredict negative sanctions where humans would expect inaction, and alignment with human judgments deteriorates as social distance increases. These findings suggest that AI systems in norm-sensitive domains from conflict mediation to policy simulation, may risk producing a distorted picture of social regulation: one that over-represents punishment and under-represents the tolerance, restraint, and relational calibration that characterize actual norm enforcement in real world.

</details>


### [207] [Simulating the Marginal Green Contribution of AI Modules in a Smart-Agriculture Platform: Evidence from Two Monte Carlo Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06740)
*Zhaoyang Li,Ruijie Zhang,Zhaoji Sun,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对智慧农业平台各组件绿色效益归属不清的问题，基于平台级蒙特卡洛评估构建组件显式化模拟框架，通过两组受控实验分别量化AI诊断与AI智能灌溉调度在农药化肥减量、节水及稻田甲烷减排中的组件级贡献，并发现农户采纳率是绿色目标达成的首要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧农业平台通常将AI诊断、物联网感知与决策推送捆绑为一体，导致难以分解各组件各自的绿色效益贡献，资源配置决策缺乏定量依据。

Method: 在既有平台级蒙特卡洛评估基础上将组件显式化，设计两组受控模拟实验。实验1沿"AI能力→农户行为→农化投入减量"链条建模（农药/化肥减量=可避免盲目施药比例×处方有效性×决策触达覆盖率×采纳率），对比经验推广模式与AI模式；实验2对比现状(P0)、IoT工程改造(P1)及P1+AI灌溉调度(P2)三组方案的节水与稻田甲烷减排效果。

Result: 实验1显示：AI模式下达到20%农药减量的概率从推广模式的接近零提升至基线20.7%，诊断精度0.95且采纳率0.85时最高达49%；15%化肥减量概率从接近零提升至52.0%。实验2中，中位综合节水量从P0的7.8%升至P1的11.0%和P2的16.0%，AI较纯工程改造成增5.0个百分点；AI调度下稻田CH4减排达30.5%，高于人工操作的19.8%，水稻灌溉-甲烷子系统碳强度下降27.9%。敏感性分析一致显示农户采纳率是首要瓶颈，而非算法精度；AI数据融合对土壤湿度传感误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了可复现的组件级绿色价值评估与推广策略优化的模拟框架，为智慧农业平台的资源分配决策提供定量证据支持。

Abstract: Smart agriculture platforms usually bundle AI diagnosis, IoT sensing and decision push into a single package, so the green benefit attributable to each component remains unclear and resource-allocation decisions lack quantitative evidence. Building on a previous platform-level Monte Carlo assessment, this paper makes the components explicit and runs two controlled simulation experiments. Experiment 1 follows the chain from AI capability to farmer behavior to agrochemical input reduction, modeling pesticide/fertilizer reduction as avoidable blind-application share times prescription effectiveness times decision-touch coverage times adoption rate, and compares an experienced-extension mode with the AI mode: the probability of reaching 20% pesticide reduction is essentially zero in the extension mode but 20.7% at baseline, up to 49% with diagnosis accuracy 0.95 and adoption 0.85 under AI; the probability of 15% fertilizer reduction rises from near zero to 52.0%. Experiment 2 compares current practice (P0), IoT engineering retrofit (P1), and P1 plus AI irrigation scheduling (P2): median aggregate water saving rises from 7.8% (P0) to 11.0% (P1) and 16.0% (P2), with AI adding 5.0 percentage points beyond engineering; paddy CH4 reduction reaches 30.5% under AI scheduling versus 19.8% under manual operation, and the rice irrigation-methane subsystem carbon intensity declines 27.9%. Sensitivity analyses of both experiments consistently indicate that the primary bottleneck for meeting green targets is farmer adoption rather than algorithm accuracy, and that AI data fusion is robust to soil-moisture sensing errors. This work provides a reproducible simulation framework for component-level green-value evaluation and promotion-strategy optimization of smart agriculture platforms.

</details>


### [208] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CriticGen是一种细粒度、生成感知的大语言模型评估框架，将评估转化为可操作的答案改进控制。它先生成样本特定的评估维度和评分标准，再据此联合产生分数、理由、可执行的改进建议和优化后的答案，实现从评估到改进的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估方法过于粗粒度且与生成过程脱耦，产生的通用性解释缺乏可操作的反馈，无法为模型改进提供具体指导。

Method: CriticGen框架包含两个关键阶段：(1)在主观、客观和自推导约束等高级类别下生成样本特定的评估维度和评分标准；(2)利用这些动态评分标准作为条件，联合生成分数、理由、可执行的细化建议以及改进后的答案，形成基于评分标准的条件化精炼过程。

Result: CriticGen在多项指标上取得显著提升：相关性/覆盖率从3.33/4.03提升至3.97/4.24；Pearson和Spearman相关系数分别达0.9556和0.9560；标准接地理由和可执行建议的F1从0.6369/0.5994提升至0.7554/0.7900；其反馈使73.17%的答案得到改进，且93.28%的非退化率保证了可靠性。

Conclusion: 细粒度评估应当既具有实例特定性又具有可操作性。CriticGen通过生成更高质量的动态评分标准并将其作为条件驱动答案精炼，验证了评估与生成紧密结合能够有效转化为可靠的答案改进，推动评估体系从"打分工具"向"改进控制信号"的范式转变。

Abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [209] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MERIT是一个评测工具型LLM智能体记忆功能的基准测试与框架，通过显式成本核算衡量记忆对任务执行的边际效用，发现记忆实现方式可将任务成功率改变高达60个百分点，且完整回放从未具备经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评测（如LoCoMo、LongMemEval）仅基于对话历史问答来衡量记忆，无法回答"记住的事实是否真正改变工具型智能体的行为"这一问题。作者希望构建一种能真正反映记忆在真实工具任务中边际价值的评测方法。

Method: MERIT提供三个领域的情节性工具使用任务，包含自动化泄漏检查确认任务对前期事实的依赖；难度阶梯直至更新后事实回忆；受控记忆损坏；以及对每个记忆操作进行完整的token与美元计量。实验涵盖两代gpt-4.1-mini试点和预注册的3模型×3种子测试矩阵，记忆侧实现保持固定。

Result: 在23,440个评分片段（$42.57）上，记忆使依赖型任务成功率从泄漏验证的0.00底限提升至0.55-1.00。对于更新后事实，嵌入检索性能不稳定（跨模型0.30-0.95，最大种子差距0.45），智能体仅在55%的情况下依据正确检索值行动；而写时更新存储（结构化事实存储和LLM摘要）保持在0.70-1.00，混合方案效果差于纯事实存储。最新一代抽查（Claude Sonnet 5）复现了该模式。交换记忆实现最多可改变任务成功率60个百分点，完整回放从未经济：每域最佳条件每美元提供2.7-3.9倍的边际效用。

Conclusion: 记忆实现的具体方式对工具型智能体的任务执行效果有决定性影响，嵌入检索在事实更新场景下可靠性不足；评测记忆应基于其在任务执行中的边际收益而非单纯的事实回忆能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [210] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoFyn，一个基于Expert Iteration算法的智能体框架，通过更新持久状态而非模型权重来适配冻结模型。系统在每轮从全新会话开始，通过显式接口（如持久记忆文件、报告、仓库状态）注入信息，由编排器协调多个专业智能体探索多种方案，任务验证器提供客观奖励并蒸馏回持久状态以改进下一轮策略。在奥林匹克数学、数据科学和网络安全三个领域得到验证：在2026年IMO六个全新题目上优于各模型提供商的编码智能体，在Spider 2.0 dbt基准上排名第一，并在多个知名开源项目中发现了16个维护者确认的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决冻结模型在复杂推理任务中无法通过权重更新持续改进的问题，期望通过外部持久状态和验证信号驱动的循环机制，在不修改模型参数的情况下提升模型在推理和构建任务上的表现。

Method: 借鉴Expert Iteration算法，构建多轮迭代循环：每轮从冻结模型的干净会话开始，编排器调度多个专业智能体进行探索、规划和构建多种备选方案；任务验证器负责检验成果并给出客观奖励；奖励被蒸馏进持久状态（包括记忆文件、报告、仓库状态等显式接口），从而更新下一轮的有效策略。

Result: 在三大领域验证有效：1）2026年国际数学奥林匹克六道新题上，所有有改进空间的模型在AutoFyn下得分均高于其各自提供商的编码智能体；2）AutoFyn构建的智能体在Spider 2.0 dbt基准中排名第一；3）在Next.js、MetaMask、pnpm、Warp、LiteLLM、Langflow和Open WebUI等项目中产生了16个维护者确认的安全漏洞公告。

Conclusion: AutoFyn证明仅通过维护和更新外部持久状态而非模型权重，就能显著提升冻结模型在多领域复杂任务上的性能，验证了基于验证信号的状态蒸馏作为模型增强范式的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [211] [Damage-Aware Bandit Pruning for Vision and Language Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05448)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将Transformer结构化后训练剪枝中的功能单元选择建模为"损伤感知的多臂老虎机"问题。在固定评估预算下，通过临时掩码注意力头和MLP通道组，使用成对损伤（同批次上的掩码损失减基础损失）来构建平滑有界奖励，驱动UCB风格策略或分数Beta Thompson Sampling，逐步构建最终掩码。在多个语言和视觉Transformer上验证，通常优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化后训练剪枝需要选择完整的功能单元（注意力头、MLP通道组等），使得抑制这些单元后模型性能退化最小。然而传统的贪心或基于静态显著性选择方法在高维单元空间中效率有限，需要一种在有限评估预算内既能有效探测单元损伤又不过度开销的单元选择策略。

Method: 1) 将单元选择形式化为多臂老虎机问题，每个候选单元视为一只"臂"；2) 通过成对损伤（masked loss - base loss，同一batch上测量）减少批次间方差；3) 设计平滑有界奖励，支持UCB风格策略或Fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling两种bandit策略；4) 在每一步通过添加一个单元逐步构建最终掩码；5) 选中的单元在原始稠密checkpoint中功能性归零，表征有效结构抑制而非物理压缩。

Result: 在WikiText-2、LAMBADA和Imagenette上，覆盖GPT-2、OPT、Pythia、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、ViT-B/16、DeiT-Tiny、Swin-Tiny等模型。对比随机、幅度、静态显著性和预算化贪心选择。5个种子上，bandit方法在成对语言模型比较中通常比预算化贪心退化更小。28个比较中23个bootstrap置信区间排除零，11个配对测试p<0.05，经Benjamini-Hochberg校正后6个q<0.05。ViT-B/16和Swin-Tiny的匹配评估表明收益不仅由更大评估预算解释。

Conclusion: 损伤感知的多臂老虎机方法为Transformer结构化后训练剪枝中的单元选择提供了一种有效的、预算敏感的框架。通过成对损伤设计和bandit探索-利用平衡，该方法在多个模型和数据集上相较于现有基线的退化率更低，统计检验支持其显著优势，且收益不单纯来自更大的评估预算。

Abstract: Structured post-training pruning of transformers requires selecting complete functional units whose suppression causes limited degradation. We formulate structured-unit selection for language and vision transformers as a damage-aware multi-armed bandit problem under a fixed candidate-evaluation budget. Attention heads and MLP channel groups are temporarily masked on calibration batches. Paired damage is the masked loss minus the base loss on the same batch, reducing batch-to-batch variation. A smooth bounded reward drives either a UCB-style policy or fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling, and the final mask is constructed sequentially by adding one unit at each step. The selected units are functionally zeroed in the original dense checkpoint; therefore, the reported parameter effects represent effective structural suppression rather than physical compression or measured speedup. Experiments on WikiText-2, LAMBADA, and Imagenette cover GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, and Swin-Tiny, with comparisons against random, magnitude, static-saliency, and budgeted-greedy selection. Across five seeds, the bandit methods usually reduce degradation relative to budgeted greedy in the paired language-model comparisons. Of 28 comparisons highlighted in the paper, 23 bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero and 11 paired tests have p < 0.05; six have q < 0.05 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the full family of 116 dataset-wise tests. Matched-evaluation results for ViT-B/16 and Swin-Tiny indicate that their gains are not explained solely by a larger candidate-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [212] [Compiling VGDL into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05459)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个确定性框架，将用Video Game Description Language描述的电子游戏直接编译为动态结构因果模型（Dynamic Structural Causal Models），为游戏AI提供因果保真的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体常依赖虚假相关性，而大语言模型容易幻觉化游戏规则，且当前缺乏将复杂游戏机制正式映射到因果模型的方法论。

Method: 提出一个确定性编译框架，将VGDL规格中的精灵动力学、交互规则和终止条件直接转化为显式结构方程，每个游戏tick对应从时间t到t+1的状态变量因果转换，而非从游戏轨迹或LLM输出中推断因果结构。

Result: 该框架保证了与真实游戏机制绝对因果保真度，生成的模型支持反事实推理、因果强化学习智能体训练和程序化内容验证。

Conclusion: 该框架在符号游戏描述与因果基础的游戏AI之间建立了原则性桥梁，提供了透明的因果路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and large language models often struggle to accurately capture the causal mechanics of game environments. Standard reinforcement learning agents tend to rely on spurious correlations, while large language models are prone to hallucinating game rules. Although causal reinforcement learning improves interpretability, there is currently no formal methodology to map complex game mechanics directly into causal models. To address this, we propose a deterministic framework that compiles games specified in the Video Game Description Language into Dynamic Structural Causal Models. Rather than inferring causal structures from gameplay traces or noisy large language models' outputs, our methodology directly translates game components, including sprite dynamics, interaction rules, and termination conditions, into explicit structural equations. Each game tick represents a causal transition from state variables at time $t$ to $t+1$. By establishing this grounded mapping, the approach guarantees absolute causal fidelity to the ground-truth game mechanics. The resulting models offer transparent causal pathways that support counterfactual reasoning, causal reinforcement learning agent training, and procedural content validation. This framework provides a principled bridge between symbolic game descriptions and causally grounded game AI.

</details>


### [213] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-Bench基准，直接审计冻结JEPA风格潜在世界模型中的"潜在距离可对行动排序"这一隐含假设。结果表明该假设在导航和操作基准上严重且结构性失败，且该缺陷因闭环重规划机制而被掩盖——降低重规划频率会导致成功率崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型（如JEPA-WM）通过在冻结的潜在空间中比较距离来规划行动，隐含假设"潜在空间中的接近程度可以正确排序候选行动"。然而这一核心假设从未被直接审计，而闭环成功率可能系统性高估表示的可排序性，导致缺陷被掩盖。

Method: 提出ARC-Bench：一个无泄漏、固定候选的协议，直接衡量冻结JEPA风格目标是否能够正确排序候选行动（即潜在距离排序与真实成本排序是否一致）。应用于官方发布的JEPA-WM检查点（导航和操作风格控制域），并使用官方操作审计（top-scored候选几乎总是次优）和迷宫域中的反转现象进行验证。通过用视频预训练的V-JEPA 1和V-JEPA 2编码器（ViT-L/ViT-G规模）替换DINOv2进行视觉骨干扩展实验，并用来源、欠训练、匹配预算骨干控制以及指标循环控制排除琐碎解释。最后通过降低规划器的重规划频率来揭示缺陷掩盖机制。

Result: 潜在距离可排序行动这一假设严重结构性失败：官方操作审计中得分最高的候选行动几乎总是次优的，迷宫域中出现相同反转。用V-JEPA 1/V-JEPA 2替换DINOv2后缺陷依然存在。重规划频率降低导致导航和操作领域成功率崩溃，得救的片段在第一规划诊断中富集严重排序失败，证明闭环成功率系统性高估冻结潜在表示的可排序性。

Conclusion: 闭环重规划掩盖了潜在空间规划器的排序缺陷，因此在评估潜在世界模型时不应仅依赖闭环成功率。ARC-Bench提供直接测量，掩盖机制提供解释，对适应、分摊或围绕潜在空间规划器进行重规划的相关方法具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>


### [214] [RAPID: Reliability-Aware Pair Importance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05481)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPID（Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation），一种用于示例间关系蒸馏的可靠感知对重要性蒸馏方法，通过分离"可靠性门控的关系目标"与"全支持集自适应对提议"两个模块，在有限关系预算下提升蒸馏效率，在两个文本分类任务上达到最高或次高的学生准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的示例间关系蒸馏在对教师表示几何进行迁移时，需要在一个小批次内匹配示例之间的关系。计算所有关系对具有随批次大小二次增长的计算复杂度，而均匀子采样未能有效利用有限的关系预算（relation budget）。因此需要一种更智能的对采样策略，既能控制计算复杂度，又能在预算内最大化蒸馏收益。

Method: RAPID将关系蒸馏分解为两个独立模块：(1) 可靠性门控的关系目标（reliability gated relational target）：通过校准的教师熵来确定哪些教师关系值得强调，形成门控的小批次目标；(2) 全支持集自适应对提议（full support adaptive pair proposal）：利用分离的学生-教师残差（detached student-teacher residuals）确定评估哪些关系对。采用精确的逆提议校正（exact inverse proposal correction），使得损失和梯度估计量相对于门控小批次目标满足条件无偏性。

Result: 在AG News（BERT到DistilBERT蒸馏，三对随机种子，关系预算256）中，可靠性门控关系蒸馏达到最高平均学生准确率94.285±0.054%，RAPID排名第二（94.241±0.025%），优于交叉熵基线（94.154±0.124%）。在SST-2（DistilBERT到DistilBERT蒸馏，三对种子，预算64）中，门控目标达到88.800±0.532%，RAPID为88.685±0.462%，交叉熵基线仅为87.271±0.162%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持模块化观点：目标可靠性（决定哪些教师关系被强调）和评估优先级（决定哪些关系被评估）是关系蒸馏中可分离的两个设计维度。门控目标策略在两种设置下均取得最高平均准确率，而自适应提议与门控目标的组合（RAPID）也能稳定超越基线，处于种子级差异范围内。

Abstract: Inter example relational distillation transfers a teacher's representation geometry by matching relations among examples within a mini batch. Computing all pairs has quadratic complexity in the batch size, whereas uniform subsampling may use a limited relation budget inefficiently. We introduce Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation, or RAPID, which separates a reliability gated relational target from a full support adaptive pair proposal. Reliability determines which teacher relations are emphasized, while calibrated teacher entropy and detached student-teacher residuals determine which relations are evaluated. Exact inverse proposal correction makes the loss and gradient estimators conditionally unbiased with respect to the gated mini batch target. We evaluate RAPID in two text classification settings: AG News with BERT-to-DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 256, and SST-2 with DistilBERT to DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 64. Reliability gated relational distillation achieves the highest observed mean student accuracy on both datasets: 94.285 plus or minus 0.054 percent on AG News and 88.800 plus or minus 0.532 percent on SST-2. RAPID ranks second, achieving 94.241 plus or minus 0.025 percent and 88.685 plus or minus 0.462 percent, respectively, compared with 94.154 plus or minus 0.124 percent and 87.271 plus or minus 0.162 percent for the cross entropy baseline. Pilot evaluations are counted toward the same total budget as the main relation evaluations. Across both settings, the gated target yields the highest mean accuracy, while the adaptive proposal remains within seed-level variation. These results support the modular view that target reliability and evaluation priority are separable design dimensions.

</details>


### [215] [PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN: Prior-Guided Joint Probabilistic Forecasting of Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05488)
*Weizhi Nie,Rihao Chang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN，一个以轨迹为先的框架，用于多变量生理数据的联合概率预测。它结合了缺失感知时间编码器、软器官系统先验、患者特定关系、非线性Kolmogorov-Arnold消息和低秩多元Student-t头部。在冻结的MIMIC-IV队列（6,882名患者和54,694个窗口）上评估，在13个模型中取得第二低的归一化MAE和最低的RMSE，但轨迹衍生的风险评分弱于专用GRU-D分类器。
