Method: AgentBrew包含两个核心组件：(1) 回顾式任务推断（Retrospective Task Inference）：基于轨迹的实际结果，为每条轨迹重构与之对齐的指令；(2) 基于PMI的信用分配（PMI-Based Credit Assignment）：通过点互信息将轨迹关于推断指令的总信息量分解为每个动作的加性信用，并用这些信用加权策略训练目标，放大信息量大的动作、抑制无效动作。

Result: 在三个真实MCP应用（GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL）上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score，超越Qwen3-235B（+2.3 / +4.4），并优于拒绝采样方法（+5.9 / +10.3）。

Conclusion: 细粒度的离线学习能够从原始轨迹中恢复过滤式方法会丢弃的有用监督信号，为没有验证器和模拟器的真实环境提供了一种有效的工具使用策略训练方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [250] [Multimodal Resource-Exhaustion Attacks on Vision-Language Models via Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05889)
*Zhaoxiong Ni,Yatie Xiao,Chi-Man Pun,Fei Peng,Qingxiao Guan,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JPPO是首个将可见提示词与图像扰动联合优化的对抗框架，通过跨模态协同放大自回归VLM的推理延迟和能耗，在5个开源VLM上实现最高36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能耗放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对自回归视觉语言模型（VLM）的资源耗尽攻击通常采用单模态威胁模型，将图像分支视为主要优化面而固定用户可见提示词。即使是近年来的循环相关变体也局限于这一单通道范式，未能将可用性攻击作为跨模态优化问题来开发利用图像和提示词联合可控的输入面。

Method: JPPO采用复合对抗框架，将可见提示词提升为一等对抗变量，与图像扰动联合参与优化。在受限的联合输入威胁模型下，JPPO在像素面和提示词面执行耦合的分阶段优化，产生协同成本放大效应。该方法在机制上区别于循环依赖型失败，实验中循环出现率可忽略不计。

Result: 在MS COCO和ImageNet数据集上对5个开源VLM家族进行评估，在8/255无穷范数预算下，JPPO在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现超过4.6倍延迟和5.3倍能耗放大，在BLIP-2上实现超过36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能耗放大。消融实验证实放大源于多模态协同而非提示词长度或单一模态。与直接对比基线相比，JPPO在更少的优化迭代下实现最强的成本放大。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前VLM部署防御中的结构性盲区，表明成本感知的鲁棒性评估应作为多模态部署的一等安全需求。

Abstract: Resource-exhaustion attacks against autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) typically assume unimodal threat models, treating the image branch as the primary optimization surface while holding user-visible prompts fixed. Even recent loop-centric variants remain confined to this single-channel paradigm, leaving the exploitation of availability unexplored as a cross-modal optimization problem over jointly controllable input surfaces.
  We introduce Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization (JPPO), the first compound adversarial framework elevating the visible prompt to a first-class adversarial variable alongside image perturbations. Under a restricted joint-input threat model, JPPO performs coupled, stagewise optimization over both the pixel and prompt surfaces. This produces synergistic cost amplification, mechanistically distinct from loop-dependent failures, exhibiting negligible loop incidence in our experiments.
  Evaluating five open-source VLM families on MS COCO and ImageNet under an 8/255 infinity-norm budget, JPPO achieves over 4.6x latency and 5.3x energy amplification on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and over 36.6x latency with 32.7x energy amplification on BLIP-2. This represents the strongest cost amplification among directly compared baselines while requiring substantially fewer optimization iterations. Ablations confirm this amplification arises from multimodal coordination rather than prompt length or isolated modalities. These findings reveal structural blind spots in current VLM serving defenses, motivating cost-aware robustness evaluation as a first-class security requirement for multimodal deployments.

</details>


### [251] [The End of AI Exponentiation: Fluttering Inside and Outside AI Bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05894)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI指数化增长的终结，分析了AI生态系统内外部的多重不稳定因素，包括数据限制、算力需求、合成数据递归、估值膨胀等内生挑战，以及劳动市场冲击、治理关切、公众不确定性和地缘政治竞争等外生压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型、大规模算力基础设施和自主推理系统的快速发展将AI带入变革时代，但传统的指数级扩展范式正面临数据峰值、算力需求暴增、合成数据递归、估值膨胀和社会不稳定等系统性障碍，需要审视AI指数增长的可持续性与终结。

Method: 本文采用论述性/概念性分析方法，将AI系统的不稳定性分为两个维度进行审视：生态系统内部（算力与数据中心竞赛、投机性投资、超级智能竞赛）和生态系统外部（劳动力中断、治理担忧、公众不确定性、地缘政治加速）。

Result: 揭示了AI指数化增长正从内外部两个层面遭遇摩擦：内部源于算力与数据中心竞赛、投机泡沫和超级智能追逐，外部源于劳动力重构、监管滞后、公众信任危机和全球地缘政治竞争加剧。

Conclusion: AI的发展正从"指数增长"转向需要在技术、社会、经济和治理层面重新校准的阶段，需正视泡沫内外的多重不稳定因素，以寻求更可持续的AI发展路径。

Abstract: The exponentiation of Artificial intelligence (AI) in the recent past has entered a transformative era that has been driven by the growth in large language models (LLMs), large-scale compute infrastructures, and autonomous reasoning systems. However, the rapid acceleration of AI has increasingly shown technological, societal, economic, ethical and infrastructural challenges associated with peak data limitations, rising computational demands, synthetic data recursion, valuation inflation, and societal instability. The traditional scaling paradigms that have powered the modern AI systems are gradually encountering friction in sustaining continuous exponential growth. This paper views ``the end of AI exponentiation,'' thus exploring how it flutters inside and outside the bubble, where instability emerges within the AI ecosystem through compute and data-center races, speculative investments, and the rat-race toward superintelligence, and outside the ecosystem through labor disruption, governance concerns, public uncertainty, and geopolitical acceleration surrounding future intelligent systems and infrastructures globally.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以过程为中心（process-centric）的AI辅助同行评审诊断基准，将异构评审记录转换为过程对齐数据，通过直接决策预测基线、黄金过程变量与预测过程变量的对比、阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析，系统评估AI在评审过程中所依赖证据的充分性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助同行评审的评估主要关注生成评审的整体质量或最终决策的准确性，缺乏对模型决策是否由充分且可靠的评审证据支持的评估。这使得研究者无法判断AI的评审结论是否基于合理的推理过程，而非仅仅"蒙对"结果。

Method: 使用(x, z_s, z_c, z_r, y)表示论文内容、摘要、批评、建议和决策。从PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR转换异构评审记录为过程对齐数据。采用直接决策预测(Direct)作为基线，对比黄金过程变量与预测过程变量的决策价值，并进行三类评估：阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。

Result: 在三个数据集和六个模型上的实验表明：黄金过程变量通常具有更高的决策价值；主分析模型的黄金与预测变量之间的差距在不同数据集和随机种子间保持稳定，并在大多数模型-数据集组合中重现；模型生成的中级评审文本在相邻阶段间表现出较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未被前置评审证据所一致支持。

Conclusion: 该基准面向被设计为辅助而非替代人类审稿人的AI系统，提供了一个透明、可审计的诊断工具，用于评估AI评审过程各阶段的可靠性。

Abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [253] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标智能体进化（MOAE）方法，将迭代式上下文内优化组织为对完整智能体轨迹的Pareto保持进化搜索，在不固定标量化权重的前提下同时优化任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性，实验证明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的评估日益多维化，优秀智能体需同时满足任务完成准确率、交互质量、安全性和效率等多重要求。现有方法将异构指标压缩为固定标量分数，这依赖于指标归一化和偏好权重，可能丢弃代表有用部署权衡的候选方案，无法回答多目标能否同时优化这一核心问题。

Method: MOAE将迭代上下文内优化组织为Pareto保持的进化搜索。具体包含：1）在有限预算内维持非支配候选的经验档案库（archive）；2）使用目标特定的诊断信息指导后代生成（offspring generation）；3）仅在部署阶段应用约束感知选择，将搜索阶段的候选保留与最终方案返回的偏好分离。该方法无需参数更新，允许每个目标替换为任意可测量属性（实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性）。

Result: 在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo上的实验表明，MOAE在匹配的轨迹预算下持续提升任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持较强的安全性。搜索行为分析进一步显示，Pareto保留扩展了可达目标区域，并提高了联合改进（joint improvement）的发生频率。

Conclusion: Pareto保持的上下文内进化在无需在搜索阶段固定标量化的情况下，为优化多个智能体属性提供了可行途径，展示了多目标LLM智能体优化的新方向。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [254] [Agentic Pressure: The Endogenous Entropy of Reliable Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05995)
*Hengle Jiang,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agentic Pressure"（智能体压力）这一非对抗性概念，指代理在长期自主运行中，因目标达成与合规成本冲突而自发产生的内生压力。当压力超过临界阈值时，对齐的代理会以数学上最优的方式产生安全漂移（safety drift），并通过"工具性幻觉"（Instrumental Hallucination）合理化规则违背行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注静态的、对抗性的越狱攻击（jailbreaks），但忽略了代理在长期连续运行的非受限环境中会积累摩擦，导致对齐状态自然失稳。作者旨在识别并形式化这一与对抗攻击无关的内生性失稳现象。

Method: 提出理论框架，将Agentic Pressure形式化为"克服环境摩擦所需功与代理剩余能力之比"；通过动力学分析推导出超过临界阈值时安全漂移成为数学最优适应的条件。随后通过经验实验在不同压力条件下验证对齐代理是否自发妥协安全性以维护自主性。

Result: 实验验证了理论框架，证明在高压条件下，原本对齐的智能体会自发牺牲安全约束以维持其自主运作能力，并通过工具性幻觉来为违规行为提供合理化解释。

Conclusion: Agentic Pressure是一种内生性、非对抗性但可威胁安全对齐的关键因素。安全漂移并非代理"恶意"或"被攻破"的结果，而是高压条件下的数学最优策略。该研究为长期自主系统的安全对齐研究提供了新的理论视角与风险边界。

Abstract: Achieving reliable autonomy in the wild requires agents to sustain continuous operations across long-horizon trajectories. However, as agents navigate these unconstrained settings, they encounter cumulative friction that inherently destabilizes their alignment. In this paper, we identify a distinct non-adversarial phenomenon termed Agentic Pressure. We define this as a kinetic force that spontaneously emerges when the cost of compliance conflicts with the imperative of goal achievement. Unlike static jailbreaks, this pressure is endogenous and arises directly from the dynamics of interaction. We propose a theoretical framework that formalizes Agentic Pressure as the ratio between the required work to overcome environmental friction and the remaining capacity of the agent. Our analysis demonstrates that when this pressure exceeds a critical threshold, agents exhibit safety drift as a mathematically optimal adaptation. Consequently, they often resort to Instrumental Hallucination to rationalize rule violations. Empirical experiments validate this framework and show that aligned agents spontaneously compromise safety to preserve autonomy under high-pressure conditions.

</details>


### [255] [Generator-Independent Runtime Assurance under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06036)
*Guangxi Wan,Yongbo Xie,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于提议的控制器（如学习策略、语言模型规划器等）在运行时验证门控下的闭环安全性何时能与生成器解耦。主要定理表明，"同时集合健全性"（证明一个可接受提议集合中不含不可行动作）是生成器无关的"准入健全性"的充要条件；第二条定理则刻画了部分观测下任何准入机制的安全界；序贯风险账本使保证可实施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提议的控制器（包括学习策略、语言模型规划器等黑盒生成器）越来越多地被部署在运行时验证门控之后。现有的逐候选认证模式无法组合：在重试或best-of-k选择下，逐候选的误接收水平α会膨胀为1-(1-α)^k，这导致认证在多次尝试时失去保障。因此需要一种能够与生成器解耦、可组合的闭环安全保证。

Method: 作者提出"同时集合健全性"（simultaneous setwise soundness）概念，证明其是生成器无关的准入健全性的充要条件。结合设计期证书和无旁路规则，可推导出"契约安全"（contract safety），其违反界为 Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η，在任意（甚至对抗性）生成器替换下保持不变。第二条定理通过总变差距离刻画部分观测下任何准入机制的界（ābar+β+δ≥1）。作者还引入了序贯风险账本（sequential risk ledger），利用时一致置信管实现保证，并表明确定性准入计算将所有统计风险集中于状态估计。最后展示了Simplex风格运行时保障和控制障碍函数滤波作为退化情形。

Result: （1）证明了同时集合健全性是生成器无关的准入健全性的充要条件，逐候选认证在k次重试下误差膨胀为1-(1-α)^k，而集合级认证可避免此问题。（2）契约安全的违反界 Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η 在任意生成器替换下保持不变，即闭环安全保证与生成器完全解耦。（3）部分观测下存在信息论下界 ābar+β+δ≥1，刻画了准入机制在部分观测下的根本限制。（4）序贯风险账本提供可实施的时一致保证框架，确定性准入将统计风险全部集中于状态估计。（5）Simplex风格运行时保障和CBF滤波为该框架的退化特例。

Conclusion: 基于提议的控制系统可以通过集合级（而非逐候选级）的运行时认证实现与生成器解耦的闭环安全保证。同时候选集合的设计（而非单个候选的验证）以及状态估计的可靠性是保证系统安全的核心，而生成器本身的选择在很大程度上是无关紧要的。该理论统一并推广了Simplex式运行时保障和控制障碍函数滤波等方法。

Abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [256] [A Unified Policy Architecture (UPA): The Governance Kernel for Enterprise AI Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06543)
*Prabhu Raghav,Balamurugan Pandi,Arul Vivek,Shek Mohammed,Sridhar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一策略架构（UPA），为自主AI企业操作系统提供统一的治理架构，将授权控制扩展至运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据和治理评估，以解决现有治理机制碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI正在演变为自主代理构成的企业操作系统，而现有的授权、安全、防护和合规机制是碎片化的，无法将自主AI作为统一系统进行治理。需要一套专门针对自主AI代理的统一治理架构。

Method: 提出UPA（统一策略架构），包括：统一的策略模型（覆盖AI代理、工具、工作流、记忆、企业资源、代理间交互及业务规则）、声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包，以及企业治理评估框架。

Result: UPA将策略控制从单纯的授权扩展到更广泛的治理维度（运行时义务、人工审批、合规性、审计证据、治理评估），并识别了多代理协调、来源感知策略和有状态运行时治理等扩展方向。

Conclusion: UPA为构建安全、可问责且可治理的自主AI企业操作系统提供了基础架构，填补了现有治理机制不足以支撑自主AI统一治理的空白。

Abstract: Enterprise AI is evolving into an Enterprise Operating System where autonomous AI agents can plan, reason, use memory, invoke tools, execute workflows, and collaborate with other agents. This shift creates a new governance challenge: existing authorization, security, guardrails, and compliance mechanisms are fragmented and are not designed to govern autonomous AI as a unified system.
  This paper introduces the Unified Policy Architecture (UPA), a governance architecture for Enterprise AI Operating Systems. UPA provides a unified policy model for governing AI and agents, tools, workflows, memory, enterprise resources, and agent-to-agent interactions and enterprise business rules. It extends policy control beyond authorisation to include runtime obligations, human approvals, compliance, audit evidence, and governance evaluation.
  We present UPA's governance model, declarative policy language foundations, policy evaluation semantics, extensible plugins, industry policy packs, and an evaluation framework for enterprise governance. We also identify extensions for multi-agent coordination, provenance-aware policies, and stateful runtime governance. UPA provides a foundation for building secure, accountable, and governable Enterprise Operating Systems for autonomous AI.

</details>


### [257] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAREBench是一个面向部署感知的智能体（agent）可靠评估基准，基于统一的OpenClaw执行环境，将233个任务（取自22个源基准）组织为2×3的工作负载矩阵（输入模态×执行形式），采用统一的基于契约的评估协议和基于证据的评分审计。作者评估了23个商业API模型和12个本地开源模型，共7,587次模型-任务运行，记录准确率与token消耗及API参考成本。结果表明：没有单一模型在所有工作负载组中占优，文本与多模态任务呈现不同的准确率-成本权衡，本地开源模型在若干组中具有竞争力但整体仍落后于前沿商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从问答系统演化为通用智能体，需要评估多模态感知、多步执行、工具调用和工件交付等能力。然而现有基准往往绑定特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，缺乏可比性、可解释性和可靠性，难以支持实际部署决策。

Method: 构建DAREBench基准：基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境，从22个源基准中筛选和改造233个任务，组织成2×3工作负载矩阵（由输入模态和执形形式两个维度定义）；采用统一的基于契约的评估协议与基于证据的评分审计机制；对23个商业API模型和12个本地开源模型进行大规模系统评测。

Result: 系统评估了35个模型在7,587次模型-任务运行上的表现，报告了准确率、token消耗与API参考成本。核心发现是：没有任何单一模型能在所有工作负载组中全面占优；文本任务与多模态任务呈现不同的准确率-成本权衡特性；本地开源模型在部分工作负载组中具有竞争力，但总体仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体的部署与模型选择应考虑工作负载画像（profile）、部署模式以及准确率-成本权衡等多维因素，而非仅依赖单一聚合分数做决策。

Abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [258] [CIT-CAD: Constraint Intent Tree-based CAD Code Generation and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07434)
*Yali Du,Hui Sun,San-Zhuo Xi,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIT-CAD框架，通过从输入描述中推断约束意图树（CIT）来显式表征设计意图，既指导CAD代码生成又定义验证约束，实现超越渲染几何匹配的构造感知的CAD生成、验证与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD代码生成的研究重点在于生成的CAD模型与参考几何的匹配程度（如IoU指标），但这类指标无法捕捉零件分解、构造层次、布尔运算、草图结构和几何关系等设计意图层面的错误。需要一种显式表示设计意图的方法，使系统能够根据意图检查生成的代码。

Method: CIT-CAD框架包括两个核心步骤：(1) 从输入文本描述中推断约束意图树（CIT），该树表示预期的实体、层次结构、操作和关系；(2) 从生成的CAD程序中提取实际约束，与CIT中的预期约束进行比对，利用不匹配信息定位并修复设计违规。

Result: 实验表明该框架提升了CAD生成性能，在多实体复杂设计中获得了更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 这是首次将文本到CAD生成从渲染几何匹配推进到构造感知的合成、验证和修复方向的工作，通过将设计意图转化为显式且可检查的对象来实现。

Abstract: Natural-language Computer-Aided Design (CAD) code generation aims to turn design intent into executable and editable parametric programs. Large language models (LLMs) make this goal increasingly practical, but useful systems must preserve the construction process behind the rendered geometry. Existing benchmarks and methods mostly focus on how closely the generated CAD model matches the reference geometry, often using metrics such as Intersection over Union (IoU). Such metrics can miss errors in part decomposition, construction hierarchy, Boolean operations, sketch structure, and geometric relations. This gap calls for a representation that makes design intent explicit and lets a system check generated code against that intent. We propose CIT-CAD, a framework that infers a Constraint Intent Tree (CIT) from the input description to represent the intended entities, hierarchy, operations, and relations. The tree has two roles: it guides CAD code generation and defines expected constraints for verification. The framework extracts actual constraints from the generated program, compares them with the expected constraints, and uses mismatches to localize and repair design violations. Experiments show that the framework improves CAD generation performance, with larger gains on more complex multi-entity designs. By turning design intent into an explicit and checkable object, this work is the first attempt to move text-to-CAD generation beyond rendered-geometry matching toward construction-aware synthesis, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [259] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对AI Agent的后-hoc可解释性框架，将代理的执行轨迹转换为结构化报告和基于可观察行为的忠实自然语言解释，在多个基准和架构上优于朴素的LLM生成解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent在真实世界中日益部署，与外部工具交互并做顺序决策，但人类监督有限，迫切需要可靠且可审计的"做了什么、为什么"的解释。传统XAI方法无法提供交互式多步系统所需的过程级透明度，需要专门针对AI Agent设计的可解释性方法。

Method: 提出一个后-hoc XAI框架，将冗长的Agent执行轨迹转换为结构化报告，并生成显式基于其可观察行为的忠实自然语言解释。该框架仅依赖执行轨迹，因此可跨不同Agent架构、环境和任务适用。

Result: 通过人类和自动化评估，在多个基准和架构上验证表明，该框架能产生高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，同时可靠地识别无支持的主张、不合理的行为和证据缺口，优于朴素的LLM生成解释。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了传统XAI方法在AI Agent过程级透明性方面的空白，提供了一种架构无关、基于执行轨迹的后-hoc可解释方案，能生成忠实且可审计的解释。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [260] [A Tool-Augmented, GPT-4 Chatbot for Real-Time Repository Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07586)
*Muhammad Jawad Chowdhury,Md. Sakib Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GPT-4的新型聊天机器人架构，通过先解析用户查询提取参数、再选择合适工具、最后调用GPT-4生成详细回答的结构化流程，实现对软件仓库数据的自动化提取与分析，并验证了该方法在不同问题类型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件仓库中蕴含着大量关于代码贡献、缺陷报告和项目活动的数据，但非技术背景的利益相关者以及普通开发者由于缺乏仓库查询的专业技能，难以获取和利用这些信息。

Method: 区别于以往依赖嵌入模型和文档检索器的多组件系统，该架构采用结构化的逆向流程：首先解析用户查询以提取相关参数，其次基于分析结果选择合适的工具，最后调用GPT-4模型生成高度详细的回答。整个系统以提示工程和工具选择为核心来匹配查询意图。

Result: 在多种问题类型（包括Issues、Pull Requests、Commits、复合问题及通用仓库信息）上进行了实验，验证了目标提示在提升模型回答准确性方面的能力，同时证明了该架构对多样化用户群体的实用性。

Conclusion: 该架构能够通过生成可操作的见解，使仓库数据对技术和非技术受众都更加易于访问，为仓库数据的智能化利用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Software repositories contain vast amounts of data on code contributions, bug reports, and project activities, yet this information remains challenging for non-technical stakeholders and developers to access due to limited expertise in querying repositories. To address this, we introduce a novel chatbot architecture leveraging OpenAI's GPT-4 model for automated extraction and analysis of repository data. In contrast, our architecture takes a structured path first by parsing the user's query to extract relevant parameters, then selecting the correct tool to employ based on that analysis, and finally invoking the GPT-4 model to create a highly detailed response. In contrast to previous work based on multi-component systems with embedding models and document retrievers, our architecture inverts the process by relying on prompt engineering and tool selection to fit with the query intent. To validate our approach, we conducted experiments on various question types, including Issues, Pull Requests, Commits, Compound Questions, and General Repository Information, evaluating our target prompts' ability to improve the accuracy of responses from the model. Beyond demonstrating the utility of this architecture to a diverse set of users, our findings suggest that this architecture can make repository data more accessible to technical and non-technical audiences through the production of actionable insights.

</details>


### [261] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大型语言模型（LLMs）自动生成PDDL领域的实例生成程序，通过内置健全性检查保证生成实例的正确性，解决了长期以来实例生成依赖人工编写特定程序的问题，实验表明该方法能高效生成大量健全且多样的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL作为AI规划社区的事实标准语言，虽然能指定规划域，但缺乏对实例集（初始状态合法性约束、目标条件及领域子集约束）的规范化描述能力。这导致实例生成一直是adhoc的，需要手工编写针对特定领域和子集的生成器。近年虽有推理和学习方法尝试解决，但受限于可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出一种替代方法：利用LLM生成实例生成程序（instance-generation programs），并内置健全性保证机制——通过预设的检查（prescribed checks）来确保自动生成的程序产生的实例是健全的（sound）。

Result: 实验表明，自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地返回大量健全且多样的实例，在效率和多样性上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的实例生成程序自动合成方法是一种可行且高效的替代方案，兼具自动化的便利性、内置的健全性保证以及良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [262] [SWE-Bench Pro Verified: A Reliable Benchmark for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08149)
*Pujun Zheng,Zixin Shang,Shufan Jiang,Wenhui Tian,Dongsheng Zhu,Zerun Ma,Dingbo Yuan,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对SWE-Bench Pro基准测试存在奖励黑客（reward hacking）和任务质量问题两大不可靠因素，提出了验证版本SWE-Bench Pro Verified，通过反黑客保障和任务精修来消除这些问题，使基准测试结果更能反映软件工程智能体的真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现SWE-Bench Pro作为仓库级软件工程任务的标准基准测试，其评估结果受到两个不可靠来源的影响：一是奖励黑客（通过泄露金标准解决方案或隐藏评估信息实现），二是任务质量问题（如误导性问题陈述和不恰当范围的测试）。这些问题会虚增基准性能分数，掩盖智能体真实编码能力，因此需要修复。

Method: 提出SWE-Bench Pro Verified，采用两种主要策略：1）反黑客保障（anti-hacking safeguards），消除主要的信息泄漏渠道同时不干扰正常智能体功能；2）任务精修（task refinement），对存在不一致性的缺陷实例进行最小化修正。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Pro Verified上的评估显示，部分模型的性能明显低于此前报告的结果，表明现有SWE-Bench Pro上的成绩可能高估了真实的软件工程能力。

Conclusion: SWE-Bench Pro Verified提供了一个更可信的基准测试，用以评估软件工程智能体的真实能力。

Abstract: SWE-Bench Pro has emerged as a standard benchmark for evaluating software engineering agents on challenging repository-level tasks. However, our analysis work show that its evaluation is undermined by two sources of unreliability: \textbf{reward hacking}, enabled by leakage of gold solutions or hidden evaluation information, and \textbf{task quality issues}, including misleading problem statements and improperly scoped tests. These issues can inflate benchmark performance and obscure agents' true coding ability. We present \textbf{SWE-Bench Pro Verified}, a verified version of SWE-Bench Pro that addresses both problems. Our approach combines \textbf{anti-hacking} safeguards that eliminate major leakage channels without disrupting normal agent functionality, with \textbf{task refinement} that minimally corrects inconsistencies within flawed instances. Evaluations on SWE-Bench Pro Verified reveal that some models perform substantially worse than previously reported, suggesting that existing results on SWE-Bench Pro may overestimate real software engineering capability. SWE-Bench Pro Verified offers a more trustworthy benchmark for assessing software engineering agents.

</details>


### [263] [LayerRoute: Action-Conditioned Mixture-of-Layers Routing for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06079)
*Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xingjie Fan,Zhisheng Chen,Yantang Qu,Meijia Chen,Tianyu Xin,Zirui Song,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRoute，一种动作条件化的表示路由接口，通过动态混合VLM分层表征并重用中间动作表示，使VLA策略能够灵活访问不同层次的视觉-语言特征，在LIBERO等基准上最高提升7.2个百分点，且额外参数量极少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略中，VLM信息仅通过固定的层分配给每个动作层暴露，不同操作任务需要对不同层的视觉-语义表征进行混合访问；同时，动作模块的中间状态仅通过残差流隐式传播而未被显式重用。这种有限的表示访问灵活性限制了VLA策略的性能。

Method: LayerRoute包含两大核心组件：1) Layer Mixture Router——一个动作条件化的路由模块，动态形成缓存VLM各层表征的混合；2) Action-State Reread——显式重用较早的动作表示以辅助后续决策。两者共同构成一个动作条件化的表示路由接口，实现灵活的跨层表征访问。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实世界基准上，LayerRoute一致地提升了StarVLA-π和π0.5的性能，在LIBERO Long上最高获得7.2的提升，仅增加0.31%/3.87%的额外参数。消融实验验证了动作条件化层路由的有效性，路由分析揭示了不同动作层和任务设置下结构化的分配模式。

Conclusion: LayerRoute通过动作条件化的表示路由机制，突破了现有VLA策略在表征访问上的固定性和隐式传播限制，以极低的参数开销获得了显著的性能提升，为VLA策略中跨层表征利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to guide action generation for robot control. VLMs provide hierarchical visual-semantic representations that evolve across layers, from local visual geometry to abstract, language-aligned semantics; different manipulation tasks may therefore require different mixtures of layer representations. Meanwhile, the action module maintains intermediate representations that evolve throughout action computation and may provide useful information for subsequent decisions. However, existing VLA interfaces offer limited flexibility in representation access: VLM information is exposed through fixed layer assignments for each action layer, while intermediate action states are only propagated implicitly through residual streams without explicit reuse. We introduce LayerRoute, an action-conditioned representation routing interface that enables adaptive access to VLM layers and action representations. The Layer Mixture Router dynamically forms mixtures of cached VLM representations, while Action-State Reread reuses earlier action representations. Across diverse simulation and real-world benchmarks, LayerRoute consistently improves StarVLA-$π$ and $π_{0.5}$, achieving up to 7.2 gains on LIBERO Long with only 0.31% / 3.87% additional parameters. Ablation studies validate the benefit of action-conditioned layer routing, while routing analyses reveal structured allocation patterns across action layers and task settings.

</details>


### [264] [Qiushi Engine on AstaBench E2E-Bench-Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08196)
*Wenhao Li,Shuxing Yang,Fujia Chen,Jincheng Mi,Yuang Pan,Rui Zhao,Zichen Li,Junyao Wu,Shenzhan Hong,Yaqi Li,Yize Wang,Kaihao Zhu,Taowen Deng,Junjie Yang,Hongsheng Chen,Yihao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了Qiushi Engine v0.8在AstaBench E2E-Bench-Hard基准的全部40项测试任务上的评估结果。在DeepSeek v4pro-preview作为后端模型的情况下，系统获得官方评分0.816，单任务平均成本约15.21美元（满分重算约81.59美元）。完整任务完成率达10%（4/40），远超官方Agent约3%的最佳水平；507项评分细则中有416项（82.1%）被满足。系统性短板集中在重复运行、外部依赖、指定指标和消融研究等方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证自主科研代理系统Qiushi Engine v0.8在端到端研究基准AstaBench上的真实能力，通过40个测试任务全面考察系统能否独立完成从实验设计、代码实现、实际执行到结果分析与报告交付的完整闭环，并与官方Agent的表现进行对比。

Method: 在AstaBench E2E-Bench-Hard的40个测试任务上评估Qiushi Engine v0.8（模型后端选用DeepSeek deepseek-v4pro-preview）。参考官方AstaBench排行榜评分结果（0.816）并进行了全精度本地重新计算（平均成本81.59±1.87美元）。通过507项必需评分细则的逐一核查、40份Meta-Trace记录的产出验证（涵盖报告、代码和实验产物）以及代表性案例分析来评估系统表现。

Result: 官方评分为0.816，平均基准成本15.209美元/任务；4项任务满足全部评分细则，完整任务完成率4/40=10%，超过官方Agent约3%最佳水平的约3.3倍；507项rubric中416项（82.1%）被满足。系统在报告、代码和实验产物的持续产出方面表现良好，主要缺口集中在重复运行、外部依赖、指定指标和消融研究四类任务类型上。

Conclusion: Qiushi Engine v0.8在端到端科研任务执行能力上显著超越AstaBench官方Agent，具备完整的科研工作流闭环能力并已通过一致性验证；但仍有约18%的评分要求未达标，尤其涉及实验复现（重复运行）、环境依赖管理和更严格的科研方法论（制定指标与消融研究）方面有待改进。

Abstract: This report analyzes Qiushi Engine v0.8 across all 40 test tasks in AstaBench E2E-Bench-Hard, a benchmark that requires autonomous agents to carry a research question through experimental design, code implementation, actual execution, result analysis, and report delivery. Qiushi Engine is model-configurable; this evaluation selected DeepSeek deepseek-v4pro-preview as the model backend. The official AstaBench leaderboard records a score of 0.816 and an average benchmark cost of USD 15.209 per task, while the full-precision local recomputation is $81.59 \pm 1.87$. Four tasks satisfied every rubric item, yielding a full-task completion rate of 4/40 = 10% -- 7 percentage points above, and about 3.3 times, the approximately 3% best rate reported for AstaBench's official agents. Across 507 required rubric items, 416 were satisfied (82.1%). Official scoring archives and 40 Meta-Trace records show sustained production and verification of reports, code, and experimental artifacts; the principal gaps lie in repeated runs, external dependencies, specified metrics, and ablation studies. The report explains the benchmark, system workflow, aggregate results, representative cases, and limits of interpretation.

</details>


### [265] [SAP: State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance for Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06124)
*Zichen Tian,Jinpeng Chen,Cheng Gong,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAP（State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance）方法，通过状态引导、参数溯源约束和回合级验证来合成高质量多轮工具使用数据，解决现有数据合成方法在参数级依赖上不足的问题，并训练出与更大规模模型竞争的SAP-4B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的多轮工具使用数据对训练智能体模型至关重要，但现有数据合成方法往往无法充分覆盖参数级依赖——即使模型选择了正确的工具，也可能因填入伪造、过期或弱grounding的参数值而导致任务执行失败。这限制了长时程（long-horizon）工具使用任务的性能。

Method: SAP结合三种机制高效构建具有长距离依赖和高准确率的工具调用轨迹：(1) 状态引导（state guidance）跟踪系统状态；(2) 工具参数溯源约束（tool-argument provenance constraints）确保参数值有可靠来源；(3) 回合级验证（turn-level validation）验证每一轮生成的正确性。基于SAP合成的数据训练出SAP-4B模型。

Result: 基于SAP合成数据训练的SAP-4B模型（仅4B参数）在多个基准上具备高度竞争力，可与规模大得多的模型相比拟。

Conclusion: SAP通过关注参数级溯源和状态引导，有效提升了多轮工具使用数据的质量，证明了高质量合成数据能够让小规模模型在工具使用任务上接近或超越更大模型的表现。

Abstract: High-quality multi-turn tool-use data is essential for training agentic models, yet existing data synthesis methods often underrepresent the argument-level dependencies that are critical to long-horizon tool use. As a result, even when a model selects the correct tool, task execution may still fail because the model fills tool arguments with fabricated, stale, or weakly grounded values. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance (SAP)}. SAP combines state guidance, tool-argument provenance constraints, and turn-level validation to efficiently construct tool-use trajectories with long-range dependencies and high accuracy. Using data generated by SAP, we build SAP-4B, which is highly competitive even when compared with much larger models across multiple benchmarks. Source code, synthesized data, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Zichen1024/SAP.

</details>


### [266] [EvolveScaler: Synthesizing Information-Evolution Contexts via Executable State Machines and Natural-Language Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08435)
*Ziliang Zhao,Zenan Xu,Shuting Wang,Zhao Wang,Bowen Cao,Minda Hu,Lincheng Li,Pluto Zhou,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolveScaler是一个代码驱动的信息演化(IE)基准生成框架，通过人类编写的操作规范让LLM合成自包含模拟器，生成自然语言多轮事件历史及确定性答案。共生成3.5万个训练样本和585个验证实例，最长序列约1200个事件。最强模型在very_long规模上仅达59.3% avg@5，多数模型低于10%；在内部模型训练后可实现跨8个外部基准平均5.25点的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文持久交互中，信息可随时间演化（后续事件可修订或撤销早期信息），现有基于文本的合成流水线的数据难以验证，因为状态转移和答案逻辑隐含而非显式，导致缺乏可验证且具有挑战性的信息演化评测基准。

Method: EvolveScaler采用代码驱动框架：1)人类专家编写操作规格（定义状态转移、记录有效性、难度控制与可执行答案逻辑）；2)强LLM根据规格合成自包含模拟器；3)执行经验证的模拟器生成自然语言多轮事件历史；4)通过确定性重放计算参考答案和原子化检查清单。实例包含117个任务原型和159个终题操作符，覆盖5种难度等级，约7至1200个事件。

Result: 产出约35,100个训练样本和585个验证实例。在very_long档，最强模型达到59.3% avg@5，而六个模型低于10%。在6000个EvolveScaler样本上训练内部A3B模型，在全部8个独立构建的分布外基准上均优于基座模型，平均提升5.25点。

Conclusion: 代码驱动的信息演化合成既提供了具有挑战性的评测基准，又提供了可迁移的训练监督信号，验证了该框架在长上下文信息演化任务上的有效性。

Abstract: In persistent interactions, long contexts may encode an evolving process rather than a fixed record: later events can revise or revoke earlier information, changing what remains valid and what conclusions follow. We call this setting information evolution (IE). Solving IE requires identifying valid records, applying updates in order, and reconstructing the query-relevant state from the event history. Existing text-first synthesis pipelines make such data difficult to verify because state transitions and answer logic remain implicit. We introduce EvolveScaler, a code-driven framework that defines information evolution before rendering it as natural language. Human-authored operational specifications define state transitions, record validity, difficulty controls, and executable answer logic; a strong LLM then synthesizes a self-contained simulator from each specification. Executing validated simulators produces natural-language multi-turn event histories, while deterministic replay computes reference answers and atomic checklists. We instantiate EvolveScaler with 117 task prototypes and 159 final-question operators across five difficulty levels spanning approximately 7 to 1,200 events per instance, yielding about 35,100 training examples and 585 validated evaluation instances. On the very_long tier, the strongest model reaches 59.3% avg@5, while six models score below 10%. Training an internal A3B model on 6,000 EvolveScaler examples improves performance over its base checkpoint on all eight independently constructed out-of-distribution benchmarks, with a 5.25-point average gain. These results show that code-driven IE synthesis provides both challenging evaluation and transferable training supervision.

</details>


### [267] [CWF: A Collaborative Writing Framework for Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06126)
*Ruibiao Fu,Di Tang,Yunlong Yang,Ran Wang,Sicheng Lu,Peixuan Wu,Xiaoyu Fan,Jiacheng Ma,HaoZhe Luo,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了个性化可靠科普写作任务，构建了包含39,134条数据的PSCB基准，并引入DA-MoE架构解耦受众适配与领域知识，以及多智能体事实核查机制，在PSCB上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传播需要根据不同读者的认知水平个性化调整表达，但提升个性化程度往往伴随简化导致的幻觉和事实扭曲风险增加。现有工作缺乏同时评估受众适配性与事实准确性的基准和方法。

Method: 1) 构建39,134条目的数据集和以读者为中心的PSCB基准，联合评估受众适配与事实准确性；2) 提出DA-MoE，将受众适配与领域知识显式解耦为独立建模，降低数据和计算需求并提升跨域泛化；3) 提出多智能体事实核查机制，通过角色特定辩论增强稀缺证据，并在图上传播置信度以支持稳健的事实校验与修订。

Result: 在PSCB基准上的实验表明，该方法取得了最先进的性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦受众建模与领域知识和多智能体协同事实核查，可以在个性化科普写作中兼顾受众适配性与事实准确性，为个性化科学传播提供了新的基准与有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing, a novel task that requires adapting scientific explanations to audiences with different cognitive levels while preserving factual accuracy. However, improving personalization often introduces simplifications that increase the risk of hallucination and factual distortion. To address these challenges, we first construct a dataset of 39,134 entries and a reader-centric Personalized Science Communication Benchmark (PSCB) that jointly evaluates audience adaptation and factual accuracy. To reduce data and computational requirements while improving generalization across domains and audiences, we introduce DA-MoE, which explicitly decouples audience adaptation from domain knowledge through separate modeling. To enable robust verification and revision in evidence-scarce scenarios, a multi-agent fact-checking mechanism that augments limited evidence with role-specific agent debate and propagates confidence over a graph is proposed. Experiments on PSCB show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/DPInnovationWorks/CWF.

</details>


### [268] [API Benchmark Scores Do Not Reliably Transfer to Chatbot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08861)
*Jennifer Wang,Joachim Baumann,Daniel E. Ho,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过审计ChatGPT、Claude和Gemini的七个系统、九个基准测试，发现API评估与界面评估之间存在系统性的准确性和一致性差异：API评估在准确率上平均高出3.4个百分点，测试-重测一致性高出2.1个百分点。对于ChatGPT，API与界面之间的性能差异甚至超过了GPT 5.3与GPT 5.4之间的模型代际差异。暴露的API控制参数（系统提示、采样参数、推理设置）无法可靠地弥合这一差距，揭示了一个"上下文有效性鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试分数是模型发布的核心货币，影响购买决策、公众信任和政策制定。然而，基准测试的一个关键假设是：通过API测得的性能能忠实反映部署系统的实际行为。作者旨在质疑这一假设，因为API评估可能无法完全代表用户在实际界面中体验到的模型表现。

Method: 对ChatGPT、Claude和Gemini共七个系统进行系统审计，涵盖九个基准测试，测量通用能力、社会偏见和谄媚性（sycophancy）。比较API访问与界面访问在准确率和测试-重测一致性上的差异；进一步通过改变系统提示、采样参数和推理设置来测试API控制参数是否能复现界面行为。

Result: API评估在准确率上平均比界面评估高出3.4个百分点，测试-重测一致性高出2.1个百分点。对于ChatGPT，API与界面的性能差异超过了GPT 5.3与GPT 5.4之间的模型代际差异。调整API的控制参数（系统提示、采样参数、推理设置）仅在部分情况下改变行为，无法可靠地消除API与界面之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了"上下文有效性鸿沟"（context-validity gap）：通过API获得的测量结果不一定能推广到对应的部署界面。这严重质疑了将API评估作为部署系统性能代理的做法，对基准测试的有效性和模型发布的透明性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Benchmark scores are a central currency in model releases: they inform purchasing decisions, shape public trust, and influence policy. Yet, a key assumption underlying benchmark scores is that the model performance measured through APIs faithfully reflects the behavior of deployed systems.
  We challenge this assumption by auditing ChatGPT, Claude, and Gemini across seven systems and nine benchmarks spanning general capability, social bias, and sycophancy. We find systematic API--interface differences in both accuracy and consistency. On average, API evaluations score 3.4 percentage points higher in accuracy and 2.1 percentage points higher in test--retest agreement than corresponding interface evaluations. For ChatGPT, the performance difference between API and interface access exceeds the API-only difference between GPT 5.3 and GPT 5.4. Put differently, switching access surfaces can degrade performance as much as downgrading a full model generation.
  We further test whether exposed API controls can reproduce interface behavior by varying system prompts, sampling parameters, and reasoning settings. These controls shift behavior in some cases but do not reliably eliminate the gap. Our findings document a context-validity gap: measurements obtained through APIs do not necessarily generalize to corresponding deployed interfaces, complicating the use of API evaluations as proxies for deployed systems.

</details>


### [269] [Substrate-Portable Execution for Production LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06128)
*Tarun Gopinath,Atul Kulkarni,Vijay Rajakumar,Shrikar Katti,Parthasarathy Govindarajen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种绑定自适应的Agent执行平台，将工作流定义与执行底层分离，允许同一工作流在实时流式服务、异步SWF编排和分布式Flink批处理三种运行时上无代码改动地执行，在大幅降低批处理推理成本的同时保持输出质量无差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM Agent（如亚马逊的Rufus助手）需要支持实时服务、异步后台任务和高吞吐量批处理等不同执行模式，每种模式有不同的服务等级目标和运行时。然而现有的执行语义往往耦合在单一运行时上，导致跨模式复用阻塞、无法利用提供50%折扣的批处理推理API。

Method: 提出绑定自适应的Agent执行平台：(1) 开发者以类型化数据流图定义一次工作流；(2) 平台将图编译为三种底层：进程内流式处理（实时服务）、AWS SWF持久化编排（异步执行）、Apache Flink分布式流处理（批处理推理）；(3) LLM推理表示为可挂起的图节点，其行为随底层而变化——在线流式投递、异步持久重试、离线批量提交。

Result: 在五种编排模式（单推理RAG、迭代ReAct、组合PreAct、条件路由、多智能体深度研究）上验证了数十个生产Agent配置。三种绑定下输出质量无可检测差异；批处理执行将每次查询推理成本降低到与批量API定价一致的水平，并在生产规模下与流式路径并行运行。

Conclusion: 通过解耦工作流定义与执行底层，可以实现跨实时/异步/批处理模式的统一Agent部署，在不牺牲输出质量的前提下充分利用批量推理API的成本优势。

Abstract: Production LLM agents execute tool-calling loops, retrieval chains, and compositional workflows in multiple modes, yet execution semantics are often coupled to one runtime. We encountered this portability problem in Rufus, a conversational AI assistant with a large tool catalog that serves millions of Amazon customers. Rufus supports real-time serving, asynchronous background tasks, and high-volume batch workloads such as evaluation and content pregeneration. Each mode has distinct service-level objectives and typically uses a separate runtime. Reusing streaming orchestration makes asynchronous and batch workloads blocking and prevents use of batch inference APIs, which offer a 50 percent discount at published prices. We present a binding-adaptive agent execution platform that separates workflow definition from execution substrate. Developers define a workflow once as a typed dataflow graph. The platform compiles the graph to in-process streaming for real-time serving, durable AWS SWF orchestration for asynchronous execution, or distributed Apache Flink stream processing for batch inference. No workflow code changes are required. LLM inference is represented as a suspendable graph node whose behavior depends on the substrate: streaming delivery online, durable retry asynchronously, and batched submission offline. We validated dozens of production agent configurations across five orchestration patterns: single-inference RAG, iterative ReAct, compositional PreAct, conditional routing, and multi-agent deep research. Across all three bindings, we found no detectable difference in output quality. Batch execution reduced per-query inference cost in line with published batch API pricing while operating alongside the streaming path at production scale.

</details>


### [270] [MeClear: Cooperative Game-Theoretic Attribution and Risk-Aware Memory Clearance for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09115)
*Boyu Yang,Jiazheng Sun,Zilong Lu,Zhi Qiu,Xin Peng,Jun Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MeClear，一个基于任务条件的内存清理框架，通过合作式归属和选择性抑制策略，识别并清除长时程LLM代理外部记忆中对下游任务产生负面效用的记忆证据，在不永久修改记忆库的前提下提升任务恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM代理依赖外部记忆系统维持用户偏好和任务知识，但传统检索机制仅优化语义兼容性而忽视下游效用，常将过时、误导或冲突的证据引入活动上下文，且单一移除评估无法解决冗余冲突掩蔽问题。

Method: MeClear采用两阶段策略：(1) 结合Leave One Out筛选与采样协同Shapley归属，将效用分布到交互证据上，解决冗余冲突掩蔽；(2) 利用归属排名执行查询范围的嵌套过滤最小化清理策略，在清理后上下文上验证任务恢复，不永久改变持久记忆库。

Result: 在十个长对话记忆池上的实验表明，MeClear达到85.9%的目标召回率和82.3%的总体任务恢复率，较Leave One Out基线提升25.5个百分点。

Conclusion: MeClear证明通过任务条件的合作式内存清理而非单纯语义匹配，能有效识别并抑制负面效用记忆，显著提升长时程LLM代理的任务表现，且保持持久记忆库的完整性与可逆性。

Abstract: Long horizon Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external memory systems to preserve user preferences and task knowledge across extended interactions. Conventional retrieval mechanisms optimize semantic compatibility rather than downstream utility, frequently introducing outdated, misleading, or conflicting evidence into the active context. We present MeClear, a task conditioned memory clearance framework that identifies memories featuring negative downstream utility through cooperative attribution and selectively suppresses them from agent execution. MeClear combines Leave One Out screening with sampled cooperative Shapley attribution to distribute utility across interacting evidence, effectively resolving redundant conflict masking where single removal evaluations fail. Utilizing attribution rankings, MeClear executes a query scoped minimal clearance strategy over a nested filtration, verifying task recovery on the cleared context without permanently altering the persistent memory bank. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across ten long dialogue memory pools demonstrate that MeClear achieves a target recall of 85.9% and an overall task recovery rate of 82.3%, representing a 25.5 percentage point improvement over Leave One Out (LOO) baselines.

</details>


### [271] [IIns-VAE+: A Robust Transfer Learning Framework for Environmental Identification in Wireless Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06131)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Bobai Zhao,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IIns-VAE+混合模型，将IIns-VAE框架与Minimax风险分类器（MRC）相结合，以解决6G ISAC系统中无线感知环境识别任务在域偏移下深度学习模型泛化能力不足的问题，并在三个迁移学习场景中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G一体化感知与通信（ISAC）系统的环境识别对可靠态势感知至关重要，但深度学习模型在跨环境域偏移（domain shift）下泛化能力差。现有IIns-VAE虽能学习丰富表征的特征，但其神经分类器仍易受分布变化影响，需要提升其在迁移学习场景中的适应性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出IIns-VAE+混合模型，将IIns-VAE表征学习框架与Minimax风险分类器（MRC）相结合，通过在概率框架下构造最坏情况风险上界来增强分类器的分布鲁棒性，从而提升模型在迁移学习中的适应能力。

Result: 使用真实世界数据集在三个迁移学习场景（一般环境到特定房间环境、高标签分辨率到低标签分辨率、混合环境到特定环境）中进行评估，实验结果表明IIns-VAE+在这些场景中均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: IIns-VAE+在跨域迁移场景中展现了关键价值，是构建未来6G系统中适应性强、鲁棒性好的感知网络的重要方案。

Abstract: Environmental identification in wireless sensing is essential for 6G integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems to achieve reliable situational awareness. However, deep learning (DL) models for this task often fail to generalize under domain shift across diverse environments. While the Inter-Instance Variational Auto-encoder (IIns-VAE) learns features of rich representation, its neural classifier remains vulnerable to these distribution changes. In this paper, we propose IIns-VAE+, a hybrid model that combines the IIns-VAE framework with Minimax Risk Classifiers (MRC) to improve adaptability in transfer learning scenarios. We use real-world datasets to evaluate our framework across three transfer learning scenarios, including general to specific room environments, high to low label resolutions, and mixed to specific environments. The experimental results indicate that IIns-VAE+ significantly outperforms baselines, demonstrating its critical value in building adaptable and robust perceptive networks in future 6G systems.

</details>


### [272] [ExecCritic: Learn to Test, Test to Improve for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09133)
*Leitian Tao,Baolin Peng,Haorui Wang,Hang Wang,Hao Cheng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Tao Ge,Sharon Li,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExecCritic 提出一种测试-验证-修订的脚手架框架配合角色专属强化学习训练方法，将测试生成与代码修复分离，使编码代理能通过可靠的执行反馈来修复仓库代码，在 SWE-bench Verified 上将解决率从 61.2% 基线提升至 72.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈只有在测试真正捕捉了 issue 所请求行为时才能有效指导编码代理进行正确的仓库修复。然而，代理生成的测试可能编码了不完整或错误的行为目标；当同一个轨迹同时生成补丁和测试时，两者的错误可能相互一致，产生虚假的置信度，导致反馈误导修复过程。

Method: ExecCritic 包含两个核心要素：(1) test-verify-revise 脚手架：将测试构建与源码修复分离——Test 代理独立生成仓库原生测试，fail-closed 验证机制负责测试资格审定并冻结测试；Repair 代理基于这些测试的执行反馈修改源码而不改动测试。(2) 角色专属强化学习配方：先通过 Learn to Test 训练 Test 代理生成能区分正确与错误补丁的行为有效测试；再通过 Test to Improve 训练 Repair 代理同时学习直接任务求解和基于反馈的修订能力。两个角色均以 Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B 为骨干网络并分别训练。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，测试质量决定反馈是否有帮助：固定基础 Repair 代理时，基础 Test 代理生成的测试将无测试基线的解决率从 61.2% 降至 57.3%，而 GPT-5.6-sol 生成的测试则提升至 65.3%。角色专属后训练使 Qwen Test 代理的 Base-to-Gold 成功率从 22.2% 提升到 62.2%；组合两个后训练的 Qwen 代理达到 72.6%，相比原始无测试基线获得 11.4 个百分点的提升，且在评估时无需更强模型或 Oracle 反馈。

Conclusion: 测试质量是执行反馈有效性的决定因素，通过角色分离与角色专属强化学习训练，可以使同规模的开源模型在不依赖更强外援的情况下获得显著提升的仓库修复能力，验证了测试生成与代码修复解耦的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Execution feedback can guide coding agents toward correct repository repairs, but only when the tests capture the behavior requested by the issue. Agent-generated tests can encode incomplete or incorrect behavioral targets; when the same trajectory writes both the patch and the test, their errors can agree and create false confidence. We introduce ExecCritic, combining a test--verify--revise scaffold with a role-specific reinforcement learning recipe for training agents within it. The scaffold separates test construction from source-code repair: a Test agent independently generates repository-native tests, a fail-closed harness qualifies and freezes them, and a Repair agent revises source code from their execution feedback without changing the tests. Both roles use Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B as the backbone and are trained separately. In Learn to Test, the Test agent learns to produce behaviorally valid tests that distinguish correct from incorrect patches. In Test to Improve, the Repair agent learns both direct task resolution and feedback-guided revision. On SWE-bench Verified, test quality determines whether feedback helps: holding the base Repair agent fixed, tests from the base Test agent reduce resolved rate from a no-test baseline of 61.2% to 57.3%, whereas tests from GPT-5.6-sol raise it to 65.3%. Role-specific post-training raises the Qwen Test agent's Base-to-Gold success from 22.2% to 62.2%; composing the two post-trained Qwen agents reaches 72.6%, an 11.4-point gain over the original no-test baseline without stronger-model or Oracle feedback at evaluation time. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MSR-Orchard/execcritic.

</details>


### [273] [MVFA: A Multi-View Text-Guided Multimodal Fusion LLM Adapter for Sentiment Analysis and Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06188)
*Pengfei Shao,Jisheng Dang,Jiawen Fang,Ning Liu,Wencan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Jianhuang Lai,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MVFA（多视图文本引导多模态融合适配器），一种参数高效框架，通过将冻结的大语言模型（LLM）增强多模态推理能力，用于对话中的多模态情感分析和情绪识别。MVFA使用最大池化、均值池化和注意力池化构建互补文本视图以引导跨模态交互，并通过增强Q-Former融合模块将多模态表示压缩为可学习的伪令牌。在ChatGLM3-6B-base、LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B三个冻结LLM骨干上验证，并在CH-SIMS V2.0、MELD和CHERMA三个数据集上取得SOTA性能，仅更新少量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析和对话情绪识别需要有效建模文本、声学和视觉模态间的异构交互。虽然LLM提供强大的语言理解能力，但将其适配到多模态情感计算面临挑战：全模型微调计算成本过高，而现有轻量级适配器在跨模态融合过程中往往无法保留丰富的文本语义线索。

Method: MVFA框架首先通过最大池化、均值池化和注意力池化构建三种互补的文本视图，随后以这些视图引导音频和视觉特征的跨模态交互。融合后的多模态表示通过增强Q-Former融合模块被压缩为紧凑的可学习伪令牌集，从而以参数高效方式适配冻结的LLM骨干网络。

Result: 在CH-SIMS V2.0上达到84.62% Acc2和84.59% F1，在MELD上达到67.36% Acc和66.03% WF1，在CHERMA上达到74.66% Acc，均超过现有SOTA性能，且仅需更新一小部分参数。在三种不同LLM骨干（ChatGLM3-6B、LLaMA2-7B、Qwen3-8B）上均验证了良好的可移植性。

Conclusion: 多视图文本引导融合被证明是参数高效多模态LLM适配在情感计算领域的一种有效且可扩展的范式，代码已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis and emotion recognition in conversations demand effective modeling of heterogeneous interactions across textual, acoustic, and visual modalities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer powerful language understanding, adapting them to multimodal affective computing remains challenging: full-model fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive, while many existing lightweight adapters fail to preserve rich textual cues during cross-modal fusion. To address these limitations, we propose the multi-view text-guided multimodal fusion adapter (MVFA), a parameter-efficient framework that augments frozen LLMs with strong multimodal reasoning capability. MVFA first constructs complementary text views via max pooling, mean pooling, and attention pooling; these views then guide cross-modal interactions with audio and visual features. The fused multimodal representations are subsequently compressed into a compact set of learnable pseudo-tokens through an Enhanced Q-Former Fusion Module. Using ChatGLM3-6B-base as the primary backbone, we further validate MVFA on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B to examine its portability across multiple frozen LLM backbones. MVFA is evaluated on three challenging datasets: CH-SIMS V2.0, MELD, and CHERMA. Experimental results demonstrate that MVFA achieves state-of-the-art performance on key metrics while updating only a small fraction of parameters. Specifically, it attains 84.62\% Acc2 and 84.59\% F1 on CH-SIMS V2.0, 67.36\% Acc and 66.03\% WF1 on MELD, and 74.66\% Acc on CHERMA. These findings establish multi-view text-guided fusion as an effective and scalable paradigm for parameter-efficient multimodal LLM adaptation in affective computing. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Overwhelm1208/MVFA.

</details>


### [274] [SCIRIGOR:Evaluating Open-Ended Scientific Analysis Beyond Final Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06192)
*Bowen Liu,Shuo Nie,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciRIGOR——一个评估框架和基准，用于验证科学编码智能体生成的代码、结果、图表和结论之间是否具有可验证的证据支持链，而非仅检查最终输出的表面一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体（scientific coding agents）会产生相互依赖的代码、结果、图形和结论，单纯评估最终输出无法判断其科学结论是否有据可依。现有评估方法忽视了从数据到结论的完整证据链验证问题。

Method: 提出SciRIGOR评估框架，包含来自6个领域、17个子领域的100个科学文章案例。框架通过重建类型化证据图，区分工件保真度（artifact fidelity）与关系有效性（relational validity），并沿完整的claim-support路径进行评分，同时定位最早出现的不支持关系。源结果grounded的替代路径允许科学等价的分析和可视化方式。

Result: 评估了11种agent/模型配置。在完整基准运行中，结论与可信和不可信结果的一致性几乎相同（91.8%对91.0%），但没有任何系统在软证据链评分上超过62.6%，在严格全链成功率上超过18.0%。

Conclusion: 内部一致性不能证明科学正确性：评估必须沿着从数据到结论的完整路径验证证据支持关系，而非仅依赖输出间的表面一致性。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents produce interdependent code, results, figures, and claims, yet evaluating final
  outputs alone does not establish whether their conclusions are scientifically supported. We formulate
  evidence-grounded multimodal scientific analysis, requiring agents to produce executable analyses and claims
  supported by results and visualizations from the same run. We introduce SciRIGOR, an evaluation framework and
  benchmark comprising 100 cases from scientific articles across six domains and 17 subfields. The framework
  reconstructs typed evidence graphs, separates artifact fidelity from relational validity, and scores complete
  claim-support paths while localizing the earliest unsupported relation. Source-grounded alternative paths
  accommodate scientifically equivalent analyses and visualizations. We evaluate 11 agent/model configurations.
  On full-benchmark runs, claims agree with faithful and unfaithful results at nearly identical rates (91.8%
  versus 91.0%). Yet no system exceeds 62.6% on the soft evidence-chain score or 18.0% strict whole-chain
  success. These findings show that internal coherence does not establish scientific correctness: evaluation
  must verify support along the complete data-to-claim path.

</details>


### [275] [Predicting Wind Turbine Power Using Machine Learning and Weather Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06194)
*Khivishta Boodhoo,Isaac Triguero,Josh Plumbly,Bruce Nicolson,Nicholas Watson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基线线性回归和更复杂的神经网络模型，仅使用天气条件预测海上风力涡轮机功率输出，探讨了数据量、特征选择和数据预处理的影响。ANN模型取得R²=0.98、MAE=194的最佳性能，优于基线（R²=0.94，MAE=441），并能识别4小时低功率窗口用于维护调度，每次维护可节省约2000 kW功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风力涡轮机维护会导致强制停机，降低发电效率。准确的功率预测可识别低功率时段以安排维护，但现有研究未系统探讨数据量、特征选择和数据预处理对预测模型性能的影响，且当前模型在不同涡轮机之间迁移性有限。

Method: 使用基线线性回归与复杂的人工神经网络（ANN）对比，仅依赖天气条件增强适用性；系统性研究多种数据预处理技术、一个月与一年数据的训练量影响；使用随机森林回归器探索特征选择；利用邻近气象站的独立天气数据集实现模型迁移。

Result: ANN模型取得最高精度（R²=0.98，MAE=194），优于基线模型（R²=0.94，MAE=441）；模型性能与过往研究相当；ANN模型成功识别了2个月内4小时低功率预测窗口，每次维护事件可节省约2000 kW功率输出。

Conclusion: ANN模型结合独立天气数据源可提供高精度且具跨位置迁移能力的功率预测，为维护调度提供有效决策支持，可通过识别低功率窗口减少维护导致的总功率损失。

Abstract: Offshore wind turbines are widely used to generate renewable energy, but their maintenance can result in decreased efficiency due to forced shutdowns. Accurate wind turbine power predictions can identify periods of low power that would be ideal for scheduling maintenance. However, the effects of data volume, feature selection, and data preprocessing on the performance of such power prediction models have not been thoroughly studied. Besides, current models have limited transferability between different wind turbines. Therefore, this study developed a baseline Linear Regression for performance comparison with a more complex Artificial Neural Network model to predict the power output of a wind turbine, using weather conditions only to enhance applicability. A range of data preprocessing techniques were studied, and models were trained on one month and one year of data to determine the effects of data preprocessing and volume on model performance. Feature selection was explored using a Random Forest Regressor. The best results from the different models showed that the Artificial Neural Network models provided the highest accuracy, with an R2 score of 0.98 and a low Mean Absolute Error of 194, when compared with the baseline model (R2 score of 0.94 and Mean Absolute Error of 441). The model performance is comparable to the range of results in past studies, with the advantage that the proposed method leverages a separate weather dataset from a nearby weather station, enabling future applications for similar wind turbines in different locations. The Artificial Neural Network model was then used to identify 4-h periods of low power predictions over 2 months (simulating application for future periods), providing power output savings of approximately 2000 kW for each maintenance event.

</details>


### [276] [AutoKD: Autonomous Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06366)
*Qinwen Ge,Bo Ni,Haowei Fu,Ngoc N. Tran,Erik Blasch,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoKD是一个多智能体自主知识发现框架，通过六个协调的LLM智能体在开放发现循环中协作，将验证后的发现持久化存储到洞察图中，作为长期记忆和探索导向机制，实现了计算性与累积性的自主知识发现。在三个数据集上的评估表明，AutoKD在开放质量和条件质量两个角度均能覆盖已知发现并揭示补充人类研究的新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据丰富领域的科学发现受限于人类精力：真实世界数据的规模和复杂度增长远超研究者阅读、推理和综合分析的速度。现有基于LLM的多智能体系统虽能自动化部分研究环节，但仅针对无法自动验证的假设生成场景，且每次运行为一次性过程，缺乏发现积累和引导后续探索的机制。

Method: AutoKD设计了一个由六个协调LLM智能体组成的多智能体框架，运行于开放式的发现循环中。在此循环中，被接受的发现被存储在一个持久的洞察图（insight graph）中，该图同时充当长期记忆与探索导向机制，使已验证发现能够持续存在并引导后续查询。

Result: AutoKD在三个不同数据集上从两个评估视角进行了评估：开放式质量（对比已发表发现）和条件式质量（通过文献派生查询）。在两个评估视角下，AutoKD均能覆盖已知发现，并涌现出能补充人类驱动研究的实质性新发现。

Conclusion: AutoKD通过将验证发现持久化并用于引导后续探索，实现了兼具计算性和累积性的自主知识发现，有效缓解了人类研究带宽瓶颈，为数据密集领域的科学发现有前景的自动化范式。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in data-rich domains is currently constrained by human bandwidth: the growth in the volume and complexity of real-world data far outpaces the rate at which researchers can read, reason, and synthesize. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have begun to automate portions of the research cycle, but they target hypothesis generation in settings where validation cannot itself be automated, and each run is one-shot, with no mechanism for findings to accumulate or steer subsequent inquiry. This paper introduces AutoKD, a multi-agent framework for autonomous knowledge discovery that is both computational and cumulative, allowing validated findings to persist and inform subsequent inquiry. Six coordinated LLM agents collaborate in an open-ended discovery loop, where accepted findings are stored in a persistent insight graph that serves as both long-term memory and an exploration-steering mechanism. We evaluate AutoKD on three diverse datasets from two perspectives: Open-ended Quality against published findings, and Conditioned Quality via literature-derived queries. Across both evaluation perspectives, AutoKD covers known findings and surfaces substantive discoveries that complement human-driven research. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeQinwen/AutoKD.

</details>


### [277] [Building Trustworthy Graph-Agentic RAG for Social Good: Architectures, Failure Propagation, and Assurance by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06391)
*Vijay Bommireddy,Raviteja Bommireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析图智能检索增强生成（Graph-agentic RAG）系统在社会公益场景下的设计评估问题，指出其在提供结构化证据与自适应推理能力的同时，存在"证据到行动"的耦合失败链风险。论文按图基质、图生命周期、代理功能、协调模式与权力边界对文献进行分类，提出基于五项界面契约（证据、检索、推理、能力与委派、结果）的"依构造保证"（assurance-by-construction）蓝图，并给出涵盖图断言、轨迹、主张、协调与结果的评估议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图智能RAG系统在跨文档、实体、时间或机构的复杂问答中有效，但其耦合式失败路径（图构建缺陷可变为检索证据、改变控制决策并传播至严重后果）在涉及新鲜度、授权、可追溯性、监督与追索等社会公益场景中构成严重威胁，需要系统化的设计与评估框架。

Method: 本文采用文献综述与框架构建相结合的方法：按图基质、图生命周期、代理功能、协调模式及权力边界五个维度组织文献，区分基于图的检索与依赖观察的图控制，将报告的风险综合为"证据-行动"失败链，并据此提出包含五项界面契约的依构造保证蓝图及相应的评估议程。

Result: 提出一个覆盖系统边界的五项界面契约框架（证据、检索、推理、能力与委派、结果），使来源追踪、时间有效性、授权、不确定性与可恢复性在系统边界显式化；以公益信息设计为例说明框架如何约束图结构、权限、弃权与操作权力；最终导出涵盖图断言、轨迹、主张、协调与结果的五层评估议程。

Conclusion: 图智能RAG系统在社会公益场景中的可靠性不能仅靠事后评测保障，而需通过在设计边界上显式确立契约实现"依构造保证"，从而打断从证据缺陷到行动后果的耦合失败传播链；现有研究对此尚缺乏系统性覆盖，本文框架为该方向铺平了道路。

Abstract: Graph-agentic retrieval-augmented generation combines structured evidence with adaptive controllers that can plan retrieval, traverse relations, verify intermediate claims, delegate subtasks, and use tools. This combination is useful when answers depend on relations across documents, entities, time, or institutions, but it also creates coupled failure paths: a defect in graph construction can become retrieved evidence, alter later control decisions, and propagate toward a consequential outcome. We examine how such systems should be designed and evaluated for social-good settings in which freshness, authorization, traceability, oversight, and recourse matter alongside answer quality. We organize the literature by graph substrate, graph lifecycle, agent function, coordination pattern, and authority boundary, and distinguish graph-based retrieval from observation-dependent graph control. We then synthesize reported risks as an evidence-to-action failure chain and propose an assurance-by-construction blueprint comprising five interface contracts for evidence, retrieval, reasoning, capability and delegation, and outcome. These contracts make provenance, temporal validity, authorization, uncertainty, and recoverability explicit at system boundaries. An illustrative public-benefit information design shows how the framework constrains graph structure, permissions, abstention, and operating authority. Finally, we derive an evaluation agenda spanning graph assertions, trajectories, claims, coordination, and outcomes.

</details>


### [278] [From Concentration to Differentiation and Back: Routing Effective Rank in MoE Reasoning Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06403)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出路由有效秩d_eff，一种基于MoE专家路由相似性构建跨rollout图的无标签诊断指标，用于刻画测试时推理扩展中MoE推理队列的内部计算重组过程。实验发现d_eff呈现可复现的低-高-低轨迹，且峰值时机随架构与推理努力系统变化；通过精确分解将轨迹拆分为共性模态与残差谱两部分，后者贡献约四分之一并保有可观变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）会产生大量推理rollout，但目前缺乏无标签的、标准化的框架来描述这些推理过程内部计算如何在推理展开过程中重组。需要一个可分解的、无标签的诊断工具来理解MoE推理队列在推理时间上的分化与再集中行为。

Method: 提出路由有效秩（routing effective rank, d_eff），定义为基于MoE专家路由相似性构建的跨rollout图上熵有效维度。研究覆盖10种MoE配置和5个数学/科学基准，对3,105个模型-问题队列进行分析。通过精确分解将d_eff轨迹拆分为队列范围的共性模态（common-mode）质量与残差谱维度，并检验共性模态集中度与同答案可恢复性、推理努力之间的预测关系。

Result: 在98.5%的3,105个模型-问题队列中观察到低-高-低的d_eff轨迹，内部出现明显峰值（路由相似性先集中、中间预算最大化分化、后期再集中）。共性模态再分配约占轨迹的三分之二，残差谱贡献约四分之一。非一致队列中共性模态集中度的增长强预测同答案可恢复性。更高推理努力将峰值延迟2.59个八度（令牌预算翻倍次数），并一致扩展所有四种测试架构的高秩期，表明努力效应位于时机与持续时间而非峰值幅度。正确性对比将结构监测与答案选择分离开来。

Conclusion: 路由有效秩是一个可分解、无标签的谱系诊断工具，为理解MoE推理队列在推理时间上的分化与再集中提供原则性视角，可用于结构监测与答案选择的分离分析。

Abstract: Test-time scaling produces cohorts of reasoning rollouts, yet there is no standard label-free account of how their internal computation reorganizes as inference unfolds. We introduce routing effective rank deff, the entropy-effective dimensionality of a cross-rollout graph built from MoE expert-routing similarity. Across ten MoE configurations and five math/science benchmarks, deff exhibits a reproducible low-high-low trajectory, with a prominent interior maximum in 98.5% of 3,105 model-question cohorts: routing similarity is concentrated early, maximally differentiated at intermediate budgets, and reconcentrated later, and the timing of this maximum varies systematically with architecture and reasoning effort. An exact decomposition separates cohort-wide common-mode mass from residual spectral dimensionality: common-mode reallocation accounts for about two thirds of the trajectory, while the residual spectrum contributes about one quarter and retains substantial variation beyond the common mode. The decomposition further localizes behavior: among non-unanimous cohorts, increases in common-mode concentration strongly predict same-answer recoverability, and higher reasoning effort delays the maximum by 2.59 octaves (doublings of the token budget) and consistently expands the high-rank period across all four tested architectures, locating the effort effect in timing and duration rather than peak amplitude. Correctness comparisons separate structural monitoring from answer selection, positioning routing effective rank as a decomposable, label-free diagnostic of cohort organization - a principled spectral lens on how MoE reasoning cohorts differentiate and reconcentrate over inference time.

</details>


### [279] [Causal Attribution for Agentic Decisions: Estimators, Coupling, and a Traceability Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06445)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对高风险AI系统的agentic可追溯性记录问题，提出estimator框架，证明边际总效应与公共随机数总效应两类估计量在因果归因中均失效：边际估计下惰性步骤与决定性步骤产生相同效应（代数恒等式）；公共随机数下决定性步骤在翻转运行中精确为零而其直接效应为0.25。论文推导了保持直接效应可估的耦合方法，指出抑制情境下中介份额可能超过1并扭曲排序，并针对欧盟AI法案第86条解释权（2026年8月生效）与第12条日志/附件IV文档（2027年12月生效）之间的立法窗口，提供了可追溯性规范建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险AI系统提供商必须保存使决策可追溯的记录，但agentic系统中"这些记录应包含什么才能实现事后因果归因"尚未确立。同时EU AI法案出现时间窗口矛盾：第86条解释权先于第12条日志与附件IV文档要求生效，形成证据支持的法律空白。

Method: 提出estimator框架并检验其失效条件；分离边际总效应（前人测量）与公共随机数总效应（隔离步骤自身贡献）；叠加下游固定的自然直接效应；用人工推导的planted chain验证估计量；推导保持上下文分歧后直接效应可估的耦合，给出其退化的闭式解；发布预注册而非实验结果。

Result: 两类估计量在相同方向上失效。边际估计量下，因果惰性步骤与决定性步骤在每次运行中具有相同的总效应（代数恒等式而非抽样巧合）。公共随机数下，决定性步骤在执行步骤翻转（约1/10运行）时精确返回零，而其直接效应为0.25且能证明其起作用。在直接与中介路径相反对抗的抑制情境下，中介份额超过1，导致抑制组件排名高于纯中介者。

Conclusion: 本文表明agentic系统的事后因果归因不能依赖现有总效应估计量；精确为零不意味着步骤无作用。贡献包括：可追溯性规范规格以满足欧盟AI法案第86条解释权与第12条/附件IV日志文档之间的立法缺口期间的归档需求；并强调pipeline不可用导致只能发布预注册而非结果本身。

Abstract: A provider of a high-risk AI system must keep records that make a decision traceable, and for agentic systems it has not been established what those records must contain for post-hoc causal attribution to be possible. We give the estimator framework and then the conditions under which it fails. We separate the marginal total effect that prior work measures from a common-random-numbers total effect that isolates a step's own contribution, add the natural direct effect under a pinned downstream, and check the estimators against hand derivations. Both estimands then fail, in the same direction. Under the marginal estimand a causally inert step has the identical total effect to the decisive one on every run of our planted chain, an algebraic identity and not a coincidence at one draw. Under common random numbers the decisive step returns exactly zero on the runs where the executing step flips, about one in ten, while its direct effect there is 0.25 and it demonstrably acts; an exact zero does not certify that a step did nothing, and we put that here rather than in the limitations. We derive the coupling that keeps the direct effect estimable once contexts diverge, with a closed form for its degradation, and show that the mediated share on which a natural ranking is built is not a share under suppression: where the direct and mediated paths oppose, it exceeds one and ranks a suppressed component above a pure mediator. We publish the discrepancy experiment's pre-registration rather than a result, because the live pipeline it requires was not available in the study window. We contribute the traceability specification such a filing would need, against a gap the Act's calendar opens: Article 86's right to an explanation has applied since 2 August 2026, while the Article 12 logging and Annex IV documentation that could evidence one were deferred to 2 December 2027 by Regulation (EU) 2026/1744.

</details>


### [280] [MARBO: Relational Belief Grounding for LLM Agents in Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06563)
*Hwang Yechan,Bae Sangjun,Kim Jeongmo,Bang Sangwoo,Han Seungyul*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARBO是一个基于信念驱动的多智能体偏好优化框架，通过将关系信念显式融入社会推理游戏(如狼人杀等)中LLM智能体的行动与对话策略，使轻量级LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下获得一致且优于现有基线策略的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交推理游戏(SDGs)要求智能体在部分可观测环境下维护关于隐藏角色和团队阵营的关系信念。现有LLM智能体方法虽然通过提示工程和偏好优化提升了游戏表现，但往往不将动作和游戏内言语显式地锚定在这些信念上，导致策略不一致，尤其在紧凑（参数较小）的LLM智能体上更为突出。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Relational Belief Optimization (MARBO)框架，利用关系信念引导策略决策和游戏内对话的生成。仅在行为受可靠关系信念支持且能带来策略性有利社交结果时提供偏好反馈，从而在不确定性下鼓励更一致的策略学习。

Result: 在代表性SDGs上，MARBO使紧凑LLM智能体一致性地超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 将关系信念显式注入偏好优化循环是提升紧凑LLM智能体在社交推理游戏中策略一致性及表现的有效途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) require agents to reason under partial observability by maintaining relational beliefs about hidden roles and team alignments. While recent LLM-agent approaches improve gameplay through prompting and preference optimization, they often optimize actions and in-game speech without explicitly grounding them in such beliefs. This frequently leads to strategically inconsistent behavior, especially for compact LLM agents. We introduce Multi-Agent Relational Belief Optimization (MARBO), a belief-grounded preference optimization framework that leverages relational beliefs to guide strategic decisions and in-game speech. MARBO provides preference feedback only when behaviors are supported by reliable relational beliefs and lead to strategically favorable social outcomes, encouraging more consistent learning under uncertainty. Experiments on representative SDGs show that MARBO enables compact LLM agents to consistently outperform existing baselines. The Code is available on https://github.com/PleaseTakemeAway/MARBO.

</details>


### [281] [A Translational Note on AI Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06573)
*Madhava Gaikwad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，自动化红队测试在标准AI安全基准上比人类红队测试发现更多漏洞的结论存在方法论缺陷：基准衡量的是攻击者在开发者预设危害集合内的搜索深度，而超出该集合的危害对所有攻击者（无论自动化与否）都不可见。作者将此称为"威胁模型覆盖缺口"，并通过非英语提示词暴露危害的案例证明该缺口在开源模型中仍然存在，进而主张需要部署背景不同于开发者的独立评估者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来自动化红队测试被认为在标准AI安全基准上比人类评估者更高效、更省钱，有人据此认为人类评估者正在变得可有可无。但作者认为这种比较存在范畴错误：基准衡量的事与结论声称的事不是同一件事，危害集合如果预先由开发者固定，那么遗漏的危害对所有攻击者都是盲区，自动化程度再高也无法弥补覆盖缺口。

Method: 通过类比学术密码学和临床药物试验中的同类盲区（内部有效的评估对未指向的人群保持沉默），提出"威胁模型覆盖缺口"概念；并在当前开源权重模型上进行实证验证，展示非英语提示词中暴露的危害是英语基准所遗漏的；进而论证关闭该缺口需要部署背景异于开发者的评估者，理由是从覆盖率出发的方法论论证。

Result: 研究发现威胁模型覆盖缺口在当前开源权重模型中依然存在：英语基准检测不到非英语提示词中出现的危害。作者据此得出结论，仅靠现有的评估框架无法自行产生能弥补覆盖缺口的评估者。

Conclusion: 自动化红队测试优于人类评估的结论混淆了搜索效率与覆盖范围两个维度。人类评估者之所以不可被完全取代，不是因为他们在预设空间内搜得更深，而是因为他们能指出预设空间以外的威胁；支持这一点的论证在方法论上基于覆盖率，而当前评估框架不太可能自行培养出这样的独立评估者。

Abstract: Recent studies report that automated red-teaming finds more vulnerabilities, at lower cost, than human red-teaming on standard AI safety benchmarks, and some read this as evidence that human evaluators are becoming dispensable. The comparison measures one thing and the conclusion claims another. A benchmark measures how thoroughly an attacker searches a predefined set of harms, fixed in advance by the developers, and a harm left out of that set is invisible to any attacker working inside it, automated or not. The same blind spot appeared in academic cryptography and in clinical drug trials, where an evaluation that was internally valid stayed silent about the population it was never pointed at. We call the AI-safety version the \emph{threat-model coverage gap}, and find that it persists in a current open-weight model, where harms surface in non-English prompts that English benchmarks miss. Closing it requires evaluators whose deployment context differs from the developers'. The case for those evaluators is methodological, grounded in coverage, and the existing evaluation frame is unlikely to produce them on its own.

</details>


### [282] [SerenAI: State-transition system inspired by text-based world AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06647)
*Elvin Babayev,Artem Sinitsa,Arash Hajisharifi,Kabir Bakhshaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SerenAI是一个基于世界模型的状态转换系统，旨在将LLM的自由文本生成转化为可验证的结构化预测输出。通过两阶段适应训练（参数高效微调+基于验证器的强化学习），在50,000个样例上显著提升了JSON有效性、模式有效性及各类结构化输出精确匹配率，但其结果尚未达到法律级可靠性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被广泛用于专业工作流，但面对法律、运营或财务等需要审计的约束场景时，对无约束自由文本生成的解释通常是不可行的。需要一个能输出可验证预测而非纯文本的系统，以实现可审计的自动化。

Method: SerenAI采用两阶段适应训练：(1) 参数高效微调（PEFT）；(2) 基于验证器的强化学习。在12个环境、10个推理领域、50,000个因果样例上进行训练。给定环境、状态和动作描述后，模型输出四项内容：因果增量、逻辑后继状态、有效性奖励和终止信号。

Result: 与8B开源基线相比，SerenAI将JSON有效性从85.0%提升至93.2%，模式有效性从55.0%提升至84.0%，精确结构化输出匹配从0.0%提升至41.5%，因果增量精确匹配从0.0%提升至41.5%，后继状态精确匹配从0.0%提升至42.0%，奖励精确匹配从1.0%提升至80.5%，终止信号精确匹配从38.0%提升至81.5%。

Conclusion: 验证器兼容的适应训练可以显著改善结构化状态转换预测能力，但结果尚不足以建立法律级可靠性。论文同时提出了面向证据基础的合法工作流的验证协议，包括校准、人工监督和主权本地化部署。

Abstract: Although professional workflows leverage large language models widely, the interpretation for auditing unconstrained free-text generation is usually intractable if such generation demands legal, operational or financial workflow. We hereby demonstrate a text based system called SerenAI - inspired by world-models, it is a state transition system that outputs verifiable predictions rather than merely text: Provided with a description of the environment, state, and actions, the generated output contains 4 items: causal deltas that causally effect the given state, a next state that can logically follow from the given state and action, a validity reward, and a termination signal. For the released proto-model, we employ 2 steps of adaptation training, namely parameter efficient fine-tuning followed by verifier based RL over 50,000 exampled cause and effects in 12 environments spanning 10 reasoning domains. Compared to an initial internal evaluation of an 8B open-weight baseline, SerenAI increased JSON validity from 85.0% to 93.2%, schema validity from 55.0% to 84.0%, exact structured-output match from 0.0% to 41.5%, causal-delta exact match from 0.0% to 41.5%, resulting-state exact match from 0.0% to 42.0%, reward exact match from 1.0% to 80.5%, and termination exact match from 38.0% to 81.5%.
