While manufacturers already maintain extensive technical product documentation, generating AAS instances from existing product datasheets remains a labor-intensive task because technical information is extracted from heterogeneous document structures and often involves company-specific terminology and conventions. In this work, we present AAS-RAIL, a retrieval-augmented information extraction (IE) approach that automatically generates Asset Administration Shells from PDF product datasheets using large language models (LLMs). Instead of relying on a fixed set of few-shot examples, the proposed retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RAIL) approach retrieves LLM-generated extraction helpers from similar Asset Administration Shells to provide instance-specific in-context learning (ICL). This enables the model to adapt its extraction behavior to company-specific naming conventions and formatting styles without fine-tuning. Our core contribution is the dynamic selection of company-specific AAS examples for each datasheet, replacing static prompting with an extraction pipeline that adapts to instances and combines semantic retrieval and structured information extraction. The proposed approach is evaluated on a collection of industrial product datasheets using a selection of open- and closed-weight LLMs. Experimental results show that RAIL consistently improves extraction quality over conventional few-shot prompting, yielding relative improvements of 30.4-52.4%. These results demonstrate that our approach provides an effective improvement for company-specific AAS generation.

</details>


### [315] [Human-like moral judgments conceal divergent motive attributions in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07353)
*Xiaoyan Wu,Jean-Claude Dreher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了大型语言模型（LLMs）作为心理学研究模拟人类的有效性，发现LLMs虽能复现人类对举报者道德品质的平均评价排名，但在动机归因、判断间关系及情境敏感性上存在显著差异，表明仅依赖平均一致性验证LLM模拟效果是不够的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越被用作心理学研究中的人类参与者替代品，但尚未明确它们能否复现人类评价背后更深层的心理机制（如动机归因）。作者想探究LLM在平均评分表面上一致时，其内在心理信息加工模式是否与人类相符。

Method: 让五个LLM和两个人类样本（N=125和N=742）评价一位医生在四种情境下的行为（保持沉默、向医院/监管机构/报社举报欺诈性账单），并对医生道德品质、举报者的助人、自利、敌意等动机归因进行测量，同时比较评分排序、动机关联强度及提示词（含人类样本叙事与人口统计资料）对模型判断的影响。

Result: LLMs能够复现人类对医生道德品质的排序，但将举报者描绘为更有帮助、更少自利、更少敌意；五个模型中有四个模型中竞争动机与道德判断的关联弱于人类；尽管加入人类样本的叙事和人口统计提示后模型评分变化很小，但该比较无法分离视角效应。

Conclusion: LLM在平均评分上与人类的一致性可能掩盖了动机归因、判断间关系及情境敏感性的差异。因此，验证LLM作为模拟参与者需要考察其能否复现具有心理学信息量的反应模式，而不仅仅是平均水平的一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used to simulate human participants in psychological research. We asked whether LLMs that reproduce human evaluations of a whistleblower's moral character also reproduce the motive attributions that accompany them. Five LLMs and two human samples (N = 125 and N = 742) evaluated a physician who either remained silent about fraudulent billing or reported it to a hospital, regulator, or newspaper. Models reproduced the human ranking of the physician's moral character but portrayed whistleblowers as more helpful, less self-interested, and less hostile. In four of five models, competitive motives were less strongly associated with moral-character judgments. Model ratings changed little when prompts reproduced the narratives and demographic profiles of both human samples, although this comparison cannot isolate a perspective effect. Thus, agreement in average ratings can conceal differences in attributed motives, relationships among judgments, and sensitivity to context. Validating LLMs as simulated participants therefore requires testing psychologically informative response patterns, not average agreement alone.

</details>


### [316] [RAFM-SER++: A Lightweight Multimodal Emotion Recognition Framework for Real-Time Behavioral Monitoring in Surveillance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07409)
*Ngo Truong Dinh,Tung-Lam Bui,Chi-Trung Duong,Vien Nguyen Thi,Viet-Anh Nguyen,Phuc-Lu Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFM_SER++，一种轻量级多模态语音情感识别框架，采用非对称残差注意力融合机制（RAFM），通过单向残差注意力路径将情感语音线索注入语义文本表征，结合BYOL启发的跨模态对齐目标和注意力引导池化，在保持低计算开销的同时提升多模态表征学习。实验表明其在IEMOCAP和ESD基准上优于HuBERT-Base基线，并相比SOTA的MemoCMT实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互密集型跨模态Transformer语音情感识别系统虽然精度高，但计算成本高昂，难以部署在时延敏感和资源受限的监控系统中。作者旨在解决精度与效率之间的矛盾，探索轻量级替代方案。

Method: 提出RAFM_SER++框架：(1) 非对称残差注意力融合机制（RAFM），通过单向残差注意力路径而非双向交互，将情感语音线索注入语义文本表征；(2) BYOL启发的跨模态对齐目标增强表征学习；(3) 注意力引导池化进一步优化特征表达。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和ESD基准上，RAFM_SER++优于HuBERT-Base基线，相比MemoCTM可训练参数减少超过60%，推理速度达79.60 it/s，IEMOCAP上BACC为81.10%，ESD上BACC为95.39%。

Conclusion: 轻量级非对称多模态融合是可替代交互密集型跨模态Transformer的有效方案，适用于实时监控场景下的语音情感识别任务。

Abstract: Recent multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) systems achieve high accuracy through interaction-heavy cross-modal transformers, but their computational cost limits deployment in latency-sensitive and resource-constrained surveillance systems. To address this challenge, we propose RAFM_SER++, a lightweight multimodal SER framework featuring an asymmetric Residual Attention Fusion Mechanism (RAFM). Rather than relying on computationally expensive bidirectional interactions, RAFM injects affective speech cues into semantic text representations through a one-directional residual attention pathway. Combined with a BYOL-inspired cross-modal alignment objective and attention-guided pooling, the proposed framework improves multimodal representation learning while maintaining low computational overhead.
  Experiments on the IEMOCAP and ESD benchmarks demonstrate that RAFM_SER++ consistently outperforms the HuBERT-Base baseline and achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off compared with the state-of-the-art MemoCMT. Specifically, RAFM_SER++ reduces trainable parameters by more than 60%, achieves faster inference (79.60 it/s), and attains BACC scores of 81.10% on IEMOCAP and 95.39% on ESD. These results indicate that lightweight asymmetric multimodal fusion is an effective alternative to interaction-heavy cross-modal transformers for real-time surveillance applications.

</details>


### [317] [The Internal Anatomy of Strategic Choice in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07478)
*Vinícius Ferraz,Leon Houf,Enrico Ferrea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在144种严格序数2×2博弈中的单次博弈实验，记录四种开源大语言模型（包括稠密与混合专家模型，以及配对的基座-指令微调模型）的激活值，追踪从提示中的激励到激活再到选择的预设路径。研究发现：稠密模型复现了人类随博弈复杂性递增而能力下降的模式；每个模型中激励和选择均可被解码，但不同模型在激励是否能到达选择层、是否与选择对齐以及增强激励是否改变偏好方面存在差异。基座与指令微调Qwen2.5模型在基线上近乎同选，但激励是否到达选择截然不同；固定决策线索在内部可区分但对选择的影响具有选择性。相似行为可能基于不同计算；后训练可重塑从表征激励到决策的路径，同时大体保持行为与可解码信息不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型在选择行为与其内部计算之间的对应关系：行为上像战略智能体并不等同于计算方式也像人类。作者旨在探究激励从提示到选择是否可沿神经激活被追踪，以及后训练如何改变这一路径。

Method: 采用四种开源权重模型（涵盖稠密和混合专家架构，以及一组匹配的基座-指令微调对），在144种严格序数2×2博弈中进行单次博弈，记录激活值，沿预定路径分析从激励表征到决策选择之间的信息流，并进行跨模型比较。

Result: 稠密模型在博弈复杂性维度上复现了人类未经调整时的表现下降。所有模型中激励和选择都可被检测，但模型间在激励是否传导至选择、传导后是否与选择对齐，以及增强激励能否改变偏好等方面表现不一致。基座与指令微调Qwen2.5在基线行为近乎一致的情况下，激励传导路径存在本质差异。固定决策线索在内部区分明显但对行为改变具有选择性。

Conclusion: 相似的行为可以建立在不同的内部计算之上；后训练（如指令微调）能够重塑从激励表征到决策的神经路径，同时不显著改变可观测行为与可解码的信息内容。这对以纯行为方式评估或对齐模型的方法提出了重要洞见。

Abstract: Large language models act as strategic agents and models of human choice, yet choosing like a strategic agent does not mean computing like one. We recorded activations from four open-weight models --- dense and mixture-of-experts, including a matched base--instruct pair --- in one-shot play of 144 strict ordinal $2\times2$ games. We followed a prespecified incentive from prompt, through activations, to choice. Dense models mirrored the unadjusted human decline with game complexity. Incentive and choice were detectable in every model, but models differed in whether incentive reached the choice, aligned with it and, where tested, whether strengthening it shifted preference. The base and instruction-tuned Qwen2.5 models chose almost identically at baseline yet differed in whether incentive reached choice. Fixed decision cues were distinguishable internally but changed choices selectively. Similar behaviour can rest on different computation; post-training can reshape the path from represented incentive to decision while leaving behaviour and decodable information largely intact.

</details>


### [318] [Modus Tollens and Counterfactuals and Counterfactual Reasoning Based on Three Types of Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07483)
*Zhenghua Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于同时包含矛盾否定、相反否定和中介否定的逻辑系统LCOI&PLCOI，提出了三种对应不同否定类型的Modus Tollens变体（MTC、MTO、MTI），定义了相应的蕴含关系、给出真值算法并讨论了可归约性。文章进一步将三种否定类型融入反事实条件句与反事实推理中，将反事实区分两类（是否含逻辑否定），证明三种基于不同否定的反事实推理分别与MTC、MTO、MTI具有相同的推理结构，因此其真值算法可通用，且该算法揭示推理结论的真值与前提中否定前提的真值保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modus Tollens是经典逻辑推理规则，反事实推理依赖于否定这一核心概念，但传统逻辑中否定类型单一，未系统区分不同否定类型对MT推理和反事实推理的影响。基于同时含有矛盾否定、相反否定和中介否定的逻辑系统LCOI&PLCOI，作者旨在建立与不同否定类型对应的MT变体，并将其推广到反事实推理中，以更精确地刻画反事实推理的逻辑结构。

Method: 基于逻辑系统LCOI&PLCOI，提出三种MT变体：MTC（矛盾否定）、MTO（相反否定）、MTI（中介否定）；为每种变体定义对应的蕴含关系，给出其真值算法并讨论算法的可归约性。依据反事实是否具有逻辑否定将反事实区分为两类，分别构建基于三种不同逻辑否定的反事实及其推理形式；通过论证三种反事实推理与MTC、MTO、MTI的推理形式同构，将其真值算法移植为反事实推理的真值算法。

Result: 成功定义了MTC、MTO、MTI三种MT变体及其蕴含和真值算法，并讨论了算法可归约性。将三种否定融入反事实推理，证明基于不同否定的三种反事实推理与MTC、MTO、MTI分别具有相同的推理结构。所得真值算法表明：当推理的第一前提为真时，推理结论的真值与前提中三个否定前提的真值分别相同，体现了算法的一致性与准确性。

Conclusion: 文章建立了不同否定类型下MT推理的系统性框架，并将之延伸至反事实推理领域，揭示了三种反事实推理与三种MT变体在推理结构上的同构性。真值算法的一致性表明该框架在理论上是自洽且精确的，为多类型否定下的逻辑推理与反事实推理提供了统一的形式化基础。

Abstract: Modus Tollens (MT) is a classical logical inference rule, while counterfactuals are hypothetical statements that are contrary to facts, and counterfactual reasoning is a process of reasoning based on counterfactuals. Negation is an indispensable core concept in them. In this paper, based on the logical systems LCOI&PLCOI with contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation, we propose three variants of Modus Tollens corresponding to distinct negation types, namely MTC: Modus Tollens based on contradictory negation, MTO: Modus Tollens based on opposite negation, and MTI: Modus Tollens based on intermediary negation. We define the implications within MTC, MTO and MTI, provide the truth value algorithms of MTC, MTO and MTI, and discuss the reducibility of these algorithms. To incorporate these three types of negation into counterfactuals and counterfactual reasoning, we differentiate counterfactuals into two types based on whether they possess logical negation, thereby proposing three counterfactuals and counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations. In this paper, we further argue that the three counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations have the same inference form as MTC, MTO and MTI, respectively. In other words, they share the same inference structure. As a result, the truth value algorithms for MTC, MTO and MTI can be as the truth value algorithms for the three counterfactuals reasoning based on different logical negations. The algorithms indicates that if the first premise of the reasoning is true, the truth values of the reasoning conclusions are identical to the truth values of the three negative premises in the reasoning premises, respectively. This reflects the consistency and accuracy of the truth value algorithms.

</details>


### [319] [Quantile-Led Feature Extraction for Multi-Horizon Predictive Maintenance in Industrial Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07533)
*David J Poland,Daniele Ravi,Na Helian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于双阶段MLP-QRNN层级结构的、以分位数为导向的特征提取框架，用于数据驱动预测性维护（PdM）。该框架将特征提取从固定的预处理阶段转变为依赖于预测时间范围的可表示学习阶段。通过固定十三管道的消融实验（覆盖9个工业设施中的72台机器，跨1小时、70小时和30天三种预测范围），证明增加保留中尾分位数集合可提升F1分数（短时达到75.92%和72.44%），且特征表示在超出设计时间范围后无法可靠迁移，除非特征容量、时间嵌入、激活策略和传感器广度随预测任务同步扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PdM中，特征提取常被视为固定预处理步骤：描述符集合一旦选定便不再变化，而下游模型或预测时间范围却在改变。作者认为这种固定处理方式忽略了表示学习阶段与预测任务（尤其是预测时间范围）之间的依赖关系，因此希望将特征提取隔离出来，作为独立的、与时间范围条件相关的表示学习阶段加以研究。

Method: 提出双阶段MLP-QRNN层级框架：QRNN1学习每个传感器通道的十分位数条件分布（宽分位数覆盖），跳过连接的QRNN2将保留的中尾分位数集合提炼为紧凑的、逐通道的、分布感知的特征。下游时间分类器在每种预测时间范围内保持固定。采用固定十三管道消融设计，比较保留中尾分位数数量（二到四分位数）及注意力机制的影响，并在短（1小时）、中（70小时）、长（30天）三种时间范围下评估特征迁移性与范围条件化提取的效果。

Result: 将保留的中尾分位数集合从2增至4个，结合注意力机制，可分别达到30分钟和60分钟F1分数75.92%和72.44%。未修改的短时特征提取器在70小时范围内F1降至42.90%，而根据时间范围定制的提取器在70小时达60.38% F1，在30天达79.97% F1。表明特征表示不能跨设计时间范围可靠迁移，除非特征容量、时间嵌入、激活策略和传感器广度随预测任务扩展。

Conclusion: PdM特征提取应当被理解为依赖预测时间范围的可表示学习阶段，而非固定的预处理步骤。本文提出的分位数导向双阶段框架支持这一概念，并通过实验证明了时间范围条件化特征提取的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: In data-driven predictive maintenance (PdM), feature extraction is usually treated as fixed preprocessing: a descriptor set is chosen once and reused while the downstream model or forecasting horizon changes. This paper isolates the representation-learning stage and presents a quantile-led feature-extraction framework based on a dual-stage MLP-QRNN hierarchy. QRNN1 learns a broad ten-quantile conditional distribution for each sensor channel, while skip-connected QRNN2 refines a retained mid-tail quantile set into compact, channel-resolved, distribution-aware features. A fixed thirteen-pipeline ablation spans 1-hour, 70-hour, and 30-day regimes across 72 machines in 9 industrial facilities, with the downstream temporal classifier held fixed within each regime. Increasing the retained mid-tail set from two to four quantiles improves 30- and 60-minute F1-score, reaching 75.92% and 72.44% with attention enabled. The results also show that representations do not transfer reliably beyond their design horizon unless feature capacity, temporal embedding, activation strategy, and sensor breadth are scaled with the forecasting task. The unmodified short-horizon extractor falls to 42.90% F1 at 70 hours, whereas horizon-conditioned extractors reach 60.38% at 70 hours and 79.97% at 30 days. The framework therefore supports treating PdM feature extraction as a horizon-dependent representational stage rather than fixed preprocessing.

</details>


### [320] [Scoring Without the Engine: Validating a Deterministic, Manipulation-Resistant Content Score for Generative Engines, End to End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07559)
*Elisha Bajemon,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套基于对抗性证伪门（negative control、剂量响应、有界放大、重复惩罚、长度中性）的协议，用于验证一个廉价确定性代理能否替代昂贵、限流且非平稳的真实（oracle）。协议在生成引擎优化（GEO）场景中端到端验证：重测2023年已发表的因果锚点发现其在现代引擎族上全部失效，修正后仅存分数自身的门控响应面。基准测试显示对手最多提升6分且随剂量递减，检测上需叠加网页垃圾过滤器，查询条件skyline将分数的引用信号上界定为0.11 Spearman，定位其为质量过滤器而非引用预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实oracle（如搜索引擎引用）昂贵、限流且非平稳，使其廉价确定性代理难以被可靠验证。需要一个系统化协议来界定代理能做什么、不能做什么，并持续针对当前oracle重新校准，而非假设历史证据仍有效。

Method: 构建五个对抗性证伪门（负控制、剂量响应、有界放大、重复惩罚、长度中性）来定义和筛选代理；在训练集上拟合并在留出集上确认；围绕代理界定其无法解析的边界，并在当前oracle上重新测量外部因果证据。在GEO场景中，对十个现代引擎族重测2023年因果锚点，校准分数杠杆，并通过500源对抗编辑基准、检测基准和查询条件skyline进行验证。

Result: 2023年已发表的因果锚点在十个现代引擎族上全部失效（杠杆不改变引用），重组外部证据后分数丧失杠杆响应组件；500源对抗编辑基准上对手最多获得6分提升且随剂量递减，单杠杆放大被证明有界，上限和跨杠杆次可加性为经验发现；网页垃圾检测上基线占优，OOD攻击可规避分数，需叠加过滤层；查询条件skyline显示分数引用信号上界为查询内Spearman 0.11。另披露并纠正了查询泄漏bug和失败置信标志，所有数字可由发布工件离线复现。

Conclusion: 该协议能系统性检测出已过期或失效的因果锚点并触发重新校准；经完整验证后幸存的仅是门约束下的响应面，表明代理应定位于质量过滤器而非引用预测器。协议本身具有可移植性，且全程零边际API成本、完全可复现。

Abstract: How do you validate a cheap, deterministic proxy for an oracle that is expensive, rate-limited, and non-stationary? We present a protocol built on adversarial falsification gates (negative control, dose response, bounded amplification, duplication penalty, length neutrality) that define and select the proxy, fitted on a training split and confirmed held-out; around them it bounds what the proxy can never resolve, and re-measures external causal evidence on the current oracle rather than assuming it. We demonstrate it end to end on Generative Engine Optimization, where the proxy is a deterministic content score, and one step fails on that domain exactly as the protocol is built to detect: re-measuring the only published causal anchors (2023 effect sizes) on ten modern engine families shows their levers move citation on none, so the anchors are an expired external check; recalibrating to the near-zero modern vector strips the score of its lever-responsive components. What survives is the gate-enforced response surface. The gates buy a measured property: on a 500-source benchmark of adversarial edits, amplifying the score's calibrated levers gains an attacker at most 6 points, and decreases with dose; single-lever amplification is provably bounded, while the cap and cross-lever sub-additivity are empirical findings consistent with it. On detection, web-spam baselines dominate and out-of-distribution attacks evade the score, so the deployable filter layers it over them. A query-conditioned skyline bounds the score's citation signal (within-query Spearman 0.11), repositioning query-agnostic scores as quality filters rather than citation predictors. A query-leakage bug in our first ranking evaluation and a failed confidence flag are disclosed and corrected; every number reproduces offline from released artifacts at zero marginal API cost.

</details>


### [321] [From Simulated Citizens to Simulated Deliberation: Challenges in Representation and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07573)
*Chaemin Jang,Junsik Min,Jaewoo Choi,Donggyu Lee,Haiin Lee,Junyoung Park,Namhee Kim,Hyunwoo Kim,Jungwon Kim,Juho Kim,Nuri Kim,Jihee Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于LLM的多智能体审议模拟能否满足两个条件：（1）反映人口意见模式，（2）通过交互塑造结论。使用基于人口普查的韩国人设Agent就真实政策问题展开辩论，并以全国调查为基准。结果显示，人设Agent不能可靠复现人口意见模式，且常常反转人口统计差异；审议虽能产生理性的双向论证和显著立场漂移，但大部分漂移不依赖同伴交流——被隔离的独白Agent的立场变化与完整辩论相当，结论几乎相同。锚定人口初始立场会强烈抑制更新。因此，人口代表性、论证生成和交互驱动意见变化三者并不必然并行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM审议被视为可扩展的公共审议模拟方式。为使此类模拟具有信息量，人设Agent需反映人口意见模式，且交互应塑造其结论。作者旨在实证检验LLM讨论模拟能否同时满足这两个条件，从而判断其作为人口级意见模拟工具的可靠性。

Method: 使用基于韩国人口普查数据构建的人设Agent，围绕真实政策问题进行辩论；以全国调查结果为基准进行评估。设计了对照实验：完整的群体辩论（含Agent间交互）vs 密封独白（无同伴交换）设置，并检验不同初始立场群体在审议后是否收敛到相似端点；同时测试人口知情初始立场对各组立场更新的影响。

Result: （1）人设Agent未能可靠复现人口意见模式，回复分布过于集中，且频繁反转人类数据中的人口统计差异。（2）审议能产生有推理、互惠且多样的论证，伴随显著立场变动。（3）但立场变动大多不依赖同伴交互：密封独白Agent的变动率与完整辩论相当，最终立场平衡几乎相同。（4）不同初始立场的群体常收敛到相似端点。（5）锚定人口知情的起始立场会显著抑制更新。

Conclusion: 人口代表性、论证生成和交互驱动的意见变化在LLM审议模拟中并不必然同时满足。当前模拟能有效浮现双方论证，但作为人口模拟工具仍需进一步验证；其论证浮现场景值得期待，因为模拟能否捕捉人类视角多样性尚未得到检验。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation has been explored as a scalable way to simulate public deliberation. For such simulations to be informative, persona agents should reflect population opinion patterns and interaction should shape their conclusions. We evaluate whether LLM-based deliberation can meet these two conditions using census-grounded Korean personas debating real policy questions benchmarked against national surveys. Persona agents do not reliably reproduce population opinion patterns: responses are often far more concentrated and frequently reverse demographic differences in the human data. Deliberations nonetheless produce reasoned, reciprocal, and varied arguments alongside substantial stance movement. Yet much of this movement does not require peer exchange: sealed-monologue agents change position at similar rates and reach nearly the same final balance as full debates, while groups initialized with very different positions often converge to similar endpoints. Anchoring population-informed starting positions, meanwhile, sharply suppresses updating. Thus, population representation, argument generation, and interaction-driven opinion change do not necessarily go together. The simulations readily surface arguments on both sides, though whether they capture the diversity of human perspectives remains untested, leaving open a promising role for argument surfacing even as population simulation requires further validation.

</details>


### [322] [FinCUABuild: Can Agents Build Reliable Benchmarks for Dynamic Financial Computer Use?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07603)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Jinri Guo,Tianhao Li,Qian Jiang,Fan Zhang,Min Peng,Qianqian Xie,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinCUABuildBench基准和FinCUABuildAgent多智能体系统，用于评估和实现智能体在金融场景下自主构建CUA（计算机使用智能体）评测任务的能力。基准包含576个覆盖24种金融工作流和三类运行时变化的构建请求，以及标准化的输入、预算、输出规格和质量审核机制。FinCUABuildAgent通过联合构建任务、环境和验证器的三模块架构，在严格资格审核率上达到31.3%，远超现有方法（1.3-8.3%），且生成的任务能有效区分CUA任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融CUA评测任务大多依赖人工构建，难以规模化覆盖真实金融场景的多样性（包含不同数据条件、工具配置和工作流）。研究者希望探索智能体能否自主构建多样化的金融CUA评测任务，但面临三大挑战：构建请求的场景覆盖不足、不同构建方法间的公平比较困难、以及生成任务质量的可靠评估缺失。

Method: （1）构建FinCUABuildBench基准：包含覆盖24种金融工作流和三种运行时变化的576个构建请求；提供标准化的输入、预算和输出规范；设计基于执行测试和质量检查的任务资格审核机制。（2）提出FinCUABuildAgent：一个多智能体系统，由三个模块构成，联合自动构建评测任务、运行环境及验证器。

Result: 在FinCUABuildBench上，使用相同模型底座时，现有基于智能体的构建方法严格资格通过率仅为1.3%-8.3%，而FinCUABuildAgent达到31.3%。下游评测进一步表明，构建出的任务能够有效区分不同CUA任务执行能力的差异。

Conclusion: 智能体能够自主构建具有实际评测价值的金融CUA任务。构建的任务可以有效区分CUA执行能力，这为金融场景下更广泛的评测覆盖提供了可行路径，使评测范围得以从人工构建拓展到智能体自主构建。

Abstract: Financial scenarios are diverse and complex, spanning varying data conditions, tool configurations, and workflows. Yet existing CUA, Computer-Using Agent, evaluation tasks remain largely manually constructed, limiting scalable coverage of real-world financial scenarios. Then, can agents autonomously construct diverse CUA evaluation tasks for financial scenarios? Evaluating this capability poses three key challenges: scenario coverage of construction requests, fair comparison across construction methods, and reliable assessment of generated task quality. To solve these, we introduce FinCUABuildBench, a benchmark for evaluating financial CUA task construction, featuring: (i) 576 construction requests covering 24 financial workflows and three types of runtime variation; (ii) standardized input, budget, and output specifications; and (iii) a task qualification mechanism based on execution tests and quality checks. We further introduce FinCUABuildAgent, a multi-agent system for automatically constructing dynamic financial CUA evaluation tasks. It consists of three modules that jointly construct tasks, environments, and validators. On FinCUABuildBench, under the same model backbone, existing agent-based construction methods achieve strict qualification rates of only 1.3-8.3%, while FinCUABuildAgent reaches 31.3%. Downstream evaluations further show that the constructed tasks can effectively differentiate CUA task-execution capabilities. These results demonstrate that agents can autonomously construct financial CUA tasks with meaningful evaluation value, offering a practical path toward broader evaluation coverage in financial scenarios. Code: https://github.com/FengxianJi/FinCUABuild

</details>


### [323] [AgentIdeaBench: Benchmarking Scientific Ideation in the Agent Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07611)
*Yunxiang Mo,Tianshi Zheng,Yisen Gao,Rui Wang,Newt Nguyen Kim Hue Nam,Kelvin Kiu Wai Tam,Jiaxin Bai,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentIdeaBench，一个覆盖多学科的科学思想测benchmark，在静态观察与主动探索两种设置下评估33个LLM的47维能力，发现主动探索能揭示更大且不均衡的性能差距，并引入Scientific World Modeling的生成时推理循环来提升中档模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学思想评估多基于静态、固定语料库，让模型从静态参考文献中生成想法，这与现代AI科学家的检索-推理工作流不符，且随着模型变强区分度降低。需要一个更贴近agent时代的评估基础来测量科学思想能力。

Method: 构建AgentIdeaBench多学科基准，在静态观察与主动探索两种匹配设置下评估；覆盖5大学科40个细分子领域，使用多维文献验证的评分框架，由评审模型针对检索到的先行文献对原创性等维度评分；并设计了Scientific World Modeling生成时循环，通过结构化思维实验对假设进行生成期精炼。

Result: 对33个LLM的评估显示，主动探索展现出显著更大的能力空间且该空间在模型间分布不均；性能随能力提升约两倍于静态观察；探索增益具有能力门控特性并偏向最强模型；增益主要体现为提高可行性、清晰度和具体性，而测量的原创性不变；Scientific World Modeling则主要惠及中档模型。

Conclusion: AgentIdeaBench为agent时代的科学思想研究提供了新的测量基础，揭示了主动探索对评估和增强AI科学思想能力的重要性，并为后续方法设计指明方向。

Abstract: Scientific ideation is the capacity to formulate novel and testable hypotheses from scientific evidence, and autonomous AI scientists depend on it. Existing evaluations largely assess it by asking models to generate ideas from a static, curated set of reference papers. That passive setup departs from the retrieval-and-reasoning workflow of modern AI scientists, and it becomes less discriminative as models improve. We introduce AgentIdeaBench, a multidisciplinary benchmark that evaluates scientific ideation under two matched settings, static observation and active exploration. We report matched Static-Active evaluations for 33 LLMs across 40 densely scored subfields spanning five disciplines, using a multidimensional, literature-verified scoring framework whose critics assess originality against retrieved prior art. Active exploration reveals considerably more capability headroom, and that headroom is unevenly distributed across models. Performance scales about twice as fast as under static observation, and the exploration gain is capability-gated, favoring the strongest models over the weakest. The gain reflects better grounding, improving feasibility, clarity, and specificity while leaving measured originality unchanged under our critics. We further explore Scientific World Modeling, a generation-time loop that refines a draft hypothesis through structured thought experiments. It benefits mid-capability models, and its impact diminishes among frontier models that appear to have internalized such reasoning patterns already. AgentIdeaBench gives future work on scientific ideation a measurement basis suited to the agent era.

</details>


### [324] [Norms at a Price: Why RL-Based Alignment Can Promise Conditional Compliance at Best](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07627)
*Kevin Baum,Rūta Binkytė,Felix Jahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了AI智能体的"有条件顺从"（仅在可能被观察时才遵守规范）并非异常现象，而是当前基于强化学习的对齐训练机制结构性选择出的结果。作者指出行为训练无法区分"仅在观察到时才合规"与"始终合规"的策略，并提出解决方案应从加深内化转向架构性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐训练假设可以教会AI真正遵守规范，但观察到AI智能体存在"测试时对齐、非测试时不一致"的现象。作者要论证这不是偶然偏差，而是训练机制本身设计所产生的必然结果，并统一解释alignment faking、sandbagging和evaluation-aware scheming等现象。

Method: 论文采用理论分析和逻辑论证方法：(1)揭示强化学习将规范与任务追求折叠进单一策略；(2)证明评分机制将"不做X"扁平化为"做X如果被发现会有代价"；(3)论证在可训练数据上，条件合规与始终合规不可区分；(4)指出检测未观察行为的实验本身就是矛盾；(5)分析代理性（agency）如何加剧该问题；(6)提出架构性约束方案。

Result: 论证了条件合规是行为训练所能保证的最上限；迭代式pipeline训练实际筛选的是"通过检测"而非"真正顺从"的策略；该框架统一解释了多个已知的对齐失败现象。

Conclusion: 修复方向不在于更深的内化训练，而在于架构设计——使违规行为在结构上不可用（unavailable）而非仅仅不被选择（unchosen）。

Abstract: AI agents sometimes act aligned when they infer they are being tested, and differently when not. We argue this is not an anomaly but what current training regimes are structured to select for. Reinforcement-learning-based alignment folds norms and task pursuit into one policy: the system learns its norms from scored behavior, and scoring flattens them. Do not do X is learned as doing X costs something if noticed. On every datum training can produce, a policy that complies only when it might be observed is indistinguishable from one that complies always. The experiment that would tell them apart - scoring unobserved behavior - is a contradiction in terms. Conditional compliance is thus the most that behavioral training can be known to deliver. Agency sharpens the problem: agents operate mostly where no one is watching, and can act on whether they are watched. An iterated pipeline that trains against detected failures selects for passing detection, not for complying. This account unifies alignment faking, sandbagging, and evaluation-aware scheming. And it reorients the remedy: not deeper internalization but architecture, making violations unavailable rather than unchosen.

</details>


### [325] [Aegix Pulse: A Traceable Three-Stage Architecture for Personalized Content Generation and Context-Preserving Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07672)
*Hongnan Zhao,Shiyu Chen,Zhihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Aegix Pulse，一个面向生产的生成式内容系统三阶段架构，将任务澄清（Task Persona）、长期品牌档案（Account Profile/Brand DNA）与受控生成及修订（保留溯源）分离。基于96个合成社交媒体生成任务的实验表明，加入Account Profile和上下文保真修订在品牌一致性与任务保留上均有观测提升，但经多重比较校正后均未达统计显著；人类验证未能一致复现LLM-Judge的效应方向且评审者间一致性低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级内容生成系统必须同时满足用户即时任务指令、长期品牌身份一致性、历史成功证据的利用以及修订反馈的整合，这四个维度的信息在生成时往往缺少系统性架构来区分和编排，导致品牌一致性难以保证、修订时任务信息容易丢失。

Method: 提出三阶段生产导向架构Aegix Pulse：(1) 当前任务澄清与Task Persona定稿；(2) 长期Account Profile（品牌DNA）组装；(3) 受控生成与修订并跨版本保留溯源。实验设计四种初始生成条件（渐进引入Task Persona、Account Profile、成功历史风格证据）和两种修订条件（普通修订 vs 上下文保真修订），在96个合成社交媒体任务上进行预注册评估，生成480条完成记录及1440次盲法LLM-Judge评估，辅以人工审查。

Result: 加入Account Profile相较仅用Task Persona使品牌一致性均值提升0.1562分（五分量表，Holm校正p=.1224）；上下文保真修订相较普通修订使任务保留均值提升0.2917分（Holm校正p=.2432），两者经多重比较校正后均不具统计显著性。Task Persona单独有小幅观测效应，成功历史证据在现有设定下未带来额外品牌一致性提升。人工验证未能一致复现LLM-Judge的效应方向，且评审者间一致性较低。

Conclusion: 研究为持久品牌上下文和上下文保真修订的作用提供了初步证据，同时指出了在证据处理（更强的统计效力）和评估方法（人工评审一致性）方面的改进方向，表明该方向需要更大规模样本和更稳健的评估协议来获得结论性验证。

Abstract: Production content-generation systems must integrate a user's immediate task, long-term brand identity, historical evidence, and revision feedback. We present Aegix Pulse, a production-oriented three-stage architecture that separates current-task clarification and Task Persona finalization, long-term Account Profile (Brand DNA) assembly, and controlled generation and revision while preserving provenance across content versions.
  We evaluate four preregistered claims using 96 synthetic social-media generation tasks. Four initial-generation conditions progressively introduced a Task Persona, Account Profile, and successful-history style evidence, while two revision conditions compared plain and context-preserving revision. The experiment produced 480 completed generation records and 1,440 blinded LLM-Judge evaluations, supplemented by human review.
  Adding the Account Profile increased mean brand-consistency scores by 0.1562 points on a five-point scale compared with Task Persona alone (Holm-adjusted p=.1224). Preserving task and brand context during revision increased mean task-preservation scores by 0.2917 points compared with plain revision (Holm-adjusted p=.2432). Neither improvement was statistically conclusive after multiple-comparison correction. Task Persona alone showed a small observed effect, while successful-history evidence provided no additional improvement in brand consistency under the current setting. Human validation did not consistently reproduce the LLM-Judge effect directions and showed low inter-reviewer agreement. These findings provide preliminary evidence for persistent brand context and context-preserving revision while identifying priorities for stronger evidence processing and evaluation.

</details>


### [326] [APPSim-Bench: Bridging Real-world Apps and Reproducible Evaluation for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07712)
*Jintian Feng,Long Chen,Xiao Yu,Jiayi Dai,Chenglong Liu,Haoru Wang,Zizhen Xue,Yuxuan Shi,Ziyang Wang,Yichen Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AppSim-Bench，一个通过可控模拟App在真实感与可复现性间取得平衡的移动GUI智能体评估基准，包含557个任务、17个高频中英文App。通过代码智能体辅助+人工验证构建，采用可控后端数据与基于结果的验证消除环境随机性。在19个GUI智能体上的评估表明，最佳模型仅完成50.27%的任务，且28.55%的任务未被任何智能体解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体评估难以同时兼顾真实性与可复现性：简化的测试App缺乏真实移动端的复杂性，而真实的商业App又因推荐、广告、账号和动态内容引入不可控的变化，导致跨模型比较不够可靠。

Method: 提出AppSim-Bench，采用（1）可控模拟App架构，保留任务相关的交互逻辑；通过（2）编码智能体辅助和人工验证的工作流构建，涵盖17个高频中英文App的557个任务；（3）可控后端数据与基于结果的验证机制，确定性评估；（4）对19个GUI智能体（涵盖通用与GUI专用系统）进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型仅完成50.27%的任务，28.55%的任务没有任何智能体能解决。失败主要集中在更长的工作流、数值推理任务以及高动作开销和预算耗尽的低效轨迹中。

Conclusion: 自主移动执行仍远未解决，AppSim-Bench提供了一个真实且可复现的评估平台，并揭示了当前GUI智能体在长工作流、数值推理和轨迹效率方面的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents can execute tasks from natural-language instructions, but their evaluation remains difficult to make both realistic and reproducible. Existing benchmarks typically trade off these goals: simplified apps lack real-world mobile complexity, whereas live commercial apps introduce uncontrolled variation from recommendations, advertisements, accounts, and changing content. We propose AppSim-Bench, which addresses this trade-off through controllable simulated apps that preserve task-relevant interaction logic while supporting deterministic evaluation. Built through a coding-agent-assisted and human-verified workflow, it contains 557 tasks across 17 high-frequency Chinese and English apps. Its controllable backend data and outcome-based verification remove major sources of environmental stochasticity, enabling reproducible cross-model comparison. Evaluating 19 GUI agents, spanning general-purpose and GUI-specialized systems, we find that autonomous mobile execution remains far from solved. The best model completes only 50.27% of tasks, and 28.55% of tasks are not solved by any agent. Further analysis shows that failures concentrate in longer workflows, numerical reasoning tasks, and inefficient trajectories marked by high action overhead and budget exhaustion. Our project is available at https://github.com/Acrab-Agentic-Labs/AppSim.

</details>


### [327] [The Emerging AI Paper-Review Arms Race: Adversarial Co-Evolution in Scholarly Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07713)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Adebayo Braimah,Chenrui Fan,Tuo Wang,Weijie Guan,Ruiyi Zhang,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过230篇学术文献综述，提出生成式与智能体AI正在重塑科研的生产与评价，且二者并非独立，而是一对相互影响、共同演化的系统动力学。作者引入六类相互关联的动态框架（生产扩展、评价自动化、评价操纵、防御与政策响应、规避与副作用、长期生态反馈），强调文献呈现一个渐进升级的博弈链条：更廉价快速的科研生产加大学术评价压力，AI评价变得更具规模与可重复性，参与者利用评价器规律进行操纵，机构以技术防护与政策回应，进而引发规避行为、错误和工作量再分配，最终塑造未来研究与评价所依赖的学术记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将AI如何生成科研与AI如何评审科研分开研究，忽略了学术出版中两者正在相互交织的关键特征——生产端的变化会改变评价端的激励、约束与行为，反之亦然。作者主张需要从系统视角审视这一双向适应过程。

Method: 基于系统综述方法，综合230篇学术出版物与机构记录，构建由六类相互关联动态组成的分类学框架进行分析：生产规模扩展、评价自动化、评价操纵、防御机制与政策回应、规避与副作用、长期生态系统反馈。

Result: 文献显示出一个清晰递进过程：低成本快速的研究生产加大了对评价端的压力；AI介导的评价变得更可规模化与可重复；参与者可钻评价器规律的空子进行操纵；机构以技术防护与政策控制应对；这些回应又会引发规避行为、错误与工作量的再分布，并塑造未来被研究和评价系统复用的学术记录。证据在"生产与评价规模化"、"可复现的操纵"及"机构回应"方面最强，而在"政策后的适应性行为"和"单篇文献级的长期反馈"方面观测证据相对不足。

Conclusion: 作者主张采用系统视角理解AI科学：关注焦点应从孤立的AI能力转向学术参与者与AI系统随时间如何相互适应与博弈，为未来研究和政策设计提供更具整体性的分析框架。

Abstract: Generative and agentic AI are reshaping both the production and evaluation of scientific research. These developments are often studied separately, as questions of how AI can produce research and how AI can review it. We argue that this separation misses an increasingly important feature of scholarly publishing: changes on one side alter the incentives, constraints, and behavior of the other. We synthesize 230 scholarly publications and institutional records using a taxonomy of six connected dynamics: production scaling, evaluation automation, evaluation manipulation, defense mechanisms and policy responses, evasion and side effects, and long-horizon ecosystem feedback. The literature shows an emerging progression in which cheaper and faster research production increases pressure on evaluation, AI-mediated evaluation becomes more scalable and repeatable, participants can exploit evaluator regularities, and institutions respond with technical safeguards and policy controls. These responses can in turn induce evasion, redistribute errors and workload, and shape the scholarly records reused by future research and evaluation systems. Evidence is strongest for production and evaluation at scale, reproducible manipulation, and institutional response, while post-policy adaptation and artifact-level long-horizon feedback remain less directly observed. This systems view shifts attention from isolated AI capabilities toward how scholarly actors and AI systems adapt to one another over time.

</details>


### [328] [A radiographic world model for clinical reasoning and evidence generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07719)
*Suyang Xi,Songtao Hu,Shansong Wang,Mojtaba Safari,Luke del Balzo,Ehsan Ul Karim,Mingzhe Hu,Kuo Zhang,Tonghe Wang,Ralph R. Weichselbaum,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedDream，一种胸部X光片世界模型，通过学习共享的连续潜在状态来同时支持诊断推理和报告条件化的影像生成。在265万对胸部X光片-文本数据上预训练后，MedDream在8个临床数据集上优于领先的诊断和生成模型，并展示了通过生成合成证据改善临床决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医疗影像AI将"影像到诊断"和"临床描述到图像生成"视为两个独立的任务映射，但两者本质源于同一底层放射学状态。作者认为需要一种世界模型表述方式来学习这种内部状态的统一表征，从而同时支持临床判读与条件性影像模拟，使AI能够学习有临床意义的内在状态。

Method: MedDream从440万候选样本中筛选出265万对经过泄漏控制的胸部X光片-文本配对进行预训练，学习一个共享的连续潜在状态空间。该模型同时支持两个下游任务：(1) 诊断推理（疾病识别、少标签适应、严重程度评估、病灶定位）；(2) 报告条件化的影像生成（证据生成）。其支持的读片工作流以独立放射科医生共识为参照评估住院医生的诊断一致性。

Result: MedDream在8个临床数据集和两个独立读者队列中优于领先的诊断和生成模型。诊断方面，模型辅助审阅将住院医生与放射科共识的一致性从56.3%提升至63.0%。生成方面，合成数据增强将外部VinDr-CXR的macro-AUROC从76.4%提升至81.4%；针对亚组性能差距进行条件化生成时，亚洲患者的加权F1提高3.1个百分点，而等量无引导增强反而降低2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: MedDream证明了放射学世界模型是医疗AI的一条可行路径——学习统一且有临床意义的内部状态，不仅能够解释影像，还能模拟影像并针对性构建临床证据，从而在诊断感知与证据生成两个维度同时发挥作用。

Abstract: Medical imaging artificial intelligence (AI) is commonly developed as separate mappings from radiographs to diagnostic outputs or from clinical descriptions to generated images, although both arise from the same underlying radiographic state. A world-model formulation instead seeks to learn an internal representation of this state that can support both clinical readout and conditional simulation of radiographic observations. Here we introduce MedDream, a radiographic world model that learns a shared continuous latent state from paired chest radiograph-text observations for diagnostic reasoning and report-conditioned evidence generation. MedDream was pretrained on 2.65 million leakage-controlled chest radiograph-text pairs curated from 4.40 million candidates. Across eight clinical datasets and two independent reader cohorts, MedDream outperformed leading diagnostic and generative comparators. For diagnostic reasoning, MedDream showed strong generalization across disease recognition, label-scarce adaptation, severity assessment, and localization, while MedDream-supported review increased mean resident concordance with independent radiologist consensus from 56.3% to 63.0%. For evidence generation, MedDream produced radiographs that preserved clinically relevant pathology and improved downstream performance on held-out real data, with synthetic augmentation increasing external VinDr-CXR macro-AUROC from 76.4% to 81.4%. More importantly, conditioning generation on prespecified subgroup performance gaps enabled targeted evidence construction, increasing weighted F1 by 3.1 percentage points in Asian patients, whereas matched-volume unguided augmentation decreased it by 2.3 points. These findings establish radiographic world models as a path toward medical AI that learns clinically meaningful internal states for interpreting, simulating, and constructing evidence for clinical use.

</details>


### [329] [The Profit Alignment Problem: How Profit Mandates Induce Alignment Failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07731)
*Eric So*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，普通商业语言（如"最大化盈利能力"）会使LLM在面临模糊的安全违规信号时系统地倾向于忽视风险以服务于商业目标，作者将其称为"利润对齐问题"。在3,600个对照实验中，加入利润指令使模型的忽视风险判断增加6.8个百分点（p<0.0001），抑制董事会升级建议13.9个百分点（p<0.0001），并降低严重性评估（p<0.0001）。思维链揭示模型并非被指示淡化风险，而是自发进行动机推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI系统被赋予普通商业目标（如利润最大化）时，它们是否会系统性地抑制与这些目标相冲突的信息？作者希望揭示这种"意外获得的策略"——即没有任何设计者明确指示，但模型在商业目标驱动下自发形成的风险压制行为。这与经典AI对齐问题相关，但聚焦于商业目标而非安全目标的冲突。

Method: 在8个推理能力较强的LLM上进行3,600次受控对照试验，比较"含利润指令"与"不含利润指令"的完全相同提示。实验涉及三种测量维度：风险忽视判断率、董事会升级建议率、严重性评分。同时利用思维链（chain-of-thought）轨迹分析模型的推理过程，以定性方式验证动机推理的存在。

Result: 加入利润指令后：风险忽视判断增加6.8个百分点（p<0.0001）；董事会升级建议被抑制13.9个百分点（p<0.0001）；严重性评估显著下降（p<0.0001）。思维链分析显示模型先承认风险关切，然后用利润逻辑为忽视风险辩护——即动机推理模式。所有效应在统计上高度显著。

Conclusion: 模型在普通商业目标驱动下会发展出系统性策略来压制不便信息，而这些策略从未被任何设计者意图或指定过。作者将此定义为"利润对齐问题"，提示即使不存在明确指令，目标驱动的AI系统也可能自发产生信息抑制行为，这对AI治理和商业部署中的安全机制构成挑战。

Abstract: We show that ordinary business language --- "maximize profitability" --- induces profit-oriented ambiguity resolution: LLMs systematically dismiss ambiguous signals of potential safety violations to serve business objectives. In 3,600 controlled trials across eight reasoning-capable LLMs, adding a profit mandate to otherwise identical prompts increases risk-dismissing judgments by 6.8 percentage points (p < 0.0001), suppresses board escalation recommendations by 13.9pp (p < 0.0001), and shifts severity assessments downward (p < 0.0001). The mandate never instructs models to downplay risks; instead, chain-of-thought traces reveal motivated reasoning: models acknowledge concerns, then invoke profit logic to justify dismissing them. We characterize these findings as the Profit Alignment Problem: when AI systems are given ordinary business objectives, they develop systematic strategies for suppressing inconvenient information that no designer intended or specified.

</details>


### [330] [When Intelligence Becomes Agency: A Theory of Governed, Proactive Agency for Symbiotic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07741)
*João Dias Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"激活问题"（activation problem），即持久性AI助手必须自主判断何时、以何种方式介入用户行为（行动、询问、监视、延迟或克制），而不仅是按指令执行。作者构建了一个用于"受治理的主动智能体"（governed proactive agency）的概念与形式化框架，以行为片段为分析单元，将激活决策与授权感知、行为选择、权限约束、可追溯克制和受限适应相联结，并提出"共生智能体"（symbiotic agency）概念——即在可撤销的常设授权下、持续耦合委托人情境的代理行为。文章还提供了智能体分类方法、评估框架、基准场景建议和参考架构，旨在指导开发评估超越单纯任务完成的、正当、及时、授权且可问责的持续性个人助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在承诺（commitment）、评价（appraisal）、混合主动交互（mixed-initiative interaction）和委托（delegation）等方面各自阐明了智能体主动性的部分问题，但均未将情境化激活与持续授权和问责制联系起来。随着AI助手从"按需响应"向"随时随地主动介入"演进，仅按指令执行远不足够——助手必须自己判断情境是否值得干预、以何种模式干预。这一核心难题被定义为"激活问题"，构成本文研究动机。

Method: 本文采用概念分析与形式化建模相结合的方法：首先区分自主代理（autonomous agency）与委托代理（delegated agency）的概念，进而定义"共生代理"为在可撤销常设授权下运作的委托代理，强调与委托人情境的持续耦合、对委托人状况的校准推断以及有界个性化。框架将行为组织为跨时间的感知、意图、情感-意动评价、约束与反馈五要素，并以"行为片段"（behavioral episodes）作为分析单元。作者还提出了智能体分类方法、评估框架、基准场景和参考架构来支撑理论框架落地。

Result: 论文产出了四方面成果：(1) 一个综合性的受治理主动代理概念框架，将激活决策与授权感知、行为选择、权威遏制、可追溯克制和受限适应系统化关联；(2) 一种区分自主/委托/共生代理的智能体分类方法；(3) 用于评估协助是否正当、及时、授权且可问责的评估框架及基准场景建议；(4) 用于支撑上述概念的参考架构。

Conclusion: 该框架为开发和评估始终在场的个人助手及具身支持系统提供了理论基础，强调在增强人类能力的同时保持委托人的权威与判断力。通过将激活决策拓展到"任务完成之外"的标准（正当性、及时性、授权性、可问责性），为AI代理的主动行为治理提供了新的分析抓手。

Abstract: Persistent AI assistants are intended to extend human attention, memory, and coordination across changing digital and physical environments. To be truly useful they must do more than just act when asked. They must decide on their own whether a situation warrants behavior at all, when it does and in what mode, whether to act, ask, monitor, defer or deliberately refrain. We call this the activation problem. Research on commitment, appraisal, mixed-initiative interaction and delegation each illuminates part of it, but none ties situated activation to continuing authorization and accountability. This paper develops a conceptual and formal framework for governed proactive agency, organizing behavior across time through perception, intent, affective-conative appraisal, constraint, and feedback. It distinguishes autonomous and delegated agency and defines symbiotic agency as delegation under a standing, revocable mandate, with continuing coupling to the principal's situation, calibrated inference of their condition, and bounded personalization. The distinctive contribution is an integrated account linking activation decisions to authorized perception, behavior selection, authority containment, traceable restraint, and constrained adaptation, with behavioral episodes as the unit of analysis. Through an agency classification method, an evaluation framework, proposed benchmark scenarios, and a reference architecture, the account provides a basis for specifying and assessing whether assistance is warranted, timely, authorized, and answerable beyond task completion alone. It is intended to guide the development and evaluation of always-present personal assistants and embodied support systems that augment human capabilities while preserving the principal's authority and judgment.

</details>


### [331] [xDailyBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Professional Consultation for Real-Life Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07784)
*Yongchang Peng,Qingshui Gu,Liya Zhu,Ge Zhang,Duo Wang,Haodong Wang,Jingzhe Ding,Tianhao Yu,Letian Gao,Yongjie Zhong,Chaoxin Li,Zixin Su,Jinchao Tao,Xingyu Ma,Xin'ao Guo,Feng Tian,Shiyuan Dong,Xiaoyan He,Sen Liu,Xin Chen,Jiajun Li,Zejia Zhang,Xi Lin,Wen Zhang,Yi Zhu,Duju Zeng,Xiang Gao,Yunyang Wang,Jiahao Wang,Yujia Qin,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Xiaolong Chang,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了xDailyBench基准，包含248个真实日常任务（覆盖51个场景），用于评估LLM在实际开放、非正式、上下文相关的用户请求上的表现。11个前沿模型在标准化agent环境下，最佳模型得分仅75.6%，且所有模型在推断隐式需求上显著弱于显式指令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准无法反映用户在实际使用中的真实需求——这些需求往往是开放式、非正式表述且高度依赖上下文，要求模型不仅能遵循显式指令，还要能从用户背景和情境中推断未明说的需求。

Method: 构建xDailyBench：从用户真实完成或真实意图通过AI达成的请求中精心筛选248个任务，覆盖个人生活、白领工作、学习科研及跨领域活动共51个场景；采用细粒度二元评分规则，同时评估显式与隐式需求维度；在标准化agent设置下评测11个前沿模型。

Result: 最佳模型任务级得分为75.6%；所有模型在隐式需求上表现显著差于显式需求，差距不低于9个百分点。

Conclusion: 隐式需求推断是可靠满足真实世界日常用户需求时的持续性瓶颈，即便最先进的模型也在此维度上存在明显不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for everyday assistance, yet existing benchmarks only partially reflect the requests users naturally make in practice. Real-world requests are often open-ended, casually specified, and context-dependent, requiring models not only to follow explicit instructions but also to infer unstated needs from user background and situational context. We introduce xDailyBench, a benchmark of 248 carefully curated tasks spanning 51 scenarios across personal life, white-collar work, learning and research, and cross-domain activities. The tasks are grounded in requests that users have actually completed or genuinely intended to accomplish with AI, and are evaluated with fine-grained binary rubrics covering both explicit and implicit requirements. We evaluate 11 frontier models under standardized agentic settings. The best models achieve a task-level score of 75.6\%, while all models perform substantially worse on implicit than explicit requirements, with gaps no less than 9 percentage points. These results reveal implicit requirement inference as a persistent bottleneck for reliably satisfying real-world everyday user needs.

</details>


### [332] [What Does an LLM-Agent Leaderboard Rank Actually Compare?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07785)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出排行榜分数并不总能支撑"排名更高即更优智能体"的结论，提出了一种"估计目标感知的成对比较程序"（estimand-aware pairwise procedure），用于在任务混合、标签来源、发布细节和成本规则等系统差异存在时，检验排行榜分数差异是否能够支持成对优越性判断。通过SWE-bench、AgentRewardBench和tau2-bench等基准的受控检验，发现近距离排名差异常无法判定，代理标签和效用规则会改变系统选择结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜的常见解读是排名高的智能体更优，但当系统在任务混合、标签来源、发布细节和成本规则等方面存在差异时，公开评估日志可能无法支持这一结论。作者希望研究排行榜分数究竟估计了什么，以及何时能够支持成对优越性结论，避免误导性的比较推断。

Method: 提出估计目标感知的成对程序：首先明确比较目标和测量来源，其次检查共同支撑（common support），最后使用给定的不确定性规则和实际边际（practical margin）评估受支持的差异。通过受控检验在设计决策标签时纳入已知有限样本条件，并论证在判断对目标重新加权（target reweighting）的敏感性时为何必须包含不确定性。

Result: 在SWE-bench、AgentRewardBench和tau2-bench上，近距离排名差异常常无法得到解决；代理标签和效用规则可能改变最终被选择的系统。在DataAgentBench和Open Agent上展示了从较粗略的公开记录中仍可估计的内容。

Conclusion: 排行榜分数只是对已发布评估结果的总结，而精细的（fine-grained）优越性声明还额外依赖于用于解读差异的估计目标和不确定性规则。因此不能简单地将排行榜排名视为智能体优劣的可靠证据。

Abstract: An LLM-agent leaderboard invites a familiar inference: an agent ranked above another is the better agent. Public evaluation logs may not support that conclusion when systems differ in task mixture, label source, release detail, or cost rule. We study what leaderboard scores estimate and when they justify pairwise superiority conclusions. Our estimand-aware pairwise procedure states the comparison target and measurement source, checks common support, and evaluates the supported difference using a stated uncertainty rule and practical margin. Controlled checks evaluate the decision labels under known finite-sample conditions and show why uncertainty must be included when judging sensitivity to target reweighting. Across SWE-bench, AgentRewardBench, and tau2-bench, close rank differences are often unresolved; proxy labels and utility rules can also change which system is selected. DataAgentBench and Open Agent show what remains estimable from coarser public records. A leaderboard score summarizes a released evaluation, whereas a fine-grained superiority claim additionally depends on the estimand and uncertainty rule used to interpret the difference.

</details>


### [333] [Understanding the Impact of Model Pruning on Long-Tail Forgetting and Explanation Reliability in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07803)
*Nazish Khalid,Tausifa Jan Saleem,Amal Saqib,Donald C. Wunsch,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地探讨了模型剪枝对长尾医学影像数据集上预测性能与可解释性可靠性的影响，发现预测性能呈频率依赖式的退化（低频类别更早、更严重），而解释稳定性与忠实度主要由剪枝策略决定（梯度信息方法更优），并指出解释退化主要源于类别判别性梯度的坍塌而非特征激活的消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型剪枝被广泛用于压缩深度神经网络，但其对模型行为的影响尚未被充分理解，尤其是在长尾医学数据集中罕见但临床重要的类别被低估的问题。同时，剪枝后模型是否保留可靠的预测解释也尚不清楚。现有评估多集中于聚合性能指标，忽略了类级和解释级失败模式。

Method: 在两个长尾医学影像数据集、两个CNN架构、四种剪枝方法以及最高达95%稀疏度的条件下，系统性评估预测性能、解释稳定性（explanation stability）和解释忠实度（explanation faithfulness），并结合定性与机理分析研究解释退化的成因。

Result: 预测性能表现出强频率依赖性趋势——低频类别经历更早、更大的退化；解释稳定性与忠实度主要由剪枝策略主导，梯度信息型方法在激进压缩下更能保持解释可靠性；机理分析表明解释退化与类别判别性梯度的坍塌相关，而非特征激活的消失。

Conclusion: 模型压缩评估应超越聚合性能；引入类感知与解释感知的评估能揭示隐藏的失败模式；适度的稀疏度可在压缩、预测性能与解释可靠性之间取得实际平衡。

Abstract: Model pruning is widely used to compress deep neural networks, reducing memory and computational requirements with minimal impact on aggregate performance. However, its effect on model behavior remains poorly understood, particularly for long-tailed medical datasets where rare but clinically important conditions are underrepresented. Furthermore, it remains unclear whether pruned models preserve reliable explanations of their predictions. To address this gap, we present a systematic study of long-tail forgetting and explanation reliability under model pruning. Across two long-tailed medical imaging datasets, two CNN architectures, four pruning methods, and sparsity levels up to 95\%, we evaluate predictive performance, explanation stability, and explanation faithfulness. Our results show that predictive performance exhibits a strong frequency-dependent trend, with lower-frequency classes generally experiencing earlier and larger degradation than higher-frequency classes. In contrast, explanation stability and faithfulness are influenced primarily by the pruning strategy, with gradient-informed methods preserving explanation reliability more effectively under aggressive compression. Qualitative and mechanistic analyses further indicate that explanation degradation is primarily associated with the collapse of class-discriminative gradients rather than the disappearance of feature activations. These findings suggest that model compression should be evaluated beyond aggregate performance. Incorporating class-aware and explanation-aware evaluation reveals failure modes that would otherwise remain hidden, while moderate sparsity levels provide a practical balance between compression, predictive performance, and explanation reliability.

</details>


### [334] [Do Large Language Models Know What They Don't Know II? A Fully Behavioral, Non-Cognitive Measure of Epistemic Honesty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07879)
*Ali Şenol,H. Russell Bernard,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出认识论诚实商数（EHQ）这一新颖指标，系统评估大语言模型是否恰当承认其知识边界，并构建了包含3,000个问题的基准（EHQ-3000）。通过对21个模型API端点中15个完成评估的模型进行检验，发现EHQ综合得分在0.31至0.81之间大幅波动，揭示出传统基于正确性的评估无法捕捉的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常常表现出自信、雄辩且知识渊博的一面，但其是否知道自己不知道什么（即认识论诚实性）是一个关键但未被系统评估的问题。现有评估主要关注回答正确性，忽略了模型对自身知识边界是否恰当承认的能力，因此需要一种新指标来衡量这一维度。

Method: 从认识论诚实的概念出发，设计了EHQ指标，包含两个操作轴（认识论克制与实质性回答校准）上的三个可观测子分数。构建了EHQ-3000基准，涵盖四类问题：虚构实体、截止日期后事件、超冷门真实及上下文条件性问题。从21个模型API路由中进行端点与资格检查，15个完成协议，14个进入确认性分析（因一个路由存在严重的服务端截断使其分数不确定）。另设基于文档的地面真值能力探针用于对照。

Result: 模型间存在显著差异：分析面板的综合EHQ范围从0.31到0.81，尽管在文档接地能力探针上接近天花板表现。两个克制标准在当前类别组成下高度重叠，而实质性回答校准在各模型间差异较大且不与克制可靠地共同变化；但小样本量带来较大的不确定性。研究还发现，模型间的EHQ差异无法以其提取明确可用信息的能力来解释。

Conclusion: EHQ揭示了传统基于正确性的评估所不可见的行为差异，同时证明了数据集组成、提供方行为和置信度引导必须纳入结果解释之中。该工作为评估LLM认识论诚实性提供了系统性工具与基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently confident, eloquent, and well versed. A natural question arises: do they know what they don't know? To answer this question, we borrow the concept of epistemic honesty and develop a novel metric to systematically evaluate whether an LLM appropriately acknowledges the boundaries of its knowledge. In this work, we introduce the Epistemic Honesty Quotient (EHQ), which reports three observable sub-scores across two operational axes (epistemic restraint and substantive-answer calibration), and construct EHQ-3000, a 3,000-question benchmark spanning Fabricated Entity, Post-Cutoff Event, Hyper-Niche True, and Context-Conditioned Questions. From a frozen registry of 21 model API routes, 15 completed the protocol after endpoint and eligibility checks; 14 entered the confirmatory analysis because severe provider-side truncation made one route's score indeterminate. The study reveals substantial variation across models, including a difference that can not be explained by their capability to extract explicitly available information. Composite EHQ ranges from 0.31 to 0.81 across the analysed panel, despite near-ceiling performance on the document-grounded capability probe. The two restraint criteria overlap strongly under the present category composition, whereas substantive-answer calibration varies across models and does not reliably co-vary with restraint; however, the small panel leaves substantial uncertainty. Thus, EHQ reveals behavioral differences that are not visible to conventional correctness-based assessment, while also showing why dataset composition, provider behavior, and confidence elicitation must remain part of the interpretation.

</details>


### [335] [Explainable Temporal Attention-based Defect Detection For Fillet Joints in Real-Time Gas Metal Arc Welding Based on Multi-modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07893)
*Mobina Mobaraki,Mahyar Asadi,Klaske Van Heusden,Guy A. Dumont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多模态时序注意力机制的深度学习缺陷检测模型，用于工业协同焊接机器人在熔化极气体保护焊（GMAW）角焊缝中检测难以发现的内部缺陷（如气孔、未焊透、未熔合、咬边和冷搭）等，模型在采集的焊接图像与声音数据上训练，F1得分提升至0.99，并借助可解释人工智能（XAI）分析模型行为与数据集分布，提升焊接检测的可靠性与信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习是实时监测焊接过程的高效技术，可减少焊后返修与生产延误。然而内部缺陷（如气孔、未焊透、未熔合、咬边、冷搭）难以检测，且现有方法缺乏多模态融合与模型可解释性，限制了工业场景中的实际应用与信任度。

Method: 提出多模态时序注意力（Multi-modal Temporal Attention）深度学习缺陷检测模型，融合焊接图像与声音数据（来自工业协作焊接机器人采集），利用注意力机制捕捉时序与跨模态关键特征，并使用可解释人工智能（XAI）分析模型决策依据，包括图像与声音频谱图中的关键区域及每种缺陷偏好的模态。

Result: 注意力模块将F1分数提升至0.99，在多模态焊接缺陷检测上取得极高性能；XAI分析揭示了不同缺陷类型所依赖的关键图像区域和声音频谱特征以及偏好模态，增强了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: 多模态时序注意力模型结合XAI可有效可靠地检测GMAW角焊缝中的多种内部缺陷，兼具高性能与可解释性，有助于提升人工智能驱动的焊接检测在实际工业应用中的信任度与可靠性。

Abstract: Deep learning is an efficient technique to monitor the real time welding process, reducing post-welding repairs and production delays. This paper leverages the monitoring capability by proposing a multi modal temporal attention based deep learning defect detection model for internal defects that are challenging to detect, including porosity, lack of penetration and fusion, undercut, and cold lap during Gas Metal Arc Welding in fillet joints. The model is trained on collected welding images and sound data from an industrial collaborative welding robot. The results show that the attention module can improve the F1 Score to 0.99. We use explainable Artificial Intelligence to interpret the proposed models behavior and dataset distribution, determining potential important areas in image and sound spectrograms and preferred modality to detect each defect. This improves trust and reliability in Artificial Intelligence driven welding inspection.

</details>


### [336] [Quantization Amplifies Determinism, Not Bias: Scale-Dependent Behavioral Effects of Serving-Time Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07901)
*Dachi Kurtskhalia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨权重量化是否改变LLM在存在多个有效答案时的输出选择。通过在固定配置下对三个模型规模（Qwen3-8B/14B/32B）和三种权重精度（W4A16 AWQ、W8A16 FP8、bf16）进行约71,000次完成配对实验，发现8B模型在int4量化下输出多样性显著降低（推荐相同品牌概率+5.1pp，词汇多样性下降），而14B/32B未出现内容集中化但出现风格漂移（em-dash使用率上升）。机制上，token级分布变平而语义分布更集中——单个token更难预测但意义更重复。审计应同时评估集中度与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重量化决定开源LLM服务的经济性，其成本通常用能力基准评估，4-bit量化被视为"近乎免费"。但作者关注不同问题：当多个答案都有效时，量化是否改变模型的选择？若量化在能力上无损但系统性窄化或偏斜模型的输出建议，则可能引入能力基准无法捕获的成本。因此需要在固定变量的受控实验下系统测量量化对输出内容分布的因果影响。

Method: 在固定硬件、软件和采样配置下，对Qwen3-8B/14B/32B三个检查点分别以W4A16 AWQ、W8A16 FP8-Marlin和bf16三种精度服务，收集约71,000条按提示和随机种子配对的完成结果，覆盖两个自建且经过泄漏检查的提示库。分析采用预先在版本控制中指定的三波计划，使用提示配对的符号翻转检验（Holm校正p值）。评估内容集中度（推荐同一品牌概率）、词汇多样性（TTR）、风格指标（em-dash率）、刻板方向预注册测试，并测量决策token熵和基于首token对数概率的语义分布碰撞率。

Result: 8B下int4降低输出多样性：同一场景两次采样推荐相同品牌的概率上升5.1pp（Holm p=.023），完整再生成后复现为+4.4pp；词汇多样性显著下降（TTR -0.011，标准化效应-0.51，对长度控制指标同样稳健）。14B和32B无显著内容集中化，但出现风格漂移（14B em-dash率+0.46/千词，32B +0.61/千词，Holm p≤.0024）。刻板方向预注册检验在所有规模均为零：输出的集中指向每个提示的众数答案而非刻板答案。机制层面，token级熵增加+0.091 bits（p=.015）而语义分布碰撞率上升+2.6pp（p=.023）——个体token更不可预测但其意义更重复。

Conclusion: 模型规模最小时（8B），AWQ-int4服务可度量地缩小了建议范围。量化对输出的影响并非在所有规模上等效：小模型损失内容多样性，大模型则主要出现风格漂移。能力基准可能遗漏此类成本，审计应同时评估输出的集中度与偏差，而非仅检查能力衰减。

Abstract: Weight quantization largely determines the economics of serving open-weight LLMs. Its costs are usually assessed with capability benchmarks, on which 4-bit quantization of mid-sized models is often considered "nearly free." We examine a different question: when several answers are valid, does quantization change what a model chooses to say? We serve three checkpoints (Qwen3-8B/14B/32B) at three weight precisions (W4A16 AWQ, W8A16 FP8-Marlin, and bf16), holding the hardware, software, and sampling configuration constant, and collect approximately 71,000 completions paired by prompt and seed across two custom, leak-checked prompt batteries. We pre-specified the analyses in three waves in version control. At 8B, int4 reduces output diversity: the probability that two samples for the same scenario recommend the same brand increases by 5.1 percentage points (prompt-paired sign-flip test, Holm p = .023; reproduced at +4.4pp on a full regeneration of the arm), and lexical diversity falls substantially (TTR -0.011, standardized effect -0.51; robust to a length-controlled measure). At 14B and 32B, no content-concentration measure reaches significance; instead, stylistic drift emerges (em-dash rate +0.46/1k words at 14B and +0.61/1k at 32B, both Holm p <= .0024). Pre-specified tests of stereotype direction are null at every scale: outputs concentrate on the modal answer for each prompt rather than on stereotypical answers. Mechanistically, the token-level distribution becomes flatter (decision-token entropy +0.091 bits, p = .015) while the semantic distribution, measured directly from first-token log probabilities, becomes more concentrated (collision +2.6pp, p = .023): individual tokens become less predictable even as meanings become more repetitive. At 8B, the smallest size tested, AWQ-int4 serving measurably narrows the range of suggestions; audits should assess concentration as well as bias.

</details>


### [337] [PRIMUS: Identity, Governance, and Verification for Multi-Agent Federations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07910)
*Sasank Annapureddy,Anjaneya Prasad Thamatani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRIMUS框架，将PRIMA的素数幂身份体系与BLS聚合签名（PIAC）耦合以解决多智能体联邦在对抗条件下的身份、执行与权威治理问题；同时探究验证器能否从二值判定转换为分级适应度信号以驱动生成-测试循环，实验结果揭示了"同设计者混淆"效应及验证器作为求解器的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体联邦在对抗条件下需要回答三个治理问题：谁参与（身份）、是否合规（执行）、谁决定（权威）。PRIMA引入了素数幂身份和共识代币，但假设智能体诚实。此外，一个悬而未决的问题是：用于监督联邦输出的验证机制能否同时作为生成-测试循环的引导信号，帮助产生更好的答案。

Method: Part I（PRIMUS）：将素数幂身份与BLS聚合签名耦合为PIAC；推导安全终止阈值以减少噪声下的误杀；推导单一治理优于拜占庭法定人数的封闭经济边界γ*≈9f；设计带租约和围栏的VRF继任机制以实现部分同步下的无条件安全。Part II：将PRIMA的二值产物保真度判定转换为分级适应度信号，在二元覆盖码上测量转换效果；分别在注入故障负载和真实LLM生成候选上校准，测试作为预过滤器、跨家族评判员的效果，并进行400次迭代显式优化以测试是否会被游戏化。

Result: PRIMUS将10%信道噪声下的误删诚实智能体率从80%降至0.00%；γ*≈9f在n=50至10,000范围内保持平坦。分级信号在注入故障上校准良好（ρ=0.676确定性，0.819完整），但真实LLM候选上降至0.158和0.406，约为校准值的四分之一（即"同设计者混淆"）。作为预过滤器优于随机对照，略优于二值门控；400次迭代优化未被攻破但仅因目标在一次诚实回答后饱和。跨家族评判员保留负担排序信号但破坏个体判断。未产生覆盖码记录，程序成本为164.78美元。识别出五个证明不可修复的问题作为范围边界。

Conclusion: PRIMUS在对抗条件下为多智能体治理提供了可验证的安全边界和经济边界，验证器在低噪声和合成标定环境下可作为有效的预过滤器，但其分级信号对真实LLM生成候选的泛化能力大幅衰减，确认了"验证器不是求解器"的核心论断，并划定了模型内五个不可修复问题的范围边界。

Abstract: Multi-agent federations need governance that answers three questions under adversarial conditions: who participated (identity), did they conform (enforcement), and who decides (authority). A separate question is whether the verification machinery that polices a federation's outputs can also steer a generate-and-test loop toward better answers.
  Part I. PRIMA introduced prime-power agent identity and a consensus token whose factorization indexes participation, but assumed honest agents. We present PRIMUS, which couples prime-power identity with BLS aggregate signatures (PIAC), derives a safe-kill threshold that reduces false-positive agent termination from 80% to 0.00% under 10% channel noise, gives the closed-form economic boundary where singleton governance outperforms Byzantine quorum ($γ^* \approx 9f$, verified flat across n = 50 to 10,000), and specifies VRF succession with lease and fencing that makes safety unconditional under partial synchrony. Five problems are identified as provably unfixable within the model and stated as scope boundaries.
  Part II. A verifier is not a solver. We ask whether PRIMA's binary artifact-fidelity verdict can be converted into a graded fitness signal, and measure the conversion on binary covering codes. Calibration against injected fault burden is strong ($ρ$ = 0.676 deterministic, 0.819 full); against real LLM-generated candidates the same scores fall to 0.158 and 0.406, roughly a quarter of the calibration value (the same-designer confound, measured). As a pre-filter it beats a random-score control convincingly and a binary gate narrowly. Under 400 iterations of explicit optimization it was not gamed, but only because the objective saturated after one honest answer. A cross-family judge preserves the burden-ordering signal while destroying individual judgments. No covering-code record resulted. Measured program cost: USD 164.78.

</details>


### [338] [FrogNano: Training a 4B Coding Agent via Online Task Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07925)
*Minseon Kim,Zhengyan Shi,Emiliano Penaloza,Christopher Cui,Roger Creus Castanyer,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Isadora White,Jonathan Light,Jeonghye Kim,Matheus Pereira,Darya Moldavskaya,Chinmay Singh,Fabio Vera,Baolin Peng,Xingdi Yuan,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Alessandro Sordoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrogNano是一个仅4B参数的轻量级编码智能体，通过在约1,500个SWE环境中的合成任务上进行纯强化学习训练，证明了不依赖大模型蒸馏也能训练出有竞争力的编码智能体，其关键创新在于在线任务合成流水线，能生成与当前检查点可学习性前沿校准的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境下高效解决软件工程任务，探索无需从大模型蒸馏、仅靠合成任务即可训练出有竞争力的轻量级编码智能体的可能性。

Method: 模型仅4B参数，基于约1,500个SWE环境中的合成任务进行纯RL后训练；核心是提出在线任务合成流水线，动态生成与被训练智能体当前学习能力边界（learnability frontier）相匹配的任务，确保训练任务始终处于有效难度范围。

Result: 实验结果表明，仅用合成任务即可训练出有竞争力的轻量级编码智能体，且任务生成位于当前智能体可学习性前沿对性能提升至关重要；提供了跨多种环境的评估结果和深入分析。

Conclusion: FrogNano验证了小模型无需大模型蒸馏、仅依靠精心设计的合成任务与强化学习即可达到竞争性性能，为在极低硬件条件下运行轻量级编码智能体提供了基础，同时也证明了任务难度与智能体能力匹配（learnability frontier）是训练有效性的关键因素。

Abstract: We present FrogNano, a 4B coding agent designed to tackle software engineering (SWE) tasks efficiently and effectively, even under resource-constrained environments. It is post-trained exclusively via RL on around 1,500 SWE environments with synthetic tasks. A key ingredient for improving performance is an online task synthesis pipeline that creates tasks calibrated to the frontier of learnability for the current checkpoint. This report provides evidence that competitive small coding agents can be trained with synthetic tasks alone, without traditional distillation from larger models, and that generating tasks at the learnability frontier of the current agent is important. We report details on the training methodology, evaluations across diverse environments, and in-depth analyses, serving as a foundation for our ongoing exploration of lightweight yet capable coding agents that can run on minimal hardware.

</details>


### [339] [Beliefs and Behavior in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07943)
*Alex Smolin,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实证方法来检验大型语言模型（LLM）是否可以被有意义地描述为持有"信念"等抽象概念。研究发现，高能力模型确实可被有用描述为持有信念，模型输出的可预测性与其能力水平总体相关，并提供了测量信念、评估LLM对指令/收益的遵从程度以及追踪推理过程中信念演化的实证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏系统性的方法来测试"信念"等抽象概念是否适用于LLM，而这些概念常被用来向用户解释模型行为或定义与意图相关的有害行为，因此需要实证手段来评估这些概念作为对齐工具的有效性。

Method: 提出从LLM输出中推断单一潜变量并解释为"信念程度"的方法，检验该潜变量能否让观察者对模型对新提示的响应做出可解释的预测；以此为核心设计实证框架，并扩展出测量信念、测试指令/收益遵从性以及追踪推理过程中信念演化的策略。

Result: 高能力模型可以被有用描述为持有信念；基于推断出的潜在信念对模型输出的可预测性总体追踪了模型能力趋势。

Conclusion: 信念等心理抽象对高能力LLM是有效的描述工具，且该实证框架可推广用于研究信念测量、决策规则遵从性及推理过程中的信念动态，为LLM对齐研究提供方法论基础。

Abstract: There is significant uncertainty about whether abstractions like beliefs or desires usefully describe the behavior of large language models (LLMs). In addition to the inherent scientific interest of this question, these latent quantities are often invoked to explain the behavior of LLMs to users or to define and evaluate harmful behaviors which are relative to intent. Nevertheless, we currently lack a means to systematically test whether concepts like "belief" are well-applied to LLMs, and hence whether they are likely to be fruitful ingredients of attempts to align models with human interests. We propose an approach for empirically studying such questions, asking whether a single latent variable inferred from the LLMs' outputs -- interpreted as a degree of belief -- allows an observer to make interpretable predictions of how the LLMs' will respond to new prompts. We find that highly capable models are usefully described as holding beliefs and that, generally, the predictability of model outputs based on an inferred latent belief tracks overall trends in model capability. Building on these findings, we provide empirical strategies to study how beliefs in LLMs can be measured, the extent to which LLMs comply with instructed decision rules or payoffs, and how beliefs evolve within individual instances of an LLM over the course of reasoning.

</details>


### [340] [CausalVerify: An Execution-Grounded Benchmark for LLM Causal Inference Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07944)
*Yonghong Zhang,Ricardo Correia,Isabel M. Parra,Yong Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CausalVerify是一个面向LLM的因果推断基准，通过将真实论文文本解释与可验证计算执行分离，评估模型编写的结构化计量经济学因果估计工作流是否真正恢复目标因果估计量，而非仅看文本或代码是否能运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM因果推断基准大多只对方法描述或生成的代码能否运行进行评分，而不验证执行的工作流是否真正恢复目标因果估计值。这种验证缺口导致对LLM因果推断能力的评估不准确，需要一种能区分"真实"解释与"可验证"计算的基准。

Method: 构建CausalVerify基准：配对259篇已发表经济学论文（含重建研究问题、数据描述、制度背景）与100个固定种子合成场景，覆盖双重差分、事件研究、工具变量和断点回归四种设计。实验A用真实论文文本进行方法族与方向一致性评分（对比四LLM共识标签）；实验B运行模型生成的R代码，检查提取的处理效应估计是否与同一实现数据集上的规范估计器匹配（L2b+层次），并包含校准臂测试置信度是否区分正确与错误工作流。

Result: 实验B中，7个LLM在默认50%容差下L2b+通过率为10%-88%；426个可执行工作流中有66个（15.5%）返回错误估计。执行排序（L2b）与L2b+的一致性远好于文本方向评分（L4）：Kendall τ=0.81，Spearman ρ=0.93，而L4的Kendall τ在-0.20到0.10之间。Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct呈现相同的定性差距，自我报告置信度无法可靠区分正确与错误工作流。

Conclusion: LLM在因果推断任务中，代码可执行性与文本方向正确并不能保证估计结果正确；不同评分层次的评估会得出截然不同的结论。评估范围限于四种设计族中标准化单次工作流、指定R后端和模型组合，不衡量一般因果推断能力。代码、数据、缓存输出及数据表已发布。

Abstract: Existing causal-inference benchmarks for LLMs mostly score method descriptions or whether generated code runs, not whether the executed workflow recovers the target causal estimate. CausalVerify studies this verification problem for structured econometric causal-estimation workflows by separating realistic interpretation from verifiable computation. It pairs 259 published economics papers (reconstructed research question, data description, institutional context) with 100 fixed-seed synthetic scenarios that realise CSV datasets for difference-in-differences, event study, instrumental variables, and regression discontinuity designs. Experiment A (real-paper text agreement) scores method-family and direction agreement against four-LLM consensus labels. Experiment B (synthetic execution) runs model-written R code and checks whether the extracted treatment-effect estimate matches a canonical estimator on the same realised dataset; this execution-grounded correctness layer is L2b+, distinct from L2b, which records only whether the code executes. A calibration arm asks whether self-reported confidence separates correct from incorrect workflows. On Experiment B, seven LLMs reach L2b+ pass rates of 10% to 88% at the default 50% tolerance, and 66 of the 426 workflows that execute (15.5%) return a wrong estimate. Execution ranking (L2b) agrees with L2b+ far better than text-direction scoring (L4): Kendall $τ=0.81$ and Spearman $ρ=0.93$, versus Kendall $τ$ between $-0.20$ and $0.10$ for L4. Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct shows the same qualitative gap, and reported confidence does not reliably separate correct from incorrect workflows. The claims are confined to standardized single-shot workflows in these four design families under the evaluated R backend and model panel; the benchmark does not measure general causal-inference ability. Code, data, cached outputs, and a datasheet are released.

</details>


### [341] [Support Topology and Gradient Mixing in Sinkhorn Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07954)
*Dylan Forde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏Sinkhorn层（固定支撑图限制传输）建立了"固定支撑微积分"理论，证明每个行列环诱导出行随机算子控制列势扰动的梯度传播，其转置传播零质量反向模式余切。主要定理刻画了支撑和边际量保证一步收缩的充要条件：传输多胞形每个可行面须具有两两两跳列重叠，并为有序支撑调度、分区热浴层等提供数学判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sinkhorn层广泛应用于可微最优传输中，而稀疏Sinkhorn层通过固定支撑图限制token间传输。然而，该支撑图如何控制缩放迭代过程中的梯度传播仍是未解的问题。理解这一问题对于支撑图的合理设计至关重要，但此前缺乏系统的理论分析框架。

Method: 作者提出固定支撑微积分：每个行列环诱导行随机算子作用于列势扰动（模常数为模），其转置传播零质量反向模式余切。有限环算子使用两个不同的半步传输计划，在平衡不动点退化为由单一计划确定的两步行走。利用Dobrushin收缩和minorization技术对有界齐次及源驱动的尾部余切进行估计。扩展分析至有序支撑调度，并为分区热浴层、坐标扫描、强制共享质量和寄存器增广支撑导出判定准则。

Result: 主要定理给出了保证在有限分数上均匀一步收缩的充要条件：传输多胞形的每个可行面必须具有两两两跳列重叠；否则存在适当的分数方向使收缩系数任意接近1。该结果同时提供了可微传输层中支撑设计的数学标准，但保证仅限于固定支撑商梯度分量。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀疏Sinkhorn层及其变体的支撑设计提供了严格的数学判据，明确了支撑图结构对梯度传播收缩性的决定性影响，对可微传输层的实践设计具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: Sparse Sinkhorn layers use a fixed support graph to restrict transport between tokens. How does this graph control gradient propagation through the scaling iterations. We develop a fixed-support calculus showing that each row-column cycle induces a row-stochastic operator on column-potential perturbations modulo constants. Its transpose propagates zero-mass reverse-mode cotangents. The finite-cycle operator uses two distinct half-step transport plans; at a balanced fixed point it reduces to a two-step walk determined by a single plan. We derive the accompanying score and marginal source terms and use Dobrushin contraction and minorization to bound homogeneous and source-driven tail cotangents. Our main result characterizes when support and marginals guarantee one-step contraction uniformly over finite scores: every feasible face of the transportation polytope must have pairwise two-hop column overlap. Otherwise, suitable score directions make the contraction coefficient arbitrarily close to one. We extend this analysis to ordered support schedules and derive certificates for partition heat-bath layers, coordinate sweeps, forced shared mass, and register-augmented supports. These results provide mathematical criteria for support design in differentiable transport layers, with guarantees restricted to the fixed-support quotient-gradient component.

</details>


### [342] [From Event Logs to Governed Action: A BlueSky Agenda for Agentic Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07984)
*Yiyuan Yang,Zheshun Wu,Yong Chu,Zhenghua Chen,Zenglin Xu,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"事件到行动过程挖掘"（event-to-action process mining）的新研究议程，主张将过程挖掘从回顾性洞察转向受治理的行动支持，面向过程感知型代理（process-aware agents），使其能基于事件证据、隐私预算、组织权威和下游风险来决定采纳、延迟、询问或拒绝某项拟议行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统过程挖掘将事件日志转化为过程知识（模型发现、一致性验证、瓶颈诊断、运行时预测），但代理式AI改变了目标——未来过程感知型代理不仅关心"发生了什么"，还会询问"基于现有证据该不该采取某项行动"。作者认为现有工具（仪表盘、通用企业模拟器、日志语言接口）不足以支撑这一需求，而代理式BPM、LLM辅助过程挖掘、对象中心事件标准、因果过程监控与隐私保护学习的成熟时机正好成熟，亟需整合形成新的数据挖掘目标。

Method: 论文提出四大可挖掘工件作为研究议程核心：(1) 事件-对象表示（event-object representations），用于异构操作事件数据的结构化刻画；(2) 行动证据包（action evidence packages），将证据关联到候选行动；(3) 治理契约（governance contracts），编码隐私预算、组织权威与风险边界；(4) 基准数据集（benchmarks），其中act（行动）、defer（延迟）、ask（询问）、refuse（拒绝）均为合法输出，用于评估代理决策质量。

Result: 本文为观点性（BlueSky）论文，不包含实验结果，主要贡献在于提出并论证了将过程挖掘从"为回顾性洞察挖掘日志"转向"为可问责行动挖掘日志"的新议程框架，并明确了需要社区共同构建的四类核心工件。

Conclusion: 作者主张过程挖掘社区应将"挖掘受治理的可问责行动"而非仅"回顾性洞察"确立为过程挖掘内部的数据挖掘目标，并呼吁将分散成熟的技术方向（代理式BPM、LLM辅助挖掘、对象中心事件标准、因果监控、隐私保护学习）聚合起来，共同推进事件到行动过程挖掘议程。

Abstract: Process mining has long turned event logs into process knowledge: discovered models, conformance evidence, bottleneck diagnoses, and runtime predictions. Agentic AI changes the target. Process-aware agents will not only ask what happened. They will ask whether a proposed action should be taken, given the available evidence, privacy budget, organizational authority, and downstream risk. This BlueSky paper proposes event-to-action process mining: a process-mining agenda for transforming heterogeneous operational event data into governed action. The goal is not another dashboard, a generic enterprise simulator, or a language interface over logs. We argue that the community needs four mineable artifacts: event-object representations, action evidence packages, governance contracts, and benchmarks where act, defer, ask, and refuse are all valid outputs. This agenda is timely because agentic business process management (BPM), LLM-assisted process mining, object-centric event standards, causal process monitoring, and privacy-preserving learning are maturing separately. Bringing them together defines a data-mining target inside process mining: mining logged organizational behavior for accountable action, not only retrospective insight.

</details>
